      }
      for(let end of [0,L]){let x=x0+end;poly('#5e6554',[[x-14,cy-12],[x+14,cy-12],[x+17,cy+11],[x-16,cy+11]]);for(let i=0;i<3;i++){r('#a29d80',x-12+i*2,cy-9+i*6,23-i*4,4);r('#535d51',x-12+i*2,cy-5+i*6,23-i*4,1);}}
      if(stage<=1){for(let i=0;i<23;i++){let x=x0+(i%2?1:0)*L+(rng()-.5)*25, yy=cy-15+rng()*26;poly('#615e4f',[[x-4,yy],[x-2,yy-4],[x+4,yy-3],[x+5,yy+2],[x-2,yy+3]]);r('#a5a28b',x-2,yy-3,4,1);}}
      if(stage===1||stage===2)for(let end of [0,L]){let x=x0+end;for(let q of [-17,17]){r('#483c2b',x+q,cy-29,2,19);r('#d7bd83',x+q,cy-29,1,18);}ln('#b5a67d',x-17,cy-23,x+17,cy-23,1);r('#e8d4a4',x-3,cy-25,6,4);}
      return;
    }
    const xs=Array.from({length:Math.floor(L/8)+1},(_,i)=>x0+i*8);
    if(layer==='deck'){
      if(stage<5){for(let end of [0,L]){let x=x0+end;for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const q=x+(end?-i:i)*7;ln('#403f32',q,cy-14,q+(end?-6:6),cy-23+i*2,4);ln('#826b4c',q,cy-15,q+(end?-5:5),cy-23+i*2,2);}}return;}
      // The wooden truss follows the very same height function as the player.
      for(let i=1;i<xs.length;i++){let a=xs[i-1],b=xs[i],ya=y(a),yb=y(b);poly('#493d30',[[a,ya-14],[b,yb-14],[b,yb+22],[a,ya+22]]);poly('#776044',[[a,ya+11],[b,yb+11],[b,yb+19],[a,ya+19]]);ln('#c3a175',a,ya+11,b,yb+11,2);if(stage>=6){poly('#8b7658',[[a,ya-15],[b,yb-15],[b,yb+10],[a,ya+10]]);ln(i%3===0?'#d8b584':'#b79a70',a+1,ya-13,b-1,yb-13,2);ln('#4e4938',a,ya-14,a,ya+9);for(let j=0;j<2;j++){const g=a+2+rng()*3;ln('#66563e',g,ya-7+j*9,g+3,ya-6+j*9);}}}
      for(let i=0;i<xs.length-4;i+=4){let a=xs[i],b=xs[i+4];ln('#3c342c',a,y(a)+21,b,y(b)+12,5);ln('#a18156',a,y(a)+19,b,y(b)+11,2);ln('#4b3d31',a,y(a)+12,b,y(b)+21,3);r('#bfaa7d',a-1,y(a)+12,3,3);}
      if(stage>=7)for(let i=0;i<xs.length;i+=3){let a=xs[i];r('#484335',a-2,y(a)-28,4,17);r('#c3aa7a',a-1,y(a)-28,2,14);if(i>=3){let p=xs[i-3];ln('#594732',p,y(p)-25,a,y(a)-25,3);ln('#c6a575',p,y(p)-26,a,y(a)-26,1);}}
      return;
    }
    if(stage<7)return;
    // Near railing occludes the player's feet on the upper deck, but not the head.
    for(let i=0;i<xs.length;i+=3){let a=xs[i];r('#413b2c',a-2,y(a)-4,5,19);r('#ad8c5d',a-1,y(a)-4,2,17);r('#dec393',a-2,y(a)-5,5,2);if(i>=3){let p=xs[i-3];ln('#514334',p,y(p)+1,a,y(a)+1,4);ln('#c19f70',p,y(p),a,y(a),1);ln('#746043',p,y(p)+9,a,y(a)+9,2);}}
    // A physical closed-work sign becomes an open trail sign at operational stage.
    for(let end of [0,L]){const x=x0+end,dir=end?-1:1;const sx=x-dir*13,sy=cy-6;r('#3c3d30',sx-1,sy-26,4,28);r('#aa8e63',sx,sy-25,2,25);poly('#554939',[[sx-10,sy-29],[sx+10,sy-29],[sx+14,sy-23],[sx+10,sy-17],[sx-10,sy-17]]);poly('#a38a62',[[sx-9,sy-28],[sx+9,sy-28],[sx+12,sy-23],[sx+9,sy-18],[sx-9,sy-18]]);ln('#d2b686',sx-7,sy-27,sx+7,sy-27);r(stage>=8?'#4b795d':'#a78554',sx-6,sy-25,12,5);r('#e8d5ae',sx-4,sy-23,8,1);if(stage===7){ln('#5c4733',x-10,cy-16,x+10,cy+4,4);ln('#dbb37c',x-9,cy-16,x+9,cy+3,1);}}
    if(stage===9)for(let q of [x0+80,x0+240]){r('#3c4b43',q-2,y(q)-30,4,28);r('#c19b64',q-3,y(q)-34,6,6);r('#f7d68b',q-2,y(q)-33,4,4);r('#48554b',q-4,y(q)-35,8,2);}
  }
  function skywayVisual(scene,view,w,t,reduced){const b=C.SKYWAY,stage=root.LQValleyDevelopment.skywayState(w).stage,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView;
    const show=!scene.inside&&rect.right>(b.left-2)*T&&rect.x<(b.right+2)*T&&rect.bottom>(b.y-5)*T&&rect.y<(b.y+3)*T;
    if(!show){for(const s of view.skywaySprites||[])s.setVisible(false);return;}
    const X=b.left*T-20,Y=b.y*T-105,W=(b.right-b.left)*T+40,H=150;
    if(!view.skywaySprites){view.skywaySprites=['ground','deck','rail'].map((l,i)=>{const key=texture(scene,'skyway-'+l+'-'+stage,W,H,c=>skywayPaint(c,stage,l));const s=scene.add.image(X,Y,key).setOrigin(0,0).setDepth([view.depthAt(b.y*T)-.15,17,19][i]);scene.areaObjs.push(s);return s;});}
    view.skywaySprites.forEach((s,i)=>{const l=['ground','deck','rail'][i],key=texture(scene,'skyway-'+l+'-'+stage,W,H,c=>skywayPaint(c,stage,l));if(s.texture.key!==key)s.setTexture(key);s.setVisible(true);});
    const g=view.effects,light=view.light,night=w.clock.minuteOfDay<375||w.clock.minuteOfDay>=1170;
    if(stage>=5&&!night&&w.weather.kind!=='rain'){const cast=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,(w.clock.minuteOfDay-760)/375));const pts=[];for(let i=0;i<=20;i++){const xx=b.left+(b.right-b.left)*i/20,z=C.skywayHeight(xx);pts.push({x:xx*T+cast*z*.52,y:b.y*T+z*.25-12});}for(let i=20;i>=0;i--){const xx=b.left+(b.right-b.left)*i/20,z=C.skywayHeight(xx);pts.push({x:xx*T+cast*z*.52,y:b.y*T+z*.25+14});}view.shadows.fillStyle(0x273c33,w.weather.kind==='cloudy'?.10:.20).fillPoints(pts,true);}
    if(scene._lqSurface==='skyway'&&scene.pMoving&&!reduced){const x=scene.player.x,y=scene.player.y-C.skywayHeight(x/T),q=(t*7)%1;for(let i=0;i<3;i++)g.fillStyle(0xd8c6a0,(1-q)*.24).fillRect(Math.floor(x+(i-1)*(2+q*5)),Math.floor(y+q*3),2,1);}
    // Wind-driven pennants at the approaches, plus a trail of warm lantern light.
    if(stage>=7)for(const side of ['west','east']){const p=C.skywayAnchor(side),x=p.x+(side==='west'?7:-7),y=p.y-19,phase=reduced?0:Math.round(Math.sin(t*4+(side==='east'?2:0))*3);g.fillStyle(0x524b38).fillRect(x,y-14,2,24);g.fillStyle(0xcaa969).fillRect(x,y-15,3,2);g.fillStyle(0x758f83).fillPoints([{x:x+2,y:y-13},{x:x+16,y:y-10+phase},{x:x+13,y:y-5+phase},{x:x+2,y:y-3}],true);g.fillStyle(0xeee1b0).fillRect(x+4,y-10,5,1);}
    if(stage===9&&night)for(let x of [107,112]){const px=x*T,py=b.y*T-C.skywayHeight(x)-32;for(let i=4;i>=1;i--)light.fillStyle(0xfbd18a,.025+(5-i)*.012).fillCircle(px,py,i*8);g.fillStyle(0xffe0a0,.9).fillRect(px-1,py-1,3,3);}
  }

  // World portals and the underground room share the authored collision silhouette.
  function tunnelPortalPaint(ctx,stage,side){
    if(side==='north'){
      const r=random(318),x=72,y=88;shadow(ctx,x,y+3,74,17);const bank=[[29,67],[39,53],[62,47],[92,49],[111,62],[113,84],[91,91],[48,91],[28,82]];shape(ctx,'#4b5f5c',bank);shape(ctx,'#87918a',[[33,66],[44,55],[64,50],[91,53],[104,64],[94,69],[52,69]]);shape(ctx,'#253d43',[[44,62],[98,62],[99,86],[47,86]]);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){box(ctx,i%2?'#4f6465':'#627776',48,63+i*4,48,4);box(ctx,'#a6ad94',49,63+i*4,46,1);}for(const a of [36,101])for(let i=0;i<3;i++){masonryBlock(ctx,a,62+i*8,11,8,stage>=2?'#959a84':'#707973');}
      if(stage<3){for(let i=0;i<4-stage;i++)shape(ctx,'#64716c',[[48+i*12,78],[55+i*12,65],[65+i*12,72],[68+i*12,83]]);line(ctx,'#796e54',45,70,100,82,stage===0?5:2);}else for(const a of [42,102]){box(ctx,'#524c3b',a-2,54,7,19);box(ctx,'#f2cc85',a,55,3,7);line(ctx,'#c1ac7d',a-3,51,a+4,51,2);}for(let i=0;i<18;i++){const a=25+r()*94,b=90+r()*8;box(ctx,pick(['#73817b','#9ba797','#4b6260'],r),a,b,3+r()*4,2);}return;
    }const rng=random(511+side.length),x=72,y=88;shadow(ctx,x,y+7,107,24);const rock=[[12,86],[13,61],[23,45],[29,22],[43,12],[59,15],[75,5],[99,14],[117,31],[125,54],[132,86]];shape(ctx,'#39494d',rock);shape(ctx,'#697775',[[12,69],[23,45],[29,22],[43,12],[59,15],[75,5],[99,14],[117,31],[113,44],[92,32],[63,32],[43,47],[31,76]]);for(let i=0;i<46;i++){let a=21+rng()*102,b=25+rng()*55;shape(ctx,pick(['#546667','#465658','#7b8980','#596b68'],rng),[[a,b],[a+6+rng()*12,b-4],[a+12,b+3],[a+4,b+7]]);}const opening=[[43,87],[43,49],[51,34],[64,28],[82,28],[94,36],[101,49],[101,87]];shape(ctx,'#1d2830',opening);shape(ctx,'#101c23',[[51,87],[51,53],[59,40],[82,39],[92,53],[92,87]]);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const a=45+i*9;box(ctx,'#84908a',a,84,9,2);}if(stage>=2){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){masonryBlock(ctx,32,50+i*9,13,9,'#8b9184');masonryBlock(ctx,99,50+i*9,13,9,'#7c857b');}for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const a=Math.PI+i*Math.PI/6;const px=72+Math.cos(a)*32,py=49+Math.sin(a)*26;shape(ctx,i%2?'#8f9b88':'#a4a797',[[px-5,py-4],[px+7,py-4],[px+8,py+5],[px-4,py+8]]);}box(ctx,'#b3af97',66,21,12,10);}else{for(let i=0;i<6-stage*3;i++){const a=44+i*10,b=83-i%3*5;shape(ctx,'#5c6968',[[a-7,b],[a,b-13],[a+12,b-8],[a+15,b+4]]);line(ctx,'#a1a69a',a,b-11,a+9,b-7,1);}if(!stage){line(ctx,'#75674e',45,56,96,84,5);line(ctx,'#ab9570',46,54,96,82,1);}}
    if(stage>=3){for(const a of [28,116]){box(ctx,'#493b2a',a-3,53,7,13);box(ctx,'#fbd58d',a-2,55,5,8);box(ctx,'#e6b66a',a-4,51,9,2);}}
    // Asymmetric talus apron; transparent outside the stone silhouette.
    for(let i=0;i<23;i++){const a=13+rng()*118,b=90+rng()*9;shadow(ctx,a,b,5,2);box(ctx,pick(['#72807c','#9ba89a','#4a5b5c'],rng),a,b-3,3+rng()*5,2+rng()*3);}
  }
  function tunnelViews(scene,view,w,t,reduced){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,stage=D.tunnelState(w).stage,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,cache=view.tunnelSprites??=new Map();for(const p of D.tunnelPortals){const x=p.x*T,y=p.y*T,visible=!scene.inside&&x>rect.x-120&&x<rect.right+120&&y>rect.y-140&&y<rect.bottom+140;let a=cache.get(p.id);if(!visible){if(a)a.setVisible(false);continue;}const key=texture(scene,'tunnel-portal-'+p.id+'-'+stage,144,108,c=>tunnelPortalPaint(c,stage,p.id));if(!a){a=scene.add.image(x,y-8,key).setOrigin(.5,88/108).setDepth(view.depthAt(y-8));scene.areaObjs.push(a);cache.set(p.id,a);}a.setVisible(true).setTexture(key);if(stage===3){for(const off of [-44,44]){view.light.fillStyle(0xf7d087,.10).fillCircle(x+off,y-42,14);view.light.fillStyle(0xffdf9e,.5).fillRect(x+off-1,y-40,3,3);}}}}
  function releaseTunnel(view){if(!view?.tunnelSprites)return;for(const a of view.tunnelSprites.values()){const i=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(a);if(i>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(a.scene)a.destroy();}view.tunnelSprites.clear();}
  function cavePaint(ctx,l){const W=l.w*T,H=l.h*T,rng=random(19319),p=(x,y)=>[x*T,y*T];box(ctx,'#17252d',0,0,W,H);
    const top=l.top.map((v,i)=>[i*T,v*T]),bottom=l.bottom.map((v,i)=>[i*T,v*T]);top.push([W,l.top.at(-1)*T]);bottom.push([W,l.bottom.at(-1)*T]);const edge=(arr)=>arr.flatMap((p,i)=>i?[[p[0],arr[i-1][1]],[p[0],p[1]]]:[p]);const a=edge(top),b=edge(bottom);const floor=[...a,...b.slice().reverse()];shape(ctx,'#59645e',floor);ctx.save();shape(ctx,'#59645e',floor);ctx.clip();
    // Mineral bands and inset granite slabs, varied deterministic shapes rather than a repeated tile.
    for(let i=0;i<290;i++){let x=rng()*W,y=65+rng()*(H-65),ww=8+rng()*27,hh=4+rng()*12;shape(ctx,pick(['#626e65','#697267','#52645e','#536362','#6c7569'],rng),[[x,y],[x+ww*.6,y-2],[x+ww,y+hh*.3],[x+ww*.8,y+hh],[x+3,y+hh-1]]);if(i%3===0)line(ctx,'#87908255',x+2,y,x+ww*.6,y-2,1);}
    // A worn passage from the south stair to the north stair.
    const route=[[1.7,7.8],[4,7.2],[6.5,6.7],[8.5,5.6],[12,4.7],[15,5.8],[18,7.2],[20.5,6.3],[22,4.8]].map(v=>p(...v));for(const [width,color] of [[42,'#404f4d'],[36,'#878577'],[25,'#999583']]){ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineWidth=width;ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.beginPath();route.forEach(([x,y],i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y));ctx.stroke();}for(let i=0;i<route.length-1;i++){const a=route[i],b=route[i+1],len=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);for(let j=5;j<len;j+=12){const f=j/len,x=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*f,y=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*f;line(ctx,'#686f63',x-5,y-11,x+7,y+11,1);box(ctx,'#b6ad93',x-3,y-8,5,1);}}
    for(let i=0;i<145;i++){let x=rng()*W,y=rng()*H;box(ctx,pick(['#a6ab90','#53635c','#b5af91','#485b57'],rng),x,y,1+rng()*3,1);}
    ctx.restore();
    // Layered rock wall faces above the actual collision boundary. Every segment is shaded separately.
    for(let i=0;i<l.w;i++){let x=i*T,y=l.top[i]*T,prev=i?l.top[i-1]*T:y,ny=i+1<l.w?l.top[i+1]*T:y;const face=[[x,0],[x+T,0],[x+T,y],[x+T-5,y+3],[x+13,y-4],[x,y]];shape(ctx,'#394c52',face);ctx.save();shape(ctx,'#394c52',face);ctx.clip();for(let j=0;j<9;j++){const yy=j*y/8+Math.sin(i*1.3+j)*7,dy=6+rng()*10;shape(ctx,pick(['#42595c','#4c6061','#304349','#576b68','#344b50'],rng),[[x,yy],[x+13,yy-5],[x+T,yy+1],[x+T,yy+dy],[x+17,yy+dy+5],[x,yy+dy-1]]);line(ctx,'#7e8c7a55',x+1,yy,x+13,yy-5,1);}ctx.restore();line(ctx,'#83948b',x,y-1,x+T-5,y+1,2);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){const xx=x+4+rng()*24,yy=y-3-rng()*12;shape(ctx,'#273e44',[[xx-3,yy],[xx+3,yy-6],[xx+7,yy],[xx+4,yy+8]]);}}
    // Cross-column strata hide the repeated structural grid, while keeping collision edges fixed.
    ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,[[0,0],[W,0],...a.slice().reverse()]);ctx.clip();
    for(let j=0;j<11;j++){
      let q=[];for(let x=-12;x<=W+12;x+=12){let y=12+j*13+Math.sin(x*.019+j*.52)*14+Math.sin(x*.059+j*.25)*3; q.push([x,y]);}
      const band=[...q,...q.slice().reverse().map(([x,y])=>[x,y+5+Math.sin(x*.03+j)*3])];shape(ctx,['#2e4348','#465c5e','#6a7e7855','#34494c','#8e9e8b44'][j%5],band);for(let i=1;i<q.length;i++){const [x,y]=q[i-1],[xx,yy]=q[i];if((j+i)%5!==0)line(ctx,'#9ba9923c',x,y,xx,yy,1);}
    }
    for(let i=0;i<430;i++){const x=rng()*W,y=rng()*150,n=3+rng()*12;if(rng()<.75)line(ctx,pick(['#a2ad9135','#142f3a60','#78928a50'],rng),x,y,x+n,y+Math.sin(i)*3,1);else box(ctx,'#b7c4ad44',x,y,2,1);}
    // Weathered but distinct supporting timbers, below the rocky roof.
    for(const [x,y]of [[94,127],[242,87],[405,94],[565,115],[703,91]]){shape(ctx,'#293b3e',[[x-13,0],[x+10,0],[x+5,y],[x-11,y+8]]);shape(ctx,'#7d7360',[[x-9,0],[x+5,0],[x+2,y-3],[x-8,y+3]]);line(ctx,'#c0ac85',x-8,0,x-7,y-1,1);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const yy=i*y/5;line(ctx,'#524d43',x-6,yy,x+2,yy+2,1);}box(ctx,'#4b5048',x-10,y-11,12,4);box(ctx,'#bdad85',x-5,y-9,2,2);}
    let shade=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,155);shade.addColorStop(0,'#101e29b8');shade.addColorStop(.5,'#192a3350');shade.addColorStop(1,'#13272c00');ctx.fillStyle=shade;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,165);ctx.restore();
    // Fine floor seams near the sheltered path, not tiled noise.
    ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,floor);ctx.clip();for(let i=0;i<210;i++){let x=rng()*W,y=105+rng()*(H-105),dx=4+rng()*15;line(ctx,pick(['#3d525044','#c2c1a744','#91a28e55','#263c3d33'],rng),x,y,x+dx,y+(i%3-1)*2,1);if(i%4===0)line(ctx,'#28394055',x+dx,y+(i%3-1)*2,x+dx+4,y+5,1);}ctx.restore();
    // Underground water at the foot of the walls, outside the navigable floor.
    const pond=[...b,...[[W,H],[0,H]]];shape(ctx,'#223b47',pond);ctx.save();shape(ctx,'#223b47',pond);ctx.clip();for(let i=0;i<160;i++){const x=rng()*W,y=210+rng()*H;line(ctx,pick(['#315767','#3c6970','#477781','#294b5b'],rng),x,y,x+8+rng()*35,y+Math.floor(rng()*2),1+rng()*2);}let grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,216,0,H);grad.addColorStop(0,'#729b9b25');grad.addColorStop(.65,'#234a5860');grad.addColorStop(1,'#1426339c');ctx.fillStyle=grad;ctx.fillRect(0,200,W,H-200);for(let i=0;i<160;i++){const x=rng()*W,y=220+rng()*160;box(ctx,pick(['#80b5b738','#2e596d80','#6d9fa947','#2a4e5b88'],rng),x,y,1+rng()*11,1);}ctx.restore();for(let i=0;i<l.w;i++){const x=i*T,y=l.bottom[i]*T;line(ctx,'#253e44',x,y,x+T,y,4);line(ctx,'#7a9994',x+1,y-2,x+T-1,y-2,1);for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const xx=x+3+rng()*25,yy=y-2+rng()*7;shape(ctx,pick(['#66716c','#536762','#98a497'],rng),[[xx-4,yy],[xx,yy-6],[xx+7,yy-3],[xx+6,yy+3]]);}}
    // Three geological niches, carved tablets and distinct crystal growths.
    for(const [j,s] of l.stations.entries()){const x=s.x*T,y=s.y*T;if(s.text){shadow(ctx,x,y-12,25,8);shape(ctx,'#333e42',[[x-13,y-18],[x-11,y-37],[x+9,y-40],[x+15,y-18]]);shape(ctx,'#93998a',[[x-10,y-20],[x-8,y-37],[x+7,y-38],[x+10,y-20]]);for(let k=0;k<4;k++){box(ctx,'#50625f',x-6,y-33+k*3,12-k%2*4,1);}}else{for(let k=0;k<5;k++){box(ctx,'#394c4d',x-23,y-22+k*5,46,6);box(ctx,'#a6a99a',x-21,y-22+k*5,42,2);}line(ctx,'#b49a6a',x-25,y-28,x-25,y+4,3);line(ctx,'#b49a6a',x+25,y-28,x+25,y+4,3);}}
    for(const [x,y,n] of [[119,105,5],[308,83,7],[451,107,5],[634,102,8],[283,265,6],[610,309,6]])for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const xx=x+(j-n/2)*5,yy=y+Math.sin(j)*8,h=8+rng()*18;shape(ctx,'#31595e',[[xx-4,yy],[xx-4,yy-h+4],[xx,yy-h],[xx+5,yy-h+5],[xx+4,yy]]);shape(ctx,'#70b1b0',[[xx-2,yy-2],[xx-2,yy-h+4],[xx,yy-h],[xx+1,yy-2]]);box(ctx,'#c1e0ce',xx-1,yy-h+5,1,4);}
    for(const [x,y] of [[3,4],[7,3.1],[11,3],[15,3.5],[19,3.6],[22,3]]){const a=x*T,b=y*T;box(ctx,'#2e3736',a-5,b-21,10,21);box(ctx,'#795e40',a-5,b-20,10,3);box(ctx,'#ebc27a',a-3,b-16,6,9);box(ctx,'#fff0b4',a-2,b-15,2,6);box(ctx,'#b58b55',a-6,b-7,12,3);}
    // Uneven foreground rock masks at the edges keep the underground framing deep.
    for(let i=0;i<l.w;i++){const x=i*T,y=H-10-rng()*8;shape(ctx,'#17282f',[[x,y],[x+8,y-10],[x+21,y-3],[x+T,y-15],[x+T,H],[x,H]]);}
  }
  function tunnelEnter(scene,w,side){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,l=D.tunnelLayout,portal=D.tunnelPortals.find(p=>p.id===side);if(scene.inside||!D.tunnelOpen(w)||!portal)return false;const W=l.w*T,H=l.h*T,k=1,X=ROOM_X,out={x:portal.x*T,y:portal.y*T},objs=[];const bg=scene.add.image(X,0,texture(scene,'blackstone-underground-floor',W,H,c=>cavePaint(c,l))).setOrigin(0).setDepth(1);objs.push(bg);const fx=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(7),foreground=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(11);objs.push(fx,foreground);for(const s of l.stations){const text=scene.add.text(X+s.x*T,s.y*T+(s.text?8:15),s.text?'◇':s.id.toUpperCase()+' STAIR',{fontSize:s.text?'13px':'10px',color:'#e6d6a5',backgroundColor:'#20333b',padding:{x:4,y:2}}).setOrigin(.5).setDepth(9);objs.push(text);}const cam=scene.cameras.main;scene.inside={b:{id:'blackstone-underpass',name:'Blackstone · Underground'},spec:{w:l.w,h:l.h,items:[],labels:[],spots:{},guests:[]},k,rects:D.tunnelRects().map(o=>({x:X+o.x*T,y:o.y*T,w:o.w*T,h:o.h*T})),objs,out,floor:0,millEntryZoom:cam.zoom,tunnel:{side,fx,foreground,next:0}};const st=l.stations.find(a=>a.id===side);scene.player.setScale(k).setPosition(X+st.x*T,st.y*T);scene.doorCooldown=.8;cam.stopFollow();cam.removeBounds();cam.setZoom(Math.max(.7,Math.min(1.65,(cam.width-50)/W,(cam.height-140)/H)));cam.centerOn(X+W/2,H/2);scene.prompt?.setVisible(false);return true;}
  function tunnelFrame(scene,w,time,reduced){const u=scene.inside?.tunnel;if(!u||time<u.next)return;u.next=time+(reduced?170:45);const g=u.fx,X=ROOM_X,l=root.LQValleyDevelopment.tunnelLayout;if(reduced)time=0;g.clear();for(const [i,p]of [[3,4],[7,3.1],[11,3],[15,3.5],[19,3.6],[22,3]].entries()){const x=X+p[0]*T,y=p[1]*T-12,f=reduced?1:.86+Math.sin(time/180+i*3)*.11;for(let j=12;j>0;j--)g.fillStyle(0xeec684,(.006+j*.0018)*f).fillEllipse(x,y+18,21+j*10,13+j*7);g.fillStyle(0xd8a764,.06*f).fillEllipse(x,y+38,65,25);g.fillStyle(0xffe9ad,.85).fillRect(x-1,y-1,3,4);}
    for(let i=0;i<16;i++){const x=(i*59+time*.018)% (l.w*T-25),y=l.bottom[Math.min(23,Math.floor(x/T))]*T+9+i%4*5;if(y<l.h*T-18)g.lineStyle(1,0x90c3c3,.20+Math.sin(time/260+i)*.09).lineBetween(X+x,y,X+x+9+i%7,y);}
    if(!reduced){for(let j=0;j<7;j++){const x=90+j*89,xx=X+x,base=l.bottom[Math.floor(x/T)]*T+6,f=((time/1500+j*.27)%1),y=base-46+Math.min(1,f*1.8)*46;if(f<.56)g.fillStyle(0xb8dfdf,.8).fillRect(xx,y,1,4);else{const r=(f-.56)*22;g.lineStyle(1,0xb1d6d2,(1-f)*.8).strokeEllipse(xx,base,r*2,r*.6);}}}
  }

  // Expedition architecture uses individually composed silhouettes, a shared upper-left
  // key light, and deterministic texture wear. Each layer is a real construction stage.
  function frontierPaint(ctx,p,stage,frame){
    const rng=random(7123+C.FRONTIER.indexOf(p)*926),P=p.palette,[dark,mid,hi,lit]=P,wood=['#3c322a','#755c43','#ac8b5f','#d1b082'],rock=['#354341','#63726b','#8d9b87','#b8c0a2'];
    const poly=(c,a)=>shape(ctx,c,a),rect=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),ln=(c,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,a[0],a[1],b[0],b[1],w);
    const masonry=(x,y,w,h,colors=rock)=>{rect(colors[0],x,y,w,h);for(let yy=y+1,row=0;yy<y+h-1;yy+=6,row++)for(let xx=x+1-(row%2)*4;xx<x+w-1;xx+=9){let a=Math.max(x+1,xx),b=Math.min(x+w-1,xx+8);if(b-a<1)continue;rect(colors[1+(rng()>.7?1:0)],a,yy,b-a,Math.min(5,y+h-yy-1));rect(colors[2],a,yy,b-a,1);if(b-a>=5){rect(colors[3],a+1,yy+1,2,1);rect(colors[0],b-2,yy+4,2,1);}}};
    const post=(x,y,b,w=6)=>{rect(wood[0],x,y,w,b-y);rect(wood[1],x+1,y+1,w-2,b-y-2);rect(wood[3],x+1,y+1,1,b-y-2);for(let i=y+9;i<b-3;i+=11)rect(wood[0],x+3,i,1,4);rect('#b69b71',x-1,b-4,w+2,2);};
    const beam=(a,b)=>{ln(wood[0],a,b,7);ln(wood[1],[a[0]-1,a[1]-1],[b[0]-1,b[1]-1],4);ln(wood[3],[a[0]-1,a[1]-2],[b[0]-1,b[1]-2],1);};
    const tiled=(a,b,c,d,colors)=>{poly(colors[0],[a,b,c,d]);let n=8,m=12;for(let r=0;r<n;r++){let f=r/n,g=(r+1)/n,A=[a[0]+(d[0]-a[0])*f,a[1]+(d[1]-a[1])*f],B=[b[0]+(c[0]-b[0])*f,b[1]+(c[1]-b[1])*f],D=[a[0]+(d[0]-a[0])*g,a[1]+(d[1]-a[1])*g],E=[b[0]+(c[0]-b[0])*g,b[1]+(c[1]-b[1])*g];for(let j=0;j<m;j++){let u=j/m,v=(j+1)/m;const f1=[A[0]+(B[0]-A[0])*u,A[1]+(B[1]-A[1])*u],f2=[A[0]+(B[0]-A[0])*v,A[1]+(B[1]-A[1])*v],f3=[D[0]+(E[0]-D[0])*v,D[1]+(E[1]-D[1])*v],f4=[D[0]+(E[0]-D[0])*u,D[1]+(E[1]-D[1])*u];poly(colors[1+(rng()>.64?1:0)],[f1,f2,f3,f4]);ln(colors[3],f1,f2);if((j+r)%3===0)ln(colors[0],f2,f3);}}ln(colors[3],a,b,2);ln(colors[0],d,c,2);};
    const windowPane=(x,y,w,h)=>{rect('#263b3c',x-2,y-2,w+4,h+4);rect('#648e91',x,y,w,h);poly('#9bc8bd',[[x,y],[x+w,y],[x,y+h]]);rect('#d6dfb9',x+2,y+1,1,h-2);rect('#3d5145',x+w/2-1,y,2,h);rect('#405647',x,y+h/2,w,2);};
    const bench=(x,y,w)=>{post(x+3,y+5,y+17,4);post(x+w-8,y+5,y+17,4);poly(wood[0],[[x,y],[x+w,y],[x+w+3,y+7],[x-2,y+7]]);rect(wood[2],x+2,y+1,w-3,4);for(let k=4;k<w-5;k+=5)rect(wood[1],x+k,y+3,3,1);};
    const lantern=(x,y,on)=>{rect('#3b4234',x-4,y-2,9,13);rect(on?'#f8dc94':'#8a9785',x-2,y,5,8);rect('#5e593f',x,y,1,8);rect('#b0a46f',x-4,y-3,9,2);rect('#343b33',x-5,y+9,11,2);};
    const rubble=(n)=>{for(let k=0;k<n;k++){let x=26+rng()*94,y=84+rng()*17,w=3+Math.floor(rng()*8);poly(k%3===0?rock[2]:rock[1],[[x-w,y],[x-2,y-4],[x+w-1,y-3],[x+w,y+2],[x,y+4]]);ln(rock[3],[x-2,y-4],[x+w-1,y-3]);if(k%4===0){rect('#52684d',x-2,y-3,4,1);rect('#83905e',x-1,y-4,2,1);}}};
    const seedling=(x,y,shade)=>{rect('#3b4030',x-4,y,9,5);rect('#9d7955',x-4,y-1,9,2);ln('#587c50',[x,y],[x,y-9],1);poly(shade,[[x,y-4],[x-6,y-8],[x-5,y-11],[x,y-8]]);poly('#9ab67a',[[x,y-7],[x+5,y-11],[x+6,y-8],[x,y-5]]);};
    // Broken perimeter and material islands, no identical rectangular platform.
    shadow(ctx,72,104,49,8);
    const footprint=p.kind==='spring'?[[29,94],[43,77],[100,75],[117,92],[105,105],[49,108]]:p.kind==='seed'?[[30,93],[34,81],[102,79],[118,93],[101,105],[42,106]]:p.kind==='granary'?[[39,94],[42,82],[104,82],[112,94],[103,104],[44,105]]:p.half<.5?[[45,95],[55,83],[91,83],[103,96],[94,108],[56,108]]:[[23,95],[34,85],[107,82],[123,99],[109,105],[34,106]];
    ctx.save();poly(stage>=3?'#777f68':'#5f6f55',footprint);shape(ctx,null,footprint);ctx.clip();for(let r=0;r<6;r++)for(let c=0;c<16;c++){const x=19+c*7+(r%2)*3,y=80+r*5;if(rng()<(stage>=3?.16:.50))continue;rect('#596653',x,y,6,4);rect(rock[2],x+1,y,4,1);if(rng()>.65)rect('#758560',x+1,y+2,3,1);}ctx.restore();
    if(stage<=2){
      // The abandoned silhouette communicates use even before reconstruction.
      rubble(stage===0?18:stage===1?11:4);
      if(p.kind==='spring'){poly('#354f50',[[38,77],[49,69],[96,70],[109,78],[98,89],[47,89]]);poly('#587779',[[44,79],[51,73],[91,74],[100,79],[93,85],[52,85]]);for(let i=0;i<6;i++)if(i!==2&&i!==3||stage===2)masonry(38+i*11,85,10,9);masonry(73,56,16,20);rect('#8f8e71',77,56,5,3);if(stage===0)beam([34,64],[62,89]);}
      else if(p.kind==='rest'){masonry(33,82,17,11);masonry(96,81,18,12);if(stage<2){post(41,43,91,5);post(101,58,91,5);beam([40,44],[71,31]);poly('#705a3e',[[40,43],[70,31],[84,46],[58,58]]);beam([57,60],[95,94]);}else bench(41,79,20);}
      else if(p.kind==='seed'){masonry(35,81,72,10);if(stage<2){post(38,44,90,4);beam([38,45],[67,27]);beam([67,27],[96,49]);poly('#769c95a8',[[41,47],[63,31],[73,40],[51,57]]);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)poly('#a6c5b4',[[69+i*10,80],[74+i*10,75],[79+i*10,82],[74+i*10,84]]);}for(let i=0;i<3;i++)seedling(45+i*20,88,'#71885d');}
      else if(p.kind==='granary'){if(stage<2){post(40,52,90,7);post(91,60,91,7);beam([38,56],[62,25]);poly('#948052',[[38,56],[62,25],[82,45],[60,66]]);for(let k=0;k<4;k++)rect(wood[1],42+k*8,60,7,21);}masonry(42,86,59,9);poly('#9e8c60',[[91,91],[89,82],[96,79],[103,84],[105,94]]);}
      else if(p.kind==='stone'){masonry(35,73,35,18);masonry(79,84,31,11);if(stage<2){post(39,35,92);beam([40,36],[91,55]);ln('#936c45',[88,55],[88,69],2);}masonry(68,65,20,12);}
      else if(p.kind==='wind'){masonry(51,78,46,14,P);if(stage<2){post(69,30,86,5);beam([69,30],[96,49]);rect('#b3a17d',96,48,8,4);masonry(63,65,18,13,P);}}
      else{masonry(52,61,39,32,P);if(stage<2){masonry(60,42,24,19,P);masonry(60,28,9,14,P);rect('#243a3c',68,48,10,13);poly('#788c8b',[[76,52],[94,58],[99,65],[83,67]]);}else masonry(65,53,15,8,P);}
      if(stage>=1){post(18,75,104,3);rect('#cfbd86',20,74,10,6);rect('#645c48',22,77,5,1);}return;}
    masonry(31,93,82,9);masonry(36,85,72,9);
    const active=stage>=8;
    if(p.kind==='spring'){
      // Circular stone catchment, open front, roof on narrow timber posts.
      poly(rock[0],[[34,76],[42,69],[102,69],[112,77],[112,91],[101,99],[45,99],[34,90]]);poly(rock[2],[[36,76],[45,70],[101,70],[109,77],[99,84],[46,84]]);poly('#304f50',[[43,77],[49,73],[96,73],[103,77],[96,81],[50,81]]);if(stage>=7){poly('#416e74',[[46,77],[52,74],[96,74],[100,77],[96,80],[51,80]]);for(let k=0;k<7;k++){const x=49+(k*9+frame*3)%48;rect('#91bdba',x,76+k%3,5,1);}}
      for(let i=0;i<7;i++)masonry(38+i*10,84+(i===0||i===6?-3:0),9,11);
      if(stage>=4){post(35,35,91);post(103,35,91);beam([33,38],[108,38]);}
      if(stage>=5){masonry(50,57,44,16);rect('#424a3d',65,56,14,16);rect('#9f986e',68,60,8,3);}
      if(stage>=6){tiled([25,31],[78,16],[119,35],[67,51],[dark,mid,hi,lit]);tiled([25,31],[67,51],[67,55],[25,35],[dark,mid,hi,lit]);}
      if(stage>=7){rect('#524a36',67,65,13,5);rect('#c7ad72',75,66,4,10);if(active){rect('#96cfd0',76,74,2,6);rect('#c0dfd7',74+frame%3,80,6,1);}}
    }else if(p.kind==='rest'){
      // Long open pavilion, deep timbers, asymmetrical cedar roof and seats.
      if(stage>=4){for(const x of [31,58,104,116])post(x,43,94,5);beam([29,44],[119,44]);beam([31,67],[54,44]);beam([104,44],[119,68]);}
      if(stage>=5){masonry(35,81,78,8);for(let x=41;x<112;x+=9){rect(wood[1],x,65,6,17);rect(wood[2],x+1,65,2,16);}bench(38,79,28);bench(89,79,24);}
      if(stage>=6){tiled([17,39],[75,18],[129,36],[73,59],[dark,mid,hi,lit]);tiled([17,39],[73,59],[73,63],[17,43],[dark,mid,hi,lit]);beam([17,42],[73,62]);}
      if(stage>=7){post(78,58,90,4);beam([66,64],[91,64]);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let len=12+i*3,x=69+i*4;rect('#b4a176',x,65,2,len);rect('#e7d7a3',x-1,65+len,3,2);}if(active){const a=frame%4;poly('#c7a568',[[113,50],[131,51+a],[128,57+a],[113,56]]);}}
    }else if(p.kind==='seed'){
      // Greenhouse: glazed rear and roof, planting benches readable through glass.
      if(stage>=4){for(const x of [34,57,87,109])post(x,46,94,4);beam([32,48],[111,48]);beam([34,49],[68,24]);beam([68,24],[110,47]);}
      if(stage>=5){masonry(34,78,76,15);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)windowPane(38+i*18,53,14,24);}
      if(stage>=6){poly('#354e4d',[[27,49],[66,20],[115,43],[78,72]]);poly('#759998',[[31,48],[66,24],[111,44],[78,68]]);for(let j=1;j<6;j++){const f=j/6,ax=31+(66-31)*f,ay=48+(24-48)*f,bx=78+(111-78)*f,by=68+(44-68)*f;ln('#d4deb9',[ax,ay],[bx,by],2);}ln('#e0e8c8',[31,48],[66,24],2);ln('#405e5a',[66,24],[111,44],3);ln('#526f64',[78,68],[111,44],3);poly('#c0ded4a8',[[40,45],[61,30],[75,35],[54,51]]);}
      if(stage>=7){bench(38,86,68);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){seedling(46+i*13,84,hi);if(active&&frame%3===i%3)rect(lit,44+i*13,76,2,1);}}
    }else if(p.kind==='granary'){
      // Raised storehouse: timber stilts, plank walls, steep thatch roof, loading stoop.
      if(stage>=4){for(const x of [38,61,88,107])post(x,49,97,7);beam([35,79],[113,79]);for(const x of [43,97])beam([x,80],[x+10,95]);}
      if(stage>=5){rect(wood[0],38,47,72,34);for(let x=40;x<108;x+=8){rect(wood[1],x,49,7,29);rect(wood[2],x+1,49,1,29);rect(wood[0],x+4,55,1,16);}rect('#3f392b',64,55,20,26);rect(wood[2],64,53,20,2);windowPane(45,56,12,11);windowPane(93,56,10,11);}
      if(stage>=6){tiled([25,42],[69,10],[124,39],[80,65],['#5a4c35','#a88a52','#bfa366','#e1c58e']);ln('#e6d29d',[25,42],[69,10],3);ln('#64523b',[80,65],[124,39],3);}
      if(stage>=7){for(let i=0;i<4;i++)masonry(65-i*2,82+i*5,20+i*4,5);for(const x of [43,101]){poly('#8f754c',[[x-7,89],[x-5,80],[x+4,79],[x+9,85],[x+8,94],[x-6,95]]);rect('#d9bd84',x-4,82,6,2);rect('#6c583a',x-2,79,3,2);}if(active){rect('#e3c478',70,58,8,17);rect('#ad874d',70,65,8,2);}}
    }else if(p.kind==='stone'){
      // Masonry yard: split stone block table and timber hoist with suspended load.
      if(stage>=4){post(38,31,92,8);post(96,31,92,8);beam([33,32],[112,32]);beam([41,55],[60,33]);beam([99,55],[82,33]);}
      if(stage>=5){masonry(31,74,40,18);masonry(74,80,42,13);masonry(52,63,24,11);}
      if(stage>=6){poly(dark,[[30,30],[48,16],[113,16],[121,33],[42,38]]);tiled([30,30],[48,16],[113,16],[121,33],[dark,mid,hi,lit]);}
      if(stage>=7){const y=52+(active?frame%4:0);rect('#4d4a37',78,34,2,y-34);poly('#716e61',[[71,y],[87,y],[90,y+9],[75,y+13],[70,y+8]]);rect('#b4b1a0',73,y+1,13,2);masonry(76,68,28,11);rect('#ad9b6e',47,62,16,3);rect('#4c4740',59,56,3,9);}
    }else if(p.kind==='wind'){
      // Narrow alpine instrument tower, not a recoloured house.
      if(stage>=4){post(61,30,93,6);post(82,30,93,6);beam([62,42],[86,42]);beam([62,61],[86,42]);beam([62,80],[86,61]);}
      if(stage>=5){masonry(50,77,49,16);masonry(62,58,24,18);windowPane(69,62,10,8);}
      if(stage>=6){poly('#334b54',[[50,55],[71,39],[100,54],[76,65]]);tiled([50,55],[71,39],[100,54],[76,65],[dark,mid,hi,lit]);}
      if(stage>=7){rect('#524c36',72,15,4,34);rect('#cdbb7d',73,15,2,32);const angle=2*Math.PI*frame/8;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const a=angle+i*Math.PI*2/3,x=74+Math.cos(a)*20,y=22+Math.sin(a)*7;ln('#9e916a',[74,22],[x,y],2);rect('#444b43',x-4,y-2,9,5);rect('#dabd78',x-3,y-2,7,2);}poly('#d4bb81',[[74,12],[91,8],[87,15],[91,20],[74,16]]);}
    }else{
      // Beacon: stepped masonry tower, lantern chamber, copper cupola.
      if(stage>=4){masonry(52,65,45,29,P);masonry(59,48,31,17,P);}
      if(stage>=5){masonry(57,37,34,30,P);rect('#263a3e',67,60,12,28);rect('#a6b8b3',67,60,12,2);for(const x of [58,88])masonry(x,27,6,25,P);}
      if(stage>=6){poly(dark,[[49,29],[73,10],[100,29],[74,42]]);tiled([49,29],[73,10],[100,29],[74,42],[dark,mid,hi,lit]);rect('#7b846b',72,3,3,11);}
      if(stage>=7){rect('#263d43',63,30,21,21);rect(active?(frame%3?'#e6cb8a':'#ffdf8b'):'#7b8d89',67,35,13,12);rect('#7b7450',73,32,2,17);rect('#9a9a70',61,48,26,3);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)masonry(65-i*3,88+i*4,15+i*6,4,P);}
    }
    if(stage===8||stage===9){lantern(24,79,true);if(stage===9){post(120,65,96,3);poly(lit,[[121,65],[134,67],[129,73],[121,73]]);rect(dark,125,68,3,2);}}
    if(stage<7){for(const x of [22,125]){post(x,61,100,3);ln(wood[2],[x,65],[x+(x<70?12:-12),86],2);}if(stage===3)rubble(4);}
  }
  function frontierRelease(view,id){const map=view.frontierSprites;if(!map)return;for(const [key,sprite]of [...map]){if(id&&id!==key)continue;const i=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(sprite);if(i>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(sprite.scene)sprite.destroy();map.delete(key);}}
  function frontierViews(scene,view,w,t,reduced){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment;if(scene.inside){frontierRelease(view);return;}const map=view.frontierSprites??=new Map(),r=scene.cameras.main.worldView,night=w.clock.minuteOfDay>=1170||w.clock.minuteOfDay<375;for(const p of C.FRONTIER){const x=p.x*T,y=p.y*T,visible=x>=r.x-120&&x<=r.right+120&&y>=r.y-110&&y<=r.bottom+110;if(!visible){frontierRelease(view,p.id);continue;}const st=D.frontierState(w,p.id).stage,phase=st>=7&&!reduced?Math.floor(t*(p.kind==='wind'?5:3)+p.x)%8:0,key=texture(scene,'frontier-'+p.id+'-'+st+'-'+phase,144,116,c=>frontierPaint(c,p,st,phase));let sprite=map.get(p.id);if(!sprite){sprite=scene.add.image(x,y,key).setOrigin(.5,101/116);map.set(p.id,sprite);scene.areaObjs.push(sprite);}else if(sprite.texture.key!==key)sprite.setTexture(key);const scale=p.half<.5?.72:.92;sprite.setPosition(x,y).setScale(scale).setDepth(view.depthAt(y+8));const obscured=scene.player.y<y&&scene.player.y>y-90*scale&&Math.abs(scene.player.x-x)<40*scale;sprite.setAlpha(obscured?.46:1);if(st>=8&&night){const f=reduced?1:.84+Math.sin(t*4+p.x)*.12;for(let i=8;i>=1;i--)view.light.fillStyle(0xe4c780,.015*f).fillEllipse(x,y-18,20+i*15,14+i*9);}if(st>=8&&!reduced&&p.kind==='spring'){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const a=(t*.8+i/3)%1;view.effects.lineStyle(1,0xc3e2db,.35*(1-a)).strokeEllipse(x+4,y-17,5+a*16,2+a*5);}}}}

  // Custom eastern architecture; native pixels, consistent NW lighting.
  // Purpose-built architecture, at native canvas-pixel resolution. The footprint is the existing 131×59 px site envelope.
  function civicPaint(ctx,p,stage){
    const i=C.EAST_SITES.indexOf(p)-18,colors=p.palette,rng=random(88243+i*1739),R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),P=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts),L=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(a),Math.round(b),w);
    const stone=['#3f4942','#69746b','#939b88','#bac1a6'],wood=['#49382e','#76533d','#b38556','#e0bb82'],metal=['#3e5252','#6a8581','#a5b9aa','#d3d8ba'];
    const brick=(x,y,w,h,pal=stone)=>{R(pal[0],x,y,w,h);for(let yy=y+1,row=0;yy<y+h-1;yy+=6,row++)for(let xx=x+1-(row%2)*5;xx<x+w-1;xx+=10){let a=Math.max(x+1,xx),b=Math.min(x+w-1,xx+9),hh=Math.min(5,y+h-1-yy);if(b>a){R(pal[rng()<.18?2:1],a,yy,b-a,hh);R(pal[3],a,yy,b-a,1);if(rng()<.23)R(pal[0],b-2,yy+2,1,2);}}};
    const boards=(x,y,w,h)=>{R(wood[0],x,y,w,h);for(let xx=x+1;xx<x+w-1;xx+=6){R(wood[1],xx,y+1,Math.min(5,x+w-1-xx),h-2);R(wood[2],xx,y+1,1,h-2);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)R(wood[0],xx+2,y+2+rng()*Math.max(1,h-5),2,1);}};
    const post=(x,y,h,w=6)=>{R(wood[0],x,y,w,h);R(wood[1],x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);R(wood[3],x+1,y+1,1,h-2);for(let a=y+4;a<y+h-3;a+=9)R(wood[2],x+2,a,2,1);};
    const beam=(a,b,c,d)=>{L(wood[0],a,b,c,d,6);L(wood[2],a-1,b-1,c-1,d-1,3);L(wood[3],a-1,b-2,c-1,d-2,1);};
    const roofs=[['#423a2f','#786549','#a4895c','#ccb382'],['#4e5040','#878b64','#b8b381','#e1cb99'],['#423d52','#756783','#a08aac','#d3b8c9'],['#3c4b52','#627985','#8e9ca0','#c1c8c1'],['#5d4636','#956e4e','#c79b6e','#e4c59a'],['#533c35','#96674e','#be8e6a','#e7b992'],['#344e4a','#5c8275','#8cab92','#cad9b3'],['#3d5745','#72966d','#a9bc8f','#dce0b0'],['#554832','#a27c4d','#c5a164','#f0d391'],['#624641','#a07962','#c3a18a','#e7c8ac'],['#344b58','#668997','#9cb2b9','#d0d9cb'],['#3b4c62','#687f9a','#99a9c2','#cdd4d9']];let roof=(p.expansion||p.monument)?p.roof:roofs[i];
    const tileRoof=(a,b,c,d)=>{P(roof[0],[a,b,c,d]);const point=(u,v)=>[a[0]*(1-u)*(1-v)+b[0]*u*(1-v)+c[0]*u*v+d[0]*(1-u)*v,a[1]*(1-u)*(1-v)+b[1]*u*(1-v)+c[1]*u*v+d[1]*(1-u)*v];const rows=Math.max(4,Math.ceil(Math.abs(d[1]-a[1])/4)),cols=Math.max(4,Math.ceil(Math.abs(b[0]-a[0])/8));for(let m=0;m<rows;m++)for(let n=0;n<cols;n++){const A=point(n/cols,m/rows),B=point((n+1)/cols,m/rows),CC=point((n+1)/cols,(m+1)/rows),D=point(n/cols,(m+1)/rows);P(roof[rng()>.72?2:1],[A,B,CC,D]);L(roof[3],...A,...B);L(roof[0],...B,...CC);}L(roof[3],...a,...b,2);L(roof[0],...d,...c,3);};
    const arch=(x,y,w,h,col='#263c39')=>{P('#3a3d36',[[x,y+h],[x,y+9],[x+3,y+4],[x+w/2,y],[x+w-3,y+4],[x+w,y+9],[x+w,y+h]]);P(col,[[x+3,y+h-1],[x+3,y+10],[x+5,y+6],[x+w/2,y+3],[x+w-5,y+6],[x+w-3,y+10],[x+w-3,y+h-1]]);L(stone[3],x+2,y+8,x+w/2,y+1);};
    const window=(x,y,w=16,h=19)=>{R(wood[0],x-2,y-2,w+4,h+4);R('#344f51',x,y,w,h);P('#779b9b',[[x+1,y+1],[x+w-1,y+1],[x+1,y+h-1]]);R('#c5d5be',x+1,y+1,1,h-2);R(wood[2],x+Math.floor(w/2),y,2,h);R(wood[2],x,y+Math.floor(h/2),w,2);R(stone[2],x-3,y+h+2,w+6,3);};
    const door=(x,y,w=22,h=33)=>{arch(x,y,w,h,'#6b533b');for(let j=4;j<w-3;j+=5)R(wood[0],x+j,y+10,1,h-11);R(wood[3],x+w-6,y+Math.floor(h*.61),2,3);};
    const barrel=(x,y,col=wood[1])=>{P(wood[0],[[x-8,y],[x-9,y-9],[x-5,y-19],[x+5,y-19],[x+9,y-9],[x+8,y]]);R(col,x-6,y-16,12,14);R(wood[3],x-5,y-15,2,12);R(metal[0],x-8,y-13,16,2);R(metal[0],x-8,y-5,16,2);R(wood[3],x-5,y-19,10,2);};
    const boxStack=(x,y,n)=>{for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const a=x+j%2*15,b=y-Math.floor(j/2)*11;R(wood[0],a,b-10,13,11);R(wood[1],a+1,b-9,11,9);R(wood[3],a+1,b-9,11,1);L(wood[2],a+2,b-8,a+11,b-1);}};
    const cloth=(x,y,w,h,c)=>{P(wood[0],[[x-1,y-1],[x+w+1,y-1],[x+w+1,y+h],[x-1,y+h]]);P(c,[[x,y],[x+w,y],[x+w,y+h-3],[x+w*.7,y+h],[x+w*.4,y+h-2],[x,y+h]]);for(let j=3;j<w;j+=6)R('#e6d5b688',x+j,y+2,1,h-4);R(roof[3],x,y+h-4,w,2);};
    const garden=(x,y,c)=>{R('#504332',x-6,y-5,12,6);for(let j=0;j<4;j++){L('#4f7651',x,y-5,x-5+j*3,y-15-j%2*3,1);P(colors[1],[[x,y-7],[x-7+j*3,y-15],[x-2+j*3,y-14]]);R(c,x-6+j*3,y-16-j%2*3,3,3);}};
    const lamp=(x,y)=>{R('#414b43',x-3,y-3,7,11);R('#eed7a0',x-1,y-1,3,6);R('#9a7144',x-4,y-4,9,2);};
    const gable=(x,y,w,h)=>{const top=y;tileRoof([x-5,top+3],[x+w*.55,top-25],[x+w+5,top+3],[x+w*.45,top+22]);P(roof[0],[[x-5,top+3],[x+w*.45,top+22],[x+w+5,top+3],[x+w+5,top+8],[x+w*.45,top+26],[x-5,top+8]]);};
    // Each plan defines a different building mass and roof profile. Coordinates are bespoke, not recolours.
    const plans=[
      [[38,57,114,69,'wood','gable']], // wide salvage barn
      [[45,57,108,69,'wood','saltbox'],[112,31,34,43,'wood','lean']], // drying loft
      [[38,65,64,61,'wood','gable'],[102,55,53,71,'wood','gable']], // split loom hall
      [[55,55,91,71,'stone','hip'],[100,22,35,43,'stone','tower']], // survey turret
      [[36,74,126,52,'stone','lean']], // open stonecut arcade
      [[42,58,106,68,'stone','gable'],[122,43,35,49,'wood','lean']], // annex repair loft
      [[55,56,85,70,'stone','hip'],[104,28,39,50,'stone','tank']], // water tower/pump
      [[42,62,113,64,'wood','hip']], // apothecary with drying awning
      [[43,62,113,64,'stone','gable'],[126,26,21,60,'stone','chimney']], // community kitchen
      [[39,61,122,65,'wood','saltbox']], // clothier balcony
      [[38,59,72,67,'stone','gable'],[110,67,47,59,'stone','hip']], // bindery wing
      [[36,68,128,58,'stone','hip'],[72,36,55,39,'wood','gable']] // academy central upper floor
    ];
    if(p.expansion||p.monument)plans[i]=p.architecture;
    shadow(ctx,103,130,76,12);P(stage<3?'#62694f':'#827d65',[[24,120],[48,104],[150,104],[174,120],[161,137],[39,137]]);for(let j=0;j<80;j++){const x=36+rng()*124,y=114+rng()*20;R(stage<3?colors[1]:stone[2],x,y,2+rng()*5,1+rng()*2);}
    if(stage<3){for(let j=0;j<(stage===0?27:stage===1?17:8);j++){let x=31+rng()*137,y=110+rng()*20;P(j%3?stone[1]:wood[1],[[x-4,y],[x-2,y-4],[x+5,y-3],[x+8,y+1],[x+1,y+4]]);L(stone[3],x-2,y-4,x+5,y-3);if(j%4===0)R(colors[1],x-1,y-4,3,2);}const [x,y,w,h,mat]=plans[i][0];if(mat==='stone'){brick(x,y+15,22,h-15);brick(x+w-27,y+Math.floor(h*.55),27,Math.ceil(h*.45));}else{post(x,y+8,h-8);post(x+w-7,y+16,h-16);beam(x,y+11,x+w*.45,y-12);beam(x+w*.45,y-12,x+w-15,y+12);P(roof[1],[[x,y+11],[x+w*.45,y-12],[x+w*.57,y-1],[x+18,y+18]]);}if(stage===1){post(165,103,25,4);R('#d6c18c',156,103,20,10);}if(stage===2)for(let j=0;j<3;j++)R(stone[2],45+j*40,120,12,3);if(i===6&&stage<2)brick(108,69,29,41);if(i===3&&stage<2)brick(105,49,25,63);if(i===8&&stage<2)brick(135,45,18,56);return;}
    brick(35,116,130,12);brick(39,109,122,8);if(stage===3)return;
    for(const [x,y,w,h,mat,mode] of plans[i]){if(mode==='chimney'||mode==='tank'||mode==='tower')continue;post(x,y,h);post(x+w-6,y,h);post(x+Math.floor(w/2)-3,y,h);beam(x,y,x+w,y);if(stage===4)beam(x,y,x+w-6,y+h-7);}

      if(mat==='stone')brick(x,y,w,h);else{boards(x,y,w,h);for(let xx=x;xx<x+w;xx+=32)post(xx,y,h,5);}

      if(stage>=6){if(mode==='hip'){const a=[x-7,y+1],b=[x+w*.28,y-26],cc=[x+w*.72,y-26],d=[x+w+7,y+1];tileRoof(b,cc,[x+w+7,y+1],[x-7,y+1]);P(roof[0],[a,b,[x+w*.25,y+5]]);P(roof[2],[a,b,[x+w*.25,y+3]]);L(roof[3],...b,...cc,2);}else if(mode==='lean'){tileRoof([x+1,y-16],[x+w-2,y-16],[x+w+6,y+6],[x-6,y+6]);}else if(mode==='saltbox'){tileRoof([x+28,y-30],[x+w-3,y-24],[x+w+6,y+5],[x-6,y+5]);P(roof[0],[[x-6,y+5],[x+28,y-30],[x+40,y-12],[x+2,y+10]]);}else gable(x,y,w,h);}}
    if(stage>=6&&i===3){const[x,y,w,h]=plans[i][1];P(roof[0],[[x-6,y+2],[x+w/2,y-17],[x+w+6,y+2]]);P(roof[2],[[x-6,y+2],[x+w/2,y-17],[x+w*.5,y+2]]);L(roof[3],x-6,y+2,x+w/2,y-17,2);}
    if(stage>=7){switch(i){
      case 0:boxStack(25,130,4);for(let j=0;j<4;j++){R(wood[0],131,100+j*5,35,6);R(wood[3],132,100+j*5,33,1);}break;

      switch(p.style){

      }
    }
    // Monument-specific architectural additions within original exterior canvas and footprint.
    if(p.monument){
      const gilt='#cdb27e',shade='#384644',light='#e0dac0',glass='#719f9c';
      if(stage<3){const [x,y,w,h]=plans[i][0];for(let j=0;j<7-stage*2;j++){const a=x+8+j*(w-17)/7,b=119+(j%3)*3;P(stone[0],[[a-4,b],[a-3,b-7],[a+5,b-9],[a+8,b-2],[a+3,b+3]]);L(stone[2],a-3,b-7,a+5,b-9);if(j%2===0){L('#4b664d',a,b,a+3,b-12,2);R('#87a66b',a+1,b-10,4,2);}}}
      if(stage>=7){
        const column=(x,y,h)=>{R(stone[0],x-2,y,10,h);R(stone[2],x,y+1,6,h-2);R(stone[3],x,y+1,2,h-2);R(stone[1],x+5,y+1,2,h-2);R(light,x-3,y-2,12,3);R(stone[2],x-3,y+h-2,12,3);};
        const rail=(x,y,w)=>{R(shade,x,y,w,3);for(let a=x+3;a<x+w-3;a+=7){R(metal[0],a,y+3,2,10);R(gilt,a,y+3,1,7);}R(metal[2],x,y+13,w,2);};

        const steps=(x,y,w)=>{for(let j=0;j<3;j++){R(stone[0],x-j*3,y+j*3,w+j*6,3);R(stone[3],x-j*3,y+j*3,w+j*6,1);}};
        switch(p.style){
          case 0: for(const x of [40,153]){column(x,91,27);L(wood[2],x,94,100,70,3);}rail(50,89,100);break;

          case 3: rail(46,94,108);for(const x of [72,128])column(x,76,41);rose(100,62,10);break;

          case 5: for(const x of [48,70,124,146])column(x,83,37);P(stone[0],[[65,78],[100,58],[135,78]]);P(stone[2],[[71,77],[100,62],[129,77]]);rose(100,72,5);steps(78,126,44);break;
          case 6: for(const x of [31,70,109,148]){column(x,86,29);L(glass,x-2,84,x+24,84,2);}L('#b7ded2',31,78,169,78,1);break;
          case 7: for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let x=56+j*18;P(shade,[[x,71],[x+8,52],[x+17,71]]);P(glass,[[x+2,70],[x+8,56],[x+15,70]]);L(light,x+8,56,x+8,70);}rail(48,106,104);break;
          case 8: for(const x of [34,74,119,159])column(x,102,18);for(let j=0;j<7;j++){const x=42+j*18;R(j%2?gilt:roof[2],x,95,9,7);R(roof[0],x,101,9,1);}break;
          case 9: for(const x of [77,117])column(x,83,34);rail(43,111,32);rail(125,111,32);rose(100,85,7);break;
          case 10: for(const x of [29,55,139,165])column(x,70,49);rose(100,62,13);rail(70,87,60);steps(79,126,42);break;
          case 11: for(const x of [55,140])column(x,82,39);rail(77,61,46);rose(100,45,10);steps(79,126,42);break;
        }
      }
    }

    if(stage>=8){lamp(70,88);lamp(130,88);R(wood[0],79,77,42,10);R(colors[2],81,79,38,6);for(let j=0;j<5;j++)R(colors[0],84+j*7,81,4,2);}
    if(stage===9){post(166,54,73,4);cloth(170,56,22,24,colors[1]);R(colors[3],170,56,22,2);}
    if(stage>=4&&stage<7){post(27,65,65,4);post(166,65,65,4);beam(27,74,166,74);beam(27,75,166,121);}
  }
  function civicExteriorFrame(view,p,x,y,t,active,night,reduced,w){const g=view.effects,f=root.LQValleyDevelopment.civicFacility(w,p.id);if(f.banner!==undefined){const color=parseInt(root.LQValleyDevelopment.civicFlags[f.banner].slice(1),16),sway=reduced?0:Math.sin(t*1.8)*3;g.fillStyle(0x5f4834,1).fillRect(x+59,y-75,3,75);g.fillStyle(color,1).fillTriangle(x+62,y-73,x+91+sway,y-66,x+62,y-49);g.lineStyle(1,0xe4d8aa,.8).lineBetween(x+62,y-73,x+91+sway,y-66);}if(reduced)return;if(p.kind==='pump'&&active){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const a=(t*.65+j/4)%1;g.lineStyle(1,0xa7d9c5,.6*(1-a)).strokeEllipse(x-55,y-6,5+a*26,3+a*8);}}else if(p.kind==='loom'||p.kind==='clothier'||p.kind==='paper'){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){let xx=x-72+j*7;g.lineStyle(1,0xe0d1b1,.6).lineBetween(xx,y-36,xx+Math.sin(t*2+j)*3,y-12);}}else if(active&&p.kind==='kitchen'){for(let j=0;j<6;j++){let f=(t*.44+j/6)%1;g.fillStyle(0xb6c3b3,.22*(1-f)).fillEllipse(x+36+f*19,y-103-f*38,5+f*13,3+f*7);}}else if(active&&p.kind==='stonecut'){for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let f=(t*.8+j/5)%1;g.fillStyle(0xd7c7a1,.7*(1-f)).fillRect(x-58+(j-2)*f*6,y-10-f*13,2,2);}}}

  // V102.3: bespoke massing within the original 200×150 sprite envelope.
  // The collision footprint and central ground-floor entrance are unchanged.
  const monumentPlans=Object.freeze([
    {name:'Transit / raised timber concourse', masses:[[28,81,144,45,'wood','shed'],[35,43,34,68,'stone','spire'],[112,47,41,51,'wood','gable']],detail:'trestle'},

  function monumentRender(ctx,p,stage){
    const plan=monumentPlans[p.style]; if(!plan)return false;
    const R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),P=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts),L=(c,x,y,X,Y,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(X),Math.round(Y),w),rng=random(49361+p.style*9817),roof=p.roof;
    const stone=['#394742','#68786e','#a0a897','#d8d4bb'],wood=['#3d322c','#785741','#b2865d','#e1c08c'],metal=['#30484a','#5d8584','#a5c0b5','#e0e6cb'],glass=['#294c50','#487f86','#9bc2b8','#e0e9cb'];
    const color=m=>m==='wood'?wood:m==='glass'?glass:m==='metal'?metal:stone;
    function stonework(x,y,w,h,mat){const a=color(mat);R(a[0],x,y,w,h);if(mat==='wood'){for(let X=x+2;X<x+w-2;X+=6){R(a[1],X,y+1,Math.min(5,x+w-X-1),h-2);R(a[2],X,y+1,1,h-2);for(let Y=y+5;Y<y+h-4;Y+=11){R(a[0],X+2,Y+(X%3),2,1);R(a[2],X+2,Y+3,3,1);}}}else if(mat==='glass'){for(let X=x+2;X<x+w-2;X+=13){let z=Math.min(11,x+w-X-1);R(a[1],X,y+2,z,h-4);P(a[2],[[X+1,y+3],[X+z-1,y+3],[X+1,y+h*.58]]);R(a[3],X+1,y+3,1,h-6);R(a[0],X,y+h*.48,z,2);}}else{for(let Y=y+2,i=0;Y<y+h-1;Y+=6,i++)for(let X=x+1-(i%2)*6;X<x+w-1;X+=12){const left=Math.max(x+1,X),right=Math.min(x+w-1,X+11),hh=Math.min(5,y+h-Y-1);if(right<=left)continue;R(a[rng()<.15?2:1],left,Y,right-left,hh);R(a[2],left,Y,right-left,1);if(rng()<.28&&hh>2){R(a[0],right-2,Y+2,1,hh-2);if(Y>y+h*.68)R('#66785a',left,Y+hh-1,Math.min(4,right-left),1);}}}if(ctx.__lqDetailScale===2){ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(x+2,y+2,Math.max(0,w-4),Math.max(0,h-4));ctx.clip();for(let Y=y+4;Y<y+h-3;Y+=6)for(let X=x+3;X<x+w-3;X+=6){const n=C.hash(X,Y,102715+p.style);if(mat==='wood'){fineRect(ctx,a[2],X+.5,Y, .5,3+n*2);if(n>.7)fineRect(ctx,a[0],X+1.5,Y+1,.5,2);}else if(mat==='glass'){if(n>.62)fineRect(ctx,a[3],X+.5,Y,2.5,.5);}else if(n>.44){fineRect(ctx,a[2],X,Y+.5,2.5,.5);fineRect(ctx,a[0],X+1,Y+1.5,2,.5);}}ctx.restore();}R(a[3],x+1,y+1,1,h-2);R(a[0],x+w-3,y+2,2,h-3);}
    function column(x,y,h,mat='stone',w=7){const a=color(mat);R(a[0],x,y,w,h);R(a[2],x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);R(a[3],x+1,y+1,1,h-2);R(a[1],x+w-3,y+2,2,h-4);R(a[0],x-2,y+h-4,w+4,4);R(a[3],x-2,y-2,w+4,2);}
    function pane(x,y,w,h,arch=false){const a=glass; if(arch){P(a[0],[[x-2,y+h],[x-2,y+5],[x+w/2,y-3],[x+w+2,y+5],[x+w+2,y+h]]);P(a[1],[[x,y+h-1],[x,y+6],[x+w/2,y],[x+w,y+6],[x+w,y+h-1]]);}else{R(a[0],x-1,y-1,w+2,h+2);R(a[1],x,y,w,h);}P(a[2],[[x+1,y+5],[x+w-1,y+5],[x+1,y+h-3]]);R(a[3],x+1,y+6,1,h-7);R(a[0],x+w/2,y+5,1,h-5);R(a[0],x,y+h*.55,w,1);R(stone[2],x-2,y+h,w+4,2);}
    function door(x,y,w,h){P('#323831',[[x,y+h],[x,y+8],[x+3,y+4],[x+w/2,y],[x+w-3,y+4],[x+w,y+8],[x+w,y+h]]);P(wood[1],[[x+2,y+h-1],[x+2,y+9],[x+w/2,y+3],[x+w-2,y+9],[x+w-2,y+h-1]]);for(let X=x+5;X<x+w-3;X+=5)R(wood[0],X,y+9,1,h-10);R(wood[3],x+w-6,y+h*.65,2,2);}
    function tiles(a,b,c,d,pal=roof){P(pal[0],[a,b,c,d]);const pt=(u,v)=>[a[0]*(1-u)*(1-v)+b[0]*u*(1-v)+c[0]*u*v+d[0]*(1-u)*v,a[1]*(1-u)*(1-v)+b[1]*u*(1-v)+c[1]*u*v+d[1]*(1-u)*v];let rows=Math.max(3,Math.ceil(Math.abs(d[1]-a[1])/4)),cols=Math.max(4,Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(b[0]-a[0]),Math.abs(c[0]-d[0]))/8));for(let i=0;i<rows;i++)for(let j=0;j<cols;j++){const A=pt(j/cols,i/rows),B=pt((j+1)/cols,i/rows),C1=pt((j+1)/cols,(i+1)/rows),D1=pt(j/cols,(i+1)/rows);P(pal[(i+j)%5===0?2:1],[A,B,C1,D1]);L(pal[2],...A,...B);L(pal[0],...B,...C1);}L(pal[3],...a,...b,2);L(pal[0],...d,...c,3);}
    function roofShape(x,y,w,mode,mat){const pal=mat==='glass'?glass:roof,mid=x+w/2;
      if(mode==='spire'){P(pal[0],[[x-5,y+3],[mid,y-22],[x+w+5,y+3]]);P(pal[2],[[x-5,y+3],[mid,y-22],[mid,y+3]]);L(pal[3],x-4,y+1,mid,y-21,2);R(pal[0],mid-1,y-25,2,5);}
      else if(mode==='dome'||mode==='barrel'){const h=mode==='dome'?24:19,pts=[[x-5,y+4],[x-4,y-4],[x+w*.14,y-h*.68],[mid,y-h],[x+w*.86,y-h*.68],[x+w+4,y-4],[x+w+5,y+4]];P(pal[0],pts);P(pal[1],[[x-3,y+2],[x+w*.14,y-h*.64],[mid,y-h+1],[x+w*.86,y-h*.64],[x+w+3,y+2]]);for(let j=1;j<7;j++){const X=x+w*j/7;L(pal[2],mid,y-h+2,X,y+2,1);}L(pal[3],x+w*.14,y-h*.64,mid,y-h+1,2);R(pal[0],x-5,y+3,w+10,4);}
      else if(mode==='tank'){R(metal[0],x-3,y-13,w+6,19);R(metal[1],x-1,y-11,w+2,15);R(metal[2],x,y-10,3,13);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)R(metal[0],x-2,y-8+j*5,w+4,1);P(metal[2],[[x-3,y-13],[x+5,y-18],[x+w-5,y-18],[x+w+3,y-13]]);}
      else if(mode==='lantern'){P('#413c32',[[x-5,y+5],[mid,y-12],[x+w+5,y+5]]);R('#d7b76e',mid-6,y-3,12,15);R('#fff0a4',mid-3,y-1,6,10);R('#4b4a3d',mid-1,y-5,2,17);}
      else if(mode==='aqueduct'){R(stone[0],x-4,y-8,w+8,10);R('#477b80',x,y-6,w,5);R('#a4d5cb',x+1,y-5,w-2,1);R(stone[2],x-5,y-1,w+10,3);}
      else if(mode==='pagoda'){tiles([x+7,y-16],[x+w-7,y-16],[x+w+13,y+6],[x-13,y+6]);P(pal[0],[[x-13,y+6],[x-2,y-1],[x+4,y+8],[x+w-4,y+8],[x+w+2,y-1],[x+w+13,y+6],[x+w+7,y+11],[x-7,y+11]]);tiles([mid-12,y-30],[mid+12,y-30],[x+w-5,y-15],[x+5,y-15]);}
      else if(mode==='gable'||mode==='pediment'){tiles([mid-4,y-24],[mid+12,y-24],[x+w+6,y+5],[x-6,y+5]);if(mode==='pediment'){P(stone[0],[[x-3,y+5],[mid,y-21],[x+w+3,y+5]]);P(stone[2],[[x+4,y+3],[mid,y-15],[x+w-4,y+3]]);L(stone[3],x+4,y+2,mid,y-15,2);R(stone[0],x-5,y+5,w+10,3);}}
      else {const off=mode==='hip'?w*.23:4;tiles([x+off,y-18],[x+w-off,y-18],[x+w+6,y+4],[x-6,y+4]);if(mode==='hip')P(pal[0],[[x-6,y+4],[x+off,y-18],[x+off,y+4]]);}}
    // Forecourt and ruined fragments are stage-specific; no completed architecture at low stages.
    shadow(ctx,100,132,78,12);P(stage<3?'#60694e':'#817a66',[[22,120],[42,107],[158,107],[178,120],[160,138],[40,138]]);
    ctx.save();P(null,[[22,120],[42,107],[158,107],[178,120],[160,138],[40,138]]);ctx.clip();for(let Y=108;Y<139;Y+=5)for(let X=24+(Y%2)*5;X<177;X+=10){if(stage<3&&rng()<.55)continue;R('#666e5c',X,Y,9,4);R('#9c9b80',X,Y,9,1);}ctx.restore();

  function labWingArt(ctx,id,status){if(!status)return;const R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h);if(status==='building'){for(const x of [118,137,157])R('#795c42',x,60,3,44);line(ctx,'#ba9165',117,66,160,66,2);line(ctx,'#ba9165',119,68,157,101,2);return;}const sky=id==='mon-sky';R('#384a46',115,76,46,25);R(sky?'#797d9b':'#729e83',117,78,42,21);for(let x=120;x<154;x+=11){R('#334f55',x,80,9,15);R(sky?'#9bb5c5':'#7cbaa5',x+1,81,7,12);R('#dce0c4',x+1,81,1,10);}R('#324444',113,73,50,5);R(sky?'#c4bdaf':'#afc5a6',113,73,50,1);R('#717d65',115,99,46,3);}

  // Indoor foreground only; does not participate in collision or change room IDs.
  function cutawayOpacity(y,height,k){const d=(height-y)/k;return d<22?.17:d<52?.17+(d-22)/30*.83:1;}
  function cutawayPanelOpacity(x,y,w,h,k,side){const mid=w*k/2,dist=side===0?Math.max(0,x-mid):Math.max(0,mid-x);const near=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-dist/(32*k)));return 1-(1-cutawayOpacity(y,h*k,k))*near;}
  function cutawayArt(ctx,w,h){const R=(c,x,y,W,H)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,W,H),m=Math.floor(w/64)*32;for(const [x,W] of [[0,m-16],[m+16,w-m-16]]){R('#303e36',x,h-26,W,26);R('#b2b095',x,h-26,W,4);R('#66756b',x,h-22,W,17);for(let X=x+8;X<x+W-1;X+=13){R('#43534a',X,h-21,1,8);R('#43534a',X+4,h-12,1,7);}R('#43534a',x,h-13,W,1);R('#263730',x,h-5,W,5);}R('#bdb79b',m-16,h-7,32,2);}

  function eastPaint(ctx,p,stage){
    if(p.monument&&monumentRender(ctx,p,stage))return;
    if(p.civic||p.expansion||p.monument){civicPaint(ctx,p,stage);if(p.monument&&stage<2){let rng=random(p.x*29+p.y*83);for(let j=0;j<5-stage*2;j++){const x=37+Math.round(rng()*124),y=126+Math.round(rng()*7);shape(ctx,'#454e43',[[x-4,y],[x-3,y-6],[x+4,y-8],[x+9,y-2],[x+2,y+3]]);line(ctx,'#a1a68b',x-3,y-6,x+4,y-8,1);if(j%2===0){box(ctx,'#65805b',x-1,y-9,3,5);box(ctx,'#9bad74',x-3,y-10,5,2);}}}return;}
    const i=C.EAST_SITES.indexOf(p),rng=random(62518+i*1709),[dark,mid,hi,lit]=p.palette;
    const wood=['#3e302a','#795a42','#b28c60','#e1bd87'],stone=['#3f4541','#68716a','#91998a','#bec0a5'];
    const masonryKinds=['kiln','forge','lapidary','library','bakery','filter']; const masonryMode=masonryKinds.includes(p.kind),glass=['glass','nursery'].includes(p.kind),open=['market','tea','dye','saw','press','pottery','filter'].includes(p.kind);
    const roofColors=[['#3d4438','#6d7750','#a6a36a','#c0b17c'],['#443d50','#746784','#9b87a9','#c2acbc'],['#513c31','#905d42','#b98159','#e2ab73'],['#384f42','#6d8362','#a5ae75','#cdd2a0'],['#514735','#9b8050','#c4a16b','#ead29b'],['#334950','#577680','#82a0a2','#bec6c8']][Math.floor(i/3)];
    const rect=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),poly=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts),ln=(c,x,y,xx,yy,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,x,y,xx,yy,w);
    function brick(x,y,w,h,colors=stone){rect(colors[0],x,y,w,h);for(let yy=y+1,row=0;yy<y+h-1;yy+=6,row++)for(let xx=x+1-(row%2)*5;xx<x+w-1;xx+=10){const a=Math.max(x+1,xx),b=Math.min(x+w-1,xx+9),hh=Math.min(y+h-yy-1,5);if(b-a<1||hh<1)continue;rect(colors[rng()>.75?2:1],a,yy,b-a,hh);rect(colors[3],a,yy,b-a,1);if(hh>2&&rng()>.6)rect(colors[0],b-2,yy+2,1,2);}}
    function timber(x,y,w,h){rect(wood[0],x,y,w,h);rect(wood[1],x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);rect(wood[3],x+1,y+1,1,h-2);for(let yy=y+5;yy<y+h-3;yy+=Math.floor(6+rng()*6)){rect(wood[2],x+3,yy,Math.max(1,w-4),1);if(w>5)rect(wood[0],x+w-3,yy+2,1,2);}}
    function plankwall(x,y,w,h){rect(wood[0],x,y,w,h);for(let xx=x+1;xx<x+w-1;xx+=7){rect(wood[1],xx,y+1,6,h-2);rect(wood[2],xx,y+1,1,h-2);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)rect(wood[0],xx+2,y+Math.floor(rng()*(h-5))+2,3,1);}}
    function beam(x,y,xx,yy){ln(wood[0],x,y,xx,yy,7);ln(wood[2],x-1,y-1,xx-1,yy-1,3);ln(wood[3],x-1,y-2,xx-1,yy-2,1);}
    function tiled(a,b,c,d){poly(roofColors[0],[a,b,c,d]);const rows=10,cols=14;for(let r=0;r<rows;r++)for(let j=0;j<cols;j++){const point=(u,v)=>[a[0]*(1-u)*(1-v)+b[0]*u*(1-v)+c[0]*u*v+d[0]*(1-u)*v,a[1]*(1-u)*(1-v)+b[1]*u*(1-v)+c[1]*u*v+d[1]*(1-u)*v];let A=point(j/cols,r/rows),B=point((j+1)/cols,r/rows),C1=point((j+1)/cols,(r+1)/rows),D1=point(j/cols,(r+1)/rows);poly(roofColors[rng()>.66?2:1],[A,B,C1,D1]);ln(roofColors[3],...A,...B);if((r+j)%2)ln(roofColors[0],...B,...C1);}ln(roofColors[3],...a,...b,2);ln(roofColors[0],...d,...c,3);}
    function window(x,y,w=15,h=23){rect(wood[0],x-3,y-3,w+6,h+6);rect('#395355',x,y,w,h);poly('#7ba0a0',[[x,y],[x+w,y],[x,y+h]]);rect('#d1d7ae',x+1,y+1,1,h-2);rect(wood[2],x+w/2,y,2,h);rect(wood[2],x,y+h/2,w,2);rect(stone[2],x-4,y+h+2,w+8,3);}
    function barrel(x,y){poly(wood[0],[[x-8,y-16],[x+7,y-16],[x+9,y-7],[x+6,y],[x-7,y],[x-9,y-7]]);rect(wood[1],x-6,y-14,13,13);rect(wood[2],x-5,y-13,2,12);rect('#495050',x-8,y-12,16,2);rect('#495050',x-8,y-4,16,2);rect(wood[3],x-5,y-16,9,2);}
    function plants(x,y,n){for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const dx=j*9,hh=5+Math.floor(rng()*9);rect('#5e4737',x+dx-3,y-4,7,5);ln('#486b4e',x+dx,y-5,x+dx,y-5-hh,1);poly(hi,[[x+dx,y-7],[x+dx-5,y-10],[x+dx-5,y-13],[x+dx,y-10]]);poly(lit,[[x+dx,y-8],[x+dx+4,y-14],[x+dx+5,y-11]]);}}
    function sack(x,y){poly('#584b37',[[x-8,y],[x-10,y-9],[x-5,y-14],[x+4,y-14],[x+9,y-8],[x+8,y]]);poly('#b29a69',[[x-7,y-1],[x-8,y-8],[x-4,y-12],[x+3,y-12],[x+6,y-7],[x+6,y-1]]);rect('#e0c58c',x-4,y-13,8,2);rect('#c9ae77',x-6,y-8,2,5);}

    // Individual silhouettes: towers, ovens, open sheds, glasshouses and civic gables.
    if(stage>=6){const l=baseX-8,r=baseX+baseW+8,peak=topY-34-(p.kind==='library'?11:0);
      if(p.kind==='windmill'){brick(79,40,54,88,['#524b44','#9b9083','#b4a28f','#d4cbb5']);poly(roofColors[0],[[71,44],[106,12],[143,44]]);poly(roofColors[2],[[71,44],[106,12],[108,40]]);window(101,57,12,17);rect(wood[0],96,105,24,23);}
      else if(glass){poly('#405750',[[l,topY+3],[l+37,peak+6],[r-31,peak+6],[r,topY+3]]);for(let xx=l+8;xx<r-28;xx+=14){poly('#7ca69a',[[xx,topY],[xx+29,peak+8],[xx+40,peak+8],[xx+12,topY]]);ln('#c8d8c6',xx,topY,xx+29,peak+8,2);}ln(wood[2],l,topY+3,r,topY+3,4);}
      else if(['kiln','forge','bakery'].includes(p.kind)){tiled([l+23,peak+9],[r-27,peak+9],[r,topY+3],[l,topY+3]);const cx=p.kind==='forge'?r-19:l+12,cy=peak-7;brick(cx,cy,17,36,['#4a4239','#8f7761','#b79d7f','#d6c8a1']);rect(stone[0],cx-3,cy-3,23,4);rect('#293733',cx+3,cy,11,3);}
      else if(open){tiled([l+18,peak+13],[r-12,peak+13],[r,topY+4],[l,topY+4]);if(['market','tea','dye'].includes(p.kind)){for(let xx=l;xx<r;xx+=12){rect((xx-l)%24?hi:mid,xx,topY+6,10,5);poly((xx-l)%24?hi:mid,[[xx,topY+11],[xx+10,topY+11],[xx+5,topY+16]]);}}}
      else{tiled([l+33,peak],[r-22,peak],[r,topY+2],[l+28,topY+2]);poly(roofColors[1],[[l,topY+3],[l+33,peak],[l+28,topY+2]]);ln(roofColors[3],l,topY+2,l+33,peak,2);ln(roofColors[0],l,topY+3,r,topY+3,3);}
    }
    if(stage>=7){const y=119;switch(p.kind){case 'saw':timber(23,y-12,36,11);timber(27,y-2,4,9);timber(48,y-2,4,9);rect('#bfc5b6',26,y-15,24,3);for(let x=28;x<49;x+=4)rect('#697977',x,y-16,2,2);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)timber(139,y-5-j*5,28,4);break;
    case 'joinery':timber(21,y-13,38,10);timber(24,y-3,5,10);timber(52,y-3,5,10);plankwall(141,y-25,22,28);rect(wood[3],149,y-17,3,2);break;
    case 'press':timber(22,y-32,5,36);timber(52,y-32,5,36);beam(22,y-34,57,y-34);ln(stone[1],39,y-32,39,y-10,3);barrel(40,y+8);sack(150,y+8);break;
    case 'dye':for(let j=0;j<3;j++)barrel(26+j*12,y+6);plants(139,y+6,4);break;
    case 'windmill':sack(28,y+5);sack(44,y+3);barrel(153,y+6);break;
    case 'post':timber(25,y-24,23,25);rect('#d5c49c',28,y-20,17,11);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)rect('#81745a',30,y-18+j*3,11,1);timber(145,y-18,20,20);break;
    case 'kiln':brick(22,y-30,35,33,['#533e32','#996c53','#bb8c66','#e0b887']);poly('#263433',[[28,y-2],[28,y-17],[39,y-25],[51,y-17],[51,y-2]]);rect('#8e5137',31,y-7,16,5);brick(142,y-14,25,16);break;

    case 'glass':plants(20,y+4,5);plants(136,y+4,5);break;
    case 'tea':timber(19,y-12,40,8);timber(22,y-4,4,7);timber(51,y-4,4,7);for(let j=0;j<4;j++){rect('#c1d0b5',24+j*7,y-15,5,4);rect('#7c987f',25+j*7,y-14,3,2);}plants(144,y+4,3);break;}}
    if(stage>=8){for(const x of [baseX+7,baseX+baseW-13]){rect('#3c4036',x,frontY-46,7,12);rect('#f8d898',x+2,frontY-44,3,7);}rect(wood[0],baseX+baseW/2-18,topY+4,36,12);rect(hi,baseX+baseW/2-16,topY+6,32,8);for(let k=0;k<6;k++)rect(dark,baseX+baseW/2-13+k*4,topY+8,2,3);}
    if(stage===9){rect(lit,baseX+baseW/2-21,topY+2,42,2);for(const x of [baseX-9,baseX+baseW+6]){timber(x,frontY-45,4,48);poly(hi,[[x+4,frontY-45],[x+19,frontY-45],[x+15,frontY-34],[x+4,frontY-37]]);}}
    if(stage>=4&&stage<7){for(const x of [baseX-10,baseX+baseW+5])timber(x,topY-8,4,frontY-topY+17);beam(baseX-10,topY+14,baseX+baseW+6,topY+14);beam(baseX-8,topY+14,baseX+baseW+3,frontY);}
  }
  function eastRelease(view,id){for(const[key,s]of [...(view.eastSprites||new Map())]){if(id&&id!==key)continue;const j=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(s);if(j>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(j,1);const old=s.texture.key;if(s.scene)s.destroy();if(old.startsWith('valley-v841-hd')&&view.scene.textures.exists(old))view.scene.textures.remove(old);view.eastSprites.delete(key);}}
  function charterBed(ctx,o,p){const W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T,r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),a=p.palette; r('#30332b',2,6,W-4,H-4);r('#4d3529',3,2,W-6,H-5);r('#a48158',5,3,W-10,4);r('#b79765',5,7,W-10,H-12);r('#eadbbb',7,8,W-14,H-15);r('#c4b194',7,12,W-14,2);r('#fff0d0',9,8,W-18,5);r(a[0],7,15,W-14,H-20);r(a[1],9,16,W-18,H-23);for(let x=11;x<W-9;x+=6)for(let y=18;y<H-7;y+=5){r(a[2],x,y,2,2);r(a[3],x+1,y,1,1);}r('#d9bd82',7,H-9,W-14,2);r('#6f4c37',4,H-6,W-8,4);r('#bc975f',6,H-6,W-12,1);}

  function charterRestFrame(scene,w,h){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,p=D.eastById[h.id],resident=D.charterState(w)?.houses[h.id];if(!p||!resident)return;const l=h.layout,o=l.objects.find(a=>a.id==='rest'),spr=h.furnitureViews.get('rest');if(!o||!spr)return;const key=texture(scene,'charter-bed-'+p.id,o.w*T,o.h*T,c=>charterBed(c,o,p));if(spr.texture.key!==key)spr.setTexture(key);const floor=scene.inside.objs[0];if(!h.charterFloor){const f=texture(scene,'charter-floor-'+p.id,l.w*T,l.h*T,ctx=>{roomFloor(ctx,l,p.id);hallFinish(ctx,l,p.id);districtRoomFinish(ctx,l,p.id);civicRoomDetail(ctx,l,p);charterLodgingFloor(ctx,l,p,o);});floor.setTexture(f);h.charterFloor=true;}}

  function constructionArt(ctx,p,target,quarter,cost){const r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h);ctx.save();const wood='#73543c',hi='#c4a678',shadow='#392e29';
    if(target>=3&&target<=7){const top=target===3?99:target===4?74:target===5?51:35;for(const x of [34,62,138,166]){r(shadow,x-1,top,5,126-top);r(wood,x,top,3,126-top);r(hi,x,top,1,126-top);r(shadow,x-4,126,11,3);}for(let y=top+14;y<120;y+=22){for(const [a,b] of [[34,62],[138,166]]){r(wood,a-3,y,b-a+9,4);r(hi,a-3,y,b-a+9,1);line(ctx,wood,a,y,b,y+20,2);line(ctx,hi,a,y,b,y+20,1);}}for(let y=top+5;y<122;y+=5){r(wood,172,y,10,2);r(hi,172,y,10,1);}r(shadow,171,top,2,128-top);r(wood,182,top,2,128-top);}
    const items=Object.entries(cost||{}).filter(([,n])=>n>0);items.slice(0,3).forEach(([key,n],i)=>{const x=13+i*22,y=118;for(let j=0;j<Math.min(5,n);j++){const xx=x+(j%2)*8,yy=y-Math.floor(j/2)*5;const c=key==='stone'||key==='brick'?'#9c9a84':key==='wood'||key==='plank'?'#a48153':'#819393';r(shadow,xx,yy,9,6);r(c,xx+1,yy,7,4);r('#d4c4a0',xx+1,yy,6,1);}});

    for(const p of C.EAST_SITES){const x=p.x*T,y=p.y*T;if(x<r.x-145||x>r.right+145||y<r.y-175||y>r.bottom+130){eastRelease(view,p.id);continue;}const s=D.eastState(w,p.id),ch=D.charterState(w),h=ch?.houses[p.id],project=D.publicAt(w,p.id),works=project&&D.publicState(w,project.id),era=s.stage>=8?(ch?.era||0):0,festival=D.festivalAt(w,p.id),build=D.constructionState(w,p.id),quarter=build?Math.min(3,Math.floor(D.constructionProgress(w,p.id)*4)):0,workKey=works&&(works.stage||works.pending)?'-works-'+works.stage+'-'+Number(!!works.pending):'',key=detailTexture(scene,'v1023-east-architecture-'+p.id+'-'+s.stage+(h?'-charter-'+ch.era+'-'+Math.floor(h.wear/10):'')+'-era-'+era+workKey+(D.wingState(w,p.id)?'-lab-'+D.wingState(w,p.id).status:'')+(p.id==='mon-library'&&D.annexState(w)?'-annex-'+D.annexState(w).status:'')+(s.specialization?'-special-'+s.specialization:'')+(festival?'-festival-'+festival.id:'')+(build?'-construction-'+build.target+'-'+quarter+'-'+Object.entries(D.teamStash(w,p.id)).map(([k,n])=>k+n).join('_'):''),200,150,c=>{eastPaint(c,p,s.stage);labWingArt(c,p.id,D.wingState(w,p.id)?.status);if(p.id==='mon-library')libraryAnnexArt(c,D.annexState(w)?.status);if(h)charterFacade(c,p,h,ch.era);if(s.stage>=8) civilizationFacade(c,p,era,works);if(festival)festivalFacade(c,festival);v91Court(c,p,era,s.stage);horizonCourt(c,p,era,s.stage);if(s.specialization)specialFacade(c,s.specialization);if(build)constructionArt(c,p,build.target,quarter,D.teamStash(w,p.id));});let a=map.get(p.id);if(!a){a=scene.add.image(x,y,key).setOrigin(.5,126/150).setScale((p.scale||1)/detailFactor(key)).setDepth(view.depthAt(y+19*(p.scale||1)));map.set(p.id,a);scene.areaObjs.push(a);}else if(a.texture.key!==key){const old=a.texture.key;a.setTexture(key).setScale((p.scale||1)/detailFactor(key));if(old.startsWith('valley-v841-hd')&&scene.textures.exists(old))scene.textures.remove(old);}a.setAlpha(scene.player.y<y&&scene.player.y>y-112*(p.scale||1)&&Math.abs(scene.player.x-x)<65*(p.scale||1)?.43:1);
      const g=view.effects,active=s.stage>=8&&s.job&&s.job.phase==='working'&&D.civicPumpOpen(w,p.id)&&w.clock.minuteOfDay>=420&&w.clock.minuteOfDay<1080,k=reduced?0:t;
      if(p.civic&&s.stage>=8)civicExteriorFrame(view,p,x,y,k,active&&D.civicPumpOpen(w,p.id),night,reduced,w);if(p.kind==='windmill'&&s.stage>=7){const cx=x+5,cy=y-77;for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const q=(active?k*1.8:0)+j*Math.PI/2+.45,dx=Math.cos(q),dy=Math.sin(q),ex=cx+dx*36,ey=cy+dy*36;g.lineStyle(3,0x5a4938,1).lineBetween(cx,cy,ex,ey);g.lineStyle(7,0xd9c6a2,.95).lineBetween(cx+dx*14-dy*5,cy+dy*14+dx*5,ex-dy*5,ey+dx*5);}g.fillStyle(0xa8895b,1).fillCircle(cx,cy,4);}
      if(s.stage>=8&&night)for(let j=8;j>0;j--)view.light.fillStyle(0xf1c878,.018).fillEllipse(x,y-29,20+j*15,10+j*8);
      if(active&&!reduced){if(['kiln','forge','bakery'].includes(p.kind)){for(let j=0;j<6;j++){const f=(k*.37+j/6)%1;g.fillStyle(0xa9b3a5,.24*(1-f)).fillEllipse(x-47+Math.sin(k+j)*7+f*18,y-99-f*44,5+f*16,3+f*8);}g.fillStyle(0xf2ad53,.6+.2*Math.sin(k*9)).fillRect(x-64,y-4,8,4);}else if(['filter','nursery','glass','dye','tea'].includes(p.kind)){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const f=(k*.8+j/4)%1;g.lineStyle(1,0xc5e4d8,.65*(1-f)).strokeEllipse(x-49,y-3,3+f*23,2+f*7);}}else if(p.kind!=='windmill'){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const f=(k*1.1+j*.23)%1;g.fillStyle(0xd4be8b,.75*(1-f)).fillRect(x-55+(j%2?1:-1)*f*13,y-13-f*8,2,2);}}}
      if(s.stage>=8&&Object.values(s.stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)>8){for(let j=0;j<Math.min(4,Math.ceil(Object.values(s.stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/12));j++){const xx=x+50+j%2*12,yy=y+20-Math.floor(j/2)*9;g.fillStyle(0x5d4831,1).fillRect(xx,yy,10,9);g.fillStyle(0xad8a59,1).fillRect(xx+1,yy+1,8,7);g.fillStyle(0xe2c591,1).fillRect(xx+1,yy+1,8,1);}}}
    if(!reduced)for(let j=0;j<C.EAST_POOLS.length;j++){const p=C.EAST_POOLS[j],b=C.bounds(p);if((b.x+b.w)*T<r.x||b.x*T>r.right||(b.y+b.h)*T<r.y||b.y*T>r.bottom)continue;for(let i=0;i<15;i++){const x=(b.x+.2+C.hash(i,j,21)*(b.w-.4))*T,y=(b.y+.2+C.hash(i,j,22)*(b.h-.4))*T;if(!C.eastPool(x/T,y/T))continue;const f=(t*.25+i*.13)%1;view.effects.lineStyle(1,0xc3dbca,.44*(1-f)).strokeEllipse(x,y,5+f*15,2+f*5);}}}
  function eastPoolFinish(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){if(ox+w<220*T)return;for(const p of C.EAST_POOLS){const b=C.bounds(p);if((b.x+b.w+1)*T<ox||(b.y+b.h+1)*T<oy||b.x*T>ox+w+32||b.y*T>oy+h+32)continue;const pixel=p.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]);shape(ctx,'#687d70',pixel.map(([x,y])=>[x+(x-(b.x+b.w/2)*T)*.07,y+(y-(b.y+b.h/2)*T)*.08]));shape(ctx,'#41665f',pixel);ctx.save();shape(ctx,null,pixel);ctx.clip();for(let y=Math.floor(b.y*T/4)*4;y<(b.y+b.h)*T;y+=4)for(let x=Math.floor(b.x*T/4)*4;x<(b.x+b.w)*T;x+=4){const n=wildNoise(x,y,49,7701),d=wildNoise(x,y,15,7702);box(ctx,rgb(blend([38,81,85],[101,148,126],n*.68+d*.16)),x,y,4,4);if(d>.81)box(ctx,'#83b4a3',x,y,4,1);}ctx.restore();}}
  function eastGroundDetail(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){if(ox+w<168*T)return;for(const p of C.EAST_SITES){const cx=p.x*T,cy=p.y*T;if(cx<ox-155||cx>ox+w+155||cy<oy-155||cy>oy+h+155)continue;
    for(let y=Math.max(oy,Math.floor((cy-40)/4)*4);y<Math.min(oy+h,cy+72);y+=4)for(let x=Math.max(ox,Math.floor((cx-100)/4)*4);x<Math.min(ox+w,cx+100);x+=4){const dist=Math.hypot((x-cx)/104,(y-cy-10)/66),n=C.hash(x,y,9921),noise=wildNoise(x,y,37,9922),coverage=smooth((1.04-dist+(noise-.5)*.18)/.59);if(coverage<=.01)continue;const base=wildColor(x+2,y+2),soil=blend(base,[133,117,88],.62),col=blend(base,soil,coverage*.93);box(ctx,rgb(col.map(v=>v+(noise-.5)*10+(n-.5)*3)),x,y,4,4);if(n>.94&&coverage>.3)box(ctx,rgb(blend(col,[184,163,123],.44)),x+1,y+2,2,1);}
    }refinedPool(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);}
  // V88 eastern habitat atlas. The same sprites are used by the terrain painter and visual QA.
  const gardenSpecies=Object.freeze({"amberwood":[["Wood anemone","flower",9,7,4,"#e8e5cd","#6e9957"],["Foxglove","spike",19,5,7,"#bf85a4","#70915c"],["Wood
