  function gardenPlant(ctx,x,y,s,seed){
    const [name,kind,h,w,n,accent,leaf]=s,rng=random(seed),R=(c,a,b,ww=1,hh=1)=>box(ctx,c,x+a,y+b,ww,hh),L=(c,a,b,d,e,z=1)=>line(ctx,c,x+a,y+b,x+d,y+e,z),P=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts.map(([a,b])=>[x+a,y+b]));
    const dark='#425e4d',stem='#546f52',lit='#d4d7a3';
    const ellipseLeaf=(a,b,d,e)=>{P(dark,[[a,b],[a+d*.65,b-e-1],[a+d,b-e+1],[a+d*.65,b+2]]);P(leaf,[[a,b-1],[a+d*.7,b-e],[a+d-1*Math.sign(d),b-e+1],[a+d*.58,b+1]]);L(lit,a+d*.26,b-1,a+d*.72,b-e+1);};
    const blossom=(a,b,size)=>{P('#5c5c54',[[a-size,b-1],[a-1,b-size],[a+size,b-1],[a+size-1,b+size-1],[a,b+size],[a-size,b+1]]);R(accent,a-size+1,b-1,size*2-1,2);R(accent,a-1,b-size+1,2,size*2-1);R('#f1dfac',a,b,1,1);};
    const leafBase=()=>{for(let j=0;j<Math.min(n,5);j++){const d=j%2?1:-1;ellipseLeaf(0,-2-j%2,d*(3+w*(.38+j*.07)),2+j%3);}};
    if(kind==='flower'||kind==='bell'||kind==='spike'||kind==='reed'||kind==='grass'){
      leafBase();for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const a=Math.round((j/(Math.max(1,n-1))-.5)*w*1.45),hh=Math.round(h*(.57+rng()*.43)),b=-hh,root=Math.round(a*.2);L(dark,root,0,a,b,kind==='reed'?2:1);L(leaf,root+1,-1,a+1,b+1);if(j%2===0)ellipseLeaf(Math.round((a+root)/2),Math.round(b*.5),(j%4?-1:1)*Math.round(w*.45),3);
        if(kind==='flower')blossom(a,b,2+(j%2));else if(kind==='bell'){L(leaf,a,b,a+2,b-2);P(accent,[[a+1,b-2],[a+4,b-1],[a+5,b+3],[a+2,b+4],[a,b+2]]);R('#e9ddc9',a+2,b+2,2,1);}else if(kind==='spike'){for(let k=0;k<3+j%3;k++){R(accent,a-(k%2?2:0),b+k*2,3,2);R(lit,a,b+k*2,1,1);}}else if(kind==='reed'){R('#634f3d',a-1,b,3,7);R(accent,a,b,1,6);}else for(let k=0;k<3;k++){L(accent,a,b+k*2,a+(k%2?3:-3),b+k*2-2);R(lit,a,b+k*2,1,1);}}
    }else if(kind==='fern'||kind==='rosette'){
      for(let j=0;j<n;j++){let a=Math.PI+(j/(n-1))*Math.PI,dx=Math.cos(a)*w,dy=Math.sin(a)*h;L(dark,0,0,dx,dy,2);L(leaf,0,-1,dx,dy);if(kind==='fern'){for(let k=1;k<5;k++){const u=k/5,xx=dx*u,yy=dy*u,z=Math.max(2,5*(1-u));ellipseLeaf(xx,yy,-z,3);ellipseLeaf(xx,yy,z,3);}}else{P(dark,[[0,0],[dx*.8+3,dy*.65],[dx,dy],[dx*.8-3,dy*.65]]);P(leaf,[[0,-1],[dx*.8+1,dy*.65],[dx,dy],[dx*.8-1,dy*.65]]);L(accent,0,-1,dx,dy);}}
    }else if(kind==='mushroom'){
      for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const a=Math.round((rng()-.5)*w*1.8),b=Math.round(-h*(.4+rng()*.55)),r=2+j%3;R('#6e685c',a-1,b,3,-b);R(leaf,a,b+1,1,-b-1);P('#585856',[[a-r-1,b],[a-r,b-3],[a,b-5],[a+r,b-3],[a+r+1,b]]);P(accent,[[a-r,b-1],[a-r+1,b-3],[a,b-4],[a+r-1,b-3],[a+r,b-1]]);R(lit,a-1,b-3,2,1);}
    }else if(kind==='moss'||kind==='clover'){
      for(let j=0;j<n;j++){let a=Math.round((rng()-.5)*w*1.8),b=-Math.round(rng()*h);if(kind==='clover'){L(stem,0,0,a,b);R(leaf,a-2,b-1,5,3);R(leaf,a-1,b-3,3,5);R(accent,a,b-2,2,2);}else{P(dark,[[a-3,b+1],[a-2,b-2],[a+2,b-2],[a+4,b+1],[a+2,b+2],[a-3,b+2]]);R(leaf,a-2,b-1,5,2);R(accent,a,b-2,2,1);}}
    }else if(kind==='succulent'){
      for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const a=(j%2?1:-1)*Math.round(w*.46),b=-Math.round(h*(.4+j/n*.5));P(dark,[[a-4,b],[a-4,b-4],[a,b-6],[a+4,b-4],[a+4,b+2],[a,b+4]]);P(leaf,[[a-3,b],[a-3,b-3],[a,b-5],[a+3,b-3],[a+3,b+1],[a,b+3]]);R(lit,a-2,b-2,1,2);L(stem,0,0,a,b+1,2);if(j===n-1)blossom(a,b-6,2);}
    }else if(kind==='conifer'){
      R('#665f4e',-1,-h,3,h);for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const b=-h+Math.round((j+1)*h/(n+1)),r=Math.round(w*(.2+j/n*.65));P(dark,[[0,b-6],[-r,b+3],[-r+3,b+4],[r,b+3]]);P(leaf,[[0,b-5],[-r+1,b+2],[0,b+1],[r-1,b+2]]);L(accent,0,b-4,-r+2,b+1);}
    }else{
      L('#635d45',0,0,0,-h,kind==='sapling'?3:2);L(kind==='sapling'?'#cbbda0':stem,-1,0,-1,-h+1);for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const a=Math.round((j%2?1:-1)*w*(.4+rng()*.55)),b=-Math.round(h*(.25+j/n*.7));L(stem,0,b+4,a,b);for(let q=0;q<3;q++){const xx=a+q*2-2,yy=b-q%2*3;ellipseLeaf(xx,yy,j%2?4:-4,3);}if(kind==='berry'){R('#544b56',a,b+1,3,3);R(accent,a,b+1,2,2);}else R(accent,a,b-2,2,1);}}
  }
  function gardenPick(region,x,y){const plants=gardenSpecies[region];if(!plants)return null;const i=Math.floor(C.hash(x,y,8831)*plants.length);return plants[i];}
  function eastPlant(ctx,x,y,region,grove,rng){const species=gardenPick(region,x,y);if(species){gardenPlant(ctx,x,y,species,Math.floor(C.hash(x,y,8832)*4294967295));return true;}if(authoredPlant(ctx,x,y,region,grove,rng))return true;
    if(region==='heath'&&grove>.48){for(let j=0;j<7;j++){const dx=Math.floor(rng()*18)-9,dy=Math.floor(rng()*7),hh=4+Math.floor(rng()*6);line(ctx,'#56566a',x+dx,y+dy,x+dx-1,y+dy-hh);box(ctx,j%2?'#a287b0':'#c1a0c1',x+dx-2,y+dy-hh,3,2);box(ctx,'#ddc6df',x+dx-2,y+dy-hh,1,1);}return true;}
    if(region==='goldgrass'&&grove>.38){for(let j=0;j<8;j++){const dx=Math.floor(rng()*19)-9,dy=Math.floor(rng()*6),hh=6+Math.floor(rng()*8);line(ctx,'#8c8e50',x+dx,y+dy,x+dx-2,y+dy-hh);line(ctx,'#d6bd72',x+dx-2,y+dy-hh,x+dx-3,y+dy-hh-3,2);box(ctx,'#ead89a',x+dx-3,y+dy-hh-3,1,2);}return true;}
    if(region==='moonbloom'&&grove>.56){for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const dx=Math.floor(rng()*19)-9,dy=Math.floor(rng()*8);line(ctx,'#719785',x+dx,y+dy,x+dx+1,y+dy-6);shape(ctx,'#b2bed2',[[x+dx-3,y+dy-8],[x+dx,y+dy-11],[x+dx+4,y+dy-8],[x+dx+1,y+dy-5]]);box(ctx,'#e3d8e6',x+dx,y+dy-9,2,2);}return true;}
    if(region==='coppervale'&&grove<.44){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const dx=Math.floor(rng()*19)-9,dy=Math.floor(rng()*9),ww=3+Math.floor(rng()*5);shape(ctx,'#855d43',[[x+dx-ww,y+dy],[x+dx-2,y+dy-4],[x+dx+ww,y+dy-2],[x+dx+ww-1,y+dy+2]]);line(ctx,'#c18d61',x+dx-ww,y+dy,x+dx-2,y+dy-4);line(ctx,'#a77750',x+dx-2,y+dy-4,x+dx+ww,y+dy-2);}return true;}
    return false;
  }

  function effects(scene, view, world, seconds, quality, reduced) {
    const {effects:g,shadows,nearFog,tint,light}=view;
    g.clear();shadows.clear();nearFog.clear();tint.clear();light.clear();
    trailLayer(scene,view,world);frontierViews(scene,view,world,seconds,reduced);eastViews(scene,view,world,seconds,reduced);if(!scene.inside)districtRiseAtmosphere(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
    tunnelViews(scene,view,world,seconds,reduced);crossingViews(scene,view,world,seconds,reduced);
    if(!scene.inside){const p=scene.player,n=root.LQValleyDevelopment.tunnelPortals[1],d=Math.hypot(p.x-n.x*T,p.y-n.y*T);for(const ch of view.stream?.values()||[])for(const sprite of ch.items)if(sprite.tunnelOccluder)sprite.setAlpha(d<100?.28+.72*smooth((d-32)/68):1);}
    if(scene.inside) {reserveViews(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);skywayVisual(scene,view,world,seconds,reduced);removeMill(view);clearEchoSprites(view);natureClear(view);return;}
    const area=scene.cameras.main.worldView;
    const minute=world.clock.minuteOfDay;
    const weather=world.weather.kind;
    const daylight=Math.max(0,Math.sin((minute-350)/825*Math.PI));
    const night=minute>=1170||minute<375;
    const dawn=minute>360&&minute<555;
    const dusk=minute>=1015&&minute<1190;
    const rainy=weather==='rain'||weather==='storm';
    const damp=rainy?1:(world.weather.previous==='rain'?Math.max(0,1-(world.clock.day*1440-1440+minute-world.weather.observedAt)/92):0);
    const lx=area.x-8,ly=area.y-8,lw=area.width+16,lh=area.height+16;
    const visible=(x,y,pad=100)=>x>=lx-pad&&x<=lx+lw+pad&&y>=ly-pad&&y<=ly+lh+pad;
    const p=scene.player;
    drawPiles(view,world);
    developmentObjects(view,world);
    freightLandscape(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
    skywayVisual(scene,view,world,seconds,reduced);
    summitEffects(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
    highlandEffects(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
    waterwayEffects(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
    riverCurrent(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);horizonWaterEffects(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);horizonAir(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
    reserveViews(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
    for(const {sprite:tree,stage} of view.trees.values()) {
      const obscures=stage==='mature'&&p.y<tree.y+3&&p.y>tree.y-tree.displayOriginY*.82&&Math.abs(p.x-tree.x)<tree.width*.29;
      if(obscures!==tree.valleyOccludes) {tree.valleyOccludes=obscures;tree.setAlpha(obscures?.52:1);}
    }
    if(!night && weather!=='rain' && weather!=='cloudy') {
      const cast=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,(minute-760)/375));
      const strength=weather==='mist' ? .18 : .28;
      shadows.fillStyle(0x26332c,strength);
      for(const {entry,stage} of view.trees.values()) {
        const {x,y}=center(entry);
        if(stage!=='mature'||!visible(x,y))continue;
        const size=entry.protected?1.6:1;
        const dx=cast*32*size,wide=(26+Math.abs(cast)*58)*size,ht=(10+Math.abs(cast)*8)*size;shadows.fillStyle(0x253b31,strength*.52).fillEllipse(x+dx,y+8,wide,ht);shadows.fillStyle(0x203b2d,strength*.22).fillEllipse(x+dx+wide*.16,y+8-ht*.18,wide*.65,ht*.82).fillEllipse(x+dx-wide*.21,y+8+ht*.08,wide*.53,ht*.72);
      }
      for(const house of HOUSES) {
        if(!visible(house.x*T,house.y*T,250))continue;
        const x=(house.x+house.w/2)*T+cast*23,y=(house.y+house.h)*T+9;
        shadows.fillEllipse(x,y,house.w*T-9,21+Math.abs(cast)*16);
      }
      for(const rock of ROCKS) {
        if(!rock.solid)continue;
        const [,x,y,rx]=rock.apron;
        if(!visible(x*T,y*T,rx*T))continue;
        shadows.fillStyle(0x293932,strength*.48).fillEllipse(x*T+cast*24,y*T,rx*T*1.19,14+rock.rise*7);
      }
    }
    if(night) tint.fillStyle(0x142445,.54).fillRect(lx,ly,lw,lh);
    else if(dusk) tint.fillStyle(0x775141,.32-daylight*.07).fillRect(lx,ly,lw,lh);
    else if(rainy) tint.fillStyle(0x253b4d,.27).fillRect(lx,ly,lw,lh);
    else if(weather==='cloudy') tint.fillStyle(0x557067,.13).fillRect(lx,ly,lw,lh);
    else if(dawn) tint.fillStyle(0xd0b481,.13).fillRect(lx,ly,lw,lh);

    if(damp>0) {
      for(const [x,y,w] of dampSites) {
        if(!visible(x,y))continue;
        shadows.fillStyle(0x423c30,damp*.2).fillEllipse(x,y,w*2.3,w*.75);
        nearFog.fillStyle(0x8ba5a3,damp*.36).fillEllipse(x,y,w*(.85+damp*.5),w*.34);
        nearFog.fillStyle(0xc0cacc,damp*.24).fillRect(x-w*.27,y-2,w*.37,1);
        if(rainy&&!reduced) {
          const r=(seconds*1.23+(x%17)*.13)%1;
          nearFog.lineStyle(1,0xbdcfce,.37*(1-r)).strokeEllipse(x,y,5+r*w,w*.1+r*w*.13);
        }
      }
    }
    const [left,right]=[Math.floor(area.y/T),Math.ceil((area.y+area.height)/T)];
    for(let j=Math.max(0,left);j<Math.min(H,right);j++) {
      if(C.crossesRiver(j))continue;
      const [a,b]=riverAt(j);
      for(let k=0;k<quality.water;k++) {
        const x=a*T+11+((k*57+j*17+seconds*(reduced?1:10))%((b-a)*T-26));
        const y=j*T+7+(k*7+j*2)%19;
        g.fillStyle(night?0xa6bed9:dawn?0xe3c99a:rainy?0x9fbfc8:0xb5d1ad,.18+(k%2)*.12).fillRect(Math.floor(x),y,6+k*5,1);
      }
    }
    for(const [index,house] of HOUSES.entries()) {
      if(!visible(house.x*T,house.y*T,250))continue;
      const x=house.x*T+(index===0?151:48);
      const y=house.y*T+10;
      for(let i=0;i<(index===2||reduced?0:quality.fog);i++) {
        const r=(seconds*.37+i*.27)%1;
        g.fillStyle(0xcdcebb,(1-r)*.18).fillRect(Math.floor(x+r*22),Math.floor(y-r*32),4+r*14,3+r*8);
      }
      const storehouse=world.buildings['ruined-warehouse'].status;
      if((night||dusk) && (index<2||['completed','operational','upgraded'].includes(storehouse))) {
        const fy=(house.y+house.h)*T;
        const offsets=index===0?[47,145]:index===1?[29]:[36,179];
        for(const wx of offsets) {
          const py=fy-(index===0&&wx===47?51:44),px=house.x*T+wx;
          light.fillStyle(0xdd9e5c,.14).fillEllipse(px+8,py+7,36,23);
          const width=index===0?16:index===1?19:21;
          light.fillStyle(0xffc583,.77).fillRect(px+2,py,width-2,5).fillRect(px+2,py+8,width-2,5);
          light.fillStyle(0xf0b46c,.13).fillEllipse(px+7,fy+9,37,12);
        }
      }
      if(rainy) {
        const drip=(seconds*52+index*11)%30;
        const edge=house.x*T+house.w*T-16;
        g.fillStyle(0xaac6c2,.4).fillRect(edge,house.y*T+79+drip,1,6);
      }
    }
    const hearth=center(POINTS.fire);
    if(world.campfire.lit) {
      const flicker=.5+.5*Math.sin(seconds*12);
      light.fillStyle(0xf9a348,.07).fillCircle(hearth.x,hearth.y,66+flicker*5);
      light.fillStyle(0xfbc773,.12).fillCircle(hearth.x,hearth.y,35+flicker*4);
      light.fillStyle(0xcf6338,1).fillRect(hearth.x-5,hearth.y-8,10,11);
      light.fillStyle(0xf2a147,1).fillRect(hearth.x-3,hearth.y-12-flicker*4,6,12);
      light.fillStyle(0xf9df9f,1).fillRect(hearth.x-1,hearth.y-6,3,7);
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++) {
        const phase=(seconds*.7+i*.21)%1;
        light.fillStyle(0xf3ae65,1-phase).fillRect(hearth.x+Math.floor(phase*(i-2)*6),hearth.y-10-phase*23,2,2);
      }
    }
    if(weather==='mist') {
      for(let row=Math.max(2,left-1);row<Math.min(H,right+1);row+=3) {
        if(C.crossesRiver(row))continue;
        const [a,b]=riverAt(row);
        const x=(a+b)*T*.5+Math.sin(row*.4+seconds*.14)*19,y=row*T+17;
        if(visible(x,y))nearFog.fillStyle(0xc2d3c0,.065).fillEllipse(x,y,(b-a)*T+48,25+quality.fog*4);
      }
      const rests=[[334,430,310,36],[491,583,255,28],[704,968,176,19],[1518,251,365,40],[1644,121,340,45]];
      for(const [x,y,w,h] of rests.slice(0,quality.fog+1)) {
        if(!visible(x,y,w*.5))continue;
        const sway=Math.sin(seconds*.19+x*.01)*17;
        nearFog.fillStyle(0xbeceba,.089).fillEllipse(x+sway,y,w,h);
        if(Math.hypot(x-p.x,y-p.y)>170)tint.fillStyle(0xb8c6b4,.048).fillEllipse(x+sway*1.4,y-h*.39,w*1.1,h);
      }
    } else if(rainy) {
      for(let i=0;i<(reduced?Math.min(18,quality.rain):quality.rain);i++) {
        const x=lx+((i*137+seconds*51)%lw),y=ly+((i*197+seconds*308)%lh);
        const cold=C.biomeWeights(x/T,y/T).frost;
        if(C.hash(i,32,590)<cold)continue;
        g.lineStyle(1,0xb6cacb,.31).lineBetween(x,y,x-4,y+12);
      }
    } else if(!reduced&&!rainy) {
      for(let i=0;i<quality.ambient;i++) {
        const phase=seconds*(night?.51:.91)+i*1.91;
        const x=lx+((i*89+seconds*(night?7:17))%lw);
        const y=ly+((i*147+Math.sin(phase)*16)%lh);
        g.fillStyle(night?0xd6ec9f:0xd9b778,.23+.18*Math.sin(phase)).fillRect(x,y,2,2);
      }
    }
    seasonalLandscape(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
    natureUpdate(scene,view,world,seconds,quality,reduced);
  }

  // Local seasonal detail follows world tiles, elevation and walkable terrain.
  function seasonalLandscape(scene,view,w,t,quality,reduced){const S=root.LQValleySimulation,season=S.seasonAt(w.clock),weather=w.weather.kind,r=scene.cameras.main.worldView,g=view.nearFog,fx=view.effects;const winter=season==='winter',autumn=season==='autumn',spring=season==='spring';
    const colors=spring?[0xeab2bf,0xf4dd9e,0xb6d58e]:autumn?[0xa96d42,0xd5a45c,0xe2be76]:winter?[0xaebfc1,0xdde6db,0xedf4ed]:[0x8eae60,0xbfd477,0xe6c57e];
    let painted=0;const cap=reduced?90:230;
    for(let y=Math.max(0,Math.floor(r.y/48)*48);y<Math.min(H*T,r.bottom+48)&&painted<cap;y+=48)for(let x=Math.max(0,Math.floor(r.x/48)*48);x<Math.min(W*T,r.right+48)&&painted<cap;x+=48){const px=x+Math.floor(C.hash(x,y,671)*39),py=y+Math.floor(C.hash(x,y,672)*39),tx=px/T,ty=py/T;if(!natureLand(scene,px,py)||(trailVisualRoad(tx,ty)?.distance??Infinity)<1.5||C.eastCourt(tx,ty))continue;const h=C.hash(x,y,673);if(h>.65)continue;const cold=C.biomeWeights(tx,ty).frost; // land mask is shared with wildlife
      if(winter){if(cold<.4&&h>.26)continue;g.fillStyle(0xc2d3cf,.55).fillRect(px-5,py,12,3).fillRect(px-2,py-2,9,3);g.fillStyle(0xe3ebe1,.75).fillRect(px-3,py-1,7,2).fillRect(px+4,py+1,4,1);}
      else if(autumn){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let xx=px+(i*7)%13-6,yy=py+(i*5)%9;g.fillStyle(colors[i%3],.8).fillRect(xx,yy,3,2).fillRect(xx+1,yy-1,2,1);g.fillStyle(0x70553c,.65).fillRect(xx+2,yy+1,1,2);}}
      else{for(let i=0;i<(spring?4:3);i++){let xx=px+i*4-6,yy=py+(i%2)*3,ht=5+i%3,tip=xx+(reduced?0:Math.round(Math.sin(t*1.7+xx)*1.5));g.lineStyle(1,0x536f48,.86).lineBetween(xx,yy,tip,yy-ht);g.fillStyle(colors[i%3],.85).fillRect(tip-1,yy-ht-1,3,2);if(spring)g.fillStyle(0xf5e3b6,.9).fillRect(tip,yy-ht,1,1);}}
      painted++;
    }
    if(reduced)return;const n=Math.min(90,quality.rain||24);for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=r.x+((i*173+t*(weather==='wind'?95:24))%Math.max(1,r.width)),y=r.y+((i*113+t*(weather==='snow'?28:48))%Math.max(1,r.height));if(weather==='snow'){fx.fillStyle(0xe4eee8,.65).fillRect(x,y,i%3===0?3:2,1).fillRect(x+1,y-1,1,3);}else if(weather==='wind'){fx.fillStyle(autumn?0xd6a360:0xa2ba77,.58).fillRect(x,y,3,1);}else if(weather==='storm'){fx.lineStyle(1,0xb5cbd2,.46).lineBetween(x,y,x-12,y+17);}else if(weather==='heat'&&i<16){fx.lineStyle(1,0xffedc0,.12).lineBetween(x,y,x+8,y+Math.sin(t*2+i)*2);}}
  }
  // Living Nature: visual simulation only. No inventory, clock or save writers.

  const natureViews=new WeakMap();
  const clampNature=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const natureHash=(x,y,s)=>C.hash(Math.floor(x),Math.floor(y),s);
  function natureWind(t,weather){const strength=weather==='storm'?2.4:weather==='wind'?2.0:weather==='rain'?1.5:weather==='snow'?.6:weather==='mist'?.42:weather==='cloudy'?.85:.7;return {x:strength*(.60+.32*Math.sin(t*.54)+.20*Math.sin(t*1.21)),y:.12*Math.cos(t*.33)};}
  function natureWater(x,y){if(C.eastPool(x/T,y/T))return true;if(x<0||y<0||x>=W*T||y>=H*T)return false;const [a,b]=C.shore(y/T);return !C.crossesRiver(Math.floor(y/T))&&x>a*T+9&&x<b*T-9;}
  function natureLand(scene,x,y){const tx=Math.floor(x/T),ty=Math.floor(y/T);if(tx<1||ty<1||tx>=W-1||ty>=H-1)return false;return C.groundSample(x/T,y/T).surface!=='water'&&scene.area.blocked?.[ty]?.[tx]===false;}
  function naturePassable(scene,mode,x,y){return mode==='air'?x>16&&y>16&&x<W*T-16&&y<H*T-16:mode==='water'?natureWater(x,y):natureLand(scene,x,y);}
  function natureSegment(scene,mode,x,y,nx,ny){const steps=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(nx-x,ny-y)/7));for(let i=1;i<=steps;i++)if(!naturePassable(scene,mode,x+(nx-x)*i/steps,y+(ny-y)*i/steps))return false;return true;}
  // Hand-painted pixel clusters, feet anchored to y=32. Four animation frames; no full-body scaling.
  function animalPaint(ctx,id,frame,moving){
    const f=moving?frame:0, step=[0,2,0,-2][f],night=id==='firefly';
    const px=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),poly=(c,a)=>shape(ctx,c,a);
    if(id==='deer'){
      const d='#42382f',m='#9b6647',l='#ce9b66',h='#ead4a3';
      shadow(ctx,23,32,17,3);poly(d,[[6,17],[12,12],[28,12],[35,7],[40,8],[43,13],[40,17],[34,18],[30,24],[11,25],[6,22]]);
      poly(m,[[8,17],[14,14],[27,14],[33,11],[36,17],[29,22],[12,23],[8,21]]);px(l,13,14,15,3);px(h,29,16,4,6);
      for(const [x,p] of [[12,step],[17,-step],[27,-step],[31,step]]){px(d,x,22,3,7+p);px(m,x,23,2,5+p);px(d,x+(p>0?1:0),29+p,4,2);}
      poly(m,[[32,13],[33,7],[37,3],[41,7],[40,12],[37,14]]);px(l,35,6,5,3);px(h,39,12,4,3);px(d,40,9,2,1);px(d,42,13,2,2);
      if(!moving&&frame===2){px(d,32,4,2,6);px(l,33,2,2,4);}else{poly(d,[[33,7],[29,1],[31,0],[36,5],[36,7]]);poly(d,[[37,6],[40,0],[42,1],[40,7]]);px(l,31,2,2,3);}
      px(h,7,16,4,4);px(d,5,15,3,2);
    }else if(id==='hare'||id==='squirrel'||id==='fox'){
      const fox=id==='fox',sq=id==='squirrel';const d=fox?'#3a4446':sq?'#533b30':'#4c433e',m=fox?'#9aa9ad':sq?'#a45a36':'#a58d74',l=fox?'#d7d8c9':sq?'#d89254':'#d3bfa0';
      const jump=moving&&!fox?(f===1?3:f===2?2:0):0;ctx.save();ctx.translate(0,-jump);shadow(ctx,24,32+jump,fox?15:10,3);
      if(fox){poly(d,[[18,20],[12,18],[4,19],[0,23],[4,26],[15,25],[21,23]]);poly(m,[[2,23],[7,20],[16,20],[18,22],[11,25],[4,25]]);px(l,2,22,5,3);}
      if(sq){poly(d,[[20,24],[9,24],[4,18],[3,10],[7,4],[15,3],[19,8],[17,14],[12,17],[15,20],[21,20]]);poly(m,[[7,9],[11,5],[15,6],[16,11],[11,16],[8,19],[13,22],[17,22],[9,23],[6,18]]);px(l,8,7,3,5);}
      const bx=fox?13:18,by=fox?17:19;poly(d,[[bx,by],[bx+5,by-4],[30,by-2],[36,by+2],[35,by+9],[bx+3,by+10],[bx-2,by+5]]);poly(m,[[bx+1,by],[bx+6,by-2],[30,by],[33,by+3],[30,by+8],[bx+4,by+8],[bx,by+5]]);px(l,bx+5,by-1,8,2);
      for(const [x,p] of [[bx+3,step],[29,-step]]){px(d,x,by+7,3,5+p);px(m,x+1,by+7,2,4+p);px(d,x+1,by+11+p,4,2);}
      poly(d,[[29,by],[29,by-7],[33,by-10],[38,by-7],[41,by-2],[39,by+3],[33,by+4]]);poly(m,[[30,by-3],[32,by-7],[37,by-6],[39,by-2],[37,by+2],[32,by+1]]);px(l,35,by,5,2);px(d,36,by-4,2,1);px(d,40,by-1,2,2);
      if(!fox&&!sq){px(d,29,by-15,3,10);px(m,30,by-14,2,8);px(d,34,by-18,3,12);px(l,35,by-16,1,8);px(l,bx-4,by+2,5,5);}else{poly(d,[[30,by-6],[29,by-13],[34,by-8]]);poly(d,[[35,by-7],[39,by-12],[39,by-5]]);px(l,31,by-9,2,3);}
      ctx.restore();
    }else if(id==='duck'){
      const d='#3a4341',m='#b4ad8d',l='#e2d5b6';shadow(ctx,24,30,13,3);poly(d,[[9,23],[14,18],[28,18],[29,10],[35,9],[39,12],[39,18],[34,22],[32,27],[20,30],[11,27]]);poly(m,[[11,23],[15,20],[27,20],[32,23],[29,27],[20,28],[13,26]]);poly(l,[[15,21],[24,20],[28,22],[22,25],[15,25]]);px('#4d775d',30,11,7,8);px('#d7e1c8',32,18,4,2);px('#cda34f',38,14,6,3);px(d,35,12,2,2);px('#638d92',7-f,31,10+f*2,1);px('#80b2ad',27,31,13+f,1);
    }else if(id==='fish'){
      const d='#285761',m='#4b8f91',l='#a8c8b8';poly(d,[[9,25],[19,21],[31,22],[39,26],[32,29],[19,30],[9,27],[4,30],[5,23]]);poly(m,[[12,25],[20,23],[31,24],[35,26],[29,28],[20,28]]);px(l,20,24,9,1);px(d,33,25,1,1);px('#5b9b9b',4,24+step,4,4);px('#75a8a6',15,31,13,1);

    }else if(id==='bird'){
      const dy=[-7,0,6,0][frame],d='#293e45',m='#607987',l='#d0d8cf';poly(d,[[18,15],[10,9+dy],[3,7+dy],[8,16+dy],[18,19],[25,19],[35,16+dy],[43,7+dy],[35,9+dy],[27,15],[32,20],[35,23],[27,22],[24,24],[21,21]]);poly(m,[[19,16],[12,11+dy],[8,10+dy],[13,16+dy],[21,18]]);poly(m,[[25,17],[35,11+dy],[39,10+dy],[32,16+dy],[26,19]]);px(l,22,17,5,4);px(d,27,16,3,2);px('#caac6c',30,17,2,1);

  function extraFauna(ctx,id,f,moving){const P=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),Q=(c,a)=>shape(ctx,c,a),L=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,x,y,a,b,w),step=moving?[0,2,0,-2][f]:0,d='#403d35';
    if(id==='owl'){let y=[-4,1,4,1][f];Q(d,[[22,12],[11,8+y],[4,14+y],[10,23+y],[20,24],[28,24],[38,22+y],[44,14+y],[37,8+y],[26,12]]);Q('#9a8061',[[7,14+y],[13,12+y],[21,18],[18,22],[10,20+y]]);Q('#a28a6b',[[27,18],[36,12+y],[41,14+y],[37,20+y],[29,22]]);Q('#c9b38d',[[18,14],[22,10],[28,10],[32,14],[30,25],[24,29],[18,24]]);P('#ebd9b6',19,12,5,6);P('#ebd9b6',26,12,5,6);P('#3a3026',21,14,2,2);P('#3a3026',27,14,2,2);P('#d6a85c',24,18,3,3);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)P('#8c755e',20+i*3,22+i%2*3,2,2);
    }else if(id==='kingfisher'){let w=[-6,0,6,0][f];Q('#26454f',[[9,21],[15,13+w],[24,17],[28,9],[35,8],[40,12],[46,14],[39,16],[34,20],[25,25],[12,25]]);Q('#448d9b',[[12,20],[16,15+w],[25,18],[32,11],[37,12],[33,17],[27,21]]);Q('#c77b49',[[26,19],[33,17],[34,20],[29,24],[22,24]]);P('#def0dc',34,10,3,1);P('#292b2c',36,12,2,2);P('#34444a',40,13,6,2);
    }else if(id==='heron'){shadow(ctx,25,32,10,2);for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const x=21+i*5;L('#8c7755',x,22,x+step*(i?1:-1),31);P('#675e4d',x+step*(i?1:-1),31,4,1);}Q('#3d4d51',[[8,18],[13,12],[25,12],[31,15],[30,8],[33,1],[38,1],[41,4],[38,8],[35,9],[35,19],[27,24],[15,24]]);Q('#a0adb0',[[10,17],[15,14],[27,14],[31,18],[27,22],[15,22]]);Q('#e2e5db',[[32,8],[34,3],[38,3],[39,5],[35,8],[34,16],[31,14]]);L('#d5aa69',39,5,47,7,2);P('#262f32',37,4,1,1);
    }else if(id==='bee'||id==='dragonfly'||id==='moth'){let wing=[3,6,9,5][f];if(id==='bee'){Q('#b9d0c9',[[23,19],[13,18-wing],[19,13-wing],[25,18],[32,13-wing],[36,18-wing],[27,20]]);Q('#493b2e',[[12,20],[16,16],[29,16],[34,20],[31,25],[17,26],[12,23]]);P('#d9ae50',17,17,5,8);P('#d9ae50',25,17,5,7);P('#f3d981',18,17,3,2);P(d,31,19,4,4);L(d,33,18,36,14);}else if(id==='dragonfly'){for(const a of [-1,1]){Q('#9cbdb5',[[23,18],[23+a*15,16-wing],[23+a*19,20-wing],[25,21]]);Q('#b6d3cf',[[23,21],[23+a*15,22+wing],[23+a*17,24+wing],[25,23]]);}L('#326d69',24,12,24,31,3);P('#75b3a4',23,17,2,12);P('#2e4443',21,10,7,5);P('#a9d3c3',22,10,2,2);}else{Q('#5d5048',[[23,20],[23-wing,9],[13-wing,12],[15-wing,22],[23,26],[25+wing,9],[35+wing,12],[33+wing,22],[25,26]]);Q('#b8a08b',[[22,20],[23-wing,12],[15-wing,14],[17-wing,21],[23,24]]);Q('#c7b49e',[[26,20],[25+wing,12],[33+wing,14],[31+wing,21],[25,24]]);P('#e3ce9f',17-wing,15,3,3);P('#e3ce9f',29+wing,15,3,3);P(d,23,17,3,10);}}
    else if(id==='ladybug'||id==='beetle'){shadow(ctx,24,32,8,2);for(let i=0;i<3;i++){L(d,18+i*6,26,15+i*6,30+step);L(d,19+i*6,27,20+i*6,31-step);}Q(d,[[14,22],[18,17],[29,17],[34,22],[32,29],[18,30],[14,26]]);Q(id==='ladybug'?'#af4c40':'#644b36',[[16,22],[20,18],[29,19],[32,23],[29,28],[18,28]]);L(d,24,19,24,28);if(id==='ladybug'){for(const[x,y]of [[19,22],[21,26],[28,22],[29,26]])P('#3d3330',x,y,2,2);P('#e18562',18,19,4,2);}else{L('#a48055',18,21,18,27);L('#a48055',29,21,29,27);L(d,31,20,38,16,2);L(d,31,23,38,26,2);P('#a48055',36,16,3,2);}P(d,31,22,4,4);
    }else if(id==='snail'){shadow(ctx,24,32,12,2);Q('#58694e',[[8,29],[15,26],[28,27],[32,19],[36,19],[40,24],[36,30],[13,31]]);Q('#6e503e',[[15,28],[11,23],[12,15],[19,9],[27,11],[32,18],[28,26],[22,29]]);Q('#bf965d',[[15,23],[15,16],[20,12],[26,14],[29,19],[25,24],[20,25],[17,22]]);L('#78553d',20,15,25,18,2);L('#78553d',25,18,22,22,2);L('#78553d',22,22,19,20,2);L('#52644b',34,21,32,15);L('#52644b',37,21,40,15);P('#303d33',31,14,2,2);P('#303d33',40,14,2,2);

    }else if(id==='crab'){shadow(ctx,24,32,14,2);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){L('#7c513c',15+i*5,25,10+i*8,29+(i%2?step:-step),2);}Q('#6b4938',[[12,22],[17,17],[30,17],[36,22],[33,28],[16,28]]);Q('#ae7652',[[14,22],[19,19],[29,19],[33,22],[30,26],[18,26]]);P('#d29a67',19,20,10,2);for(const a of [-1,1]){L('#9a694b',24+a*9,22,24+a*15,15,2);Q('#b57a53',[[24+a*12,12],[24+a*20,9],[24+a*17,15],[24+a*20,18],[24+a*13,18]]);}P(d,20,18,2,2);P(d,27,18,2,2);
    }else if(id==='goat'||id==='boar'||id==='hedgehog'){const goat=id==='goat',boar=id==='boar',base=goat?'#b6aaa0':boar?'#7b5e4a':'#a08a6d';shadow(ctx,24,32,15,3);const top=goat?11:boar?15:20;Q(d,[[7,top+5],[11,top],[23,top-1],[33,top+4],[37,top+3],[42,top+8],[40,top+13],[29,top+14],[12,top+14],[7,top+10]]);Q(base,[[9,top+5],[13,top+1],[23,top+1],[30,top+5],[28,top+11],[12,top+12],[9,top+9]]);if(goat){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let x=12+i*6;P(d,x,23,3,7+(i%2?step:-step));P('#ded5bc',x,23,2,5+(i%2?step:-step));}Q(base,[[29,16],[30,8],[35,5],[39,7],[42,12],[38,17]]);L('#5b5549',33,8,30,1,2);L('#5b5549',36,7,40,1,2);P('#e6dfcc',38,11,4,3);P(d,38,8,2,2);}else if(boar){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let x=11+i*6;P(d,x,26,3,4+(i%2?step:-step));}for(let i=0;i<7;i++)P('#463a32',10+i*3,top+i%2-2,2,4);P('#9f8063',35,top+8,6,4);P(d,37,top+5,2,2);}else{for(let i=0;i<10;i++){let x=9+i*2;Q('#5b5042',[[x,top+5],[x+1,top-3+(i%3)],[x+4,top+6]]);P('#d2b894',x+1,top+2,1,2);}Q('#ccb18b',[[28,25],[34,23],[40,27],[37,30],[30,30]]);P(d,35,25,2,2);P(d,40,27,2,2);}}
    else if(id==='otter'){Q('#493c31',[[4,26],[9,22],[19,23],[26,17],[34,16],[40,20],[41,25],[35,29],[18,30],[9,29],[1,31]]);Q('#99734e',[[8,26],[15,25],[22,24],[29,19],[35,18],[38,22],[35,27],[21,28],[12,28]]);P('#d6b58b',33,22,6,4);P(d,36,19,2,2);P(d,39,23,2,2);L('#98c8be',3,32,17+step,32);L('#b2d5c5',27,31,42,31);}
  }
  function animalTexture(scene,id,frame,moving){return texture(scene,'nature-fauna-'+id+'-'+(moving?'move':'rest')+'-'+frame,48,36,ctx=>animalPaint(ctx,id,frame,moving));}
  const natureHomes=[['deer',83,39],['hare',81,38],['squirrel',85,40],['butterfly',82,40],['bird',83,36],['firefly',85,39],['firefly',78,35],['firefly',87,43],['deer',76,34],['squirrel',91,37],['duck',43,61],['fish',43,62],['frog',46,63],['butterfly',40,67],['hare',49,64],['bird',45,65],['hare',22,70],['butterfly',28,73],['bird',26,69],['butterfly',30,69],['fox',127,70],['hare',125,70],['bird',129,68],['fox',145,62],['hare',150,65],['bird',146,60],['fox',135,22],['hare',133,22],['bird',136,20],['frog',20,108],['duck',42,108],['fish',42,109],['bird',20,105],['firefly',22,108],['deer',119,118],['squirrel',121,118],['fox',117,117],['butterfly',120,117],['firefly',120,119],['deer',140,123],['squirrel',148,121],['fox',147,126],['butterfly',144,126],['bird',145,121],['firefly',147,126],['firefly',141,122],['hare',25,33],['bird',27,32],['butterfly',24,31],['squirrel',16,29],['firefly',27,34],['hare',56,31],['squirrel',65,32],['bird',72,33],['deer',66,55],['squirrel',70,68],['butterfly',55,56],['fox',108,91],['hare',91,106],['deer',96,110],['bird',112,117],['frog',27,117],['firefly',25,115],['hare',135,48],['bird',150,12],['deer',67,125],['butterfly',65,125]];
  natureHomes.push(['deer',190,20],['squirrel',184,20],['bird',198,28],['butterfly',237,21],['hare',244,27],['bird',251,29],['hare',192,73],['bird',182,62],['fox',202,76],['frog',234,65],['bird',245,75],['firefly',236,66],['butterfly',191,110],['hare',187,104],['bird',200,120],['deer',237,108],['squirrel',249,118],['firefly',243,119]);
  natureHomes.push(["owl", 237, 109],["kingfisher", 231, 63],["heron", 232, 67],["bee", 191, 110],["dragonfly", 240, 64],["moth", 244, 121],["ladybug", 194, 115],["beetle", 185, 29],["snail", 235, 83],["turtle", 245, 83],["crab", 46, 63],["goat", 135, 22],["hedgehog", 190, 31],["boar", 120, 118],["otter", 43, 62]);
  function natureHome(scene,id,tx,ty,index){const mode=NATURE_SPECIES[id].mode;for(let k=0;k<80;k++){const a=k*2.399963+rndPhase(index),r=k===0?0:Math.sqrt(k)*15;let x=(tx+.5)*T+Math.cos(a)*r,y=(ty+.5)*T+Math.sin(a)*r;if(mode==='water'){const [l,h]=C.shore(y/T);x=(l+(h-l)*(.25+.5*natureHash(index,k,991)))*T;}if(naturePassable(scene,mode,x,y))return {x,y};}return null;}
  const rndPhase=i=>natureHash(i,5,317)*Math.PI*2;
  function natureActive(id,minute,weather){const night=minute>=1170||minute<375;if(['storm','snow'].includes(weather)&&['butterfly','firefly','bird','bee','dragonfly','moth','kingfisher'].includes(id))return false;if(['owl','moth','hedgehog'].includes(id))return night||id==='hedgehog'&&minute>1050;if(['bee','dragonfly','kingfisher','ladybug','heron','turtle','goat'].includes(id))return !night&&weather!=='storm';if(id==='snail')return weather==='rain'||weather==='mist'||night;if(id==='firefly')return night&&weather!=='rain';if(id==='butterfly')return !night&&weather!=='rain';if(id==='bird')return !night&&weather!=='rain';if(id==='fox')return minute>900||minute<500;if(id==='deer'||id==='squirrel')return !night;return true;}
  function natureState(view){let s=natureViews.get(view.scene);if(!s){s={view,animals:new Map(),observed:new Set(),last:null,lastPaint:0,homes:null,updates:0,peak:0,canopyCreates:0,wind:{x:0,y:0}};natureViews.set(view.scene,s);}return s;}
  function natureRemove(s,id){const a=s.animals.get(id);if(!a)return;const i=s.view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(a.sprite);if(i>=0)s.view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(a.sprite.scene)a.sprite.destroy();s.animals.delete(id);}
  function natureClear(view,exit=false){const s=natureViews.get(view.scene);if(!s)return;for(const id of [...s.animals.keys()])natureRemove(s,id);s.last=null;if(exit)natureViews.delete(view.scene);}
  function natureCanopy(scene,info,phase,s){const sprite=info.sprite,key=info.mature;if(!scene.textures.exists(key))return;const name='valley-v841-nature-canopy-'+key+'-'+phase;if(phase===0){if(sprite.texture.key!==key)sprite.setTexture(key);return;}if(!scene.textures.exists(name)){if(s.canopyCreates>=2)return;const src=scene.textures.get(key).getSourceImage(),w=src.width,h=src.height;s.canopyCreates++;texture(scene,'nature-canopy-'+key+'-'+phase,w,h,ctx=>{const limit=h*.70;for(let y=0;y<h;y+=2){const offset=y<limit?Math.round(phase*3*Math.pow(1-y/limit,1.7)):0;ctx.drawImage(src,0,y,w,Math.min(2,h-y),offset,y,w,Math.min(2,h-y));}});}if(scene.textures.exists(name)&&sprite.texture.key!==name)sprite.setTexture(name);}
  function natureUpdate(scene,view,world,t,quality,reduced){
    if(scene.inside||!scene.player){natureClear(view);return;}
    const started=performance.now(),s=natureState(view),rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,p=scene.player,minute=world.clock.minuteOfDay,weather=world.weather.kind,night=minute>=1170||minute<375;
    const delta=s.last===null?0:clampNature(t-s.last,0,.18);s.last=t;s.wind=natureWind(t,weather);s.canopyCreates=0;
    const visible=(x,y,pad=80)=>x>=rect.x-pad&&x<=rect.right+pad&&y>=rect.y-pad&&y<=rect.bottom+pad;
    const limit=reduced?5:quality.ambient>=18?15:quality.ambient>=12?11:quality.ambient>=6?8:5;
    if(!s.homes)s.homes=natureHomes.map(([kind,x,y],index)=>({kind,index,home:natureHome(scene,kind,x,y,index)})).filter(a=>a.home);
    const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,signature=D.reserves.map(r=>D.reserveState(world,r.id).stage===3?'1':'0').join('');if(signature!==s.reserveSignature){s.reserveSignature=signature;s.reserveHomes=D.reserves.flatMap((r,i)=>D.reserveState(world,r.id).stage===3?r.fauna.map((kind,j)=>{const index=10000+i*10+j;return {kind,index,home:natureHome(scene,kind,r.x-.5,r.y-.5,index)};}):[]).filter(a=>a.home);}
    const list=[...s.homes,...s.reserveHomes].filter(a=>naturePassable(scene,NATURE_SPECIES[a.kind].mode,a.home.x,a.home.y)&&natureActive(a.kind,minute,weather)&&visible(a.home.x,a.home.y,135)).sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.home.x-p.x,a.home.y-p.y)-Math.hypot(b.home.x-p.x,b.home.y-p.y)).slice(0,limit),wanted=new Set(list.map(a=>a.index));
    for(const id of [...s.animals.keys()])if(!wanted.has(id))natureRemove(s,id);
    let movingCount=0,textureChanges=0;for(const h of list){const id=h.index,kind=h.kind,def=NATURE_SPECIES[kind];let a=s.animals.get(id);if(!a){const sprite=scene.add.image(h.home.x,h.home.y,animalTexture(scene,kind,0,false)).setOrigin(.5,32/36);scene.areaObjs.push(sprite);a={sprite,kind,id,home:h.home,x:h.home.x,y:h.home.y,target:null,step:0,wait:rndPhase(id)*.22,phase:rndPhase(id),facing:1};s.animals.set(id,a);}
      if(!naturePassable(scene,def.mode,a.x,a.y)){a.x=h.home.x;a.y=h.home.y;a.target=null;}let moving=false;const distance=Math.hypot(a.x-p.x,a.y-p.y);const flee=def.mode==='land'&&distance<(['snail','ladybug','beetle','turtle'].includes(kind)?12:48)&&kind!=='frog';
      if(!reduced){a.wait=Math.max(0,a.wait-delta);if(flee&&a.target&&Math.hypot(a.target.x-p.x,a.target.y-p.y)<distance)a.target=null;
      if(!a.target&&(a.wait===0||flee)){for(let k=0;k<12;k++){const angle=flee&&k===0?Math.atan2(a.y-p.y,a.x-p.x):rndPhase(id)+((++a.step)*2.399963),r=18+natureHash(id,a.step,302)*50;const x=a.x+Math.cos(angle)*r,y=a.y+Math.sin(angle)*r;if(Math.hypot(x-a.home.x,y-a.home.y)>100||!natureSegment(scene,def.mode,a.x,a.y,x,y))continue;a.target={x,y};break;}if(!a.target)a.wait=.7;}
      if(a.target){const dx=a.target.x-a.x,dy=a.target.y-a.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy),n=Math.min(d,delta*def.speed*(flee?1.8:1));if(d<1){a.target=null;a.wait=def.mode==='air'?.12:1.1+natureHash(id,a.step,305)*2.3;}else if(natureSegment(scene,def.mode,a.x,a.y,a.x+dx*n/d,a.y+dy*n/d)){a.x+=dx*n/d;a.y+=dy*n/d;moving=n>.001;if(Math.abs(dx)>1)a.facing=dx<0?-1:1;}else{a.target=null;a.wait=.8;}}}
      const frame=reduced?0:Math.floor(t*(def.mode==='air'?9:moving?7:2)+a.phase)%4;
      if(moving)movingCount++;const key=animalTexture(scene,kind,frame,moving);if(a.sprite.texture.key!==key){a.sprite.setTexture(key);textureChanges++;}a.sprite.setFlipX(a.facing<0);
      let height=['bird','owl','kingfisher'].includes(kind)?22:['butterfly','bee','dragonfly','moth'].includes(kind)?7:kind==='firefly'?9:0;if(height&&!reduced)height+=Math.sin(t*2+a.phase)*3;
      a.sprite.setPosition(Math.round(a.x),Math.round(a.y-height)).setDepth(view.depthAt(a.y)+(kind==='bird'?1:.002)).setAlpha(kind==='firefly'?(reduced?.85:.4+.55*Math.pow(.5+.5*Math.sin(t*2+a.phase),2)):kind==='fish'?.75:1);
      if(kind==='firefly'){const glow=reduced?.65:.37+.4*Math.pow(.5+.5*Math.sin(t*2+a.phase),2);view.light.fillStyle(0xd1ef90,glow*.11).fillCircle(Math.round(a.x),Math.round(a.y-height-10),6);view.light.fillStyle(0xe9ffc9,glow).fillRect(Math.round(a.x)-1,Math.round(a.y-height-10),2,2);}
      if(kind==='duck'||kind==='fish'){view.nearFog.fillStyle(0xc1e0ca,.22).fillRect(Math.round(a.x-12*a.facing),Math.round(a.y+1),8,1);if(!reduced)view.nearFog.fillStyle(0x8fbdb1,.35).fillRect(Math.round(a.x-16*a.facing),Math.round(a.y+4),5,1);}
    }
    // Only animate nearby, fully grown canopies. Stable trunk/footprint, no texture work per frame after warmup.
    const canopyLimit=reduced?0:quality.ambient>=12?18:quality.ambient>=6?9:0;
    let count=0;for(const info of view.trees.values()){if(info.stage!=='mature')continue;const a=info.sprite;if(!visible(a.x,a.y,75)||count>=canopyLimit){if(a.texture.key!==info.mature)a.setTexture(info.mature);continue;}count++;const phase=Math.round(Math.sin(t*.8+a.x*.015+a.y*.011)*Math.min(1,s.wind.x));natureCanopy(scene,info,phase,s);}
    // Current follows the same shoreline as collision. Reflections, foam and rain rings use world coordinates.
    const g=view.nearFog,lo=Math.max(0,Math.floor(rect.y/24)*24),hi=Math.min(H*T,rect.bottom+24);let waterCount=0;
    for(let y=lo;y<hi;y+=24){const sy=y+(reduced?0:Math.floor(t*12)%24);if(sy>=H*T||C.crossesRiver(Math.floor(sy/T)))continue;const [a,b]=C.shore(sy/T);if(!visible((a+b)*T/2,sy,155))continue;for(let k=0;k<Math.max(1,quality.water);k++){const x=a*T+13+((natureHash(0,y,k+841)*(b-a)*T+(!reduced?t*4:0))%Math.max(1,(b-a)*T-26));if(!natureWater(x,sy)||!natureWater(x+8,sy))continue;g.fillStyle(night?0x8fb6ca:0xb2d6bd,.12+.07*k).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(sy),8,1);if(weather==='rain'&&!reduced){const r=(t*.95+natureHash(k,y,953))%1;g.lineStyle(1,0xcadfda,(1-r)*.38).strokeEllipse(x,sy,3+r*12,1+r*5);}waterCount++;}
      // Broken shoreline foam follows each bank rather than appearing in the bridge or land.
      for(const edge of [a*T+11,b*T-12]){if(!visible(edge,sy,14)||!natureWater(edge,sy))continue;const drift=reduced?0:Math.sin(t*.8+sy*.045);g.fillStyle(0xb3d5bb,.20+Math.abs(drift)*.12).fillRect(Math.round(edge),Math.round(sy),2,7);g.fillStyle(0x83b4ab,.19).fillRect(Math.round(edge+2),Math.round(sy+7),2,4);}
    }
    // Local plant motion: anchored reed and grass tips, not screen-space particles.
    const plantMax=reduced?0:quality.ambient===0?8:quality.ambient>=12?42:22;let plants=0;
    for(let yy=Math.max(0,Math.floor(rect.y/48)*48);yy<Math.min(H*T,rect.bottom+48)&&plants<plantMax;yy+=48)for(let xx=Math.max(0,Math.floor(rect.x/48)*48);xx<Math.min(W*T,rect.right+48)&&plants<plantMax;xx+=48){const x=xx+Math.floor(natureHash(xx,yy,110)*38),y=yy+Math.floor(natureHash(xx,yy,111)*38),tx=x/T,ty=y/T,r=natureHash(xx,yy,112);if(r>.46||!natureLand(scene,x,y)||(trailVisualRoad(tx,ty)?.distance??Infinity)<2||(root.LQValleyDevelopment.pathSurfaceAt(world,x,y)?.level||0)>=7)continue;const region=C.wildernessRegion(tx,ty),[l,h]=C.shore(ty),bank=Math.min(Math.abs(tx-l),Math.abs(tx-h)),reed=bank<1.7&&bank>.25,flower=region==='basin'&&r<.21;
      if(!reed&&region==='frost')continue;const palette=reed?[0x455d40,0x859b62,0xc3a56b]:[0x355842,0x78995c,0xc6d18c],wave=s.wind.x*Math.sin(t*1.9+x*.031+y*.02);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const bx=x+(i-2)*3,height=(reed?12:5)+Math.floor(natureHash(xx,i+yy,114)*7),tip=bx+Math.round(wave*(reed?4:2))+(i-2);g.lineStyle(1,palette[0],.81).lineBetween(bx,y,tip,y-height);g.lineStyle(1,palette[1],.9).lineBetween(bx,y-2,tip,y-height);if(reed&&i%2===0)g.fillStyle(palette[2],.85).fillRect(tip-1,y-height-2,2,4);if(flower&&i%2===0)g.fillStyle(i===2?0xe9bf7f:0xdcc6bb,.9).fillRect(tip-1,y-height-1,3,2);}plants++;}
    // Light wells respond to canopy density: only clear/dawn/day and visible foliage.
    let wells=0; if(!night&&weather==='clear'&&quality.ambient>=6){for(const info of view.trees.values()){if(info.stage!=='mature'||wells>=9)continue;const a=info.sprite;if(!visible(a.x,a.y,30)||natureHash(a.x,a.y,129)<.48)continue;const cast=clampNature((minute-760)/375,-1,1),x=a.x+cast*23+18,y=a.y+12;const bright=.06+.035*Math.max(0,Math.sin((minute-350)/825*Math.PI));
      for(let j=0;j<3;j++)view.nearFog.fillStyle(0xe6d5a2,bright*.6).fillEllipse(x+2*j,y-2*j,75-j*18,32-j*8);
      const topX=x-32-cast*21,topY=y-86;for(let j=0;j<3;j++)view.nearFog.fillStyle(0xe4d1a0,.025).fillTriangle(topX+j*3,topY+7*j,x-22+j*4,y,x+25-j*4,y);if(!reduced){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const f=(t*.15+i*.33+info.entry.visualSeed*.001)%1;view.effects.fillStyle(0xe5d8a5,.35*Math.sin(f*Math.PI)).fillRect(Math.round(x+Math.sin(f*5+i)*18),Math.round(y-f*38),2,1);}}wells++;}}
    let drifting=0;
    if(!reduced&&quality.ambient>=6){for(const info of view.trees.values()){if(info.stage!=='mature'||drifting>=Math.min(quality.ambient,14))continue;const a=info.sprite;if(!visible(a.x,a.y,65)||natureHash(a.x,a.y,252)<.57)continue;const region=C.wildernessRegion(a.x/T,a.y/T);if(region==='frost')continue;const r=(t*.17+natureHash(a.x,a.y,253))%1,x=a.x-17+r*(23+s.wind.x*35)+Math.sin(r*10+a.x*.01)*8,y=a.y-60+r*65,col=info.entry.species===2?0xd19a63:0x9caf78;
        if(r<.94){view.effects.fillStyle(col,.8*Math.sin(r*Math.PI)).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),3,2);view.effects.fillStyle(0xe5d7a1,.45*Math.sin(r*Math.PI)).fillRect(Math.round(x)+1,Math.round(y)-1,2,1);}drifting++;}}
    // Night/precipitation cues by actual region and local wind. Snow is visual only; weather remains unchanged.
    if(weather==='rain'){for(let i=0;i<(reduced?Math.min(18,quality.rain):quality.rain);i++){const x=rect.x+((i*173+(reduced?0:t*20*s.wind.x))%Math.max(1,rect.width)),y=rect.y+((i*113+(reduced?0:t*36))%Math.max(1,rect.height));const cold=C.biomeWeights(x/T,y/T).frost;if(C.hash(i,32,590)>=cold)continue;view.effects.fillStyle(0xe0ebdf,.62).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),i%3===0?2:1,2);}}
    const trailMotion=trailAtmosphere(scene,view,world,t,quality,reduced);
    const structures=natureStructureEffects(scene,view,world,t,s.wind,reduced,quality);const heritage=heritageMotion(scene,view,world,t,reduced,quality);s.updates++;s.totalTextureChanges=(s.totalTextureChanges||0)+textureChanges;s.peak=Math.max(s.peak,performance.now()-started);s.metrics={heritage,trailMotion,animals:s.animals.size,canopies:count,plants,water:waterCount,wells,drifting,structures,moving:movingCount,textureChanges:s.totalTextureChanges,invalidAnimals:[...s.animals.values()].filter(a=>!naturePassable(scene,NATURE_SPECIES[a.kind].mode,a.x,a.y)).length,frames:s.updates,peakMs:s.peak,cap:limit,wind:s.wind.x};
  }
  // Scene-owned trail effects; no extra timers, textures, particles or save writes.
  function trailAtmosphere(scene,view,world,t,quality,reduced){
    const rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,p=scene.player,g=view.nearFog,night=world.clock.minuteOfDay<350||world.clock.minuteOfDay>1140;
    const rain=world.weather.kind==='rain',cap=reduced?0:Math.min(24,quality.ambient+quality.water*2);let wet=0,leaves=0,deck=0;
    if(!cap)return {wet,leaves,deck};
    for(let y=Math.max(0,Math.floor(rect.y/72)*72);y<Math.min(H*T,rect.bottom+72)&&wet+leaves<cap;y+=72)for(let x=Math.max(0,Math.floor(rect.x/72)*72);x<Math.min(W*T,rect.right+72)&&wet+leaves<cap;x+=72){
      const px=x+Math.floor(C.hash(x,y,512)*61),py=y+Math.floor(C.hash(x,y,513)*61),tx=px/T,ty=py/T,road=trailVisualRoad(tx,ty);
      if(!road||road.distance>road.width*.83||!natureLand(scene,px,py)||C.landscapeBridge(tx,ty))continue;
      const w=C.biomeWeights(tx,ty),phase=C.hash(x,y,514),cold=w.frost;
      if(rain&&phase>cold){const iw=7+Math.floor(phase*9),ih=3+Math.floor(phase*3),f=(t*1.16+phase)%1;g.fillStyle(0x3a4949,.23).fillEllipse(px,py+1,iw+5,ih+2);g.fillStyle(0x93b0b3,.28).fillEllipse(px,py,iw,ih);g.fillStyle(0xd0dbd2,.2).fillRect(px-iw*.32,py-1,iw*.52,1);g.lineStyle(1,0xc2d5d0,.44*(1-f)).strokeEllipse(px,py,3+f*iw,1+f*ih);wet++;}
      else if(!rain&&phase<.48&&w.heartwood+w.deepwoods>.45){const f=(t*.18+phase)%1,dx=Math.round((f-.5)*13),dy=Math.round(Math.sin(f*10)*6);g.fillStyle(night?0x9ca386:0xd5be7e,.56*Math.sin(Math.PI*f)).fillRect(px+dx,py+dy,3,2);g.fillStyle(0x7d7655,.3).fillRect(px+dx+1,py+dy+2,2,1);leaves++;}
    }
    // Learned surfaces use stable world-space weather samples; no texture churn.
    const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,layer=view.trailLayer,seen=new Set();let learnedWet=0,learnedLeaves=0,steps=0,work=0,limit=Math.min(14,Math.max(3,quality.ambient));
    for(const key of layer?.chunks.keys()||[]){const list=layer.index.get(key)||[];if(learnedWet+learnedLeaves>=limit)break;const stride=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(list.length/32));for(let i=0;i<list.length&&learnedWet+learnedLeaves<limit;i+=stride){const[e,n,road]=list[i];if(n<7||seen.has(e.id))continue;seen.add(e.id);const x=(e.x+e.bx)/2,y=(e.y+e.by)/2;if(x<rect.x||x>rect.right||y<rect.y||y>rect.bottom)continue;const phase=C.hash(e.x,e.y,6740);if(phase>.34)continue;const cold=C.biomeWeights(x/T,y/T).frost;
        if(rain&&phase>cold*.5){const f=(t*.85+phase*5)%1,width=road===2?8:12,h=road===2?3:5;g.fillStyle(0x4b6465,.43).fillEllipse(x,y,width,h);g.fillStyle(0x9bbab4,.28).fillRect(x-width*.34,y-1,width*.48,1);g.lineStyle(1,0xd4e3d7,.45*(1-f)).strokeEllipse(x,y,2+f*width,1+f*h);learnedWet++;}
        else if(!rain&&n>10&&phase<.14&&cold<.5){const f=(t*.23+phase*7)%1,dx=Math.round(Math.sin(f*6.28)*5),dy=Math.round(Math.sin(f*12)*2);g.fillStyle(night?0x9ca386:0xbba86e,.5*Math.sin(f*Math.PI)).fillRect(x+dx,y+dy,3,1);learnedLeaves++;}
      }}
    const surface=D.pathSurfaceAt(world,p.x,p.y);if(scene.pMoving&&surface&&surface.level>=4&&!scene._lqSurface){const f=(t*5)%1,wet=rain&&C.biomeWeights(p.x/T,p.y/T).frost<.6;for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const u=(f+i*.18)%1,ang=i*1.57+Math.floor(t*5)*.9,x=Math.round(p.x+Math.cos(ang)*(2+u*8)),y=Math.round(p.y+Math.sin(ang)*(1+u*4)-u*(wet?7:3));g.fillStyle(wet?0x9bbdb8:surface.road===2?0xb2b8a3:0xc6b28c,(1-u)*(wet?.58:.35)).fillRect(x,y,2,wet?2:1);steps++;}}
    const pulse=scene._lqPathBuildPulse;if(pulse){const e=D.trailDecode(pulse.id),f=(scene.time.now-pulse.at)/1500;if(e&&f>=0&&f<1){const x=(e.x+e.bx)/2,y=(e.y+e.by)/2;for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const ang=i*Math.PI/6,spread=3+f*16;g.fillStyle(i%3===0?0xe3d0a1:0xa5a38c,(1-f)*.7).fillRect(Math.round(x+Math.cos(ang)*spread),Math.round(y+Math.sin(ang)*spread*.5-Math.sin(f*Math.PI)*6),2,2);work++;}}else delete scene._lqPathBuildPulse;}

    for(const b of C.landscapeBridges){const yy=(b.from+b.to)*T/2;if(yy<rect.y-80||yy>rect.bottom+80||b.right*T<rect.x-40||b.left*T>rect.right+40)continue;
      // Broken band of moving reflected light beside the bridge, restricted to actual water.
      for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const x=(b.left+.9+(b.right-b.left-1.8)*(i/6))*T,y=b.to*T+5+(i%2)*4;if(!natureWater(x,y))continue;const f=(t*.8+i*.19)%1;g.fillStyle(0xa7c6b8,.1+.18*Math.sin(Math.PI*f)).fillRect(Math.round(x+f*5),Math.round(y),7,1);deck++;}
    }
    return {wet,leaves,deck,learnedWet,learnedLeaves,steps,work};
  }

  function naturePositions(scene){const s=natureViews.get(scene);return s?[...s.animals.values()].map(a=>({id:a.id,kind:a.kind,x:a.x,y:a.y,texture:a.sprite.texture.key})):[];}
  function natureGuide(scene){const s=natureViews.get(scene);if(!s)return {species:NATURE_SPECIES,nearby:[],observed:[]};const nearby=[...s.animals.values()].filter(a=>Math.hypot(a.x-scene.player.x,a.y-scene.player.y)<=150).map(a=>({id:a.id,kind:a.kind,distance:Math.round(Math.hypot(a.x-scene.player.x,a.y-scene.player.y)/T)}));return {species:NATURE_SPECIES,nearby,observed:[...s.observed]};}
  function observeNature(scene,id){const s=natureViews.get(scene),a=s?.animals.get(Number(id));if(!a||scene.inside||Math.hypot(a.x-scene.player.x,a.y-scene.player.y)>150)throw new Error('Move closer to observe this animal.');s.observed.add(a.kind);return NATURE_SPECIES[a.kind];}

  // Environmental art pass within existing monument bounds; no new solid footprint.
  function natureStructureEffects(scene,view,world,t,wind,reduced,quality){
    const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment;if(!D||!world.development||!view.developmentObjects)return 0;
    const rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,lit=world.clock.minuteOfDay>=1130||world.clock.minuteOfDay<390,weather=world.weather.kind;
    let n=0;const g=view.effects,ground=view.nearFog,lamp=view.light;
    const flicker=reduced?1:.8+.2*Math.sin(t*6.3);
    const glow=(x,y,color,r,alpha)=>{for(let i=0;i<4;i++)lamp.fillStyle(color,alpha*(i===3?1.5:.6)).fillEllipse(x,y,r*(1-i*.17),r*(.75-i*.12));};
    const flame=(x,y)=>{glow(x,y,0xeab875,24,.065*flicker);lamp.fillStyle(0xe4b567,.92).fillRect(x-1,y-2,3,4);lamp.fillStyle(0xfce2a0,1).fillRect(x,y-2,1,2);};
    for(const [id,info] of view.developmentObjects){const s=info.sprite;if(s.x<rect.x-110||s.x>rect.right+110||s.y<rect.y-110||s.y>rect.bottom+130)continue;const target=D.byId[id];if(!target)continue;const x=s.x,y=s.y;
      if(target.kind==='workshop'){
        const task=D.task(world,world.development.workshop.queue[0]?.id);if(task?.stage==='working'){
          // Active wood shavings and a warm workbench light (no fake products or rewards).
          const bx=x-12,by=y-46;if(lit)glow(bx,by,0xf2b978,35,.07);if(!reduced){for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const k=(t*.65+j*.21)%1;g.fillStyle(j%2?0xd8b681:0xb69362,.65*(1-k)).fillRect(Math.round(bx+(j-2)*2+k*9),Math.round(by-k*6+k*k*14),2,1);}}n++;
        }
        if(lit)flame(x+28,y-45);
      }else if(target.kind==='farm'){
        const plot=world.development.farm[id];const stage=D.cropStage(plot);if(stage>0&&plot.crop&&!reduced){const tint=stage>=5?0xceb87c:0xa6b979;for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const bx=x-21+j*14,by=y-13,dx=Math.round(Math.sin(t*1.5+j+target.x)*wind.x*2);ground.lineStyle(1,tint,.65).lineBetween(bx,by,bx+dx,by-4-(stage>=4?3:0));}n++;}
      }else if(target.kind==='build'&&world.development.build.pending&&!reduced){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const r=(t*.27+j*.25)%1;g.fillStyle(0xcbbb99,.13*(1-r)).fillRect(Math.round(x-15+j*9+r*9),Math.round(y-16-r*11),2+r*3,2+r*3);}n++;}

    }
    return n;
  }

  function windowView(ctx, x, y, w, h, kind, weather, minute) {
    const night=minute>=1140||minute<375;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(x,y,w,h);ctx.clip();
    box(ctx,night?'#364861':weather==='rain'?'#5b747a':'#91aca0',x,y,w,h);
    const bands=kind==='arven-home'?['#667c71','#506555','#344e47']:
      kind==='lyra-home'?['#87936d','#647e54','#4c694a']:['#7b8582','#586c69','#465956'];
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++) {
      const y0=y+h*(.41+i*.16);
      shape(ctx,bands[i],[[x,y+h],[x,y0],[x+w*.12,y0-3],[x+w*.31,y0+4],
        [x+w*.61,y0-8+i*2],[x+w*.81,y0-5],[x+w,y0+2],[x+w,y+h]]);
    }
    if(kind==='arven-home') {
      line(ctx,'#93aa8a',x+w*.18,y+h*.72,x+w*.71,y+h*.96,3);
    } else if(kind==='lyra-home') {
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++)line(ctx,'#a5a466',x+i*w*.33,y+h,x+w*.63,y+h*.63,2);
    } else {
      box(ctx,'#a4a984',x+4,y+h-8,w-9,2);
      box(ctx,'#665e55',x+w*.55,y+5,5,h-7);
    }
    if(weather==='rain') for(let i=0;i<6;i++)line(ctx,'#b0c4bb',x+(i*11)%w,y+(i*8)%h,x+(i*11)%w-3,y+(i*8)%h+7,1);
    if(weather==='mist')box(ctx,'#c2c8ae4d',x,y+h*.2,w,h*.5);
    if(night)box(ctx,'#222c5157',x,y,w,h);
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#332e2c';ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);
    box(ctx,'#b19670',x-3,y+h,w+6,3);
    box(ctx,'#5a5144',x+w*.5-1,y,2,h);
    box(ctx,'#5a5144',x,y+h*.55,w,2);
  }

  // Material palette and pattern library, authored at original pixel density.
  // No additional furniture footprints: all dressing remains on existing surfaces or flush walls.
  const hallSurface={soil:['#706454','#8b785b','#aa9371','#4f5144','#cfba8b'],dock:['#654e3b','#876749','#a98156','#493c31','#d6b884'],stone:['#666e65','#858c7d','#a5ae99','#47564e','#c9caad'],reed:['#746b4d','#938463','#b6a179','#495847','#dad2a0'],slate:['#52666b','#6c8586','#91aaa7','#354f59','#c9d2bf'],mosaic:['#747d6a','#919d85','#b3baa0','#50645c','#d4cfaa']};
  function hallFinish(ctx,l,id){const W=l.w*T,H=l.h*T,P=hallSurface[l.floor],rng=random(919+l.w*131+id.length*281),r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.ceil(w),Math.ceil(h)),ln=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(a),Math.round(b),w),mid=Math.floor(l.w/2)*T;
    // Flat ground plane: staggered real seams, edge wear and grouped grain. Avoid uniform noise.
    r(P[1],10,66,W-20,H-76);ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(10,66,W-20,H-76);ctx.clip();
    const wood=l.floor==='dock'||l.floor==='reed',row=wood?12:l.floor==='slate'?18:23;
    for(let y=66,i=0;y<H-10;y+=row,i++){
      let x=10-((i*31+11)%58);while(x<W-10){const width=wood?44+Math.floor(rng()*45):28+Math.floor(rng()*17),tone=rng(),color=tone>.78?P[2]:tone<.18?P[0]:P[1];r(color,x+1,y+1,width-1,row-1);r(P[3]+'aa',x,y,width,1);r(P[4]+'66',x+1,y+1,width-2,1);r(P[3]+'99',x,y+1,1,row-1);r(P[0]+'88',x+width-2,y+3,2,row-3);
        if(wood){for(let k=0;k<4;k++){const a=x+3+rng()*(width-10),b=y+3+Math.floor(rng()*(row-5)),n=6+Math.floor(rng()*14);r(P[0]+'9a',a,b,n,1);r(P[4]+'44',a+2,b+1,n-4,1);}for(const k of [3,width-4]){r('#3b382d',x+k,y+3,1,1);r('#c1af80',x+k,y+4,1,1);}}

  function millHallLight(g,l,k,w,time,task,reduced){const night=w.clock.minuteOfDay>=1140||w.clock.minuteOfDay<375,D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,s=D.millState(w),p=D.millerPose(w),oven=task?.roomWork?.millRecipe==='bake'||p?.work&&p.recipe==='bake',f=reduced?0:time/1000;const x=ROOM_X+12.5*T*k,y=5.1*T*k;
    if(oven&&s.stage>=9){for(let i=5;i>=1;i--)g.fillStyle(0xffbd6a,.025+i*.006).fillEllipse(x,y+i*k, (45+i*13)*k,(15+i*7)*k);}
    if(!night&&w.weather.kind!=='rain'&&!reduced){for(let i=0;i<18;i++){const u=(f*.12+i/18)%1,xx=ROOM_X+(29+(i%2?l.w*T-165:0)+((i*19)%94)+Math.sin(f+i)*3)*k,yy=(67+u*91)*k;g.fillStyle(0xffe9b5,.18*(1-u)).fillRect(Math.round(xx),Math.round(yy),k,k);}}
  }

  function drawMillMachine(ctx,W,H,kind,stage){const r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h)),p=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts),ln=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(a),Math.round(b),w),rng=random(W*17+H*53+kind.length*79);
    const timber=(x,y,w,h)=>{r('#392d25',x,y,w,h);r('#866344',x+2,y+1,w-4,h-3);r('#b48b58',x+2,y+1,w-4,2);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let a=x+3+rng()*(w-7),b=y+4+rng()*(h-7);r('#66472f',a,b,Math.min(9,w-4),1);}r('#47372c',x+3,y+h-4,w-6,2);};
    const stone=(x,y,w,h)=>{r('#4a4840',x,y,w,h);for(let b=0;b<h-2;b+=9){for(let a=-((b/9)%2)*7;a<w-2;a+=15){const u=Math.max(1,a+1),v=Math.min(w-1,a+14);if(v<=u)continue;r(['#8a8471','#a49a80','#767667','#b2a68b'][Math.floor(rng()*4)],x+u,y+b+1,v-u,7);r('#c5b99b',x+u,y+b+1,v-u,1);r('#595a4f',x+u,y+b+7,v-u,1);}}};
    r('#252e2866',4,H-9,W-8,8);
    if(kind==='stone'){
      timber(8,25,10,H-35);timber(W-19,25,10,H-35);timber(8,20,W-16,8);stone(20,H-27,W-40,19);
      // Polygonal elliptical mill stone with hewn grooves and front edge; wooden feed hopper.
      const cx=75,cy=61;let top=[];for(let i=0;i<24;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/12;top.push([Math.round(cx+Math.cos(a)*39),Math.round(cy+Math.sin(a)*18)]);}p('#41453d',top.map(([x,y])=>[x,y+8]));p('#66695e',top.map(([x,y])=>[x,y+5]));p('#a7a48a',top);for(let i=0;i<16;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/8;ln('#65685b',cx+Math.cos(a)*8,cy+Math.sin(a)*4,cx+Math.cos(a+.18)*34,cy+Math.sin(a+.18)*15);ln('#d2c8a4',cx+Math.cos(a)*19,cy+Math.sin(a)*8,cx+Math.cos(a+.18)*34,cy+Math.sin(a+.18)*15);}
      timber(71,23,8,39);p('#4e3829',[[35,8],[113,8],[104,27],[87,42],[62,42],[44,27]]);p('#bc945d',[[39,10],[110,10],[99,26],[85,38],[64,38],[47,25]]);for(let i=0;i<6;i++)ln('#785439',43+i*11,12,62+i*5,36);r('#e0bd7c',38,8,74,3);p('#796548',[[43,12],[105,12],[97,21],[51,21]]);for(let i=0;i<60;i++){const x=49+rng()*49,y=13+rng()*6;r(i%3?'#d6b978':'#9c7c49',x,y,2,1);}r('#55402d',66,38,19,5);timber(120,38,10,45);r('#4b4639',125,24,17,9);r('#c3ab77',125,24,17,2);
    }else if(kind==='oven'){
      stone(9,23,W-18,H-31);p('#4d4135',[[9,28],[18,12],[37,5],[W-37,5],[W-18,12],[W-9,28]]);p('#ac8062',[[13,25],[23,13],[40,8],[W-40,8],[W-24,13],[W-13,25]]);for(let y=12;y<27;y+=5)ln('#674b3a',18,y,W-18,y);stone(22,32,W-44,49);
      const cx=W/2,cy=57;for(let i=0;i<11;i++){let a=Math.PI+i*Math.PI/10;const x=cx+Math.cos(a)*28,y=cy+Math.sin(a)*21; r(i%2?'#bb9478':'#8d6b56',x-5,y-4,10,8);}p('#302a27',[[cx-22,72],[cx-22,50],[cx-15,39],[cx-6,35],[cx+8,35],[cx+17,41],[cx+22,51],[cx+22,72]]);r(stage>=9?'#644133':'#262c29',cx-18,57,36,14);r('#81715b',cx-27,73,54,6);r('#c4b08e',cx-26,73,51,1);r('#5c4836',W-35,2,19,27);stone(W-33,2,15,23);r('#4c4034',W-36,0,21,4);
      if(stage<9){timber(cx-26,50,53,9);ln('#c0aa81',cx-22,33,cx+22,70,5);}
      else {for(let j=0;j<3;j++){let x=15+j*12;r('#654b37',x,H-14,10,4);r('#ad7b44',x+1,H-17,8,4);}}
    }else{timber(7,18,8,H-26);timber(W-15,18,8,H-26);timber(7,13,W-14,13);const cx=W/2,cy=34; r('#4c5048',cx-10,24,20,27);r('#af9463',cx-8,26,16,22);for(let i=0;i<12;i++){let a=i*Math.PI/6;ln('#c5b38b',cx+Math.cos(a)*10,cy+Math.sin(a)*10,cx+Math.cos(a)*24,cy+Math.sin(a)*24,3);}r('#473829',cx-7,cy-7,14,14);r('#cab073',cx-4,cy-4,8,8);}
  }
  function millRoomAnimation(g,l,k,w,time,task,reduced){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,s=D.millState(w),f=reduced?0:time/180,stone=l.objects.find(o=>o.id==='millstone'),oven=l.objects.find(o=>o.id==='oven'),control=l.objects.find(o=>o.id==='mill-control');const cx=ROOM_X+(stone.x*T+75)*k,cy=(stone.y*T+61)*k;
    if(s.gate){for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/5+f*.25;g.lineStyle(k,0xe1cfaa,.75).lineBetween(cx+Math.cos(a)*12*k,cy+Math.sin(a)*5*k,cx+Math.cos(a+.15)*32*k,cy+Math.sin(a+.15)*14*k);}if(task?.roomWork?.millRecipe==='grind'||D.millerPose(w)?.work&&D.millerPose(w).recipe==='grind'){for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const a=(f*.27+i/12)%1;g.fillStyle(0xede0b5,(1-a)*.75).fillRect(cx+(i%6-3)*5*k+a*8*k,cy-a*24*k,2*k,k);}}}
    if(s.stage>=9&&(task?.roomWork?.millRecipe==='bake'||D.millerPose(w)?.work&&D.millerPose(w).recipe==='bake')){const x=ROOM_X+(oven.x*T+oven.w*T/2)*k,y=(oven.y*T+68)*k;for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const a=(f*.21+i/7)%1;g.fillStyle(i%2?0xe7a45e:0xf4cf80,.85).fillRect(x+(i-3)*4*k,y-a*19*k,3*k,(4+Math.sin(a*Math.PI)*7)*k);}}
    const x=ROOM_X+(control.x*T+control.w*T/2)*k,y=(control.y*T+10)*k;g.fillStyle(s.gate?0xa9cb87:0xc27756,1).fillRect(x-6*k,y,12*k,3*k);
  }

  function furniture(ctx,o,branch){const W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T,r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),ln=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,x,y,a,b,w),p=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts),rng=random(o.id.length*193+W*17);const wood=['#403127','#77583e','#af8758','#d9b780'];
    const table=(x,y,w,h)=>{r('#25332965',x+4,y+8,w,h);r(wood[0],x,y+5,w,h);r(wood[1],x+2,y+6,w-4,h-4);r(wood[0],x+4,y+h-2,5,9);r(wood[0],x+w-9,y+h-2,5,9);r(wood[2],x,y,w,h-4);r(wood[3],x+1,y+1,w-2,2);for(let j=0;j<6;j++){let a=x+4+rng()*(w-12);r('#765b3e66',a,y+4+rng()*(h-10),4+rng()*8,1);}};
    const plant=(x,y,i)=>{r('#4a392b',x-5,y+6,12,10);r('#ab7950',x-4,y+7,10,7);r('#dfac73',x-4,y+7,10,1);ln('#406541',x,y+7,x+(i%3-1)*2,y-7,2);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){let a=x+(j%2?4:-4),b=y-j*4;p(['#4d7346','#789b5a','#b0bf75'][j],[[x,b+2],[a-2,b-3],[a+3,b-3],[a+2,b],[x,b+3]]);}};
    const crate=(x,y,w=26,h=24)=>{r('#332e27',x+2,y+5,w,h);r('#755437',x,y,w,h);r('#bb8d58',x+2,y+2,w-4,h-5);for(let j=4;j<w-1;j+=7)r('#745238',x+j,y+3,1,h-7);r('#d3a56b',x,y,w,3);r('#48392b',x,y+h-5,w,3);ln('#8b643f',x+3,y+4,x+w-3,y+h-6,3);r('#d5c4a1',x+5,y+5,2,2);};
    const book=(x,y,w,h,c)=>{r('#3f3428',x,y,w,h);r(c,x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);r('#dfd0a1',x+1,y+h-3,w-2,2);r('#eee0b0',x+2,y+3,w-4,1);};
    const slab=(x,y,w,h)=>{p('#47524e',[[x,y+5],[x+w-6,y],[x+w,y+5],[x+w,y+h],[x,y+h]]);p('#a6ada0',[[x,y+5],[x+w-6,y],[x+w,y+5],[x+w-3,y+9],[x,y+9]]);r('#777f74',x+2,y+10,w-4,h-12);ln('#c2c3aa',x+4,y+5,x+w-7,y+3);};
    const glass=(x,y,c)=>{r('#384e4f',x,y+3,8,14);r(c,x+1,y+6,6,10);r('#b8d7c6',x+1,y+5,1,8);r('#ae946a',x+1,y,6,4);};
    r('#24302a66',4,H-9,W-5,10);
    switch(o.kind){
      case 'home-bed':{r('#392e27',5,5,W-10,H-8);r('#9f7b55',7,7,W-14,H-14);r('#e7ddbc',10,11,W-20,17);r('#718779',10,30,W-20,H-37);for(let y=32;y<H-8;y+=6){r('#9eab8f',10,y,W-20,1);for(let x=14;x<W-10;x+=12)r('#b9af8b',x,y+2,3,2);}r('#ad8e63',5,H-10,W-10,5);break;}
      case 'home-pantry':{r('#392f27',3,2,W-6,H-5);r('#957653',5,4,W-10,H-10);for(let j=0;j<2;j++){let x=7+j*(W-14)/2;r('#6d5139',x,7,(W-18)/2,H-18);r('#ad8c61',x+2,9,(W-22)/2,H-22);r('#dbc58e',x+3,H/2,3,4);}r('#c0a275',3,3,W-6,3);break;}
      case 'home-table':{table(3,4,W-6,H-12);r('#e2cfaa',W/2-6,5,12,10);r('#8c7052',W/2-3,8,6,3);r('#9caa90',W/2+13,7,5,7);break;}
      case 'millstone': drawMillMachine(ctx,W,H,'stone',branch);break;
      case 'breadoven': drawMillMachine(ctx,W,H,'oven',branch);break;
      case 'millcontrol': drawMillMachine(ctx,W,H,'control',branch);break;
      case 'planter': table(3,11,W-6,H-24);for(let j=0;j<Math.floor(W/16)-1;j++)plant(12+j*16,17+(j%2)*17,j+(branch||0)); break;
      case 'seed':case 'books':case 'jars':case 'display':{r(wood[0],3,2,W-6,H-5);r(wood[1],5,4,W-10,H-9);for(let y=6;y<H-12;y+=19){r(wood[0],7,y,W-14,15);for(let j=0;j<Math.floor((W-16)/12);j++){let x=9+j*12;if(o.kind==='jars')glass(x,y,['#8ca578','#b5a16e','#6f9c99'][j%3]);else if(o.kind==='display')slab(x,y+3,10,12);else book(x,y+1,8+(j%2),13,['#9d7954','#718a7d','#a9a269','#975e53'][j%4]);}r(wood[3],5,y+15,W-10,2);}r(wood[3],3,2,W-6,3);break;}
      case 'cistern':{r('#45392d',8,8,W-16,H-18);r('#947c5c',10,10,W-20,H-22);for(let x=12;x<W-10;x+=6)r('#594734',x,16,1,H-29);r('#403e35',8,10,W-16,6);r('#3e626b',13,8,W-26,11);r('#91bdbd',15,10,W-30,2);r('#42372b',8,H-16,W-16,4);break;}
      case 'balance':{table(4,12,W-8,H-24);const x=W/2;r('#43372c',x-3,6,7,37);r('#c0a06d',x-2,6,3,34);ln('#dbb975',x-36,9,x+36,9,4);for(const sign of [-1,1]){const a=x+sign*29;ln('#615243',a,10,a-9,29);ln('#615243',a,10,a+9,29);p('#68533a',[[a-12,29],[a+12,29],[a+7,36],[a-7,36]]);r('#d5bb81',a-11,29,22,2);}break;}
      case 'cargo':for(let j=0;j<3;j++)crate(4+j*28,17-j%2*7,26,31);break;
      case 'sacks':for(let j=0;j<Math.floor(W/18);j++){let x=4+j*18,y=18+j%2*8;p('#67523a',[[x+4,y],[x+13,y],[x+16,y+10],[x+17,y+24],[x+13,y+31],[x+2,y+29],[x,y+18]]);p('#bda476',[[x+5,y+3],[x+12,y+3],[x+14,y+15],[x+15,y+26],[x+4,y+27],[x+2,y+17]]);r('#e0c9a0',x+4,y+6,10,2);ln('#89724e',x+5,y+25,x+12,y+27);}break;
      case 'masonry':table(4,10,W-8,H-23);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)slab(12+i*33,14+i%2*7,28,19);ln('#a7bbc0',W-35,26,W-15,18,3);r('#62472f',W-16,16,9,5);break;
      case 'blocks':case 'samples':{for(let j=0;j<3;j++)for(let i=0;i<2;i++)slab(5+j*28,14+i*18,25,18);if(o.kind==='samples')for(let i=0;i<5;i++)r(['#b9a780','#839eab','#c8c6a9'][i%3],8+i*15,11+i%2*3,7,5);break;}
      case 'dryer':{r(wood[0],4,3,5,H-6);r(wood[0],W-9,3,5,H-6);for(let y=10;y<H-10;y+=18){r(wood[2],3,y,W-6,4);for(let x=12;x<W-9;x+=12){ln('#9d9167',x,y+4,x,y+10);p('#739258',[[x,y+7],[x-4,y+13],[x-1,y+17],[x+5,y+14]]);r('#b1bd83',x,y+11,3,2);}}break;}
      case 'scope':{table(15,22,W-30,H-33);const x=W/2;ln('#4e4b40',x,13,x-18,H-9,4);ln('#4e4b40',x,13,x+18,H-9,4);ln('#c0a477',x,12,x,H-10,3);p('#4b574e',[[x-28,30],[x+20,2],[x+28,16],[x-19,45]]);p('#ba9a61',[[x-25,30],[x+20,5],[x+24,14],[x-18,40]]);ln('#e6cb8d',x-24,29,x+19,5,3);p('#8cb3b7',[[x+20,2],[x+28,16],[x+24,19],[x+16,5]]);break;}
      case 'stove':{r('#353e3f',W/2-6,0,12,14);r('#323a3a',8,10,W-16,H-13);r('#6b7772',10,11,W-20,2);r('#1f2828',13,17,W-26,10);r('#b27e43',15,22,W-30,3);break;}
      case 'relief':case 'relic':{table(4,10,W-8,H-23);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){let x=11+j*(W-21)/3;slab(x,14,Math.floor((W-24)/3)-4,22);ln('#e2d2a4',x+4,20,x+10,16);ln('#d8ccab',x+7,17,x+7,26);}break;}
      default:{table(3,6,W-6,H-15);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)book(10+j*15,10+j%2*4,12,13,['#a79368','#7e987c','#b9a07e'][j]);r('#d9c9a8',W-38,9,24,16);for(let j=0;j<4;j++)r('#6f857c',W-35,12+j*3,17-j%2*4,1);break;}
    }
  }
  // Directional schematic views are derived from actual terrain and harvested tree state.
  function windowPhase(minute){return minute<375||minute>=1140?"night":minute<540?"morning":minute>=960?"evening":"day";}
  function roomWindowScene(ctx,w,site,side){const minute=w.clock.minuteOfDay,night=minute<375||minute>=1140,weather=w.weather.kind;const W=114,H=46;box(ctx,'#30392f',0,0,W,H);ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(4,4,W-8,H-8);ctx.clip();box(ctx,night?'#253c55':weather==='rain'?'#81969d':'#b9d4c5',4,4,W-8,H-8);const dx=side==='west'?-1:1;for(let row=0;row<7;row++){const depth=7-row;for(let col=0;col<26;col++){const x=site.x+dx*(depth*.65)+((col-13)/4)*(.7+depth*.12),y=site.y-depth*.8;const sample=C.groundSample(x,y),road=trailVisualRoad(x,y),base=wildColor(x*T,y*T),color=sample.surface==='water'?'#527f8e':road&&road.distance<road.width*.65?'#ab9b79':rgb(base.map(v=>v+(7-row)*2));box(ctx,color,4+col*4,12+row*5,5,6);if((col*7+row*13)%5===0)box(ctx,'#d2d1a94d',5+col*4,14+row*5,2,1);}}
    const list=Object.values(C.TREES_BY_ID).filter(t=>t.y<site.y&&t.y>site.y-9&&Math.abs(t.x-site.x-dx*2)<6).sort((a,b)=>a.y-b.y);for(const t of list){const x=57+(t.x-site.x-dx*2)*8,depth=site.y-t.y,y=42-depth*3.1,stage=root.LQValleySimulation.treeStage(w,t);if(stage==='stump'){box(ctx,'#665340',x-1,y-2,3,3);continue;}box(ctx,'#604d39',x-1,y-9,2,10);const c=stage==='mature'?'#47674e':'#77956b';shape(ctx,c,[[x-6,y-5],[x-5,y-12],[x-2,y-16],[x+3,y-17],[x+6,y-12],[x+7,y-6],[x+2,y-3]]);for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const a=x-4+j*3,b=y-12+(j%2)*3;shape(ctx,j%2?'#789364':'#8ba676',[[a,b-3],[a+3,b-2],[a+4,b],[a+1,b+2],[a-2,b]]);}box(ctx,'#9bb080',x-3,y-13,4,2);}
    const phase=windowPhase(minute),sunlit=(phase==='morning'&&side==='east')||(phase==='evening'&&side==='west');if(phase==='morning'||phase==='evening')box(ctx,sunlit?'#e5ae6870':'#727d9340',4,4,W-8,H-8);if(weather==='rain'||weather==='storm'){box(ctx,'#536b8550',4,4,W-8,H-8);for(let j=0;j<8;j++){const x=8+(j*17)%99,y=8+(j*13)%29;line(ctx,'#c1d9df88',x,y,x-2,y+5,1);}}if(night)box(ctx,'#15243e80',4,4,W-8,H-8);if(weather==='mist')box(ctx,'#d8dfd755',4,10,W-8,19);ctx.restore();for(const x of [0,56,110])box(ctx,'#594f3b',x,0,4,H);box(ctx,'#b49a6f',0,H-4,W,4);box(ctx,'#a88f63',0,0,W,3);}
  // Bespoke flush floor treatments: work zones are painted into the existing collision-free floor,
  // not new invisible blockers. Furniture is redrawn inside its existing physical footprint.
  function guildHallMaterial(ctx,l,id){if(id===C.MILL.id)return;const W=l.w*T,H=l.h*T,mid=Math.floor(l.w/2)*T,R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),L=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(a),Math.round(b),w),rnd=random(981+l.w*497+id.length*31);ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(10,66,W-20,H-76);ctx.clip();
    const work=l.objects.find(o=>o.action==='work'),ox=work.x*T,oy=work.y*T,ww=work.w*T,hh=work.h*T;
    if(id==='forest-ledger'){// terracotta nursery work beds, dark wet rim and a mosaic of sprouting leaves
      for(const o of l.objects.filter(o=>['planter','cistern'].includes(o.kind))){const x=o.x*T-5,y=o.y*T-5,w=o.w*T+10,h=o.h*T+12;R('#605c48',x,y,w,h);for(let b=0;b<h-2;b+=12)for(let a=0;a<w-2;a+=18){R(['#a28d6b','#b09a70','#947953'][Math.floor(rnd()*3)],x+a+1,y+b+1,Math.min(17,w-a-2),Math.min(11,h-b-2));R('#c4b18a',x+a+2,y+b+1,Math.min(14,w-a-3),1);}R('#cfbf91',x,y,w,1);R('#4d5642',x,y+h-2,w,2);}
      for(let y=82;y<H-65;y+=25){const x=mid+(Math.floor(y/25)%2?11:-12);L('#4a7050',x,y,x+5,y-5,1);R('#759367',x+3,y-6,4,2);R('#a2af70',x,y-2,3,2);}
    }else if(id==='river-station'){// painted freight lanes, loading apron with worn edges and scale calibration grid
      const x=ox-9,y=oy+hh+3,w=ww+18,h=26;R('#5c4937',x,y,w,h);R('#927a55',x+2,y+2,w-4,h-4);for(let a=x+5;a<x+w-3;a+=13){R('#c3ad80',a,y+3,2,h-6);R('#715c41',a+1,y+4,1,h-7);}for(const xx of [x+2,x+w-10])for(let j=0;j<5;j++)L('#e5d2a1',xx,y+3+j*4,xx+6,y+6+j*4,1);for(let y=H-76;y>oy+hh+35;y-=24){L('#c9b183',mid-6,y,mid,y-4,2);L('#c9b183',mid,y-4,mid+6,y,2);}
    }else if(id==='ridge-marker'){// irregular stone dust apron with embedded quarry measure marks
      for(let i=0;i<190;i++){const a=ox-8+rnd()*(ww+16),b=oy+hh-5+rnd()*30,s=1+Math.floor(rnd()*3);R(['#bfc3af88','#7c857777','#54625b66'][i%3],a,b,s,1+Math.floor(rnd()*2));}for(let x=ox-2;x<ox+ww;x+=8){R('#575d54',x,oy+hh+23,1,(x%16===0?6:3));}R('#9ca796',ox,oy+hh+29,ww,1);
    }else if(id==='marsh-archive'){// reed mat around the dryer with woven stepped interlocking pattern
      const x=ox-5,y=oy+hh+4,w=ww+10,h=24;R('#354e45',x,y,w,h);R('#638568',x+2,y+2,w-4,h-4);for(let a=4;a<w-4;a+=6)for(let b=4;b<h-3;b+=4){R((a+b)%3===0?'#c5b683':'#a39c72',x+a,y+b,4,1);}for(let a=3;a<w-3;a+=4){R('#c6bb87',x+a,y+1,2,2);R('#c6bb87',x+a,y+h-3,2,3);}
    }else if(id==='frost-observatory'){// brass sightline ticks toward the telescope and inset star geometry
      const cx=ox+ww/2,cy=oy+hh+25;for(let i=0;i<190;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/95,x=cx+Math.cos(a)*38,y=cy+Math.sin(a)*18;R(i%10===0?'#e4ce94':'#b6b78f',x,y,2,1);}for(let j=0;j<9;j++){const a=j*Math.PI*2/9,x=cx+Math.cos(a)*30,y=cy+Math.sin(a)*15;R('#e0c998',x-1,y-1,3,3);R('#5b7380',x,y,1,1);}L('#b6b483',cx-38,cy,cx+38,cy,1);L('#b6b483',cx,cy-19,cx,cy+19,1);R('#e3d09f',cx-2,cy-2,4,4);
    }else { // archive tesserae, interleaved brass & lapis strips forming a restoration court
      const x=ox-6,y=oy+hh+3,w=ww+12,h=34;R('#565f56',x,y,w,h);for(let b=1;b<h-2;b+=4)for(let a=1;a<w-2;a+=4){const border=b<6||b>h-7||a<7||a>w-8;const v=(Math.floor(a/4)+Math.floor(b/4))%4;R(border?['#b7a778','#d0c5a0','#8ba5a0','#88958a'][v]:['#898f7c','#a2a38a','#777e70','#b0af95'][v],x+a,y+b,3,3);}for(let a=12;a<w-10;a+=16){L('#d8c397',x+a,y+10,x+a+6,y+17,1);L('#d8c397',x+a+6,y+17,x+a,y+24,1);L('#6d9293',x+a,y+24,x+a-6,y+17,1);L('#6d9293',x+a-6,y+17,x+a,y+10,1);}}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function guildFurnitureMaterial(ctx,o){const W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T,R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),L=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(a),Math.round(b),w);
    if(o.kind==='planter'){for(let j=0;j<Math.floor(W/16)-1;j++){let x=12+j*16,y=17+(j%2)*17;R('#5a442f',x-6,y+6,13,11);R('#977450',x-5,y+7,11,8);R('#ad9065',x-5,y+7,11,2);R('#655438',x-4,y+8,9,2);R('#4a6845',x-1,y-7,2,14);for(let k=0;k<4;k++){let a=x+(k%2?3:-5),b=y-4+k*3;R('#58764b',a,b,5,2);R('#78965d',a+1,b-1,4,2);R('#aec183',a+1,b-1,2,1);}R('#b9c47d',x,y-8,3,2);}}
    else if(o.kind==='balance'){let x=W/2;R('#4c4234',x-8,39,16,5);R('#a78b5b',x-6,37,12,3);R('#dbbe86',x-2,6,3,30);for(const d of [-1,1]){const a=x+d*29;R('#4a4739',a-12,35,24,2);R('#bc9f70',a-10,33,20,2);R('#e7d3a1',a-8,32,16,1);}for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let a=W-35+i*6,y=26+i%2*3;R('#3f443d',a,y,5,7);R('#989888',a+1,y+1,3,3);R('#d0ccb4',a+1,y+1,2,1);}}
    else if(o.kind==='masonry'){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=12+i*33,y=15+i%2*7;R('#7a8279',x+3,y+8,20,4);R('#bdc1ad',x+3,y+6,21,2);L('#59665f',x+6,y+2,x+11,y+7);L('#e2d7ba',x+10,y+6,x+17,y+4);R('#959d91',x+5,y+12,4,2);}R('#72563a',W-33,35,22,3);R('#aeb6ab',W-18,31,11,6);R('#d8d9c7',W-18,31,9,1);}
    else if(o.kind==='dryer'){for(let y=10;y<H-10;y+=18){for(let x=12;x<W-9;x+=12){L('#7b8854',x,y+5,x-4,y+16,2);R('#668552',x-4,y+12,5,3);R('#9eac71',x-3,y+10,4,2);R('#b6b781',x-2,y+10,2,1);R('#d6ba85',x-1,y+5,3,1);}R('#d4b686',8,y,W-16,1);}}
    else if(o.kind==='scope'){const x=W/2;for(let j=0;j<3;j++){let a=x-17+j*15,y=30-j*9;L('#705c3e',a,y-7,a+7,y+5,3);L('#e7c98a',a+1,y-7,a+7,y+3,1);}R('#52777a',x+20,9,5,8);R('#a8d4cd',x+21,10,2,3);R('#a88a57',x-4,40,8,4);R('#e3c894',x-3,40,6,1);}
    else if(o.kind==='relic'||o.kind==='relief'){for(let j=0;j<3;j++){const x=11+j*(W-21)/3,y=15;L('#647873',x+6,y+4,x+13,y+1);L('#d4c8a4',x+5,y+5,x+8,y+8);L('#d4c8a4',x+8,y+8,x+12,y+5);R('#6d837f',x+9,y+9,3,6);R('#e5d6ac',x+8,y+8,2,1);}R('#775d43',W-22,34,10,2);R('#ebdbb2',W-14,32,5,5);}
  }
  function guildRoomLighting(scene,w,h,k,time,reduced){if(!root.LQValleyDevelopment.guildDefs[h.id]&&!root.LQValleyDevelopment.eastById[h.id])return;const g=h.guildLight;if(!g)return;g.clear();const l=h.layout,W=l.w*T*k,H=l.h*T*k,night=w.clock.minuteOfDay<375||w.clock.minuteOfDay>=1140,wet=w.weather.kind==='rain',dawn=Math.max(0,1-Math.abs(w.clock.minuteOfDay-720)/420),dir=(w.clock.minuteOfDay-720)/420;
    if(!night){for(const win of h.windows){const x=win.x+4*k,y=win.y+43*k,drift=dir*35*k,depth=(wet?37:100)*k;for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const off=i*4*k;g.fillStyle(wet?0xbad4d0:0xf2dfae,(wet?.012:.022)*(1+i*.15)*(.5+dawn*.5));g.fillPoints([{x:x+off,y},{x:x+104*k-off,y},{x:x+108*k+drift-off,y:y+depth},{x:x+14*k+drift+off,y:y+depth}],true);}if(!wet&&!reduced)for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const u=(time/7000+i/7)%1,xx=x+(13+(i*17)%79+Math.sin(time/1700+i)*4)*k+drift*u,yy=y+u*depth;g.fillStyle(0xffe7c3,.12+Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*.19).fillRect(Math.round(xx),Math.round(yy),k,k);}}}
    if(root.LQValleyDevelopment.roomState(w,h.id).lamp){const x=ROOM_X+Math.floor(l.w/2)*T*k,y=78*k;for(let i=6;i>0;i--)g.fillStyle(0xefc273,.018+i*.004).fillEllipse(x,y+8*k,(36+i*22)*k,(24+i*13)*k);}
    // Individual soft contact shadow under each workstation, not a full screen overlay.
    for(const o of l.objects.filter(o=>o.h)){const x=ROOM_X+(o.x+.1)*T*k,y=(o.y+o.h-.03)*T*k;g.fillStyle(0x1f2d28,.12).fillRect(x,y,(o.w-.2)*T*k,4*k);g.fillStyle(0x25352c,.06).fillRect(x+2*k,y+4*k,(o.w-.3)*T*k,4*k);}
  }

  // Paint slope crests and broken exposed strata, using the same height field used by walking.
  function districtReliefColor(col,x,y,h){const X=x/T,Y=y/T,z=(a,b)=>C.eastRiseSample(a,b)?.elevation||0,dx=z(X+.16,Y)-z(X-.16,Y),dy=z(X,Y+.16)-z(X,Y-.16),shade=Math.min(.29,Math.max(-.31,(dx+dy)*.016)),bands=h.theme==='terrace'?Math.pow(Math.max(0,Math.sin(h.r*25-.7)),4):h.theme==='ridge'?Math.pow(Math.max(0,Math.sin(h.r*17)),3):0.0,rock=h.theme==='terrace'?[157,111,77]:[116,136,124],f=blend(col,rock,bands*h.edge*.42);return f.map(v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,v*(1+shade*h.edge))));}
  function districtReliefTerrain(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){if(ox+w<190*T)return;for(const rise of C.EAST_RISES){const R=Math.max(rise.rx,rise.ry)*T*1.16,cx=rise.x*T,cy=rise.y*T;if(cx+R<ox||cx-R>ox+w||cy+R<oy||cy-R>oy+h)continue;const palette=C.EAST_BY_REGION[rise.region].palette,rock=rise.theme==='terrace'||rise.theme==='ridge',bands=rise.theme==='terrace'?[.32,.62,.86]:rise.theme==='ridge'?[.50,.83]:[.58,.81];for(let band=0;band<bands.length;band++)for(let i=0;i<160;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/80,r=bands[band]*(1+.065*Math.sin(a*3+rise.theta)+.035*Math.cos(a*5)),u=Math.cos(a)*rise.rx*r,v=Math.sin(a)*rise.ry*r,c=Math.cos(rise.theta),s=Math.sin(rise.theta),x=Math.round(cx+(u*c-v*s)*T),y=Math.round(cy+(u*s+v*c)*T);if(x<ox-20||x>ox+w+20||y<oy-20||y>oy+h+20||C.eastCourt(x/T,y/T)||C.eastPool(x/T,y/T))continue;const n=C.hash(i,band,385+Math.floor(rise.x));if(n<.60||Math.sin(a+rise.theta)<-.05&&n<.88)continue;const ww=(rock?7:3)+Math.floor(n*(rock?17:9)),hh=rock?3+Math.floor(n*6):2;const base=rock?rise.theme==='terrace'?'#865c45':'#506d67':palette[0],lit=rock?rise.theme==='terrace'?'#cfaa78':'#a6c1ab':palette[2];shape(ctx,base,[[x-ww/2,y],[x-ww/4,y-2],[x+ww/2-2,y-2],[x+ww/2,y+hh-1],[x-ww/3,y+hh]]);line(ctx,lit,x-ww/3,y-1,x+ww/2-3,y-2,1);if(n>.8)box(ctx,palette[1],x-1,y+1,3,2);}}}
  function districtRiseAtmosphere(scene,view,w,t,quality,reduced){const p=scene.player,h=C.eastRiseSample(p.x/T,p.y/T);if(!h||reduced||h.elevation<70)return;const r=scene.cameras.main.worldView,g=view.nearFog,wind=quality.ambient>=6?14:5;for(let i=0;i<wind;i++){const x=r.x+((i*91+t*(8+h.elevation*.08))%r.width),y=r.y+((i*77+Math.sin(t*.7+i)*14)%r.height);if(C.eastRiseSample(x/T,y/T)?.id!==h.id)continue;g.lineStyle(1,0xf1dfb7,.12+ h.elevation*.00012).lineBetween(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(x+5+h.elevation*.028),Math.round(y-1));}}

  function civicMachine(ctx,o,stage){const kinds=['reclaimbench','paperpress','loomframe','maptable','stonebench','repairbench','pumpbench','herbalbench','cookbench','sewbench','bindbench','academydesk'];if(!kinds.includes(o.kind))return false;const W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T,i=kinds.indexOf(o.kind),R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),P=(c,a)=>shape(ctx,c,a),L=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,x,y,a,b,w),rnd=random(7453+i*193);
    const wood=['#423328','#76573e','#a87e51','#dec18a'],metal=['#374848','#65807b','#a2b6a7','#d6dbbf'];
    const bench=()=>{R('#26362e44',8,38,W-9,25);R(wood[0],5,28,W-10,29);R(wood[1],7,30,W-14,23);R(wood[2],6,23,W-12,12);R(wood[3],7,24,W-14,2);for(let j=0;j<36;j++)R(wood[j%2],9+rnd()*(W-20),30+rnd()*16,3+rnd()*6,1);R(wood[0],11,48,7,14);R(wood[0],W-18,48,7,14);};
    const book=(x,y,c)=>{R('#4a493b',x,y,24,15);R(c,x+1,y+1,22,12);R('#f1dfaa',x+2,y+2,9,9);R('#f1dfaa',x+13,y+2,9,9);R('#746b53',x+11,y+1,2,12);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){R('#9c947b',x+3,y+4+j*2,6,1);R('#9c947b',x+15,y+4+j*2,5,1);}};
    const framework=(x,y,w,h)=>{R(wood[0],x,y,6,h);R(wood[2],x+1,y+1,2,h-2);R(wood[0],x+w-6,y,6,h);R(wood[2],x+w-5,y+1,2,h-2);R(wood[0],x,y,w,7);R(wood[3],x+1,y+1,w-2,1);};
    const jar=(x,y,color)=>{P('#3f483c',[[x-7,y-15],[x+7,y-15],[x+10,y-5],[x+7,y],[x-7,y],[x-10,y-5]]);R(color,x-6,y-13,12,11);R('#e5d7aa',x-5,y-12,2,9);R(wood[3],x-6,y-17,12,2);};
    bench();switch(i){
      case 0:for(let j=0;j<4;j++){R(wood[0],14+j*4,12+j*4,57,7);R(wood[3],15+j*4,13+j*4,55,1);}R(metal[0],104,17,33,7);R(metal[2],105,17,27,2);L(wood[1],121,21,117,33,4);break;
      case 1:framework(19,2,65,44);R(metal[0],47,1,5,22);R(metal[2],48,2,2,20);R(wood[0],27,24,48,7);for(let j=0;j<4;j++)R('#e5dbb7',101+j*4,8+j*5,32,11);jar(17,40,'#809b8d');break;
      case 2:framework(15,1,116,48);for(let j=0;j<19;j++)L(j%5===0?'#b899bb':'#cfb9a9',22+j*5,9,22+j*5,35);R(wood[1],20,24,106,5);P('#c8a8cb',[[22,33],[126,33],[126,48],[22,46]]);for(let j=0;j<12;j++)R('#e7d0dc',24+j*8,36,1,9);break;
      case 3:P('#e5dbb3',[[18,10],[112,9],[130,34],[25,36]]);for(let j=0;j<4;j++)L('#889f91',24,15+j*4,115+j*2,14+j*4);for(let j=0;j<6;j++)L('#b5b093',29+j*13,12,35+j*14,34);L('#5d7c7e',45,12,88,31,2);L(metal[0],117,5,107,34,2);L(metal[2],117,5,128,31,2);break;
      case 4:for(let j=0;j<4;j++){P('#43534d',[[14+j*27,30],[17+j*27,11],[35+j*27,9],[39+j*27,28]]);R('#aeb19c',17+j*27,13,18,12);R('#d1c8a3',18+j*27,13,17,1);}L(metal[2],98,11,123,24,4);break;
      case 5:R(metal[0],24,14,33,14);P(metal[2],[[20,14],[54,14],[64,8],[59,19],[24,20]]);for(let j=0;j<4;j++){R(metal[0],83+j*12,15,7,16);R(metal[2],84+j*12,16,5,3);}L(wood[3],70,11,84,28,4);break;

      case 7:for(let j=0;j<5;j++){jar(20+j*27,32,['#789167','#92a47c','#aa9471'][j%3]);L('#739264',20+j*27,15,15+j*27,8);L('#9fb77e',20+j*27,15,25+j*27,5);}break;
      case 8:R('#434638',13,12,133,34);R('#a38967',15,14,129,5);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){jar(34+j*43,33,'#82958b');R(metal[0],25+j*43,13,18,3);R('#d4c4a4',27+j*43,12,14,1);}R('#d1a15d',17,40,122,3);break;
      case 9:P('#c0a6ba',[[20,11],[110,8],[133,29],[22,36]]);for(let j=0;j<5;j++)L('#e4cbbc',24,14+j*4,124,13+j*4);L(metal[2],88,12,104,28,2);L(metal[0],104,28,111,8,2);R('#b58c75',20,39,64,5);break;
