    }if(stage===9){R('#b99853',W-13,2,9,10);R('#f0d39a',W-12,3,7,2);}return true;
  }
  function civicMachineFrame(g,o,x,y,k,time,reduced){const kinds=['reclaimbench','paperpress','loomframe','maptable','stonebench','repairbench','pumpbench','herbalbench','cookbench','sewbench','bindbench','academydesk'],i=kinds.indexOf(o.kind);if(i<0)return false;const f=reduced?0:Math.floor(time/90)%24,u=f/24,px=(c,a,b,w,h,alpha=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,alpha).fillRect(Math.round(x+a*k),Math.round(y+b*k),w*k,h*k);switch(i){case 0:px(0xc8dbbf,108,18+Math.round(Math.sin(u*6.28)*5),21,2);break;case 1:case 10:{const v=Math.round((1-Math.cos(u*6.28))*5);px(0xc4cdb7,i===1?48:49,4,3,18+v);px(0xb69568,i===1?27:29,24+v,i===1?48:41,4);break;}case 2:px(0xe6ce96,23+u*94,26,11,3);break;case 3:px(0x748f81,40+u*53,17+Math.round(u*12),2,3);break;case 4:for(let j=0;j<7;j++){let z=(f+j*4)%24;px(0xd9cb9d,42+j*4+z*.4,25-z*.45,2,1,.6*(1-z/24));}break;case 5:px(0xd2d1b5,70,10+Math.round(Math.sin(u*6.28)*8),7,3);break;case 6:for(let j=0;j<4;j++)px(0xb4daca,27+((f*3+j*8)%31),25+j%2*4,5,1,.8);break;case 7:for(let j=0;j<4;j++)px(0xb4cf86,18+j*31+Math.sin(u*6.28+j)*2,12+(j%2)*3,3,3);break;case 8:for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let z=(f+j*5)%24;px(0xe3d7c2,33+j*19+Math.sin(z)*2,14-z*.5,2,3,.5*(1-z/24));}break;case 9:px(0xd6d3bd,87+u*17,12+u*15,2,7);break;case 11:px(0xe3cfa0,42+u*61,20+Math.sin(u*6.28)*4,10,2);break;}return true;}

  function districtMachine(ctx,o,branch){if(civicMachine(ctx,o,branch))return;const W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T,k=o.kind,R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),L=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(a),Math.round(b),w),P=(c,p)=>shape(ctx,c,p),rnd=random(W*17+o.kind.length*967);const wood=['#3f3027','#79543b','#ad8052','#d5b17a'],metal=['#354747','#617772','#9aa89a','#d0d2b6'];
    const bench=(x=5,y=25,w=W-10,h=H-31)=>{R('#27352d66',x+4,y+7,w,h);R(wood[0],x,y,w,h);R(wood[1],x+2,y+2,w-4,h-4);R(wood[2],x+1,y-5,w-2,11);R(wood[3],x+2,y-5,w-4,2);for(let j=0;j<24;j++){const a=x+4+rnd()*(w-12),b=y+7+rnd()*Math.max(1,h-12);R(wood[j%2],a,b,3+rnd()*8,1);}R(wood[0],x+7,y+h-9,6,10);R(wood[0],x+w-13,y+h-9,6,10);};
    const gear=(x,y,r)=>{for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/6;R(metal[1],x+Math.cos(a)*r-2,y+Math.sin(a)*r-2,4,4);}for(let a=-r;a<=r;a++)for(let b=-r;b<=r;b++){const d=Math.hypot(a,b);if(d<r&&d>r-4)R(d>r-2?metal[0]:metal[2],x+a,y+b,1,1);}R(metal[0],x-4,y-4,8,8);R(metal[3],x-2,y-2,4,4);};
    const pot=(x,y,col='#b4764b')=>{P('#3f342c',[[x-8,y],[x+8,y],[x+12,y+6],[x+8,y+20],[x-8,y+20],[x-12,y+6]]);P(col,[[x-7,y+2],[x+7,y+2],[x+10,y+7],[x+7,y+17],[x-7,y+17],[x-10,y+7]]);R('#e2b383',x-6,y+3,3,12);R('#594637',x-7,y,14,3);R('#dcc095',x-6,y,12,1);};
    const foliage=(x,y)=>{R('#66734d',x,y-9,2,12);for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let d=j%2?1:-1;P(['#526d46','#83a264','#b4c781'][j%3],[[x,y-j*3],[x+d*6,y-j*3-3],[x+d*10,y-j*3-1],[x+d*4,y-j*3+2]]);}R('#e2d9a4',x-1,y-13,3,3);};
    const page=(x,y,w=20)=>{R('#5c5140',x,y,w,15);R('#e0d0a2',x+1,y+1,w-2,12);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)R('#87917a',x+3,y+3+i*2,w-6-(i%2)*4,1);};
    if(k==='alcove'){R('#29362e88',3,7,W-3,H-6);R(wood[0],4,3,W-8,H-6);R(wood[2],6,4,W-12,4);R('#e3d7b9',8,9,W-16,H-16);R('#8e9a89',8,17,W-16,H-24);R('#566b64',9,19,W-18,H-26);for(let yy=20;yy<H-7;yy+=5){R('#9aac95',10,yy,W-20,1);for(let xx=11;xx<W-9;xx+=6)R('#b2bd9e',xx,yy+1,2,2);}R('#fff0c9',10,10,W-20,4);R(wood[0],5,H-8,W-10,5);R(wood[3],6,H-9,W-12,2);}
    else if(['kilnunit','forgeunit'].includes(k)){R('#3d3833',9,5,W-18,H-9);for(let y=7;y<H-8;y+=9)for(let x=11-(y%2)*5;x<W-13;x+=16){R(['#92705a','#a67e61','#806350'][Math.floor(rnd()*3)],x,y,14,7);R('#d0a57b',x,y,13,1);}const cx=W*.38;P('#2b2927',[[cx-20,H-7],[cx-20,29],[cx-12,17],[cx+12,17],[cx+20,29],[cx+20,H-7]]);R('#b16b40',cx-15,H-20,30,8);R('#dc9150',cx-12,H-18,24,3);if(k==='forgeunit'){P('#374644',[[W*.64,20],[W-8,20],[W-19,29],[W-31,29],[W-36,39],[W*.64,39]]);L('#aebdb1',W*.64,20,W-8,20,2);R('#665342',W*.68,40,W*.23,17);}}
    else if(k==='sawbench'||k==='joinbench'){bench();for(let j=0;j<4;j++){R(wood[0],10+j*4,11+j*4,52,8);R(wood[3],10+j*4,12+j*4,51,3);}if(k==='sawbench'){R(metal[0],W-46,6,5,39);R(metal[2],W-44,9,2,30);for(let i=0;i<14;i++)R(metal[3],W-47,11+i*2,2,1);gear(W-23,32,12);}else{R(metal[0],W-49,19,27,9);R(metal[3],W-47,19,24,2);R(wood[1],W-28,13,6,13);R(wood[0],W-19,29,9,12);}}
    else if(k==='grainmill'||k==='lapwheel'||k==='potwheel'){bench();const x=W*.43,y=24,r=19;P(metal[0],[[x-r,y],[x-8,y-9],[x+8,y-9],[x+r,y],[x+r,y+12],[x-r,y+12]]);P(k==='potwheel'?'#a38060':'#a9ada0',[[x-r,y],[x-8,y-9],[x+8,y-9],[x+r,y],[x+8,y+8],[x-8,y+8]]);gear(W-27,26,12);if(k==='potwheel')pot(x,y-13);else if(k==='grainmill'){P(wood[1],[[x-12,1],[x+12,1],[x+7,18],[x-7,18]]);R(wood[3],x-12,1,24,2);}else{P('#75a8ae',[[x-6,y-8],[x+5,y-11],[x+10,y-2],[x+2,y+5],[x-8,y]]);L('#d5e5cc',x-6,y-8,x+5,y-11,2);}}
    else if(k==='filterunit'||k==='dyetub'||k==='fruitpress'||k==='teapan'){bench();for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=25+i*(W-50)/2;pot(x,15,k==='dyetub'?['#9c769e','#7a8e62','#a77852'][i]:k==='filterunit'?'#6f948e':'#a98454');R(k==='filterunit'?'#a7c9bc':k==='dyetub'?'#cc9eb5':'#d1b272',x-6,18,12,2);}if(k==='fruitpress'){R(wood[0],W/2-23,1,46,5);R(wood[1],W/2-21,5,5,37);R(wood[1],W/2+17,5,5,37);R(metal[2],W/2-2,1,4,31);R(wood[0],W/2-14,29,28,5);}if(k==='filterunit'){L(metal[2],15,5,W-15,5,3);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)R(metal[2],23+i*(W-50)/2,6,4,9);}}
    else if(['roottray','graintray','glassbench','marketstall'].includes(k)){bench();for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(W/15)-1;i++){const x=12+i*15;R(wood[0],x-6,15,13,21);R('#77674b',x-4,17,9,16);if(k==='roottray'||k==='glassbench'){foliage(x,22);foliage(x+2,29);}else for(let j=0;j<6;j++)R(k==='marketstall'?(j%2?'#b9ba68':'#bb8556'):'#d5b974',x-4+(j%3)*3,18+Math.floor(j/3)*5,3,4);}if(k==='glassbench'){L('#92b4a0',5,6,W-5,6,2);for(let x=7;x<W-5;x+=23)L('#759e92',x,6,x+4,36,2);}}
    else if(k==='breadoven'){drawMillMachine(ctx,W,H,'oven',branch);}
    else{bench();for(let i=0;i<4;i++)page(10+i*(W-30)/4,13+i%2*8,22);R('#465f67',W-24,10,7,10);L('#dcc69c',W-20,9,W-14,2,2);}
    if(branch===9){R('#b99853',W-13,2,9,10);R('#f0d39a',W-12,3,7,2);}
  }
  function civicRoomDetail(ctx,l,p){if(!p.civic)return;const i=C.EAST_SITES.indexOf(p)-18,W=l.w*T,H=l.h*T,a=p.palette,work=l.objects.find(o=>o.id==='work'),aux=l.objects.find(o=>o.id==='aux'),R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),P=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts),rng=random(7189+i*231);const x=work.x*T-7,y=work.y*T+work.h*T-5,ww=work.w*T+14,hh=27;
    // Inlaid work zone, with a different material appropriate to each craft. Sits below collision and furniture.
    if([4,5,6,8].includes(i)){const pal=i===6?['#526b61','#7d9989','#a5b9a0']:i===4?['#796455','#a68f73','#c4b49b']:['#665c50','#97826c','#c3ab88'];R(pal[0],x,y,ww,hh);for(let yy=y+1;yy<y+hh-1;yy+=7)for(let xx=x+1;xx<x+ww-1;xx+=11){const w=Math.min(10,x+ww-xx-1),h=Math.min(6,y+hh-yy-1);R(pal[rng()>.75?2:1],xx,yy,w,h);R(pal[2],xx,yy,w,1);}}
    else{R(a[0],x,y,ww,hh);R(a[1],x+3,y+3,ww-6,hh-6);R(a[2],x+5,y+5,ww-10,1);R(a[2],x+5,y+hh-6,ww-10,1);for(let xx=x+9;xx<x+ww-10;xx+=14){P(a[2],[[xx,y+hh/2],[xx+4,y+8],[xx+8,y+hh/2],[xx+4,y+hh-8]]);R(a[0],xx+3,y+hh/2-1,3,3);}}
    // Painted beams, inset signs, ornamental tiles, and narrow service mat at the auxiliary station.
    const ax=aux.x*T,ay=(aux.y+aux.h)*T;R(a[0],ax,ay-2,aux.w*T,9);for(let xx=ax+3;xx<ax+aux.w*T-3;xx+=7)R(a[2],xx,ay+2,3,2);
    const cx=W/2;R('#3b443b',cx-43,14,86,30);R('#978163',cx-41,16,82,26);R(a[0],cx-38,18,76,22);for(let j=0;j<6;j++)R(a[2],cx-31+j*11,23,5,10+(j%2)*4);R(a[3],cx-34,19,68,1);
    // Fine edge detailing behind furniture, never on paths where it suggests a barrier.
    for(let xx=14;xx<W-15;xx+=19){R(a[0],xx,H-13,13,4);R(a[2],xx+2,H-12,8,1);}
  }
  function civicAuxTexture(ctx,o,p){if(!p.civic||o.id!=='aux')return false;const i=C.EAST_SITES.indexOf(p)-18,W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T,wood=['#44372e','#77583e','#ad8156','#dcb580'],metal=['#3e4c49','#66827b','#b0beb0'],R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),L=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,x,y,a,b,w),P=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts);R('#27352e66',4,H-15,W-4,13);R(wood[0],5,22,W-10,H-26);R(wood[1],7,24,W-14,H-30);R(wood[2],5,17,W-10,13);R(wood[3],6,18,W-12,2);for(let x=10;x<W-10;x+=18)R(wood[0],x,H-11,3,7);
    const folio=(x,y,c)=>{R('#48524a',x,y,21,13);R(c,x+1,y+1,19,11);for(let k=0;k<3;k++)R('#e8dab2',x+4,y+3+k*3,14-k*3,1);};
    const jar=(x,y,c)=>{P('#3f4d41',[[x-6,y],[x-8,y-11],[x-5,y-20],[x+5,y-20],[x+8,y-11],[x+6,y]]);R(c,x-5,y-17,10,15);R('#d8cba0',x-4,y-16,2,12);R(wood[3],x-5,y-21,10,2);};
    switch(i){case 0:for(let j=0;j<3;j++){R(wood[0],11+j*24,8,18,20);R(wood[2],13+j*24,10,14,15);R(wood[3],14+j*24,10,12,2);R(metal[2],25+j*24,19,2,3);}break;
      case 1:for(let j=0;j<3;j++){jar(20+j*25,29,'#819a8c');R('#e0d5b7',15+j*25,8,10,7);}break;
      case 2:for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const c=['#aca0b9','#bd889a','#aeb58a','#c8ab76'][j];R(c,10+j*19,9,16,28);R('#e3d2b7',11+j*19,12,14,2);R(wood[0],12+j*19,34,12,2);}break;
      case 3:folio(10,9,'#b5bfa5');R('#596e68',37,7,31,23);P('#d2c298',[[40,10],[64,10],[64,26],[40,26]]);L('#627f79',42,23,60,14,2);L(metal[2],76,8,69,29,2);L(metal[2],76,8,83,29,2);break;
      case 4:for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const x=10+j*19;P('#4d5953',[[x,25],[x+4,10],[x+12,6],[x+17,23]]);P(['#b1a48b','#a5b5ae','#a69db6','#b68d6a'][j],[[x+1,22],[x+5,11],[x+12,7],[x+15,21]]);}break;
      case 5:for(let j=0;j<5;j++){R(metal[0],12+j*15,12,10,16);R(metal[2],13+j*15,13,8,3);R(wood[3],16+j*15,21,4,3);}break;
      case 6:R(metal[0],8,7,W-16,35);R(metal[1],11,10,W-22,28);L(metal[2],15,20,W-15,20,5);L(metal[0],W/2,5,W/2,37,4);for(let j=0;j<5;j++){const a=j*6.28/5;L(metal[2],W/2,20,W/2+Math.cos(a)*12,20+Math.sin(a)*12,2);}break;
      case 7:for(let j=0;j<4;j++){jar(16+j*19,29,['#8a9c65','#9c8256','#a2ae76','#b9bb89'][j]);R('#d0c2a0',12+j*19,17,8,3);}break;
      case 8:for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const x=9+j*20;P('#7c6e53',[[x,24],[x+3,15],[x+16,15],[x+19,24],[x+16,28],[x+3,28]]);R('#d6c5a0',x+4,16,11,8);}break;
      case 9:for(let j=0;j<3;j++){R(['#a4877e','#b5bd93','#a0b5b6'][j],12+j*22,8,20,25);R('#e2cdb0',13+j*22,11,18,3);}break;
      case 10:for(let j=0;j<3;j++)folio(10+j*23,10+j%2*3,['#8eada6','#b79f82','#b6a5c0'][j]);break;
      case 11:folio(10,10,'#acb8a0');folio(38,10,'#bba78e');R('#4a6259',64,4,25,24);for(let j=0;j<4;j++)R('#e5d5aa',68,8+j*4,17-j*2,1);break;
    }return true;
  }
  function civicRoomStateArt(g,h,w,k,time,reduced){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,p=D.eastById[h.id];if(!p?.civic)return;const f=D.civicFacility(w,p.id),a=h.layout.objects.find(o=>o.id==='aux'),x=ROOM_X+a.x*T*k,y=a.y*T*k,W=a.w*T*k;const col=c=>parseInt(c.slice(1),16);if(f.banner!==undefined){g.fillStyle(col(D.civicFlags[f.banner]),.95).fillRect(x+12*k,y+8*k,W-24*k,18*k);for(let j=0;j<6;j++)g.fillStyle(0xe5d6b4,.55).fillRect(x+(15+j*8)*k,y+11*k,2*k,12*k);}
    if(p.id==='fen-aqueduct'){g.lineStyle(3*k,D.civicPumpOpen(w,p.id)?0xa0cbb5:0xc18a6c,1).lineBetween(x+W/2-10*k,y+20*k,x+W/2+10*k,y+(D.civicPumpOpen(w,p.id)?20:9)*k);}
    if(f.mealDay===Math.floor(D.now(w)/1440)){for(let j=0;j<3;j++){g.fillStyle(0xe3d5a8,1).fillEllipse(x+(18+j*23)*k,y+20*k,15*k,8*k);g.fillStyle(0xbb8c56,1).fillEllipse(x+(18+j*23)*k,y+20*k,8*k,5*k);if(!reduced){const z=(time/450+j*.3)%1;g.fillStyle(0xe6dec2,.25*(1-z)).fillRect(x+(18+j*23)*k,y+(14-z*12)*k,2*k,3*k);}}}
    if(f.archived){for(let j=0;j<Math.min(7,f.archived);j++)g.fillStyle([0x91b1a9,0xbea183,0xb1a0b9][j%3],1).fillRect(x+(8+j*10)*k,y+32*k,8*k,12*k);}
    if(p.id==='moon-academy'&&f.lessons){for(let i=0;i<f.lessons;i++)g.fillStyle(0xe9d8a8,1).fillRect(x+(68+i*4)*k,y+7*k,2*k,5*k);}
  }

  function districtRoomFinish(ctx,l,id){const p= root.LQValleyDevelopment.eastById[id];if(!p)return;const W=l.w*T,H=l.h*T,r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.round(w)),Math.max(0,Math.round(h))),rng=random(137+p.x*89+p.y*17);const a=p.palette;for(let x=14;x<W-14;x+=26){r('#26372f',x,46,20,6);r(a[1],x+1,47,18,3);r(a[2],x+5,47,2,2);r(a[3],x+13,47,2,2);}const work=l.objects.find(o=>o.action==='work'),xx=work.x*T,yy=(work.y+work.h)*T;for(let i=0;i<130;i++){const x=xx-6+rng()*(work.w*T+12),y=yy+rng()*25;if(x>13&&x<W-13&&y<H-20)r(i%3===0?a[2]+'99':a[0]+'66',x,y,1+rng()*3,1+rng()*2);}const mid=Math.floor(l.w/2)*T;for(let y=H-48;y<H-18;y+=4){r(a[0],mid-17,y,34,3);r(a[2],mid-14,y+1,2,1);r(a[2],mid+11,y+1,2,1);}for(let i=0;i<4;i++){r(a[0],W/2-20+i*11,15,8,25);r(a[1],W/2-19+i*11,16,6,21);r(a[3],W/2-17+i*11,19+i%2*5,2,5);} }
  function districtMachineFrame(g,h,w,time,k,reduced){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,p=D.eastById[h.id],s=D.eastState(w,h.id);if(!p||!s.job||!D.eastDuty(w,h.id,w.clock.minuteOfDay)?.working)return;const o=h.layout.objects.find(a=>a.action==='work'),x=ROOM_X+o.x*T*k,y=o.y*T*k,W=o.w*T*k,f=reduced?0:Math.floor(time/100)%16,u=f/16;const px=(c,a,b,ww,hh,alpha=1)=>g.fillStyle(c,alpha).fillRect(Math.round(x+a*k),Math.round(y+b*k),ww*k,hh*k);if(civicMachineFrame(g,o,x,y,k,time,reduced))return;if(['sawbench','joinbench'].includes(o.kind)){px(0xb5c1bc,115,9+Math.round(Math.sin(u*Math.PI*2)*6),2,25);for(let i=0;i<8;i++)px(0xd9bb83,64+(i*7+f*2)%45,28-(i*3+f)%18,2,1,.7);}else if(['kilnunit','forgeunit','breadoven'].includes(o.kind)){for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const z=(f+i*3)%16;px(i%2?0xf4ad57:0xe3c277,43+i*5,47-z*.5,3,5+z*.25,.8);}g.fillStyle(0xf4a64b,.08).fillEllipse(x+W*.39,y+40*k,85*k,55*k);}else if(['grainmill','lapwheel','potwheel'].includes(o.kind)){const cx=x+W-27*k,cy=y+26*k;for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const a=u*Math.PI*2+i*Math.PI/3;g.lineStyle(2*k,0xc5cbb3,.95).lineBetween(cx,cy,cx+Math.cos(a)*11*k,cy+Math.sin(a)*11*k);}}else if(['filterunit','dyetub','fruitpress','teapan'].includes(o.kind)){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){px(0xd6e4cf,19+i*(o.w*T-50)/2,17+(f+i)%5,8,1,.8);const z=(f+i*5)%16;px(0xc4dfcc,23+i*(o.w*T-50)/2+Math.sin(z)*2,15-z,2,2,.45);}}else if(['roottray','graintray','glassbench','marketstall'].includes(o.kind)){for(let i=0;i<7;i++)px(0xc6d88b,12+i*15+Math.round(Math.sin(u*6+i)*2),17+i%2*4,3,2,.85);}else{px(0xe3d1a0,65+f,14+Math.round(f/4),8,2,.9);px(0x677e77,72+f,16+Math.round(f/4),3,2,.8);}}

  function domesticRoomFinish(ctx,l,id){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,h=D.homeById[id],W=l.w*T,H=l.h*T;const pal=[['#876a43','#b49e69','#516b60'],['#6d5872','#baa3b3','#a7b2a1'],['#8b6047','#d1b58b','#72554b'],['#48746c','#a2bca2','#678e84'],['#927645','#dcc88b','#ad6850'],['#526579','#b0bfc1','#859e9d']][h.style];const [a,b,c]=pal,R=(col,x,y,w,z)=>box(ctx,col,x,y,w,z);const flip=h.variant?1:0;
    // Region-specific wainscoting, shelves and patterned woven rug painted on walkable floor.

  function galleryExhibitArt(ctx,o,p){const W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T,[d,m,a,b]=p.palette,R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),L=(c,x,y,z,v,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,x,y,z,v,w);R('#25322f',3,H-10,W-6,8);R(d,5,H-17,W-10,14);R(m,7,H-15,W-14,6);R(b,7,H-16,W-14,1);R('#39463d',12,2,W-24,H-19);R('#b7c5ac',14,4,W-28,H-23);R('#56665b',16,6,W-32,H-27);const x=W/2,y=H/2-2;

    R('#ecdfbc',W/2-10,H-12,20,4);}

  function archiveFurnitureArt(ctx,o,p){if(o.kind!=='desk'){galleryExhibitArt(ctx,o,p);const W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T;box(ctx,'#41372e',6,H-14,W-12,7);box(ctx,'#9e8560',7,H-14,W-14,2);return;}const W=o.w*T,H=o.h*T,R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h);R('#222c29',3,H-8,W-6,7);R('#4c3b2d',6,9,W-12,H-12);R('#9b7951',6,7,W-12,17);R('#bc9a66',7,7,W-14,2);R('#6d513a',9,22,W-18,4);R('#d8c79b',W/2-18,10,34,12);for(let i=0;i<3;i++){R('#8c7e63',W/2-15,12+i*3,27,1);}R('#735338',W-23,12,8,7);R('#f1d69a',W-21,9,4,5);R('#77938a',14,13,7,6);}

  function heritageRoomEnter(scene,w,id,out,floor=0){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,secret=floor===2&&D.archiveOpen(w,id),upper=secret||floor>=1&&D.galleryOpen(w,id),l=secret?D.archiveLayout(id):upper?D.wingLayout(D.annexLayout(D.galleryLayout(id),id,w),id,w):D.roomLayouts[id];if(upper&&!secret)l.objects.push(D.archiveHatch(l));if(!l||!D.roomOpen(w,id)||scene.inside)return false;const W=l.w*T,H=l.h*T,k=W*2<scene.scale.width*.93&&H*2<scene.scale.height*.86?2:1,site=D.roomSite(id),objs=[],add=(x,y,key,depth,ox=0,oy=0)=>{const s=scene.add.image(x,y,key).setOrigin(ox,oy).setScale(k/detailFactor(key)).setDepth(depth);objs.push(s);return s;};const branch=D.homeById[id]?0:D.eastById[id]?D.eastState(w,id).stage:id===C.MILL.id?D.millState(w).stage:D.heritageState(w,id).branch;
    add(ROOM_X,ROOM_Y,detailTexture(scene,'v1023-guild-room-floor-'+id+'-'+(secret?2:Number(upper))+'-'+l.w+'x'+l.h,W,H,ctx=>{if(secret){archiveFloorArt(ctx,l,id);detailFloor(ctx,l,id);return;}if(upper){galleryFloorArt(ctx,l,id);archiveHatchArt(ctx,l,id);detailFloor(ctx,l,id);return;}roomFloor(ctx,l,id);hallFinish(ctx,l,id);if(D.eastById[id]){districtRoomFinish(ctx,l,id);civicRoomDetail(ctx,l,D.eastById[id]);}else if(D.homeById[id])domesticRoomFinish(ctx,l,id);else guildHallMaterial(ctx,l,id);if(id===C.MILL.id)millHallFinish(ctx,l);if(D.galleryCatalog[id])monumentStairArt(ctx,l,id);detailFloor(ctx,l,id);}),1);
    const cutaway=[0,1].map(side=>add(ROOM_X+side*W*k/2,ROOM_Y+(H-26)*k,detailTexture(scene,'v1027-front-panel-'+l.w+'-'+side,W/2,26,c=>{c.translate(-side*W/2,-H+26);cutawayArt(c,W,H);}),12));
    const furnitureViews=new Map();for(const o of l.objects){if(!o.h)continue;const s=add(ROOM_X+o.x*T*k,o.y*T*k,texture(scene,'v99-guild-room-furn-'+id+'-'+(secret?2:Number(upper))+'-'+o.id+'-'+branch,o.w*T,o.h*T,ctx=>{if(secret){archiveFurnitureArt(ctx,o,D.eastById[id]);return;}if(upper){galleryExhibitArt(ctx,o,D.eastById[id]);return;}if(D.eastById[id]&&(o.action==='work'||o.kind==='alcove'))districtMachine(ctx,o,branch);else if(D.eastById[id]&&civicAuxTexture(ctx,o,D.eastById[id])){}else{furniture(ctx,o,branch);hallFurnitureFinish(ctx,o,branch);guildFurnitureMaterial(ctx,o);}}),10+(o.y+o.h)/100);furnitureViews.set(o.id,s);}
    const windows=secret?[]:['west','east'].map((side,i)=>add(ROOM_X+(i?W-135:22)*k,10*k,texture(scene,'heritage-window-init-'+id+'-'+side,114,46,ctx=>roomWindowScene(ctx,w,site,side)),3));
    const guildLight=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(4);objs.push(guildLight);const fx=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(30);objs.push(fx);const text=scene.add.text(ROOM_X+Math.floor(l.w/2)*T*k,(H-13)*k,secret?'RETURN TO GALLERY  ▼':upper?'STAIRS DOWN  ▼':'EXIT  ▼',{fontSize:'11px',color:'#f2ddb0',backgroundColor:'#26362f',padding:{x:5,y:2}}).setOrigin(.5).setDepth(31);objs.push(text);
    const rects=l.objects.filter(o=>o.h).map(o=>({x:ROOM_X+o.x*T*k,y:o.y*T*k,w:o.w*T*k,h:o.h*T*k}));
    scene.inside={b:{id,name:l.name},spec:{w:l.w,h:l.h,items:[],labels:[],spots:{},guests:[]},k,rects,objs,out,floor:secret?2:upper?1:0,heritage:{id,upper,secret,layout:l,windows,fx,guildLight,furnitureViews,cutaway,lastWindow:'',next:0,trees:Object.values(C.TREES_BY_ID).filter(t=>t.y<site.y&&t.y>site.y-9&&Math.abs(t.x-site.x)<8)}};
    scene.player.setScale(k).setPosition(ROOM_X+Math.floor(l.w/2)*T*k,(l.h-1.55)*T*k);scene.doorCooldown=.8;const cam=scene.cameras.main;cam.stopFollow();cam.removeBounds();const large=id===C.MILL.id||!!D.eastById[id];if(large)scene.inside.millEntryZoom=cam.zoom;cam.setZoom(large?Math.max(.75,Math.min(1.65,(cam.width-90)/(W*k),(cam.height-164)/(H*k))):1);cam.centerOn(ROOM_X+W*k/2,H*k/2);if(W*k>cam.width||H*k>cam.height){cam.setBounds(ROOM_X,0,W*k,H*k);cam.startFollow(scene.player,true,.12,.12);}scene.prompt?.setVisible(false);return true;
  }
  function heritageRoomNear(scene){const r=scene.inside,h=r?.heritage;if(!h)return null;const x=(scene.player.x-ROOM_X)/(T*r.k),y=scene.player.y/(T*r.k);return h.layout.objects.map(o=>({o,d:Math.hypot(x-o.ax,y-o.ay)})).filter(a=>a.d<.95).sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d)[0]?.o||null;}
  function heritageRoomFrame(scene,w,time,task,reduced){const r=scene.inside,h=r?.heritage;if(!h)return;const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,s=D.roomState(w,h.id),k=r.k,l=h.layout,night=w.clock.minuteOfDay>=1140||w.clock.minuteOfDay<375,site=D.roomSite(h.id),g=h.fx;h.cutaway?.forEach((panel,side)=>panel.setAlpha(cutawayPanelOpacity(scene.player.x-ROOM_X,scene.player.y,l.w*T,l.h*T,k,side)));for(const a=D.archiveState(w,h.id);for(const o of l.objects){const [29,l.w*T-38]){for(let side=i?'east':'west';h.windows[i].setTexture(texture(scene,'v98-gallery-window-'+h.id+'-'+side+'-'+sig,114,46,c=>roomWindowScene(c,w,site,side)));}}if(night)g.fillStyle(0x1d293b,.22).fillRect(ROOM_X+8*k,64*k,(l.w*T-16)*k,(l.h*T-72)*k);for(const o of l.objects){const read=o.action==='wing-study'?D.wingState(w,h.id)?.read.includes(Number(o.id.slice(-1))):o.action==='annex-study'?D.annexState(w)?.read.includes(Number(o.id.slice(-1))):o.action==='archive-hatch'?D.archiveOpen(w,h.id):D.galleryProgress(w,h.id)[o.id.slice(-1)]!==undefined;g.fillStyle(read?0x9ec398:0xe6cf96,1).fillRect(ROOM_X+(o.x*T+o.w*T-10)*k,(o.y*T+5)*k,4*k,4*k);}return;}charterRestFrame(scene,w,h);g.clear();guildRoomLighting(scene,w,h,k,time,reduced);if(D.eastById[h.id]){districtMachineFrame(g,h,w,time,k,reduced);civicRoomStateArt(g,h,w,k,time,reduced);}const sig=[windowPhase(w.clock.minuteOfDay),w.weather.kind,...h.trees.map(t=>root.LQValleySimulation.treeStage(w,t))].join('|');if(h.lastWindow!==sig){h.lastWindow=sig;for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const tex=h.windows[i].texture.key,paint=texture(scene,'heritage-window-live-'+h.id+'-'+i,114,46,ctx=>roomWindowScene(ctx,w,site,i?'east':'west'));const t=scene.textures.get(paint),ctx=t.getContext();ctx.clearRect(0,0,114,46);roomWindowScene(ctx,w,site,i?'east':'west');t.refresh();h.windows[i].setTexture(paint);}}
    const X=ROOM_X, W=l.w*T*k,H=l.h*T*k,mid=X+Math.floor(l.w/2)*T*k;
    const storage=l.objects.find(o=>o.id===D.roomStorageIds[h.id]),stock=Object.values(s.stock||{}).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);if(stock){const bx=X+(storage.x*T+4)*k,by=(storage.y*T+storage.h*T-9)*k;for(let j=0;j<Math.min(6,Math.ceil(stock/6));j++){g.fillStyle(0x3c3227,1).fillRect(bx+j*7*k,by,6*k,6*k);g.fillStyle(0xc6a16c,1).fillRect(bx+j*7*k+k,by+k,4*k,3*k);}}
    g.fillStyle(0x1d293b,night?.30:w.weather.kind==='rain'?.09:0).fillRect(X+8*k,64*k,W-16*k,H-72*k);if(s.lamp){for(let j=4;j>=0;j--)g.fillStyle(0xffcc79,.035).fillEllipse(mid,67*k,(125+j*27)*k,(61+j*12)*k);g.fillStyle(0x68523a,1).fillRect(mid-4*k,44*k,8*k,14*k);g.fillStyle(0xffe3a2,.9).fillRect(mid-2*k,45*k,4*k,8*k);}if(!night){const phase=windowPhase(w.clock.minuteOfDay),overcast=['rain','storm','mist','snow'].includes(w.weather.kind);for(let i=0;i<h.windows.length;i++){const win=h.windows[i],direct=phase==='morning'?i===1:phase==='evening'?i===0:false,intensity=overcast?.018:direct?.14:phase==='day'?.065:.028;g.fillStyle(direct?0xf6c987:0xe5d8a0,intensity).fillTriangle(win.x+8*k,60*k,win.x+100*k,60*k,win.x+(i===0?116:-16)*k,(direct?193:148)*k);}}
    if(!reduced&&w.weather.kind==='rain')for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const win=h.windows[i%2],x=win.x+(6+(i*17)%101)*k,y=win.y+(5+(time/48+i*9)%30)*k;g.lineStyle(k,0xbbd9dd,.6).lineBetween(x,y,x-2*k,y+5*k);}if(h.id===C.MILL.id){millRoomAnimation(g,l,k,w,time,task,reduced);millHallLight(g,l,k,w,time,task,reduced);}if((task?.roomWork||D.guildDefs[h.id]&&D.guildPose(w,h.id)?.work)&&!reduced&&h.id!==C.MILL.id){const o=l.objects.find(o=>o.action==='work'),f=task?.roomWork?task.elapsed/task.duration:(time%2800)/2800,x=X+(o.x+o.w/2)*T*k,y=(o.y+o.h-.35)*T*k;const wave=Math.sin(f*Math.PI*6),q=Math.round(wave*4)*k;
      if(o.kind==='balance'){const bx=X+(o.x+o.w/2)*T*k,by=o.y*T*k+10*k;g.lineStyle(3*k,0xd9bb81,1).lineBetween(bx-35*k,by+q,bx+35*k,by-q);for(const sign of [-1,1]){const a=bx+sign*29*k,b=by-sign*q+19*k;g.lineStyle(k,0xc1ac89,.9).lineBetween(a,by-sign*q,a-8*k,b);g.lineStyle(k,0xc1ac89,.9).lineBetween(a,by-sign*q,a+8*k,b);g.fillStyle(0xd0b476,1).fillRect(a-9*k,b,18*k,3*k);}}
      else if(o.kind==='scope'){const bx=X+(o.x+o.w/2)*T*k,by=(o.y*T+19)*k;g.fillStyle(0x97d4d5,.13).fillCircle(bx+22*k,by-10*k,10*k+Math.abs(q));g.fillStyle(0xd8ece0,.88).fillRect(bx+22*k,by-11*k,2*k,2*k);}
      else if(o.kind==='planter'||o.kind==='dryer'){for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const bx=X+(o.x*T+12+i*14)*k,by=(o.y*T+19+(i%2)*17)*k;g.lineStyle(k,0x89ac65,.9).lineBetween(bx,by,bx+Math.round(Math.sin(f*8+i)*3)*k,by-8*k);g.fillStyle(0xc3d28d,.9).fillRect(bx+q,by-7*k,3*k,2*k);}}
      else {for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const a=(f*3+i/8)%1;g.fillStyle(o.kind==='masonry'?0xd0c8b0:0xe2c187,(1-a)*.7).fillRect(x+(i-4)*3*k+a*9*k,y-a*15*k,2*k,2*k);}}}
  }

  function roomWindow(scene,world) {
    if(scene.inside?.heritage||scene.inside?.tunnel)return;
    const room=scene.inside;
    if(!room)return;
    const minute=world.clock.minuteOfDay;
    const light=minute<375||minute>=1140?'night':minute<630?'morning':minute<1020?'day':'dusk';
    const key=room.b.id+'-'+world.weather.kind+'-'+light;
    updateRoomWear(scene,room,world,light);
    if(room.valleyWindow?.state===key)return;
    const imageKey=texture(scene,'window-'+key,76,53,ctx=>{
      windowView(ctx,7,6,62,40,room.b.id,world.weather.kind,minute);
    });
    if(room.valleyWindow)room.valleyWindow.image.setTexture(imageKey);
    else {
      const x=20000+(room.b.id==='lyra-home'?room.spec.w-3:3)*T*room.k;
      const image=scene.add.image(x,30*room.k,imageKey).setScale(room.k*.88).setDepth(3);
      room.valleyWindow={image};
      room.objs.push(image);
    }
    room.valleyWindow.state=key;
  }

  function updateRoomWear(scene,room,world,light) {
    const kind=room.b.id;
    const roofStage=world.buildings['ruined-warehouse'].status;
    const stage=kind==='ruined-warehouse' && ['damaged','repairing'].includes(roofStage)?roofStage:'kept';
    const illumination=light==='night'?'dark':world.weather.kind==='rain'?'rain':'day';
    const isLodged=Boolean(world.residents.mara);
    const key=kind+'-'+stage+'-'+illumination+'-'+(kind==='ruined-warehouse'&&isLodged?'bunk':'empty');
    if(room.valleyWear?.key===key)return;
    const {w,h}=room.spec;
    const tex=texture(scene,'room-wear-'+key,w*T,h*T,ctx=>{
      const rng=random(331);
      const left=w*T, bottom=h*T;
      if(illumination==='dark')box(ctx,'#27375555',2,12,left-4,bottom-17);
      for(let i=0;i<44;i++){
        const x=12+rng()*(left-26),y=57+rng()*(bottom-83);
        box(ctx,stage==='damaged'?'#4239325c':'#54493726',x,y,3+rng()*7,1);
      }
      if(kind==='ruined-warehouse') {
        if(stage==='damaged'||stage==='repairing') {
          const opening=[[75,10],[101,6],[123,8],[149,26],[133,34],[93,33]];
          shape(ctx,'#343a35',opening);
          line(ctx,'#ae9270',84,14,130,27,4);
          const beam=illumination==='rain'?'#89a1a74a':illumination==='dark'?'#9fb8d632':'#f0d19b4e';
          shape(ctx,beam,[[88,24],[128,27],[178,127],[101,130]]);
          line(ctx,'#635146',50,92,59,105,2);line(ctx,'#534637',59,105,71,112,1);
          box(ctx,illumination==='rain'?'#647e7d':'#5b564a',106,124,37,4);
          if(stage==='repairing')for(let i=0;i<4;i++)line(ctx,'#b28c60',249+i*9,152,271+i*8,124,4);
        } else {
          box(ctx,'#564736',77,13,72,7);box(ctx,'#b09570',81,16,64,3);
          crate(ctx,43,172,'#a18158');crate(ctx,75,172,'#876b4f');
          for(let i=0;i<3;i++)box(ctx,'#bb966b',76,198+i*4,40,3);
          if(isLodged) {
            box(ctx,'#534438',left-102,bottom-74,56,28);
            box(ctx,'#a1805e',left-102,bottom-75,56,5);
            box(ctx,'#63766c',left-94,bottom-69,43,18);
            box(ctx,'#d6c2a1',left-92,bottom-67,13,12);
            box(ctx,'#a6a696',left-73,bottom-56,18,2);
          }
        }
      } else if(kind==='arven-home') {
        box(ctx,'#534b3f',213,26,24,37);
        box(ctx,'#a58e66',215,27,20,21);
        box(ctx,'#c6b38a',221,50,9,11);
        box(ctx,'#6d4a39',224,54,2,6);
        for(const [x,y] of [[70,127],[81,133],[103,130]]) {
          box(ctx,'#dac094',x,y,12,7);box(ctx,'#896752',x+2,y+3,7,1);
        }
      } else {
        for(let i=0;i<6;i++) {
          const x=198+i*12,y=32+(i%3)*4;
          line(ctx,'#937551',x,24,x,y,1);
          box(ctx,i%2?'#88925d':'#a29b64',x-2,y,5,8);
        }
        for(let i=0;i<4;i++) {
          box(ctx,'#7e9a8c',130+i*12,93,6,9);
          box(ctx,'#c0a87d',131+i*12,90,4,3);
        }
      }
    });
    if(kind==='ruined-warehouse'&&isLodged&&!room.valleyCotCollision) {
      const k=room.k;
      room.valleyCotCollision={x:20000+(w*T-102)*k,y:(h*T-74)*k,w:56*k,h:28*k};
      room.rects.push(room.valleyCotCollision);
    }
    if(room.valleyWear)room.valleyWear.image.setTexture(tex);
    else {
      const image=scene.add.image(20000,0,tex).setOrigin(0).setScale(room.k).setDepth(2.7);
      room.valleyWear={image};
      room.objs.push(image);
    }
    room.valleyWear.key=key;
  }


  /* V85.2 settlement sprites: composited pixel materials with state-driven silhouettes.
     Trees and buildings keep the original camera, anchor and sprite scale. */
  const developmentPal={soil:['#3c4131','#61513b','#856747','#a3885b'],wood:['#3b3930','#6d5037','#946b46','#c09a66'],stone:['#384342','#5c6861','#869188','#acafa0'],roof:['#343c38','#526c61','#729184','#98aa92']};
  const devBlock=(ctx,p,x,y,w,h,rnd)=>{
    box(ctx,p[0],x,y,w,h);box(ctx,p[1],x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);box(ctx,p[2],x+1,y+1,w-2,2);box(ctx,p[3],x+1,y+1,Math.max(2,w*.35),1);
    for(let i=0;i<Math.max(1,w*h/125);i++)box(ctx,p[i%2?2:0],x+2+rnd()*Math.max(1,w-4),y+3+rnd()*Math.max(1,h-5),Math.min(4,w-3),1);
  };
  function devBoards(ctx,x,y,w,h,rnd){
    box(ctx,developmentPal.wood[0],x,y,w,h);
    for(let xx=x+1;xx<x+w-1;xx+=7){const width=Math.min(6,x+w-xx-1);devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.wood,xx,y+1,width,h-2,rnd);}
  }
  function devWall(ctx,x,y,w,h,rnd){
    box(ctx,developmentPal.stone[0],x,y,w,h);
    for(let yy=y+1,row=0;yy<y+h-1;yy+=7,row++)for(let xx=x+1-(row%2)*5;xx<x+w-1;xx+=12){const left=Math.max(x+1,xx),right=Math.min(x+w-1,xx+11);if(right-left>2)devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.stone,left,yy,right-left,Math.min(6,y+h-yy-1),rnd);}
  }
  function devRoof(ctx,x,y,w,h,rnd,palette=developmentPal.roof){
    const tip=x+w*.52,front=y+h*.72;
    shape(ctx,palette[0],[[x-4,front],[tip,y-5],[x+w+4,front],[x+w-1,y+h],[x-1,y+h]]);
    shape(ctx,palette[1],[[x,front-1],[tip,y],[x+w,front-1],[x+w-3,y+h-3],[x+3,y+h-3]]);
    for(let row=0;row<5;row++){
      const v=(row+1)/5,yy=y+(h-3)*v,left=tip-(tip-x)*v,right=tip+(x+w-tip)*v;
      line(ctx,palette[0],left,yy,right,yy,2);
      for(let xx=left+2+(row%2)*3;xx<right-3;xx+=8){line(ctx,palette[2],xx,yy-2,Math.min(right-1,xx+5),yy-2,1);line(ctx,palette[0],xx,yy-5,xx-1,yy,1);}
    }
    line(ctx,palette[3],x+2,front-1,tip,y,2);line(ctx,palette[2],tip,y,x+w-1,front-1,1);
    line(ctx,palette[0],x-2,y+h-1,x+w+1,y+h-1,3);
  }
  function plotSprite(ctx,plot){
    const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,stage=D.cropStage(plot),rnd=random(932+stage*23+(plot.crop||'').length*72),wet=plot.moisture>60;
    shadow(ctx,48,53,40,11);
    shape(ctx,'#303d2e',[[10,17],[83,17],[94,53],[6,53]]);shape(ctx,wet?'#4e4635':'#745838',[[13,19],[80,19],[90,50],[9,50]]);
    for(let row=0;row<3;row++){
      const y=23+row*9;
      line(ctx,'#403c2e',13-row*2,y+5,82+row*2,y+5,2);
      for(let col=0;col<11;col++){const x=15+col*6+(rnd()-.5)*3;box(ctx,wet?'#817158':'#a08354',x,y+rnd()*4,3+rnd()*2,1);}
      if(stage)for(let col=0;col<5;col++){
        const x=20+col*13+(rnd()-.5)*2,base=y+5,hh=[0,2,5,10,15,20][stage];
        if(stage===1){box(ctx,'#c5ac78',x,base,2,2);continue;}
        if(plot.crop==='wheat'){
          for(let k=-1;k<=1;k++){line(ctx,'#394c31',x+k*2,base,x+k*3,base-hh,1);line(ctx,stage>=4?'#bcb56d':'#759b61',x+k*2+1,base,x+k*3+1,base-hh+1,1);if(stage>=4){box(ctx,'#ddbd68',x+k*3-1,base-hh,3,5);box(ctx,'#f0d38a',x+k*3-1,base-hh,1,3);}}
        }else if(plot.crop==='carrot'){
          for(const dx of [-5,-2,2,5]){line(ctx,'#365139',x,base,x+dx,base-hh*.7,2);line(ctx,'#8ba861',x,base-2,x+dx,base-hh*.7,1);}
          if(stage>=4){box(ctx,'#894b32',x-2,base-2,5,3);box(ctx,'#d9924c',x-1,base-2,3,2);}
        }else if(plot.crop==='potato'){
          for(const [dx,dy] of [[-4,-3],[4,-4],[0,-7]]){const size=stage>=4?5:stage>=3?4:2;mass(ctx,x+dx*hh/16,base+dy*hh/12,size,['#39533a','#59764b','#688b4d','#8eaa63','#b8c37f'],rnd);}
          if(stage===5){box(ctx,'#a39a90',x+3,base-hh+3,3,2);box(ctx,'#c4afca',x+2,base-hh+2,2,2);}
        }else{
          line(ctx,'#356549',x,base,x,base-hh*.8,1);
          for(let k=0;k<Math.min(stage,4);k++){const yy=base-3-k*3;shape(ctx,'#3a6f52',[[x,yy],[x-6,yy-4],[x-4,yy-6],[x,yy-3],[x+5,yy-6],[x+7,yy-3]]);box(ctx,'#8ac493',x-4,yy-4,3,1);box(ctx,'#a7cc9d',x+2,yy-4,3,1);}
        }
      }
    }
    for(let x=11;x<91;x+=6){box(ctx,'#a28b60',x,51,5,3);box(ctx,'#594b36',x,54,5,2);}
    if(Object.values(plot.output).some(n=>n>0)){devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.wood,80,38,13,11,rnd);box(ctx,'#dbaa63',82,39,9,3);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)box(ctx,D.crops[plot.crop]?.color||'#a6bc79',83+i*3,37,3,3);}
  }
  function workshopSprite(ctx,a){
    const rng=random(4412+a.level),p=developmentPal.wood,level=a.level,job=a.laborStage||'idle';
    shadow(ctx,64,103,49,10);devWall(ctx,14,86,100,17,rng);
    devBoards(ctx,24,43,80,44,rng);
    for(const x of [24,96]){devBlock(ctx,p,x,40,8,53,rng);box(ctx,'#b09266',x+1,44,2,35);}
    // Front bench, storage shelf and tool silhouettes.
    devBoards(ctx,32,58,64,10,rng);devBlock(ctx,p,32,66,6,24,rng);devBlock(ctx,p,88,66,6,24,rng);
    devBlock(ctx,p,30,49,68,5,rng);
    for(let k=0;k<5;k++){const x=38+k*11;line(ctx,'#272f2d',x,52,x+1,40,2);box(ctx,'#9fa69e',x-3,38,7,3);box(ctx,'#b18b56',x+1,55,2,5);}
    if(level>=2){devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.stone,19,73,15,17,rng);devBlock(ctx,p,19,69,18,6,rng);}
    if(level>=3){devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.stone,94,55,13,21,rng);box(ctx,'#24322f',96,57,9,6);box(ctx,'#d5b17b',98,58,3,3);}
    if(level>=4){line(ctx,'#535e5d',100,45,110,37,4);devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.stone,105,21,10,27,rng);}
    if(level>=5){box(ctx,'#283c3a',50,42,19,12);box(ctx,'#8da6a0',52,44,15,8);line(ctx,'#c2d4cc',55,47,64,47,1);}
    const pp=level>3?['#35413f','#64756c','#93a394','#b0b8a0']:level>1?['#40352c','#805b44','#a57b55','#cfa276']:developmentPal.roof;
    devRoof(ctx,13,12,102,40,rng,pp);
    // Carved fascia, timber joints, bench front and warm brass lighting; original 2.5D footprint.
    box(ctx,'#2f362d',14,43,100,5);box(ctx,'#a99a70',16,43,96,1);box(ctx,'#756345',18,46,92,2);
    for(let x=24;x<=96;x+=12){box(ctx,'#473a29',x,44,3,4);box(ctx,'#c4ab78',x,44,2,1);}
    for(const x of [25,96]){box(ctx,'#9b8059',x,49,2,35);box(ctx,'#3b3026',x,84,6,7);box(ctx,'#b09061',x,84,5,1);}
    for(let x=35;x<94;x+=9){box(ctx,'#372e22',x,65,4,3);box(ctx,'#c2a477',x,65,3,1);}
    // A small carved shop sign and apron of stone chips that remain outside walkable anchors.
    devBlock(ctx,p,48,46,30,9,rng);box(ctx,'#dec28a',52,48,22,1);line(ctx,'#d6bb81',55,51,60,51,1);line(ctx,'#ac945f',63,51,71,51,1);
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const x=19+i*13;box(ctx,'#575241',x,99+(i%2),4,1);box(ctx,'#a09170',x,98+(i%2),3,1);}
    // Display the level as physical brass studs rather than changing the original sprite form.
    devBlock(ctx,p,40,51,47,7,rng);for(let i=0;i<level;i++)box(ctx,'#e1bd76',47+i*8,53,4,3);
    const n=Object.values(a.output).reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0),piles=Math.min(4,Math.ceil(n/20));
    for(let i=0;i<piles;i++){const x=96+(i%2)*12,y=101-Math.floor(i/2)*10;devBlock(ctx,p,x,y-8,14,10,rng);box(ctx,'#3b5140',x+3,y-7,8,2);}
    // Input bundles represent reserved stock, not decoration that implies free materials.
    if(a.queue.length){for(let i=0;i<Math.min(3,a.queue.length);i++){const x=12+i*7,y=96-i*3;devBlock(ctx,p,x,y-6,15,5,rng);box(ctx,'#d2b27a',x+1,y-5,12,1);box(ctx,'#59442c',x+5,y-6,2,5);}}
    if(job==='working'){box(ctx,'#533c25',90,60,7,7);box(ctx,'#edb858',91,59,5,5);box(ctx,'#f4d78b',92,59,3,3);box(ctx,'#f2cf8c',38,57,24,2);box(ctx,'#a67540',38,59,24,3);box(ctx,'#583f2a',39,62,23,1);}
    else if(job==='blocked'){box(ctx,'#e9ab77',89,56,4,8);box(ctx,'#e9ab77',89,66,4,2);}
    else if(job==='reserved'){box(ctx,'#c6b18a',88,58,8,6);box(ctx,'#745e46',90,60,4,1);box(ctx,'#745e46',90,62,3,1);}
    else if(job!=='idle'){box(ctx,'#a2bbac',91,59,4,5);box(ctx,'#d3ddc5',92,60,2,2);}
  }
  function constructionSprite(ctx,b){
    const rnd=random(18732+b.stage),stage=b.stage,p=developmentPal.wood;
    shadow(ctx,64,103,55,10);
    shape(ctx,'#454d3a',[[11,76],[112,76],[122,103],[7,103]]);shape(ctx,'#7f7355',[[15,78],[108,78],[117,100],[11,100]]);
    for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const x=17+i*9;box(ctx,'#5c5946',x,97,5,2);box(ctx,'#ad9a71',x,96,3,1);}
    if(stage===0){
      for(const [x,y] of [[22,64],[102,64],[22,93],[102,93]]){devBlock(ctx,p,x-2,y-13,4,16,rnd);box(ctx,'#dbc992',x-2,y-13,4,2);}
      line(ctx,'#bfb995',24,66,100,66,1);line(ctx,'#bfb995',24,68,24,92,1);line(ctx,'#bfb995',100,68,100,92,1);line(ctx,'#bfb995',24,92,100,92,1);
      shape(ctx,'#d5c6a0',[[33,80],[68,75],[84,86],[47,91]]);line(ctx,'#7d9588',46,82,70,79,1);line(ctx,'#7d9588',48,87,67,85,1);
    }
    if(stage>=1){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){devBlock(ctx,p,10+i*2,91-i*5,30,5,rnd);devBlock(ctx,developmentPal.stone,95+i*2,96-i*5,17,6,rnd);}}
    if(stage>=2){devWall(ctx,23,73,78,24,rnd);box(ctx,'#9da38c',24,73,76,3);}
    if(stage>=3){
      for(const x of [24,94])devBlock(ctx,p,x,34,8,41,rnd);
      devBlock(ctx,p,23,32,79,7,rnd);line(ctx,p[0],27,35,62,12,6);line(ctx,p[2],28,33,62,11,2);line(ctx,p[0],62,12,99,35,6);line(ctx,p[2],62,11,99,33,2);
      if(stage===3)for(const x of [41,80]){line(ctx,p[0],x,73,x+13,39,5);line(ctx,p[2],x+1,72,x+14,40,2);}
    }
    if(stage>=4){devBoards(ctx,31,39,63,34,rnd);box(ctx,'#2a3430',56,47,18,26);devBlock(ctx,p,35,48,13,13,rnd);box(ctx,'#52685f',37,50,9,8);box(ctx,'#95aa97',38,50,1,7);for(const x of [31,91])devBlock(ctx,p,x,36,5,39,rnd);}
    if(stage>=5){devRoof(ctx,13,6,102,38,rnd,['#443a35','#825e4b','#a48061','#c3a07c']);devWall(ctx,90,10,12,18,rnd);box(ctx,'#4d534a',89,8,14,3);}
    if(stage>=6){devBlock(ctx,p,57,48,16,25,rnd);box(ctx,'#d6bb79',69,59,2,3);devBlock(ctx,p,42,70,11,8,rnd);box(ctx,'#d0b275',44,69,6,3);box(ctx,'#e5c883',37,50,8,7);box(ctx,'#b6935c',41,50,1,7);}
    if(stage===7){const n=Math.min(4,Math.ceil(Object.values(b.storage).reduce((a,v)=>a+v,0)/30));for(let i=0;i<n;i++)devBlock(ctx,p,13+i%2*13,91-Math.floor(i/2)*9,12,9,rnd);devBlock(ctx,p,42,43,45,6,rnd);for(let i=0;i<5;i++)box(ctx,'#b9cb93',48+i*7,45,3,2);box(ctx,'#dfbd73',77,56,4,6);box(ctx,'#6f6744',77,63,4,3);}
    if(b.pending){for(const x of [12,113]){line(ctx,'#8d7250',x,80,x,44,3);line(ctx,'#b09b72',x-2,55,x+9,55,2);line(ctx,'#62513b',x,72,x+9,55,2);}}
  }
  // Six bespoke tall silhouettes. Only the plinth occupies the established 30x30 collider;
  // wider elements are raised eaves, foliage, or elevated machinery. Foot pixel=(78,146).
  const heritagePalette={forest:['#283a34','#526659','#7c9075','#b2bd99'],river:['#302c28','#66513c','#9e7c51','#cfb382'],ridge:['#333c40','#59666a','#899593','#c4c9b6'],marsh:['#343b2b','#666d43','#9a9c63','#c8b882'],frost:['#303f4a','#5c737e','#9cbbc0','#e0e6da'],glade:['#34453f','#607b66','#8eac85','#c2d3a8']};
  function heritageSprite(ctx,l,record,frame){
    const p=heritagePalette[l.style],rnd=random(l.x*131+l.y*379),cx=78,base=146,solved=!!record?.solved;
    const wave=Math.sin(frame*Math.PI/4),swing=Math.round(wave*3);
    const r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),poly=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts),ln=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,x,y,a,b,w);
    const grain=(x,y,w,h,count,colors)=>{for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const xx=x+Math.floor(rnd()*w),yy=y+Math.floor(rnd()*h);r(pick(colors,rnd),xx,yy,1+Math.floor(rnd()*3),1);}};
    function masonry(x,y,w,h,side=7){
      r(p[0],x-1,y-1,w+2,h+2);r(p[1],x,y,w,h);poly(p[0],[[x+w,y],[x+w+side,y-4],[x+w+side,y+h-4],[x+w,y+h]]);
      for(let yy=y;yy<y+h;yy+=7){const off=(Math.floor((yy-y)/7)%2)*5;ln(p[0],x,yy,x+w,yy);for(let xx=x-off;xx<x+w;xx+=10){const a=Math.max(x,xx+1),b=Math.min(x+w,xx+9);if(b>a){r(pick([p[1],p[2],p[1]],rnd),a,yy+1,b-a,Math.min(5,h-(yy-y)-1));r(p[2],a,yy+1,Math.min(4,b-a),1);}}}r(p[3],x,y,1,h);grain(x+2,y+2,w-3,h-3,Math.round(w*h/100),[p[2],p[1]]);
    }
    function oak(x,y,w,h){r('#352e25',x,y,w,h);r('#735638',x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);r('#ad8755',x+1,y+1,2,h-2);for(let k=4;k<w-2;k+=4)ln('#51412e',x+k,y+3,x+k,y+h-4);grain(x+3,y+3,Math.max(1,w-6),Math.max(1,h-6),Math.max(2,h/5|0),['#96734b','#4c3b2c']);}
    function metal(x,y,w,h){r('#2f3c3d',x,y,w,h);r('#627c7b',x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);r('#a9bbae',x+1,y+1,w-2,1);r('#c0ae77',x+1,y+h-2,2,1);}
    function plinth(){shadow(ctx,cx+4,base+4,22,9);poly(p[0],[[60,137],[79,131],[97,137],[97,151],[78,157],[60,151]]);poly(p[2],[[60,137],[79,131],[97,137],[78,144]]);poly(p[1],[[60,137],[78,144],[78,155],[60,150]]);ln(p[3],61,137,78,143);ln(p[0],78,144,78,155);}
    function arch(x,y,w,h){poly(p[0],[[x,y+h],[x,y+11],[x+5,y+4],[x+w/2,y],[x+w-5,y+4],[x+w,y+11],[x+w,y+h]]);poly(p[2],[[x+2,y+h],[x+2,y+12],[x+6,y+6],[x+w/2,y+2],[x+w-6,y+6],[x+w-2,y+12],[x+w-2,y+h]]);poly('#24312e',[[x+6,y+h],[x+6,y+13],[x+w/2,y+6],[x+w-6,y+13],[x+w-6,y+h]]);for(let i=0;i<5;i++)ln(p[0],x+2+i*(w-4)/4,y+8-Math.sin(i*Math.PI/4)*6,x+5+i*(w-10)/4,y+13-Math.sin(i*Math.PI/4)*7);}
    function roof(x,y,w,h,colors){const mid=x+w*.44;poly(colors[0],[[x-2,y+h],[mid,y-2],[x+w+2,y+h-3],[x+w-4,y+h+3],[x-3,y+h+3]]);poly(colors[1],[[x,y+h-1],[mid,y],[mid+8,y+h],[x,y+h]]);poly(colors[2],[[mid,y],[x+w,y+h-3],[mid+8,y+h]]);ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y+h-1);ctx.lineTo(mid,y);ctx.lineTo(x+w,y+h-3);ctx.lineTo(mid+8,y+h);ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();for(let yy=y+4;yy<y+h;yy+=4){ln(colors[0],x,yy,x+w,yy);for(let xx=x+(yy%3)*3;xx<x+w;xx+=8){ln(colors[3],xx,yy+1,xx+4,yy+1);}}ctx.restore();ln(colors[3],x,y+h-1,mid,y,1);ln(colors[0],x,y+h+2,x+w,y+h,2);}
    function panel(x,y,w,h,light=false){r('#2a302c',x,y,w,h);r('#947855',x+1,y+1,w-2,h-2);r(light?'#e6c584':'#435e59',x+3,y+3,w-6,h-6);ln('#463d31',x+w/2,y+2,x+w/2,y+h-2);ln('#463d31',x+2,y+h/2,x+w-2,y+h/2);r('#d1b68a',x+1,y+h-1,w-2,1);}
    function glyph(x,y,c){ln(c,x-3,y,x,y-4);ln(c,x,y-4,x+3,y);ln(c,x+3,y,x,y+4);ln(c,x,y+4,x-3,y);r(c,x,y,1,1);}
    function vine(x,y,len){for(let j=0;j<len;j+=3){let xx=x+Math.round(Math.sin(j*.35+frame*Math.PI/4)*2);r('#304c37',xx,y+j,2,3);r(j%2?'#78975d':'#a2b678',xx+(j%2?2:-3),y+j,3,2);}}
    function lantern(x,y){r('#302e27',x-2,y-3,5,2);r('#453829',x-3,y-1,7,8);r('#a38148',x-2,y,5,6);r(solved?'#f5d58a':'#a89e70',x-1,y+1,3,4);r('#3b3227',x-3,y+7,7,2);}
    function cloth(x,y,color){const d=Math.round(wave*3);poly('#302f29',[[x,y],[x+12,y],[x+12+d,y+25],[x+6+d,y+22],[x+d,y+26]]);poly(color,[[x+1,y+1],[x+11,y+1],[x+11+d,y+23],[x+6+d,y+20],[x+1+d,y+24]]);ln('#dfca90',x+2,y+2,x+2+d,y+21);glyph(x+6+d/2,y+11,'#d9cf9b');}
    plinth();
    if(l.style==='forest'){
      // Rootbound archive: high stone arch, carved tablet under a living root canopy.
      masonry(63,87,27,50,5);masonry(60,63,8,75,4);masonry(87,62,8,75,4);arch(64,64,26,52);
      poly('#494435',[[56,108],[59,69],[52,50],[61,35],[78,25],[94,33],[103,54],[97,79],[99,108],[92,113],[91,70],[78,46],[64,66],[64,111]]);
      for(const o of [0,4,8]){ln(o===4?'#a08d67':'#6e6046',60+o,99,63+o,65,2);ln('#8d7954',92-o,96,91-o,65,2);}
      for(const [x,y,rad] of [[43,49,17],[56,30,20],[79,22,23],[101,34,21],[112,53,15]])mass(ctx,x,y,rad,['#293f31','#425f40','#64834d','#8eaa68','#bcc78a'],rnd);
      masonry(68,94,18,29,3);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){r('#bfcbab',71,99+i*5,12-(i%2)*3,1);r('#4a6251',73,101+i*5,8,1);}glyph(77,90,solved?'#f3dc9d':'#9da77e');vine(61,53,37);vine(93,56,29);
      lantern(63,116);lantern(91,116);
    }else if(l.style==='river'){
      // Elevated weighhouse, half timber gallery, balanced hanging scales and pennant.
      for(const x of [64,84])oak(x,111,7,27);masonry(62,128,30,12,4);oak(64,68,28,45);r('#d1bf91',67,73,22,25);panel(70,75,16,20);oak(63,66,5,48);oak(88,66,5,48);ln('#493b2e',66,99,88,75,3);ln('#493b2e',66,76,88,99,3);
      roof(36,39,85,34,['#303f3b','#567b70','#3d5c54','#9db69a']);oak(57,72,4,29);oak(97,72,4,29);oak(52,99,51,7);for(let i=0;i<8;i++)r('#a78a58',54+i*6,100,4,1);
      oak(76,104,4,20);const swing=Math.round(wave*3);ln('#bbaf81',63,111+swing,96,111-swing,2);for(const [x,y] of [[63,111+swing],[96,111-swing]]){ln('#d0c5a0',x,y,x-5,y+9);ln('#d0c5a0',x,y,x+5,y+9);poly('#8f805e',[[x-7,y+9],[x+7,y+9],[x+4,y+13],[x-4,y+13]]);ln('#dacda6',x-6,y+9,x+6,y+9);}
      cloth(91,74,'#537d72');lantern(53,86);lantern(101,87);
    }else if(l.style==='ridge'){
      // Survey tower: buttressed base, engraved shaft, rotating brass wind vane.
      masonry(63,86,26,52,6);masonry(68,47,17,43,5);masonry(60,112,8,29,3);masonry(88,112,7,29,3);arch(69,103,17,33);masonry(62,78,29,8,5);masonry(65,43,23,7,5);
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++){glyph(76,54+k*7,'#d0c499');r('#9da99c',82,54+k*7,2,4);}metal(69,28,19,18);panel(71,30,15,14,solved);roof(60,16,36,14,['#303d40','#748d89','#526765','#bec6ad']);ln('#c2ad79',78,8,78,23,2);const d=Math.round(Math.cos(frame*Math.PI/4)*9);ln('#bca36f',78-d,12,78+d,12,2);poly('#d6c492',[[78+d,12],[78+d*.5,8],[78+d*.5,16]]);r('#cfb77b',77,11,3,3);
      lantern(65,103);lantern(91,103);
    }else if(l.style==='marsh'){
      // Raised reed archive, woven walls, overhanging thatch and fluttering manuscript pennant.
      for(const x of [62,86])oak(x,109,8,29);oak(62,103,31,8);oak(64,69,28,35);r('#b6ac76',67,73,22,28);for(let y=74;y<101;y+=4){ln('#766e46',67,y,89,y);for(let x=69;x<89;x+=5)r('#d5c28a',x+(y%3),y+1,2,2);}panel(70,78,16,20);oak(63,72,4,34);oak(88,72,4,34);
      roof(35,35,86,39,['#3f4632','#858951','#646e3e','#b8b477']);for(let i=0;i<13;i++){let x=49+i*4;ln('#b7a76c',x,68,x-1,74+i%3,1);}oak(48,73,5,22);oak(104,73,5,22);cloth(91,77,'#a88d4f');lantern(52,87);lantern(102,87);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){r('#d7c18b',70+i*4,114,3,7);r('#776041',70+i*4,116,3,1);}
    }else if(l.style==='frost'){
      // Observatory with ribbed dome and geared bronze telescope on a narrow tower.
      masonry(63,91,29,47,6);arch(69,112,18,26);masonry(61,77,34,16,5);for(const x of [65,87])panel(x,80,7,11,solved);
      poly('#344b57',[[49,78],[51,61],[58,48],[69,40],[81,37],[94,43],[103,55],[108,76],[99,82],[57,82]]);poly('#91b0b9',[[52,75],[55,61],[62,49],[72,43],[80,40],[85,44],[87,77]]);poly('#5b7a88',[[80,40],[95,47],[104,62],[106,76],[87,79]]);
      for(const [x1,y1,x2,y2] of [[58,60,60,78],[68,47,72,79],[81,42,87,79],[94,50,99,78]]){ln('#354d5a',x1,y1,x2,y2,2);ln('#b3cbd0',x1-1,y1,x2-1,y2,1);}poly('#dce7df',[[52,62],[60,49],[72,42],[80,38],[76,44],[68,48],[63,60]]);r('#dce5df',54,77,20,3);r('#b4ced0',89,78,15,2);
      metal(75,49,13,20);const d=Math.round(wave*3);ln('#313b3b',78,59,101,32+d,8);ln('#b3a16f',78,58,101,31+d,5);ln('#e4d0a0',78,56,101,29+d,1);metal(98,26+d,10,10);r('#94b8c4',104,29+d,2,5);lantern(65,105);lantern(92,105);
    }else{
      // Ancient memorial: layered carved stone, suspended crystal and a living crown of vines.
      masonry(62,105,32,33,5);masonry(60,64,9,70,4);masonry(87,64,9,70,4);arch(64,62,28,55);masonry(59,52,36,12,5);for(let i=0;i<5;i++)glyph(64+i*6,57,'#c6d0a1');
      poly('#394c42',[[67,50],[72,34],[78,26],[85,34],[90,50]]);poly('#98ad88',[[70,49],[75,35],[78,30],[78,49]]);ln('#d4dbb2',72,38,78,30);const dy=Math.round(-1.5+1.5*Math.cos(frame*Math.PI/4));poly('#2d4942',[[78,69+dy],[86,81+dy],[78,99+dy],[70,81+dy]]);poly(solved?'#bce8c8':'#87b6a5',[[78,71+dy],[83,81+dy],[78,94+dy],[73,81+dy]]);poly('#e1f0d5',[[78,72+dy],[78,85+dy],[74,81+dy]]);vine(60,53,39);vine(94,53,35);for(const [x,y] of [[61,64],[91,62]])mass(ctx,x,y,9,['#354e3d','#517a50','#80a66d','#acc087','#d0d1a1'],rnd);lantern(63,116);lantern(92,116);
    }
    // Cache is in the existing solid plinth rather than in the walking lane.
    const loot=record&&Object.values(record.loot||{}).some(n=>n>0);r('#302e27',69,130,17,10);r('#74573b',70,131,15,8);r('#b19564',70,131,15,2);r('#d6b46f',77,133,2,4);if(solved){r('#262f29',70,129,15,3);r('#99794e',70,125,15,3);if(loot)r('#d1cb91',73,130,9,2);}
  }
  // Eight cached poses per site/state. Change texture only when the local animation frame changes.
  // Paint the original bespoke architectural silhouette in completed layers. Interim
  // masonry, bracing, rubble and equipment are drawn as pixel clusters, not HUD bars.
  function restorationSprite(ctx,l,record,stage,branch,frame){
    const p=heritagePalette[l.style],rnd=random(l.x*129+l.y*417),r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),ln=(c,x,y,a,b,w=1)=>line(ctx,c,x,y,a,b,w),poly=(c,pts)=>shape(ctx,c,pts);
    if(stage>=7){heritageSprite(ctx,l,{...record,solved:stage>=8},stage>=8?frame:0);}
    else{
      // The old plinth and surviving shut structure are preserved; silhouette rebuilt upward.
      const cut=[109,104,117,120,91,63,33][stage];ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,164);ctx.lineTo(0,cut+9);for(let x=0;x<=156;x+=6)ctx.lineTo(x,cut+Math.floor(Math.sin(x*.23+l.x)*4));ctx.lineTo(156,164);ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();heritageSprite(ctx,l,{...record,solved:false},0);ctx.restore();
      if(stage<=2){for(let j=0;j<(stage===2?5:13);j++){const x=58+Math.floor(rnd()*43),y=124+Math.floor(rnd()*17),w=3+Math.floor(rnd()*7);poly(p[0],[[x,y],[x+w-1,y-3],[x+w+2,y+1],[x+w,y+5],[x,y+4]]);poly(p[2],[[x,y],[x+w-1,y-3],[x+w+2,y+1],[x+3,y+2]]);r(p[1],x+1,y+2,w-1,2);if(j%3===0)r('#829361',x+1,y-1,3,1);}
        if(stage!==2)for(const x of [65,88]){ln('#483f30',x,127,x+(x===65?-6:5),85,5);ln('#8a7857',x-1,126,x+(x===65?-7:4),86,2);}
      }
      if(stage>=3){for(const x of [58,97]){ln('#3e3026',x,139,x,69,5);ln('#a17d4a',x-1,138,x-1,69,2);r('#c5a878',x-3,69,6,2);}for(const y of [91,116]){ln('#473b30',53,y,103,y,5);ln('#b09665',53,y-2,103,y-2,1);for(let x=55;x<102;x+=7)r('#745b3e',x,y-1,1,2);}ln('#8d714c',59,136,97,94,2);ln('#8d714c',59,94,97,136,2);for(let y=91;y<137;y+=6){r('#c5ad80',52,y,6,1);}ln('#62503b',51,89,51,140,2);ln('#62503b',58,89,58,140,2);}
      if(stage===1||stage===2){for(const x of [47,109]){r('#574a36',x,139,2,10);r('#d7ba79',x-1,139,4,2);}ln('#b6a479',48,143,109,143,1);}
    }
    if(stage===7){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){r('#46362a',47+i*8,138-i*2,8,7);r('#a98151',48+i*8,139-i*2,6,5);r('#d2b27b',48+i*8,139-i*2,6,1);}}
    // Unique operational equipment attached to each site's existing forecourt.
    if(stage>=8){const f=frame%8;
      if(l.style==='forest'){for(const x of [43,106]){r('#4b3b2d',x,138,12,7);r('#aa8657',x+1,139,10,5);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){r('#496c40',x+2+j*3,135-j%2,2,5);r('#b0c887',x+1+j*3,134-j%2,3,1);}}}
      if(l.style==='river'){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const x=42+i*6,y=139-i*4;r('#48362a',x,y,9,8);r('#8e6b45',x+1,y+1,7,6);r('#c4a574',x+1,y+1,7,1);r('#543e2c',x+4,y+2,1,5);}}
      if(l.style==='ridge'){for(let j=0;j<3;j++){const x=103+j*4,y=140-j*3;poly(p[0],[[x,y],[x+7,y-3],[x+12,y],[x+12,y+5],[x,y+5]]);poly(p[2],[[x,y],[x+7,y-3],[x+12,y],[x+6,y+2]]);r(p[1],x+1,y+2,10,3);}}
      if(l.style==='marsh'){ln('#695338',44,125,44,145,2);ln('#695338',57,125,57,145,2);ln('#b99c69',44,128,57,128,1);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const x=45+i*3,dy=Math.round(Math.sin(f*Math.PI/4+i));ln('#799b54',x,129,x+dy,138,2);r('#b6b875',x+dy,135,2,2);}}
      if(l.style==='frost'){ln('#8b9d9c',108,121,108,140,2);ln('#879c9b',103,132,113,132,1);const d=Math.round(Math.cos(f*Math.PI/4)*5);ln('#d7bb86',108-d,121,108+d,121,2);r('#607c8c',105,128,6,3);}
      if(l.style==='glade'){for(const x of [44,108]){r('#494e3e',x,134,10,11);r('#a4a98c',x+1,134,8,2);r('#657e6b',x+2,136,6,6);r('#c8e5b5',x+4,137,2,4);}}
      if(stage===9){
        // Each specialization gets a distinct visible piece of operational apparatus.
        const c=branch===0?'#d5b775':'#8db7bd';ln('#503e2d',113,110,113,142,2);poly(c,[[114,110],[126,111+Math.round(Math.sin(f*.78)*2)],[122,116],[126,121],[114,120]]);ln('#f0dbac',115,111,115,119,1);
        const sack=(x,y,col)=>{poly('#514335',[[x+2,y],[x+7,y],[x+9,y+3],[x+10,y+8],[x+8,y+10],[x,y+9],[x-1,y+5]]);poly(col,[[x+2,y+1],[x+6,y+1],[x+8,y+4],[x+8,y+8],[x+1,y+8],[x+1,y+4]]);r('#d3b98a',x+2,y+2,5,1);r('#8b7455',x+5,y+5,1,3);};
        const board=(x,y,w)=>{poly('#46372b',[[x,y],[x+w,y],[x+w+4,y-3],[x+w+4,y+1],[x+w,y+4],[x,y+4]]);r('#b48e5a',x+1,y+1,w-1,2);ln('#d2b485',x,y,x+w,y,1);};
        if(l.style==='forest'){if(branch===0){for(let j=0;j<5;j++){let x=49+j*3;ln('#385541',x,141,x-1,133,1);r('#b0c18c',x-2,133+j%2,3,2);r('#d9c5a8',x,132+j%2,1,1);}}else{sack(43,134,'#b6a16b');sack(51,138,'#d0b981');}}
        else if(l.style==='river'){if(branch===0){for(let j=0;j<4;j++)board(103,143-j*3,15);}else{sack(42,133,'#bda775');sack(50,138,'#d1bc8a');}}
        else if(l.style==='ridge'){if(branch===0){for(let j=0;j<3;j++){r(p[0],46+j*5,138-j*3,11,6);r(p[2],47+j*5,139-j*3,9,2);}}else{for(let j=0;j<4;j++)board(44,143-j*3,16);}}
        else if(l.style==='marsh'){if(branch===0){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){ln('#ad9b64',101+j*3,129,101+j*3,142,1);r('#708c54',101+j*3,135,2,4);}}else{for(let j=0;j<3;j++){r('#564935',101+j*5,138,5,5);r('#a7c18c',103+j*5,134-j%2,2,4);}}}
        else if(l.style==='frost'){if(branch===0){r('#344c55',43,133,13,10);r('#c3d1ce',44,134,11,8);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)r('#637c85',45+j*3,135+j,2,3);}else{ln('#6e6550',49,144,49,128,2);poly('#535f62',[[43,130],[48,124],[56,127],[55,134],[48,138],[43,135]]);ln('#c3c8bc',46,127,53,133,1);r('#d6bf86',49,130,2,2);}}
        else if(branch===0){for(let j=0;j<3;j++)board(43,142-j*3,11);r('#94a993',45,129,8,4);}else{r('#6b5744',43,128,12,15);r('#b39566',44,130,10,11);r('#4b6158',46,132,6,7);r('#d9dfbf',47,133,4,3);}
      }
    }
  }

  function heritageFrame(scene,t,record,phase,stage=0,branch=null){const f=Math.max(0,Math.min(7,phase|0)),sig=[t.id,stage,branch,record?.solved?'solved':'intact',record&&Object.values(record.loot||{}).some(n=>n>0)?'full':'empty',f].join('-');return texture(scene,'heritage-'+sig,156,164,ctx=>restorationSprite(ctx,t,record,stage,branch,stage<8?0:f));}
  function heritageMotion(scene,view,world,t,reduced,quality){
    let n=0,changed=0;const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView;
    for(const [id,info] of view.developmentObjects||[]){const target=D.byId[id];if(target?.kind!=='landmark')continue;const s=info.sprite;if(s.x<rect.x-160||s.x>rect.right+160||s.y<rect.y-160||s.y>rect.bottom+160)continue;
      const motion=target.style==='frost'?4:target.style==='ridge'?5:6;const raw=Math.floor(t*motion+target.x*.17)%8;const phase=reduced?0:quality.ambient<6?raw-(raw%2):raw;
      const record=world.development.exploration[id],hs=D.heritageState(world,id),key=heritageFrame(scene,target,record,hs.stage>=8&&(!['river','frost'].includes(target.style)||(view.heritageWork?.heritage.id===id||D.guildPose(world,id)?.work))?phase:0,hs.stage,hs.branch);if(s.texture.key!==key){s.setTexture(key);changed++;}
      const player=scene.player,behind=player.y<s.y+10&&player.y>s.y-120&&Math.abs(player.x-s.x)<33;s.setAlpha(behind?.66:1);
      if(!reduced&&world.weather.kind!=='rain'&&(target.style==='forest'||target.style==='glade')){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const f=(t*.22+j*.25+target.x*.03)%1;const xx=s.x-24+f*48+Math.sin(f*7+j)*4,yy=s.y-100+f*55;view.effects.fillStyle(j%2?0xa5b87b:0xd4c88b,.5*Math.sin(f*Math.PI)).fillRect(Math.round(xx),Math.round(yy),2,1);}}
      // Subtle localized warm light varies with daylight; never simulate rewards or world changes.
      const night=world.clock.minuteOfDay>=1130||world.clock.minuteOfDay<390,active=hs.stage>=8;
      if(night&&active&&D.roomState(world,id).visits>0&&D.roomState(world,id).lamp){const y=s.y-(target.style==='ridge'?43:target.style==='river'?60:target.style==='marsh'?59:target.style==='frost'?41:30),f=reduced?1:.86+.14*Math.sin(t*5+target.x);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)view.light.fillStyle(0xf2cb86,.025*f).fillEllipse(s.x,y,44-j*10,34-j*8);}
      const work=view.heritageWork;if(work?.heritage.id===id&&!reduced){const progress=work.elapsed/work.duration,g=view.effects,service=work.heritage.service,colors=service?{forest:0xadc98a,river:0xd5b57b,ridge:0xc2bfad,marsh:0x98ba7d,frost:0xacc8d6,glade:0xd9d0a4}:{};if(progress>.24&&progress<.92){const count=quality.ambient>6?12:5;for(let k=0;k<count;k++){const a=(t*1.6+k/count)%1,x=s.x+(k%5-2)*4+Math.sin(k*5.1)*a*15,y=s.y-15-(a*23)+(k%3)*3;g.fillStyle(colors[target.style]||0xbfae91,(1-a)*.65).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),k%3===0?3:2,2);}}}
      n++;
    }return {visible:n,changed};
  }

  function developmentObjects(view,world){
    const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,scene=view.scene;
    if(!D||!world?.development||!scene.player)return;
    const active=view.developmentObjects??=new Map(),rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView;
    for(const t of D.targets){
      const x=(t.x+.5)*T,y=(t.y+.5)*T,land=t.kind==='landmark',px=land?x+39:x,py=land?y-20:y;
      const visible=!scene.inside&&px>rect.x-400&&px<rect.right+400&&py>rect.y-400&&py<rect.bottom+400;
      let info=active.get(t.id);
      if(!visible){if(info){const at=scene.areaObjs.indexOf(info.sprite);if(at>=0)scene.areaObjs.splice(at,1);info.sprite.destroy();active.delete(t.id);}continue;}
      const state=t.kind==='workshop'?{...world.development.workshop,laborStage:D.task(world,world.development.workshop.queue[0]?.id)?.stage||'idle'}:t.kind==='build'?world.development.build:t.kind==='farm'?world.development.farm[t.id]:world.development.exploration[t.id];
      const Dstage=t.kind==='farm'?D.cropStage(state):0;
      const sig=t.kind==='workshop'?[state.level,['working','blocked','reserved','idle'].includes(state.laborStage)?state.laborStage:'travel',Math.min(3,state.queue.length),Math.ceil(Object.values(state.output).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/20)].join('-'):t.kind==='build'?[state.stage,state.pending?'working':'idle',Math.ceil(Object.values(state.storage).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/30)].join('-'):t.kind==='farm'?[state.crop||'empty',Dstage,state.moisture>60?'wet':'dry',Object.values(state.output).some(n=>n>0)?'full':'empty'].join('-'):[t.id,D.heritageState(world,t.id).stage,D.heritageState(world,t.id).branch,state?.solved?'solved':'intact',state&&Object.values(state.loot).some(n=>n>0)?'full':'empty'].join('-');
      if(info?.sig===sig)continue;
      const w=t.kind==='farm'?96:land?156:128,h=t.kind==='farm'?64:land?164:116,foot=t.kind==='farm'?55:land?146:105;
      const painter=ctx=>t.kind==='workshop'?workshopSprite(ctx,state):t.kind==='build'?constructionSprite(ctx,state):t.kind==='farm'?plotSprite(ctx,state):heritageSprite(ctx,t,state,0);
      const key=land?heritageFrame(scene,t,state,0,D.heritageState(world,t.id).stage,D.heritageState(world,t.id).branch):texture(scene,'v852-'+t.kind+'-'+sig,w,h,painter);
      if(info){info.sprite.setTexture(key);info.sig=sig;}else{const sprite=scene.add.image(px,py,key).setOrigin(.5,foot/h).setDepth(t.kind==='farm'?2:view.depthAt(py+(land?10:0)));scene.areaObjs.push(sprite);active.set(t.id,{sprite,sig});}
    }
  }
  function releaseDevelopment(view){if(!view?.developmentObjects)return;for(const {sprite} of view.developmentObjects.values()){const at=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(sprite);if(at>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(at,1);if(sprite.scene)sprite.destroy();}view.developmentObjects.clear();}

  // Each existing expedition site receives a hand-authored, flat approach surface.
  // No new blockers: the original interaction anchor and monument collision stay intact.
  function landmarkGround(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){
    const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment;if(!D)return;
    for(const l of D.landmarks){const x=(l.x+.5)*T+39,y=(l.y+.5)*T-20;
      if(x+115<ox||x-115>ox+w||y+95<oy||y-95>oy+h)continue;
      const rng=random(l.x*173+l.y*379),rock=['#4b5148','#777c67','#9b9e81','#b5b397'];
      const slab=(px,py,ww,hh,pal=rock)=>{shape(ctx,pal[0],[[px-ww/2,py],[px-ww*.43,py-hh*.77],[px+ww*.32,py-hh],[px+ww*.5,py-hh*.36],[px+ww*.31,py+3],[px-ww*.38,py+3]]);shape(ctx,pal[1],[[px-ww*.43,py-hh*.77],[px+ww*.32,py-hh],[px+ww*.5,py-hh*.36],[px-ww*.34,py-2]]);line(ctx,pal[2],px-ww*.41,py-hh*.76,px+ww*.3,py-hh,1);if(rng()<.7)box(ctx,pal[3],px-ww*.15,py-hh*.55,4,1);};
      if(l.style==='forest'){
        // Moss-stained foundation remnants, irregular ring, inscriptions and root fragments.
        ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.77;shape(ctx,'#465343',[[x-72,y+28],[x-65,y-23],[x-33,y-43],[x+26,y-49],[x+66,y-26],[x+74,y+20],[x+47,y+35],[x-23,y+39]]);ctx.restore();
        for(let i=0;i<19;i++){if(i===3||i===4||i===13)continue;const a=i*Math.PI*2/19,px=x+Math.cos(a)*62,py=y+Math.sin(a)*31;slab(px,py,12+rng()*12,5+rng()*5);if(i%2===0)box(ctx,'#779355',px-4,py-3,6,2);}
        for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const px=x-48+i*10;slab(px,y+43+i%2*3,15,5);}
      }else if(l.style==='river'){
        shape(ctx,'#8b8663',[[x-62,y+36],[x-63,y-24],[x+53,y-25],[x+64,y-9],[x+57,y+36]]);
        for(let yy=y-22;yy<y+35;yy+=7){for(let xx=x-58;xx<x+57;xx+=22){const end=Math.min(xx+20,x+56);box(ctx,'#745c40',xx,yy,end-xx,6);box(ctx,pick(['#a89060','#b79b67','#998057'],rng),xx,yy,end-xx,4);box(ctx,'#483c30',xx+2,yy+2,1,1);}}
        for(let i=0;i<8;i++)slab(x-54+i*15,y+39,17,5,['#625d4b','#9e9474','#b8ad87','#ccc09c']);
      }else if(l.style==='ridge'){
        // Old survey station paved with dark flagstones and a brass datum line.
        shape(ctx,'#515c58',[[x-57,y-31],[x+44,y-34],[x+72,y-7],[x+57,y+31],[x-40,y+35],[x-72,y+8]]);
        for(let row=0;row<5;row++)for(let col=0;col<7;col++){const xx=x-49+col*15+(row%2)*4,yy=y-25+row*12;if(rng()<.12)continue;slab(xx,yy,15,8,['#3e4d4a','#77837b','#9ca89b','#adb0a0']);}
        line(ctx,'#ba9b64',x-43,y+16,x+44,y+16,2);for(let i=0;i<7;i++)box(ctx,'#dec494',x-42+i*14,y+14,1,5);
      }else if(l.style==='marsh'){
        // Grounded boardwalk and short footpath leading to the raised archive.
        for(let yy=y-30;yy<y+53;yy+=7){const xx=x-26+Math.round(Math.sin(yy/23)*5);box(ctx,'#4d4937',xx,yy,58,7);box(ctx,pick(['#8d7951','#a18d5d','#796b4c'],rng),xx+2,yy,54,5);box(ctx,'#c2ad78',xx+2,yy,52,1);box(ctx,'#373d31',xx+6,yy+3,2,1);box(ctx,'#373d31',xx+48,yy+3,2,1);}
        for(let i=0;i<5;i++)slab(x-55+i*14,y+52,13,5,['#465748','#65795b','#8b9a71','#a8ad86']);
      }else if(l.style==='frost'){
        // Circular astronomical survey dial, weathered brass bearings and snow-free center.
        ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.86;ctx.fillStyle='#718f91';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,68,35,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
        for(let i=0;i<28;i++){const a=i*Math.PI*2/28,xx=x+Math.cos(a)*62,yy=y+Math.sin(a)*31;slab(xx,yy,11,6,['#647f82','#a3b8b6','#d7e0d5','#f0f2db']);}
        for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/6,dx=Math.cos(a),dy=Math.sin(a);line(ctx,'#c7b68a',x+dx*38,y+dy*19,x+dx*54,y+dy*27,i%3===0?2:1);}
        line(ctx,'#d6c49c',x-41,y,x+41,y,1);line(ctx,'#d6c49c',x,y-23,x,y+23,1);
      }else{
        // A broken spiral mosaic records the lost glade's ancient ornamental language.
        for(let i=0;i<46;i++){const a=i*.31,r=8+i*1.4,xx=x+Math.cos(a)*r,yy=y+Math.sin(a)*r*.55;slab(xx,yy,10+rng()*4,5,['#344c40','#6e8169','#b8c198','#d4d3a7']);if(i%6===0)box(ctx,'#c0a676',xx,yy-2,2,2);}
        for(let i=0;i<9;i++)slab(x-46+i*12,y+43,10,5);
      }
    }
  }

  // Global-coordinate environmental painter. All decoration is nonblocking low ground cover.
  const wildMaterials={
    heartwood:{base:[55,77,49],sun:[102,123,69],shade:[32,57,43],leaves:['#294632','#416543','#62854e','#91a65c','#bdc17b'],density:.80},
    deepwoods:{base:[43,74,57],sun:[89,121,79],shade:[25,49,42],leaves:['#223d33','#365f46','#52835a','#80ab74','#b4c58c'],density:.88},
    basin:{base:[106,125,68],sun:[162,166,96],shade:[65,96,53],leaves:['#425e37','#65834a','#87a453','#c0c873','#e1d98c'],density:.66},
    marsh:{base:[65,91,66],sun:[112,125,74],shade:[35,64,58],leaves:['#2d5043','#456b4a','#799058','#a9aa69','#c4bf83'],density:.84},
    ridge:{base:[104,107,90],sun:[157,151,121],shade:[63,76,69],leaves:['#455c48','#5f7550','#85946b','#b5b88a','#d6c69a'],density:.32},
    frost:{base:[170,188,181],sun:[222,230,217],shade:[106,139,145],leaves:['#425f62','#587e79','#80a19b','#bed1c5','#e0e6d5'],density:.26}
  };
  const eastArt={amberwood:{base:[103,112,65],sun:[169,161,91],shade:[61,77,44],density:.77},heath:{base:[105,99,111],sun:[161,145,157],shade:[68,65,85],density:.48},coppervale:{base:[151,105,70],sun:[205,156,101],shade:[103,73,56],density:.24},glasswater:{base:[70,107,91],sun:[132,160,122],shade:[38,75,70],density:.64},goldgrass:{base:[138,139,72],sun:[198,180,99],shade:[92,105,54],density:.48},moonbloom:{base:[58,91,87],sun:[113,143,135],shade:[32,64,69],density:.79}};
  for(const r of C.EAST_REGIONS)wildMaterials[r.id]={...eastArt[r.id],leaves:[r.palette[0],r.palette[0],r.palette[1],r.palette[2],r.palette[3]]};
  const clamp01=x=>Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));
  const smooth=x=>{x=clamp01(x);return x*x*(3-2*x);};
  const blend=(a,b,t)=>a.map((n,i)=>n+(b[i]-n)*t);
  const rgb=a=>`rgb(${a.map(Math.round).join(',')})`;
  function wildNoise(x,y,scale,salt){const gx=Math.floor(x/scale),gy=Math.floor(y/scale),u=smooth(x/scale-gx),v=smooth(y/scale-gy);const a=C.hash(gx,gy,salt),b=C.hash(gx+1,gy,salt),c=C.hash(gx,gy+1,salt),d=C.hash(gx+1,gy+1,salt);return (a+(b-a)*u)*(1-v)+(c+(d-c)*u)*v;}
  function wildPatch(ctx,x,y,kind,p,rng){
    const h=3+rng()*5;
    if(kind==='fern'){
      shape(ctx,'#203c2f',[[x-15,y+3],[x-13,y-2],[x-4,y-5],[x,y-14],[x+7,y-9],[x+15,y-2],[x+12,y+4]]);
      for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const a=-Math.PI+(.15+i*.45),len=9+rng()*10,ex=x+Math.cos(a)*len,ey=y-1+Math.sin(a)*len;line(ctx,p[1],x,y,ex,ey,2);for(let j=2;j<8;j++){const t=j/9,px=x+(ex-x)*t,py=y+(ey-y)*t,span=(1-t)*5;line(ctx,p[2+(j%2)],px,py,px+Math.sin(a)*span,py-Math.cos(a)*span,1);line(ctx,p[2],px,py,px-Math.sin(a)*span,py+Math.cos(a)*span,1);}}
    }else if(kind==='flowers'){
      for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const xx=x+(rng()-.5)*25,yy=y+(rng()-.5)*11,hh=3+rng()*7;line(ctx,p[1],xx,yy,xx+1,yy-hh,1);box(ctx,p[3],xx-2,yy-3,3,1);const t=pick(['#f1d893','#d9a4a1','#b8a0cb','#e4e5bc'],rng);box(ctx,t,xx-1,yy-hh-1,4,3);box(ctx,'#f7e6af',xx,yy-hh,1,1);}
    }else if(kind==='reed'){
      for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const xx=x+(rng()-.5)*23,hh=9+rng()*14,dx=(rng()-.5)*9;line(ctx,p[0],xx,y,xx+dx,y-hh,2);line(ctx,p[2],xx+1,y,xx+dx,y-hh,1);if(i%2)box(ctx,'#9d8b57',xx+dx-1,y-hh-2,3,5);}
    }else if(kind==='shrub'){
      const r=9+rng()*6;shadow(ctx,x+3,y+1,r+3,4);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)mass(ctx,x+(i-1.5)*r*.65,y-3-(i%2)*4,r*.65,p,rng);for(let i=0;i<6;i++)if(rng()>.5)box(ctx,'#c5a973',x+(rng()-.5)*r*2,y-3-rng()*7,2,2);
    }else if(kind==='stone'){
      const w=9+rng()*15;shadow(ctx,x+3,y+2,w*.65,4);shape(ctx,'#46524e',[[x-w/2,y],[x-w*.38,y-h],[x-2,y-h-4],[x+w*.36,y-h],[x+w*.5,y-1],[x+w*.3,y+4],[x-w*.3,y+4]]);shape(ctx,'#87928a',[[x-w*.38,y-h],[x-2,y-h-4],[x+w*.36,y-h],[x+1,y-2],[x-w*.35,y-3]]);line(ctx,'#b2b5a3',x-w*.34,y-h,x-2,y-h-4,1);line(ctx,'#59645b',x+1,y-2,x+w*.3,y+3,1);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)box(ctx,p[2],x-w*.4+rng()*w*.6,y-2+rng()*3,3,1);
    }else if(kind==='mushroom'){
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const xx=x+(rng()-.5)*14,yy=y+(rng()-.5)*7,hh=3+rng()*4;box(ctx,'#c6b895',xx,yy-hh,2,hh);shape(ctx,'#9c604a',[[xx-4,yy-hh],[xx-3,yy-hh-3],[xx+2,yy-hh-4],[xx+5,yy-hh],[xx+4,yy-hh+1],[xx-4,yy-hh+1]]);box(ctx,'#e3b284',xx-2,yy-hh-3,3,1);}
    }else if(kind==='snow'){
      for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const xx=x+(rng()-.5)*20,hh=4+rng()*7;line(ctx,p[0],xx,y,xx-2,y-hh,1);box(ctx,'#edf0dd',xx-3,y-hh,4,2);}
    }else{for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const xx=x+(rng()-.5)*19,yy=y+(rng()-.5)*7;line(ctx,p[1],xx,yy,xx+(rng()-.5)*7,yy-4-rng()*6,1);if(i%2===0)box(ctx,p[3],xx,yy-3,1,2);}}
  }
  // Continuously mixed biome fields, never a single hard region palette for ground pixels.
  const roadPal={heartwood:[159,133,92],deepwoods:[132,120,89],basin:[183,156,108],marsh:[130,119,86],ridge:[144,142,122],frost:[155,161,148]};
  Object.assign(roadPal,{amberwood:[167,145,100],heath:[139,126,129],coppervale:[167,119,85],glasswater:[119,135,110],goldgrass:[180,155,95],moonbloom:[122,131,118]});
  function mixField(weights,field){const result=[0,0,0];for(const k of Object.keys(weights)){const c=field(k),w=weights[k];for(let i=0;i<3;i++)result[i]+=w*c[i];}return result;}
  function mixedMaterial(weights){return {base:mixField(weights,k=>wildMaterials[k].base),sun:mixField(weights,k=>wildMaterials[k].sun),shade:mixField(weights,k=>wildMaterials[k].shade),density:Object.entries(weights).reduce((s,[k,w])=>s+w*wildMaterials[k].density,0)};}
  function ecoChoice(weights,r){let last='heartwood';for(const k of Object.keys(weights)){last=k;r-=weights[k];if(r<=0)return k;}return last;}
  function wildColor(x,y,weights=C.biomeWeights(x/T,y/T)){
    const m=mixedMaterial(weights),n=wildNoise(x,y,183,77)*.72+wildNoise(x,y,45,78)*.28;
    let col=n<.5?blend(m.shade,m.base,n*2):blend(m.base,m.sun,(n-.5)*2);
    // Match the retained hamlet terrain at its eastern and southern edge, then fade over the first six tiles.
    const tx=x/T,ty=y/T,edge=Math.max(tx-C.WorldConfig.baseWidth,ty-C.WorldConfig.baseHeight);
    if(edge<6){const bx=Math.min(tx,C.WorldConfig.baseWidth-.12),by=Math.min(ty,C.WorldConfig.baseHeight-.12),surface=C.groundSample(bx,by).surface;
      const legacyColors={forest:[65,91,69],meadow:[85,117,79],scrub:[86,105,84],scree:[116,123,103],moss:[85,107,76],shale:[98,103,90],riverbank:[129,121,95],water:[65,108,112]};
      col=blend(legacyColors[surface]||legacyColors.meadow,col,smooth(Math.max(0,edge)/6));}
    return col;
  }
  function roadCoverage(road,x,y){if(!road)return 0;const width=road.width*(.90+.17*wildNoise(x,y,93,407)),jitter=(wildNoise(x,y,21,408)-.5)*.16;return smooth((width+.24+jitter-road.distance)/.46);}
  function roadTint(col,road,x,y,weights){const cov=roadCoverage(road,x,y);if(cov<=0)return col;const edge=mixField(weights,k=>roadPal[k]),core=smooth((road.width*.68-road.distance)/.42),track=Math.exp(-Math.pow((road.distance-road.width*.43)/.13,2));
    const n=wildNoise(x,y,29,409),dirt=blend(edge.map(v=>v*.76),edge.map(v=>v+9),core*.8+.13*n);return blend(col,dirt.map(v=>v-track*9),cov);}
  // Fine road edges and material-specific native pixel clusters, evaluated only
  // when generating a chunk. Existing logical road centerline is untouched.
  function roadFineEdge(ctx,x,y,base,road,weights){
    if(!road||Math.abs(road.distance-road.width)>.57)return false;
    const step=ctx.__lqDetailScale===2?2:4;for(let sy=0;sy<8;sy+=step)for(let sx=0;sx<8;sx+=step){const px=x+sx+step/2,py=y+sy+step/2,r=trailVisualRoad(px/T,py/T),c=roadTint(base,r,px,py,weights),n=C.hash(px,py,44021);box(ctx,rgb(c.map(v=>v+(n-.5)*5)),x+sx,y+sy,step,step);}return true;
  }
  function roadDetails(ctx,x,y,road,weights,r){
    const core=roadCoverage(road,x+4,y+4);if(core<.65)return;
    const rocky=weights.ridge+weights.frost,wet=weights.marsh,frost=weights.frost,n=C.hash(x,y,4650),m=C.hash(x,y,4651);
    const xx=x+1+Math.floor(n*3),yy=y+1+Math.floor(m*3),mid=mixField(weights,k=>roadPal[k]);
    if(rocky>.18&&r<rocky*.63){
      // Irregular worn cobbles with a dark contact plane and a lit bevel.
      const w=3+Math.floor(n*3),h=2+Math.floor(m*2),stone=blend(mid,[122,126,116],.64+rocky*.16),shade=stone.map(v=>v-28),hi=stone.map(v=>v+24);
      const a=[[xx,yy],[xx+1,yy-1],[xx+w-1,yy-1+(n>.6?1:0)],[xx+w,yy+1],[xx+w-1,yy+h],[xx+1,yy+h+1],[xx-1,yy+h-1]];
      shape(ctx,rgb(shade),a.map(([u,v])=>[u,v+1]));shape(ctx,rgb(stone),a);line(ctx,rgb(hi),xx+1,yy-1,xx+w-1,yy-1+(n>.6?1:0),1);
      if(n>.72)box(ctx,rgb(hi.map(v=>v-10)),xx+1,yy+1,2,1);
    }else if(wet>.22&&r<wet*.38){
      box(ctx,rgb(blend(mid,[67,83,76],.5)),xx-1,yy,5,2);box(ctx,rgb(blend(mid,[151,155,128],.5)),xx,yy-1,3,1);
    }else if(r>.69){
      const grain=blend(mid,[100,90,68],.48);box(ctx,rgb(grain),xx,yy+1,2+(m>.78?1:0),1);box(ctx,rgb(mid.map(v=>v+23)),xx,yy,2,1);
      if(r>.94&&frost<.25){const leaf=weights.heartwood+weights.deepwoods>.5?'#ad925d':'#b6a279';box(ctx,leaf,xx+2,yy+3,2,1);box(ctx,'#6e6949',xx+3,yy+4,1,1);}
    }
    if(frost>.36&&n>.76){box(ctx,rgb(blend(mid,[225,234,224],frost*.65)),x+5,y+5,2,1);}
  }
  // Small authored stone forecourts and trail markers link the deck ramps to
  // ground-level paths, with softened irregular borders. No surface colliders.
  function skywayGroundFinish(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){const b=C.SKYWAY;if(ox+w<(b.left-2)*T||ox>(b.right+2)*T||oy+h<(b.y-2)*T||oy>(b.y+2)*T)return;
    for(const side of ['west','east']){const p=C.skywayAnchor(side);for(let y=Math.floor((p.y-24)/4)*4;y<p.y+24;y+=4)for(let x=Math.floor((p.x-23)/4)*4;x<p.x+23;x+=4){if(x<ox||y<oy||x>=ox+w||y>=oy+h)continue;const u=(x+2-p.x)/25,v=(y+2-p.y)/22,n=C.hash(x,y,49001),d=Math.hypot(u,v)+(n-.5)*.13,a=smooth((1.1-d)/.35);if(a<.03)continue;const weights=C.biomeWeights((x+2)/T,(y+2)/T),stone=mixField(weights,k=>roadPal[k]),c=blend(stone,[125,130,110],.55);ctx.globalAlpha=a*.85;box(ctx,rgb(c.map(q=>q+(n-.5)*18)),x,y,4,4);if((Math.floor(y/4)%3===0||Math.floor(x/4+Math.floor(y/12)%2)%4===0)&&n>.35)box(ctx,rgb(c.map(q=>q-18)),x,y+3,4,1);ctx.globalAlpha=1;}}
  }

  function bridgePixel(ctx,x,y,b,r){const px=x+4,py=y+4,tx=px/T,ty=py/T,local=py-b.from*T,w=(b.to-b.from)*T,edge=local<8||local>w-8;const end=Math.min(tx-b.left,b.right-tx);
    // Longitudinal deck: narrow north/south rails, stone abutments and worn boards over the exact open collision strip.
    const plank=Math.floor(px/8),grain=C.hash(plank,Math.floor(y/29),431);let c=edge?[93,75,57]:[142+grain*24,112+grain*19,77+grain*16];
    if(end<.68)c=blend(c,[128,132,109],smooth(1-end/.68));box(ctx,rgb(c),x,y,8,8);
    box(ctx,edge?'#4c4635':'#725638',x,y,1,8);if(!edge){box(ctx,'#c5a474',x+1,y+1,1,6);if(r>.72)box(ctx,'#a18456',x+4,y+2,1,5);}
    if(edge){const upper=local<8;box(ctx,'#45392c',x,y+(upper?5:4),8,3);box(ctx,'#bda174',x,y+1,8,2);box(ctx,'#836648',x,y+3,8,1);if(plank%4===0){box(ctx,'#433a30',x+1,y,6,8);box(ctx,'#d1b58c',x+1,y,6,2);box(ctx,'#92765a',x+2,y+2,3,4);box(ctx,'#a4a18c',x+3,y+3,1,1);}}else if(local>12&&local<w-12&&plank%4===0&&(Math.floor(local)%32<8)){box(ctx,'#615b49',x+2,y+3,2,2);box(ctx,'#beb18e',x+2,y+3,1,1);}
  }
  function legacyRoadJoin(ctx){const w=C.WorldConfig.baseWidth*T,h=C.WorldConfig.baseHeight*T;ctx.save();for(let y=0;y<h;y+=8)for(let x=0;x<w;x+=8){const tx=(x+4)/T,ty=(y+4)/T,road=trailVisualRoad(tx,ty);if(!road||road.distance>road.width+.3)continue;const [a,b]=C.shore(ty);if(tx>=a-.15&&tx<=b+.15)continue;
    // Reuse the same continuous outer road palette; fade into the existing inhabited trail at its starting points.
    const r=C.hash(x,y,411),weights=C.biomeWeights(tx,ty),fade=smooth(Math.max(tx-43,ty-38)/5);if(fade<=0)continue;
    ctx.globalAlpha=roadCoverage(road,x+4,y+4)*fade;const core=smooth((road.width*.68-road.distance)/.42),pal=mixField(weights,k=>roadPal[k]);box(ctx,rgb(blend(pal.map(v=>v*.76),pal,core)),x,y,8,8);roadDetails(ctx,x,y,road,weights,r);}
    ctx.restore();}
  // Landmark composition is world-coordinate based. No new blockers, no changes to road centerlines.
  // Make the site visually legible from a distance; provide continuous, worn transitions to the trail.
  function forecourtDistance(l,x,y){const cx=(l.x+.5)*T+18,cy=(l.y+.5)*T-5;const rx=l.style==='river'?89:l.style==='frost'?82:91,ry=l.style==='marsh'?59:53;return Math.hypot((x-cx)/rx,(y-cy)/ry);}
  function nearCourt(x,y){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment;if(!D)return 4;let near=4;for(const l of D.landmarks){if(Math.abs((l.x+.5)*T+18-x)>145||Math.abs((l.y+.5)*T-5-y)>110)continue;near=Math.min(near,forecourtDistance(l,x,y));}return near;}
  function heritageForecourt(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){
    const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment;if(!D)return;
    const floors={forest:[[70,83,65],[112,119,88],[148,145,109]],river:[[110,91,65],[153,133,92],[192,167,110]],ridge:[[69,79,77],[119,128,116],[157,162,143]],marsh:[[75,87,62],[125,125,83],[154,149,98]],frost:[[111,143,150],[157,176,175],[205,216,208]],glade:[[65,88,72],[113,135,102],[156,169,128]]};
    for(const l of D.landmarks){const cx=(l.x+.5)*T+18,cy=(l.y+.5)*T-5;if(cx+135<ox||cx-135>ox+w||cy+105<oy||cy-105>oy+h)continue;const p=floors[l.style];
      for(let y=Math.floor((cy-80)/4)*4;y<cy+80;y+=4)for(let x=Math.floor((cx-125)/4)*4;x<cx+125;x+=4){
        if(x+4<ox||x>=ox+w||y+4<oy||y>=oy+h)continue;
        const tx=(x+2)/T,ty=(y+2)/T,[a,b]=C.shore(ty);if(tx>=a-.28&&tx<=b+.28||C.landscapeBridge(tx,ty))continue;
        const d=forecourtDistance(l,x+2,y+2),j=(wildNoise(x,y,22,1702)-.5)*.18,alpha=smooth((1.18+j-d)/.33);if(alpha<.025)continue;
        const road=trailVisualRoad(tx,ty),roadCore=road&&road.distance<road.width*.66;
        // Pavement is intentionally interrupted by the actual road, which remains the dominant continuous path.
        const rx=x-cx,ry=y-cy,band=Math.floor(Math.hypot(rx,ry*1.5)/8),seed=C.hash(x,y,1701);let tone=blend(p[0],p[1],.43+.49*seed);
        const joint=l.style==='river'||l.style==='marsh'?Math.floor(y/4)%3===0:Math.floor(y/4)%3===0||(Math.floor(x/4)+(Math.floor(y/12)%2)*2)%5===0;
        if(joint)tone=blend(tone,p[0],.65);else if(seed>.80)tone=blend(tone,p[2],.45);
        if(l.style==='frost'||l.style==='glade'){if(band===5||band===9)tone=blend(tone,p[2],.48);if(Math.abs(rx)<3||Math.abs(ry)<3)tone=blend(tone,p[2],.35);}
        if(l.style==='ridge'&&Math.abs(ry-20)<3)tone=blend(tone,[184,158,106],.73);
        if(l.style==='forest'&&(band===8||band===9))tone=blend(tone,p[0],.30);if(l.style==='forest'&&seed>.73)tone=blend(tone,[86,109,66],.55);
        ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(roadCore?.22:.86);box(ctx,rgb(tone),x,y,4,4);
        if(seed>.91&&!joint){ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(roadCore?.12:.67);box(ctx,rgb(p[2]),x+1,y+1,2,1);}
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    }
  }
  function terrainFineDetail(ctx,x,y,col,r,weights,road,water){
    // Sub-pixel-size clusters remove the uniform 8x8 checker impression while retaining the original biome field.
    // Bounded: at most two tiny fills per sampled cell, no extra noise or road geometry query.
    if(water||road&&road.distance<road.width+.18)return;
    const z=C.hash(x,y,1707),sx=x+1+Math.floor(z*5),sy=y+1+Math.floor(r*5),f=weights.frost;
    if(z>.54){box(ctx,rgb(col.map((v,i)=>v+(f>.55?9:6)+(i===1?2:0))),sx,sy,2+(z>.88?1:0),1);if(z>.83)box(ctx,rgb(col.map(v=>v-6)),sx-1,sy+2,2,1);}
    else if(z<.19){box(ctx,rgb(col.map(v=>v-7)),sx,sy,2,1);}
  }

  // Hand-composed ledges, cut steps and masonry. Shared world coordinates mean
  // the texture is clipped cleanly across chunk edges, without procedural seams.
  function summitTerrain(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){
    if(ox+w<142*T||ox>160*T||oy>16*T)return;
    const clip=(points)=>{shape(ctx,null,points);ctx.clip();};
    const rockPal=[['#39474b','#4c5c5d','#677575','#86948e','#a8b8b0'],['#384950','#536769','#73888a','#9eada6','#c1cec3'],['#455a64','#5f7780','#82999f','#aabfc0','#d9e5dd']];
    // The side slopes visually JOIN the three terraces, so they read as one
    // mountain rather than three detached walls. The same silhouettes block movement.
    for(const flank of C.ELEVATION_FLANKS){const top=flank.top.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]),bottom=flank.bottom.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]),poly=top.concat(bottom.slice().reverse()),b=C.bounds(poly);if(b.x+b.w<ox||b.x>ox+w||b.y+b.h<oy||b.y>oy+h)continue;
      const rng=random(1416+flank.side*781);shape(ctx,'#445b6260',poly.map(([x,y])=>[x+flank.side*6,y+8]));ctx.save();clip(poly);shape(ctx,'#50656e',poly);
      for(let i=0;i<top.length-1;i++){const a=top[i],d=top[i+1],f=bottom[i],e=bottom[i+1],q=.32+rng()*.33;const mid=(p,k)=>[p[0]+(k[0]-p[0])*q,p[1]+(k[1]-p[1])*q],m=mid(a,f),n=mid(d,e);const light=flank.side<0;shape(ctx,light?'#94a7a7':'#778c92',[a,d,n,m]);shape(ctx,pick(light?['#637982','#7d969b','#536972']:['#52686f','#657e88','#435961'],rng),[m,n,e,f]);line(ctx,'#cad7d0',a[0],a[1],d[0],d[1],2);line(ctx,'#435a64',m[0],m[1],n[0],n[1],2);if(i%2===0)shape(ctx,'#b4c5bc',[a,d,[n[0]+flank.side*3,n[1]],[m[0]+flank.side*3,m[1]-2]]);const nx=Math.min(a[0],f[0]),ww=Math.abs(a[0]-f[0]);for(let k=0;k<15;k++){const x=nx+rng()*ww,y=a[1]+rng()*14;box(ctx,pick(['#40565d','#94aaac','#b0c0b8','#647b82'],rng),x,y,2+rng()*4,1+rng()*2);}}
      ctx.restore();for(let i=0;i<bottom.length;i+=2){const [x,y]=bottom[i];shape(ctx,'#6d8286',[[x-4,y+2],[x-2,y-3],[x+5,y-2],[x+8,y+4],[x,y+6]]);line(ctx,'#a7bbb8',x-2,y-3,x+5,y-2,1);}
    }
