    for(const face of C.ELEVATION_CLIFFS){const top=face.top.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]),foot=face.bottom.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]),poly=top.concat(foot.slice().reverse()),b=C.bounds(poly),p=rockPal[face.band];
      if(b.x+b.w<ox||b.x>ox+w||b.y+b.h<oy||b.y>oy+h)continue;
      const rng=random(13903+face.band*316+Math.round(b.x));
      shape(ctx,'#33434b64',foot.concat(foot.slice().reverse().map(([x,y])=>[x+3,y+9+face.band*2])));
      ctx.save();clip(poly);shape(ctx,p[1],poly);
      // Long irregular strata with broken vertical planes and small ledges.
      for(let i=0;i<top.length-1;i++){const a=top[i],c=top[i+1],d=foot[i+1],f=foot[i],mid=.28+rng()*.35;
        const pt=(u,v,t)=>[u[0]+(v[0]-u[0])*t,u[1]+(v[1]-u[1])*t];
        const m1=pt(a,f,mid),m2=pt(c,d,mid),n1=pt(a,f,.76),n2=pt(c,d,.73);
        shape(ctx,p[2],[a,c,m2,m1]);shape(ctx,i%3===0?p[0]:p[1],[m1,m2,n2,n1]);shape(ctx,p[0],[n1,n2,d,f]);
        line(ctx,p[3],a[0],a[1]+1,c[0],c[1]+1,2);line(ctx,p[0],m1[0],m1[1],m2[0],m2[1],2);
        const k=pt(a,f,.38);line(ctx,p[0],k[0],k[1],k[0]+3,f[1]-3,1);
        for(let j=0;j<6;j++){const px=a[0]+rng()*(c[0]-a[0]),py=a[1]+rng()*(f[1]-a[1]);box(ctx,pick([p[0],p[2],p[3]],rng),px,py,2+rng()*5,1+rng()*2);}
        if(i%3===0){shape(ctx,p[4],[a,c,[c[0]-2,c[1]+4],[a[0]+3,a[1]+6]]);box(ctx,face.band===0?'#687b5c':'#9caca9',a[0]+2,a[1]+5,5,2);}
      }ctx.restore();
      for(let i=0;i<foot.length;i+=2){const [x,y]=foot[i],r=rng();shape(ctx,p[1],[[x-3,y+3],[x-1,y-1],[x+4+r*3,y],[x+6,y+4],[x+1,y+6]]);line(ctx,p[3],x-1,y-1,x+4,y,1);}
    }
    // Paving across the ramp opening is a genuine part of the road. No collider here.
    for(const [idx,b] of C.ELEVATION_BANDS.entries())for(let row=0;row<11;row++){
      const yy=b.y*T-38+row*7,xx=b.gap*T,ww=42+Math.round(9*Math.sin(row*.5));
      if(xx+ww<ox||xx-ww>ox+w||yy+8<oy||yy>oy+h)continue;
      const rng=random(1351+idx*71+row*123);shape(ctx,'#42545a',[[xx-ww/2-2,yy-1],[xx+ww/2+2,yy-1],[xx+ww/2,yy+6],[xx-ww/2,yy+6]]);
      for(let k=0;k<4;k++){let x=xx-ww/2+k*ww/4;box(ctx,pick(['#819497','#92a3a2','#a7b5b0','#899a98'],rng),x,yy,ww/4-1,4);box(ctx,'#c4d1c6',x,yy,ww/4-1,1);box(ctx,'#5e7074',x,yy+4,ww/4-1,2);}
      if(row%3===0){for(const sign of [-1,1]){const x=xx+sign*(ww/2+6);box(ctx,'#556970',x-2,yy-2,5,7);box(ctx,'#b7c6bd',x-2,yy-3,4,2);}}
    }
    // Pale paving ring around the survey house. Road follows its southern entry.
    const a=C.SUMMIT,x=a.x*T,y=a.y*T;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.8;
    for(let yy=y-28;yy<y+48;yy+=8)for(let xx=x-57;xx<x+57;xx+=12){if(Math.hypot((xx-x)/62,(yy-y-6)/48)>1)continue;const r=C.hash(Math.floor(xx),Math.floor(yy),918);box(ctx,r>.65?'#a6b6af':'#7e9393',xx,yy,11,7);box(ctx,'#c1cdc3',xx+1,yy,10,1);}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function summitHouse(ctx,stage,frame){
    const rng=random(19432),stone=['#627475','#829594','#9dada6','#b4c1b5'],wood=['#55473b','#836c54','#a88c68','#c0a17b'];
    const slab=(x,y,w,h,p=stone)=>{box(ctx,p[0],x,y,w,h);box(ctx,p[1],x+1,y,w-2,h-2);box(ctx,p[2],x+1,y,w-2,1);if(w>8)box(ctx,p[3],x+2,y+2,2,1);};
    shadow(ctx,99,101,83,13);
    shape(ctx,'#52686a',[[17,95],[24,77],[157,77],[179,94],[167,109],[28,109]]);
    for(let row=0;row<3;row++)for(let c=0;c<10;c++){const x=24+c*14+(row%2)*5,y=85+row*7;if(stage<3&&rng()<.38)continue;slab(x,y,13,6);}
    // Original ruined retaining walls: irregular courses, broken caps, old masonry retained even after rebuilding.
    for(let side=0;side<2;side++){const x0=side?129:28;for(let row=0;row<5;row++)for(let col=0;col<3;col++){const x=x0+col*11+(row%2)*3,y=81-row*7;if(stage<2&&(row>1&&rng()<.45||side===1&&row>2))continue;if(stage<5&&row>2&&col>0)continue;slab(x,y,10,7);if(rng()<.16)box(ctx,'#6f855c',x+1,y,5,2);}}
    if(stage<3){for(let i=0;i<(stage<2?37:10);i++){const x=32+rng()*122,y=72+rng()*27;shape(ctx,pick(stone,rng),[[x-4,y],[x-2,y-4],[x+4,y-3],[x+6,y+2],[x,y+4]]);}line(ctx,'#aa926c',53,77,102,88,4);line(ctx,'#4e4940',59,75,99,64,3);}
    if(stage>=3){for(let k=0;k<11;k++){const x=37+k*11;slab(x,91,10,5);}}
    if(stage>=4){for(const x of [41,76,117,149]){box(ctx,wood[0],x,33,8,57);box(ctx,wood[1],x+1,33,5,55);box(ctx,wood[3],x+1,34,1,51);for(let j=0;j<4;j++)box(ctx,wood[0],x+3,43+j*12,2,3);}
      for(const [x,y,x2,y2] of [[41,36,149,36],[41,36,92,12],[92,12,149,36],[76,56,42,36],[117,56,149,36]]){line(ctx,wood[0],x,y,x2,y2,6);line(ctx,wood[2],x,y-1,x2,y2-1,2);}}
    if(stage>=5){for(let row=0;row<7;row++)for(let col=0;col<8;col++){const x=45+col*13+(row%2)*4,y=82-row*7;if(col>=3&&col<=4&&row<5||row>=4&&(col===1||col===6))continue;slab(x,y,12,6);}
      for(const x of [55,128]){box(ctx,'#364a50',x,47,15,23);box(ctx,'#9b8d68',x-2,45,19,2);box(ctx,'#8eaaa9',x+2,49,11,18);box(ctx,'#40575d',x+7,49,2,18);box(ctx,'#40575d',x+2,57,11,2);if(stage>=8)box(ctx,'#efd49b',x+3,50,4,6);}}
    if(stage>=6){const roof=[[29,38],[91,9],[155,31],[168,48],[97,29],[38,53]];shape(ctx,'#35464d',roof);shape(ctx,'#738f92',[[31,38],[91,9],[97,29],[38,49]]);shape(ctx,'#4c666e',[[91,9],[155,31],[166,45],[97,29]]);
      for(let i=0;i<12;i++){let x=34+i*10,y=x<91?38-(x-31)*.45:10+(x-91)*.35;line(ctx,'#b4c7c2',x,y,x+6,y+14,1);line(ctx,'#405961',x+3,y,x+9,y+14,1);}
      line(ctx,'#c3d3ca',30,38,91,9,3);line(ctx,'#91a9a8',91,9,156,31,3);line(ctx,'#2c4048',38,53,97,29,3);}
    if(stage>=7){const x=99,y=75;box(ctx,'#403d33',x-4,y-19,8,22);box(ctx,'#b6a06d',x-2,y-20,4,22);const a=frame*Math.PI/12-.55;line(ctx,'#4a514c',x,y-19,x+Math.cos(a)*23,y-19+Math.sin(a)*23,8);line(ctx,'#d4bd80',x,y-21,x+Math.cos(a)*23,y-21+Math.sin(a)*23,4);box(ctx,'#88b5be',x-2,y-23,3,4);}
    if(stage>=8){for(const x of [43,146]){box(ctx,'#5c4937',x,64,6,14);box(ctx,'#e9c77f',x+1,65,4,7);}for(let i=0;i<9;i++)slab(66+i*7,99,6,5,['#6b7166','#9fae9f','#d0dac6','#e4e8d5']);}
    if(stage===9){box(ctx,'#403d32',136,70,18,16);box(ctx,'#bdab72',137,71,16,5);box(ctx,'#d2cfb1',139,72,8,2);box(ctx,'#526c72',143,76,3,6);}
    if(stage>=1){const x=158,y=83;box(ctx,'#514637',x,32,3,52);shape(ctx,'#a89a76',[[x+3,34],[x+15,35+Math.sin(frame)*3],[x+20,39],[x+14,44],[x+3,43]]);line(ctx,'#d1c5a1',x+3,34,x+15,35+Math.sin(frame)*3,1);}
    if(stage>=2&&stage<7){for(const x of [24,166]){box(ctx,wood[0],x,47,3,48);line(ctx,wood[2],x,53,x+9,60,2);line(ctx,wood[1],x,68,x+12,79,2);}}
  }
  function summitSky(ctx,mode="day"){
    const W=64*T,H=448;const colors=({day:['#719ba9','#9cbfc6','#c5d8d3','#e0e1c8'],dawn:['#786fa0','#c2899a','#e9b69c','#f0d8ad'],dusk:['#4d5b85','#967996','#d6a287','#ead2a6'],night:['#14263e','#243853','#445672','#6c7c8e'],mist:['#718b9a','#9bb1b8','#bbc9c8','#cbd7d4']})[mode];for(let y=0;y<H;y+=4){const f=y/H,n=Math.min(2,Math.floor(f*3)),v=f*3-n;const c=blend(colors[n].match(/\w\w/g).map(k=>parseInt(k,16)),colors[n+1].match(/\w\w/g).map(k=>parseInt(k,16)),v);box(ctx,rgb(c),0,y,W,4);}
    // Cloud silhouettes follow the sky palette; distant ridges occlude them naturally.
    const shade=mode==='night'?'#acbfd0':mode==='dawn'||mode==='dusk'?'#f4caa7':'#e4e8d9';
    ctx.save();for(let i=0;i<15;i++){const xx=Math.floor(C.hash(i,97,11)*W),yy=38+Math.floor(C.hash(i,97,12)*115),wid=42+Math.floor(C.hash(i,97,13)*140);ctx.globalAlpha=mode==='mist'?.2:mode==='night'?.09:.25;for(let j=0;j<Math.floor(wid/5);j++){const v=Math.sin(j*2.399+i)*3+Math.sin(j/5)*8;box(ctx,shade,xx+j*5,yy+Math.round(v),5,3+(j%3));}}ctx.restore();
    if(mode==='night'){for(let i=0;i<80;i++){const x=Math.floor(C.hash(i,97,14)*W),y=Math.floor(C.hash(i,97,15)*165);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.24+C.hash(i,97,16)*.57;box(ctx,'#dce4df',x,y,i%9===0?2:1,1);ctx.restore();}}
    // Atmospheric perspective: three continuous, hand-shaped distant ridgelines.
    const peaks=[[[0,218],[160,180],[288,210],[420,115],[502,152],[625,67],[735,147],[835,115],[1020,234],[1150,171],[1300,87],[1450,202],[1600,141],[1740,83],[1890,188],[2048,144]],[[0,298],[210,254],[330,176],[487,280],[634,219],[740,254],[872,140],[1000,208],[1080,179],[1250,288],[1395,195],[1500,222],[1634,157],[1810,275],[2048,213]],[[0,360],[190,315],[315,360],[477,272],[610,326],[745,278],[870,340],[995,262],[1183,352],[1360,302],[1490,345],[1650,273],[1825,348],[2048,290]]];
    // Raster ridges in clusters: layered wind-carved summit silhouettes, shaded
    // gullies and broken snow bands rather than flat triangular overlays.
    const pals=mode==='night'?[['#586984','#6d7c94','#485a77','#a6b5c6'],['#465874','#586c86','#384a63','#8b9daf'],['#344a60','#496278','#293c54','#788e9d']]:mode==='dawn'||mode==='dusk'?[['#9c8e9e','#bda5a6','#837b92','#e9cfb8'],['#7a8393','#a29a9d','#68758a','#d9c6ad'],['#556e7c','#848c88','#4a6071','#c3b49e']]:[['#a3bac0','#b3c7ca','#97aeb8','#c6d5d5'],['#819ba6','#96afb6','#718c9b','#b6cacc'],['#627d87','#839da2','#526d7a','#aac1c2']];
    const hash=(x,y,k)=>{const v=Math.sin(x*12.9898+y*78.233+k*31.371)*43758.5453;return v-Math.floor(v);};
    for(let i=0;i<peaks.length;i++){
      const ridge=peaks[i],pal=pals[i],step=4,heights=[];let j=1;
      for(let x=0;x<W;x+=step){while(j<ridge.length-1&&x>ridge[j][0])j++;const a=ridge[j-1],b=ridge[j],f=(x-a[0])/(b[0]-a[0]);const h=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*f+Math.sin(x*.051+i)*5+Math.sin(x*.017+i*3)*9+Math.sin(x*.123)*2;heights.push(Math.floor(h/step)*step);}
      for(let x=0;x<W;x+=step){const id=x/step,top=heights[id],slope=(heights[Math.min(heights.length-1,id+2)]-heights[Math.max(0,id-2)])/16;
        for(let y=top;y<H;y+=step){const d=y-top,ridgeNoise=Math.sin(x*.027+y*.032+i*2)+Math.sin(x*.067-y*.022)+Math.sin(x*.011+y*.045)*.6;const snow=d<12+9*Math.sin(x*.036)+13*Math.sin(x*.009+i)||d<75&&ridgeNoise>1.58;const strata=Math.sin(y*.11+x*.026+i)>.83;const lit=slope>.35||ridgeNoise>.65;let c=snow?pal[3]:lit?pal[1]:ridgeNoise<-.4?pal[2]:pal[0];if(!snow&&strata&&hash(Math.floor(x/12),Math.floor(y/8),i)>.43)c=lit?pal[0]:pal[2];box(ctx,c,x,y,step,step);}
      }
    }
    // A translucent low-cloud fringe overlaps the ground at its northern edge.
    for(let y=H-85;y<H+48;y+=2){const f=(y-(H-85))/133;ctx.globalAlpha=y<H?Math.min(.9,f*1.5):.85*(1-(y-H)/48);box(ctx,mode==='night'?'#647c8e':mode==='dawn'||mode==='dusk'?'#d2b6a0':'#bed1ce',0,y,W,2);}ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  function summitRelease(view){if(view.summitSky){const v=view.summitSky,i=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(v);if(i>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(v.scene)v.destroy();delete view.summitSky;}const s=view.summitSprite;if(s){const i=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(s);if(i>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(s.scene)s.destroy();delete view.summitSprite;}view.summitTracks=[];view.summitPrev=null;}
  function summitEffects(scene,view,world,t,quality,reduced){const p=scene.player,h=C.elevationSample(p.x/T,p.y/T),a=C.SUMMIT,x=a.x*T,y=a.y*T,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView;
    if((!h||h.id)&&(x<rect.x-250||x>rect.right+250||y<rect.y-250||y>rect.bottom+250)){summitRelease(view);return;}
    const skyMode=horizonSkyMode(world),skyKey=texture(scene,'v97-cloudrest-horizon-'+skyMode,64*T,496,c=>summitSky(c,skyMode));if(!view.summitSky){view.summitSky=scene.add.image(104*T,-448,skyKey).setOrigin(0).setDepth(.3);scene.areaObjs.push(view.summitSky);}else if(view.summitSky.texture.key!==skyKey)view.summitSky.setTexture(skyKey);
    const stage=root.LQValleyDevelopment.summitState(world).stage,phase=reduced?0:Math.floor(t*5)%8;
    const key=texture(scene,'cloudrest-house-'+stage+'-'+(stage===0?0:phase),192,116,ctx=>summitHouse(ctx,stage,phase));
    if(!view.summitSprite){view.summitSprite=scene.add.image(x,y,key).setOrigin(.5,90/116).setDepth(view.depthAt(y));scene.areaObjs.push(view.summitSprite);}else if(view.summitSprite.texture.key!==key)view.summitSprite.setTexture(key);
    view.summitSprite.setAlpha(p.y<y&&p.y>y-90&&Math.abs(p.x-x)<72?.58:1);
    if(!h||h.edge<.1)return;
    const g=view.effects,fog=view.nearFog,light=view.light,night=world.clock.minuteOfDay>=1170||world.clock.minuteOfDay<375;
    // Light drifting cloud banks lie BELOW the player's foot plane; wind and snow tracks
    // are capped, transient visual effects, not persistent entities.
    if(!reduced){for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const xx=143*T+((t*(8+i)+i*131)%(17*T)),yy=(14-i*.52)*T;if(h.elevation>190+i*56){fog.fillStyle(0xd1ded8,.07).fillEllipse(xx,yy,142+i*21,25+i%2*13);fog.fillStyle(0xc6d9d6,.05).fillEllipse(xx+30,yy+7,118,20);}}
      for(let i=0;i<14;i++){const xx=rect.x+((i*127+t*(21+h.elevation*.025))%(rect.width||1)),yy=rect.y+((i*73+Math.sin(t*.9+i)*9)%(rect.height||1));g.lineStyle(1,0xe3ece4,.18).lineBetween(xx,yy,xx+6+h.elevation*.016,yy-2);}}
    const tracks=view.summitTracks??=[],prev=view.summitPrev;if(!reduced&&h.elevation>340&&(!prev||Math.hypot(prev.x-p.x,prev.y-p.y)>11)){tracks.push({x:p.x,y:p.y,t,side:tracks.length%2});view.summitPrev={x:p.x,y:p.y};if(tracks.length>32)tracks.shift();}while(tracks.length&&t-tracks[0].t>5)tracks.shift();for(const s of tracks)fog.fillStyle(0x5b777b,.29*(1-(t-s.t)/5)).fillRect(s.x+(s.side?3:-3),s.y,4,3);
    if(stage>=8&&night){for(const dx of [-52,51]){for(let i=5;i>=1;i--)light.fillStyle(0xf3c478,.025).fillEllipse(x+dx,y-13,i*21,i*14);light.fillStyle(0xffe3a2,.85).fillRect(x+dx,y-22,3,5);}}
  }

  // World-coordinate painter: slope illumination from the same height function as movement.
  function hillColor(col,x,y,h){if(!h?.id)return col;const x0=x/T,y0=y/T,delta=.20;
    const z=(a,b)=>C.highlandSample(a,b)?.elevation||0;
    const dx=z(x0+delta,y0)-z(x0-delta,y0),dy=z(x0,y0+delta)-z(x0,y0-delta),shade=Math.max(-.31,Math.min(.24,(dx+dy)*.014));
    const ridge=h.id==='sunstone-plateau',r=h.r,rock=ridge?Math.max(0,Math.sin(r*22-1.1))*.43:Math.max(0,Math.sin(r*15))*.12;
    const soil=blend(col,ridge?[147,132,93]:[124,154,87],h.edge*.42);return blend(soil,ridge?[111,102,84]:[99,119,74],rock*h.edge).map(v=>Math.min(255,Math.max(0,v*(1+shade*h.edge))));}
  function hillTerrain(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){for(const hill of C.HIGHLANDS){const cx=hill.x*T,cy=hill.y*T,rx=hill.rx*T,ry=hill.ry*T;if(cx+rx<ox||cx-rx>ox+w||cy+ry<oy||cy-ry>oy+h)continue;
    // Authored terraced relief; localized broken clusters follow contour direction.
    const rocky=hill.theme==='stone',bands=rocky?[.36,.68,.91]:[.68,.88],rng=random(3681+Math.floor(cx));
    for(const [j,b] of bands.entries())for(let i=0;i<150;i++){const a=i/150*Math.PI*2,rr=b*(1+.07*Math.sin(a*3+.7)+.04*Math.cos(a*5)),x=Math.round(cx+Math.cos(a)*rx*rr),y=Math.round(cy+Math.sin(a)*ry*rr);if(x<ox-14||x>ox+w+14||y<oy-14||y>oy+h+14||C.landscapeRoadDistance(x/T,y/T)<1.25)continue;
      const south=Math.sin(a)>.03;if(!south&&i%4!==0)continue;const v=C.hash(i,j,781);if(v<.24)continue;const len=rocky?5+Math.floor(v*12):3+Math.floor(v*7),depth=rocky?(south?6:3):2;const k=rocky?['#524d43','#81745a','#aa9973','#c5b78c']:['#425e3d','#657b47','#81965c','#a5b56c'];
      shape(ctx,k[0],[[x-len/2,y],[x+len/2,y-2],[x+len/2-1,y+depth],[x-len/2+2,y+depth+2]]);shape(ctx,k[1],[[x-len/2,y],[x+len/2,y-2],[x+len/2-2,y+2],[x-len/2+2,y+3]]);box(ctx,k[2],x-len/2+2,y-1,len-3,1);if(v>.63)box(ctx,k[3],x-1,y-2,3,1);if(rocky&&v>.65)box(ctx,k[0],x+2,y+3,1,3);}
    // Survey cairn and panoramic patio, placed to one side of the trail; no decorative collider.
    for(let yy=-21;yy<=17;yy+=5)for(let xx=-27;xx<=27;xx+=7){if((xx/30)**2+(yy/23)**2>1)continue;const x=cx+xx,y=cy+yy,r=C.hash(xx,yy,78);box(ctx,rocky?(r>.5?'#9c8967':'#85765b'):(r>.5?'#b1ae78':'#8e9561'),x,y,6,4);box(ctx,rocky?'#c9b58b':'#c8c18d',x+1,y,4,1);}
    const bx=cx-25,by=cy-10;for(let row=0;row<5;row++){let ww=20-row*3;shape(ctx,'#494d43',[[bx-ww/2,by-row*5],[bx+ww/2,by-row*5-2],[bx+ww/2-1,by-row*5+4],[bx-ww/2+2,by-row*5+6]]);box(ctx,rocky?'#a49272':'#829075',bx-ww/2+2,by-row*5,ww-3,3);box(ctx,'#c0b99e',bx-ww/2+3,by-row*5,ww-6,1);}
  }}
  function highlandEffects(scene,view,world,t,quality,reduced){const rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,p=scene.player,g=view.effects,f=view.nearFog,light=view.light;const visible=(x,y)=>x>rect.x-40&&x<rect.right+40&&y>rect.y-50&&y<rect.bottom+50;
    const journal=root.LQValleyDevelopment.surveyState(world);
    for(const hill of C.HIGHLANDS){const x=hill.x*T-25,y=hill.y*T-36;if(!visible(x,y))continue;const flag=journal.visited[hill.id]?0xe4c183:hill.theme==='stone'?0xb9a078:0x82b59a;g.fillStyle(0x4c4232,1).fillRect(x,y-21,2,24);for(let i=0;i<15;i++){const wobble=reduced?0:Math.round(Math.sin(t*4+i*.43)*i*.17);g.fillStyle(flag,.93).fillRect(x+2+i,y-20+wobble,1,8-Math.floor(i/6));g.fillStyle(0xf2dcac,.55).fillRect(x+2+i,y-20+wobble,1,1);}}
    for(const e of C.ECHOES){const q=C.echoPoint(e,t);if(journal.echoes[e.id]||!visible(q.x,q.y)){removeEchoSprite(view,e.id);continue;}const x=Math.round(q.x),y=Math.round(q.y),bob=reduced?0:Math.round(2*Math.sin(t*3+e.x)),alt=e.id==='amber-echo'?10:3,yy=y-alt+bob;f.fillStyle(0x24382d,.23).fillEllipse(x,y+2,24,7);for(let i=4;i>0;i--)light.fillStyle(e.palette[1],.035).fillCircle(x,yy-13,7+i*4);
      const sprite=echoActor(scene,view,e.id,x,yy,t,reduced);sprite.setDepth(view.depthAt(y)+.005);const bright=e.palette[2];
      if(!reduced){for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const a=(t*.68+i/6)%1,prev=C.echoPoint(e,t-a*.65),ang=i*2.4+t*.8;g.fillStyle(bright,.56*(1-a)).fillRect(Math.round(prev.x+Math.cos(ang)*9),Math.round(prev.y-alt-6-a*14),2,2);} }
    }
    const b=scene._lqEchoBurst;if(b){const age=t-b.at;if(age>=1.5||age<0)delete scene._lqEchoBurst;else if(!reduced){for(let i=0;i<14;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/7,r=5+age*25;g.fillStyle(0xe6f4b0,.8*(1-age/1.5)).fillRect(Math.round(b.x+Math.cos(a)*r),Math.round(b.y-12+Math.sin(a)*r),2,2);}}}
    const elevation=C.highlandSample(p.x/T,p.y/T);if(elevation&&!reduced&&quality.ambient>=6){for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const x=rect.x+((i*129+t*(12+elevation.elevation*.07))%Math.max(1,rect.width)),y=rect.y+((i*93+Math.sin(t+i)*9)%Math.max(1,rect.height));if(C.highlandSample(x/T,y/T)?.id!==elevation.id)continue;g.lineStyle(1,0xe9dec0,.15).lineBetween(x,y,x+5+elevation.elevation*.025,y-1);}}
  }
  function hillEscarpments(ctx,ox,oy,w,h){
    for(const f of C.HILL_FACES){const top=f.top.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]),foot=f.bottom.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T]),b=C.bounds(top.concat(foot));if(b.x+b.w<ox-30||b.x>ox+w+30||b.y+b.h<oy-30||b.y>oy+h+30)continue;
      const green=f.site==='first-hill',pal=green?['#384640','#4f5b49','#68725a','#8b9872','#b4b78b']:['#413c34','#575248','#766d57','#a69776','#c8ba93'];const rng=random(Math.floor(b.x*17+b.y));const pts=top.concat(foot.slice().reverse());
      shape(ctx,'#28362f6e',foot.concat(foot.slice().reverse().map(([x,y])=>[x+5,y+11])));ctx.save();shape(ctx,pal[1],pts);shape(ctx,null,pts);ctx.clip();
      for(let i=0;i<top.length-1;i++){const a=top[i],c=top[i+1],d=foot[i+1],e=foot[i];const lerp=(a,b,t)=>[a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*t,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t],n=5+Math.floor(rng()*4);
        for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const u=k/n,v=(k+1)/n,aa=lerp(a,c,u),bb=lerp(a,c,v),cc=lerp(e,d,v),dd=lerp(e,d,u),j=.18+rng()*.16,m=.42+rng()*.12,l=.73+rng()*.1;
          const p1=lerp(aa,dd,j),p2=lerp(bb,cc,j),p3=lerp(aa,dd,m),p4=lerp(bb,cc,m),p5=lerp(aa,dd,l),p6=lerp(bb,cc,l);
          shape(ctx,pal[2+(k%3===0?1:0)],[aa,bb,p2,p1]);shape(ctx,pal[2],[p1,p2,p4,p3]);shape(ctx,pal[k%3===1?0:1],[p3,p4,p6,p5]);shape(ctx,pal[0],[p5,p6,cc,dd]);line(ctx,pal[3],aa[0],aa[1]+1,bb[0],bb[1]+1,2);line(ctx,pal[0],p3[0],p3[1],p4[0],p4[1],1);
          if(k%3===0){line(ctx,pal[0],bb[0],bb[1]+4,cc[0]-2,cc[1]-1,1);line(ctx,pal[4],aa[0]+1,aa[1]+3,p1[0]+2,p1[1],1);}
          for(let n0=0;n0<6;n0++){const x=aa[0]+rng()*(bb[0]-aa[0]),y=aa[1]+rng()*Math.abs(dd[1]-aa[1]);box(ctx,pick([pal[1],pal[3],pal[0]],rng),x,y,1+rng()*3,1);}}
      }
      ctx.restore();for(let i=0;i<top.length-1;i++){const [x,y]=top[i], [x2,y2]=top[i+1];line(ctx,pal[4],x,y-1,x2,y2-1,2);for(let k=0;k<9;k++){const a=k/9,xx=x+(x2-x)*a,yy=y+(y2-y)*a;if(rng()>.55){box(ctx,green?'#546843':'#777952',xx,yy-3,3,3);box(ctx,green?'#8a9f61':'#a6a06d',xx,yy-4,2,1);}}}
      for(let i=0;i<foot.length-1;i++){const a=foot[i],b=foot[i+1];for(let k=0;k<4;k++){const x=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*rng(),y=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*rng()+rng()*7;shape(ctx,pal[1],[[x-4,y+1],[x-1,y-3],[x+4,y-2],[x+6,y+2],[x,y+4]]);line(ctx,pal[3],x-1,y-3,x+4,y-2,1);}}
    }
    // Solid stone treads across the steep sections; both banks of the path remain blended into grass.
    // Only draw steps on authored straight approach segments, avoiding unaligned corners.
    const stairRuns=[{axis:'y',fixed:73.5,from:21.2,to:23.7},{axis:'x',fixed:94.5,from:65.0,to:67.1}];
    for(const s of stairRuns){const vx=s.axis==='y'?1:0,vy=1-vx;for(let u=s.from*T;u<=s.to*T;u+=7){const x=s.axis==='y'?s.fixed*T:u,y=s.axis==='y'?u:s.fixed*T;if(x<ox-40||x>ox+w+40||y<oy-40||y>oy+h+40)continue;const rng=random(Math.floor(u*34));const len=40;for(let k=0;k<5;k++){const shift=-len/2+k*8,px=x+vx*shift-(s.axis==='x'?3:0),py=y+vy*shift-(s.axis==='y'?3:0),ww=s.axis==='y'?7:6,hh=s.axis==='y'?6:7;box(ctx,'#605641',px,py,ww,hh);box(ctx,pick(['#a59675','#b1a180','#998d71','#b7aa87'],rng),px+1,py+1,ww-2,hh-2);box(ctx,'#d5c6a0',px+1,py+1,ww-2,1);box(ctx,'#81745b',px+1,py+hh-1,ww-1,1);}}
    }
  }
  // Authored original pixel sprites. No reused NPC assets or whole-body scale animation.
  // Individual limbs, fins and wings are selected from eight cached poses.
  function echoSpriteTexture(scene,id,phase){return texture(scene,'echo-original-'+id+'-'+phase,44,40,ctx=>{
    const colors=id==='moss-echo'?['#243c32','#40674c','#6a9a5d','#a4c77a','#e8f7bd','#6cbfa5','#f1d89b']:id==='amber-echo'?['#55382c','#8a5238','#c28348','#efbb70','#fff0bd','#d8a458','#f6d58f']:['#284554','#496c80','#72a4be','#b6e1e5','#effdff','#7cbfd1','#a9d6e1'];
    const cell=(x,y,c)=>box(ctx,colors[c],x*2,y*2,2,2),pattern=(ox,oy,rows,mirror=false)=>rows.forEach((row,y)=>[...row].forEach((v,x)=>{if(v!=='.')cell(ox+(mirror?row.length-1-x:x),oy+y,Number(v));}));
    const stride=[0,1,1,0,-1,-1,0,0][phase],sway=[0,0,1,1,0,-1,-1,0][phase];
    if(id==='moss-echo'){
      // A seed guardian with a leafy crown, folded fern ears and moving root feet.
      pattern(4,8,['....000...','..001110..','.01222210.','0123332210','0133332210','0122222110','.01111110.','..000000..']);
      pattern(2,7+sway,['..00.','.0530','05320','05210','.010.']);
      pattern(11,4,['...00....','..0540...','.053540..','..03530..','..0000...','.013310..','01243310.','01344310.','.1343300.','.012210..','..0000...']);
      pattern(6,3,['.00..','0320.','03230','..230','...0.']);pattern(12,1,['.00.','0320','0350','.20.','.0..']);
      pattern(8+stride,14,['00','10','20','00']);pattern(13-stride,14,['00','10','20','00']);
      cell(15,10,0);cell(15,9,4);cell(12,12,6);cell(8,11,5);cell(6,10,3);cell(10,10,3);
    }else if(id==='amber-echo'){
      // A tiny lantern moth, translucent gold wing panels with veins and scalloped edges.
      const wingPos=phase%4;const wing=wingPos===1||wingPos===2?['..00100....','.0133210...','0134443210.','0123342210.','.01232210..','..012110...','...000.....']:['....000...','..001210..','.01343210.','0134443210','0132332210','.01233210.','..013210..','...0010...','....00....'];
      pattern(1,4+Math.abs(stride),wing);pattern(11,4+Math.abs(stride),wing,true);
      pattern(9,6,['.00.','0130','0340','0340','0130','0130','0120','.00.']);
      pattern(8,3+sway,['0....0','.0..0.','..00..']);
      cell(10,7,4);cell(11,7,4);cell(10,13,4);
    }else{
      // Ice elemental: angular mask, floating crystal tail, individual paws and ear tips.
      pattern(1,5+sway,['..00....','..0320..','.034320.','03443210','03433210','.01210..','..010...','...0....']);
      pattern(5,8,['....0000..','..0012210.','.012333210','0123333210','0122222110','.01112110.','..000000..']);
      pattern(12,3,['00....00','032..230','03400340','01233210','01344310','01333310','.1344310','.012210.','..0000..']);
      pattern(7+stride,14,['00','20','00']);pattern(13-stride,14,['00','20','00']);
      cell(14,8,0);cell(17,8,0);cell(15,10,4);cell(6,11,4);cell(3,8,4);cell(13,6,4);
    }
  });}
  function removeEchoSprite(view,id){const sprite=view.echoSprites?.get(id);if(!sprite)return;const i=view.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(sprite);if(i>=0)view.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(sprite.scene)sprite.destroy();view.echoSprites.delete(id);}
  function clearEchoSprites(view){if(!view?.echoSprites)return;for(const id of [...view.echoSprites.keys()])removeEchoSprite(view,id);}
  function echoActor(scene,view,id,x,y,t,reduced){const phase=reduced?0:Math.floor(t*(id==='amber-echo'?10:7))%8,key=echoSpriteTexture(scene,id,phase);const m=view.echoSprites??=new Map();let a=m.get(id);if(!a){a=scene.add.image(x,y,key).setOrigin(.5,.88);scene.areaObjs.push(a);m.set(id,a);}if(a.texture.key!==key)a.setTexture(key);a.setPosition(Math.round(x),Math.round(y)).setDepth(view.depthAt(y)+.005);return a;}

/* Inserted into valley art engine: terrain micro-clusters and courtyard progression. */
  function v91TerrainDetail(ctx,x,y,col,r,weights,road,water,bank){
    if(water){const shallow=bank<.44,n=C.hash(x,y,19123);if(shallow&&n>.53){const c=blend(col,[174,189,148],.43);box(ctx,rgb(c),x+1,y+5,3,2);box(ctx,rgb(blend(col,[37,77,73],.42)),x+2,y+7,3,1);}else if(!shallow&&n>.84){const c=blend(col,[117,174,173],.38);box(ctx,rgb(c),x+1,y+2,4,1);box(ctx,rgb(blend(col,[106,151,160],.22)),x+3,y+3,4,1);}return;}
    if(road&&road.distance<road.width+.28)return;
    const m=wildNoise(x,y,54,19121),n=C.hash(x,y,19122),rock=(weights.ridge||0)+(weights.frost||0)+(weights.coppervale||0);if(m<.49||n<.70)return;
    const dark=rgb(col.map(v=>v-13)),light=rgb(col.map((v,i)=>v+(i===1?19:12))),mid=rgb(col.map(v=>v+5));
    if(rock>.42){box(ctx,dark,x+2,y+4,5,2);box(ctx,mid,x+3,y+3,3,2);box(ctx,light,x+3,y+2,3,1);}else{box(ctx,dark,x+2,y+5,4,1);box(ctx,mid,x+3,y+2,2,3);box(ctx,light,x+2,y+1,1,3);box(ctx,light,x+5,y+3,1,2);}
  }
  function v91Court(ctx,p,era,stage){
    // This decor lies within the existing site sprite canvas; it never extends the collision box.
    const r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),seed=Math.floor(p.x*17+p.y*31),rand=random(seed+9151);
    if(stage<8){if(stage<3){for(let j=0;j<11;j++){let x=30+Math.floor(rand()*143),y=129+Math.floor(rand()*8);r('#3f5140',x,y,3,1);r('#77915f',x+1,y-1,2,1);}}return;}
    const colors=[['#67705b','#8a8d70','#adc094'],['#797a64','#a4a78c','#d2c4a1'],['#657458','#9ea077','#d2c7a0'],['#7c7467','#ada18d','#d8caad'],['#686d66','#9caa9d','#c5d1bc'],['#6f8278','#b2b7a2','#e1d5ad'],['#82785c','#bbb18d','#f0d89a']][Math.max(0,Math.min(6,era))];
    // The apron changes from rough gravel to repaired masonry, inlay, and engraved borders.
    for(let row=0;row<2;row++)for(let j=0;j<16;j++){const x=37+j*8+(row?3:0),y=132+row*5;if(x>160)continue;if(era===0&&rand()<.6)continue;r(colors[0],x,y,7,4);r(colors[1],x+1,y,5,3);r(colors[2],x+1,y,5,1);if(era>=4&&j%4===0)r(colors[2],x+3,y+1,2,2);}
    if(era>=2){for(const x of [45,149]){r('#4b5140',x,129,9,3);r('#8b8058',x+1,128,7,2);for(let j=0;j<3;j++){r('#547450',x+2+j*2,125-j%2*2,1,4);r(era>=5?'#e5c180':'#b4c78c',x+1+j*2,124-j%2*2,2,2);}}}
    if(era>=5){for(const x of [83,114]){r('#4c5348',x,139,5,2);r(colors[2],x+1,138,3,1);}}
  }

  // V97 — relief is derived from authored elevation, not a second collision map.
  function horizonRelief(ctx,x,y,col,h){
    if(!h||h.elevation<55||h.edge<.08)return;
    const tx=(x+4)/T,ty=(y+4)/T;
    const west=C.elevationSample(tx-.26,ty)?.elevation??h.elevation;
    const north=C.elevationSample(tx,ty-.26)?.elevation??h.elevation;
    const slope=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,((h.elevation-west)*.72+(h.elevation-north)*.55)/15));
    const amount=Math.min(.20,Math.abs(slope)*.19)*(h.ramp?.55:1);
    if(amount>.012){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=amount;box(ctx,slope>0?'#303e42':'#f4dfab',x,y,8,8);ctx.restore();}
    if(h.face&&!h.ramp){const n=C.hash(x,y,9701),band=Math.floor(h.elevation/26);const tone=rgb(blend(col,band%3===0?[196,182,150]:[45,53,53],.32));
      if(n>.38){box(ctx,tone,x+1,y+2,6,1);box(ctx,rgb(blend(col,[42,49,48],.48)),x+2,y+3,5,1);if(n>.8)box(ctx,tone,x+6,y+4,1,3);}}
  }
  // Use the exact authored shoreline, cascade polygon, pool polygon and bridge mask.
  function horizonWaterAt(x,y){
    if(x<0||y<0||x>=W*T||y>=H*T)return null;
    const tx=x/T,ty=y/T;
    if(C.landscapeBridge(tx,ty)||C.crossesRiver(Math.floor(ty))&&tx>33&&tx<48)return null;
    if(C.CROSSINGS?.some?.(b=>tx>=b.left&&tx<=b.right&&Math.abs(ty-b.y)<.85))return null;
    const c=C.cascadeSample(tx,ty);if(c)return {kind:'cascade',depth:1-c.bank,fall:c.fall};
    if(C.eastPool(tx,ty))return {kind:'pool',depth:.6,fall:false};
    const [a,b]=C.shore(ty);if(tx>a+.06&&tx<b-.06)return{kind:'river',depth:Math.min(1,Math.min(tx-a,b-tx)/.8),fall:false};
    return null;
  }
  function horizonWaterStrip(x,y,w,h,kind){return [[x,y],[x+w-1,y],[x,y+h-1],[x+w-1,y+h-1]].every(([a,b])=>horizonWaterAt(a,b)?.kind===kind);}
  function horizonWaterEffects(scene,view,w,t,quality,reduced){
    const r=scene.cameras.main.worldView,g=view.effects,m=w.clock.minuteOfDay,night=m>=1170||m<375,rain=['rain','storm'].includes(w.weather.kind),wind=['wind','storm'].includes(w.weather.kind);
    let n=0,limit=reduced?38:quality.water>=4?155:90;
    // Absolute world lattice: stable when the camera moves; phase only moves along flow.
    for(let yy=Math.max(0,Math.floor(r.y/24)*24);yy<Math.min(H*T,r.bottom+24)&&n<limit;yy+=24)for(let xx=Math.max(0,Math.floor(r.x/24)*24);xx<Math.min(W*T,r.right+24)&&n<limit;xx+=24){
      const seed=C.hash(xx,yy,9721),x=xx+Math.floor(3+C.hash(xx,yy,9722)*12),base=yy+Math.floor(seed*17),y=base+(reduced?0:Math.floor((t*(rain?17:9)+seed*19)%17));const q=horizonWaterAt(x,y);if(!q)continue;
      const col=night?0x8eb3cd:0xc6e0c8,ink=night?0x223f56:0x315f63;
      if(q.fall){const len=5+Math.floor(seed*8);if(!horizonWaterStrip(x,y,2,len,q.kind))continue;g.fillStyle(ink,.32).fillRect(x+0,y,2,len);g.fillStyle(col,.62).fillRect(x,y,1,len-2);g.fillStyle(0xe7f0dc,.60).fillRect(x,y+len-2,2,1);}
      else {const len=4+Math.floor(seed*(q.depth>.4?10:5));if(!horizonWaterStrip(x,y,len,3,q.kind))continue;const phase=reduced?.55:(t*.62+seed)%1;
        g.fillStyle(ink,.24).fillRect(x,y+2,len,1);g.fillStyle(col,(q.depth>.45?.27:.42)*(rain?.75:1)).fillRect(x,y,len,1);g.fillStyle(col,.24).fillRect(x+1,y+1,Math.max(2,len-3),1);
        if(!reduced&&(rain||wind)&&phase<.5&&horizonWaterStrip(x-2,y-2,len+5,6,q.kind)){g.fillStyle(col,.40*(1-phase)).fillRect(x-2,y-2,2,1).fillRect(x+len+1,y+2,2,1);}}
      n++;
    }return n;
  }
  // Local ground-level clouds: only terrain below the observer, no overlay over the player.
  function horizonAir(scene,view,w,t,quality,reduced){
    const p=scene.player,h=C.elevationSample(p.x/T,p.y/T);if(!h||h.elevation<110)return 0;
    const r=scene.cameras.main.worldView,g=view.nearFog,night=w.clock.minuteOfDay>=1170||w.clock.minuteOfDay<375,wet=['mist','rain','storm'].includes(w.weather.kind);let n=0;
    const stride=reduced?80:56,cap=reduced?18:48;
    for(let y=Math.max(0,Math.floor(r.y/stride)*stride);y<Math.min(H*T,r.bottom)&&n<cap;y+=stride)for(let x=Math.max(0,Math.floor(r.x/stride)*stride);x<Math.min(W*T,r.right)&&n<cap;x+=stride){
      const seed=C.hash(x,y,9741),xx=x+stride/2+(reduced?0:Math.sin(t*.19+seed*8)*8),yy=y+stride/2;
      if(Math.hypot(xx-p.x,yy-p.y)<95||seed<.34)continue;
      const z=C.frontierAltitude(xx/T,yy/T),delta=h.elevation-z;if(delta<90)continue;
      const alpha=Math.min(.15,delta/2400)*(wet?1.2:.76),c=night?0x90acbe:0xc4d6d0;
      // Three irregular clusters rather than a screen-sized fog rectangle.
      g.fillStyle(c,alpha*.65).fillEllipse(xx-10,yy,49,13);g.fillStyle(c,alpha).fillEllipse(xx+7,yy-2,55,15);g.fillStyle(c,alpha*.5).fillEllipse(xx+20,yy+4,31,10);n++;
    }return n;
  }
  // Seven distinct, flat/low courtyard finishes, within existing art footprint.
  function horizonCourt(ctx,p,era,stage){
    const r=(c,x,y,w,h)=>box(ctx,c,x,y,w,h),rng=random(Math.floor(p.x*173+p.y*71)+9761);
    ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(31,131,138,17);ctx.clip();
    if(stage<8){for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const x=34+Math.floor(rng()*129),y=133+Math.floor(rng()*13);if(stage<3){r('#435840',x,y,4,2);r('#81935c',x+1,y-1,2,2);}else{r('#675c47',x,y,5,2);r('#b4a37c',x+1,y,3,1);}}ctx.restore();return;}
    const palettes=[['#5f634d','#8b8d69','#b1ac82'],['#6d6350','#a29173','#c4b48e'],['#5b674f','#899174','#c7c9a0'],['#665d51','#a29680','#d1c5a4'],['#535e5c','#879891','#c4ccc0'],['#655b58','#ab9f91','#e3cfab'],['#586455','#a8ae8c','#ebdcb0']];
    const [d,m,l]=palettes[era]||palettes[0];
    for(let y=132;y<148;y+=4)for(let x=32;x<169;x+=6){const off=(Math.floor(y/4)%2)*3,xx=x+off;if(era===0&&rng()<.45)continue;r(d,xx,y,6,4);r(m,xx+1,y,5,3);r(l,xx+1,y,4,1);if(era>=3&&((x-32)/6+Math.floor(y/4))%4===0)r(l,xx+2,y+2,2,1);}
    if(era>=1){r(d,35,146,130,2);r(l,35,146,130,1);}
    if(era>=2)for(const x of [44,148]){r('#3e5340',x,134,9,7);for(let i=0;i<4;i++){r('#75905d',x+i*2,135-i%2,2,3);r(era>=5?'#e9c991':'#c0bb7b',x+i*2,134-i%2,1,1);}}
    if(era>=4){for(const x of [61,132]){r('#394c50',x,135,8,7);r('#819894',x+1,136,6,4);r('#c0d0bf',x+1,136,6,1);}}
    if(era>=5){for(const x of [76,118]){r(d,x,140,8,5);r(l,x+1,141,6,1);r(m,x+2,142,4,2);}}
    if(era===6){for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const x=89+i*3;r('#dfce8c',x,140-Math.abs(3-i),3,2);r('#6d8c77',x,143+Math.abs(3-i),3,1);}r('#f4e3ad',98,138,3,8);}
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function horizonSkyMode(w){const m=w.clock.minuteOfDay;if(m<350||m>=1200)return 'night';if(m<550)return 'dawn';if(m>=1040)return 'dusk';if(['mist','rain','storm','snow'].includes(w.weather.kind))return 'mist';return 'day';}

  function paintWild(ctx,cx,cy,w,h,includeLegacy=false){
    const size=C.WorldConfig.chunkSize*T,ox=cx*size,oy=cy*size;ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(0,0,w,h);ctx.clip();ctx.translate(-ox,-oy);
    for(let y=oy;y<oy+h;y+=8){const ty=(y+4)/T,[a,b]=C.shore(ty);for(let x=ox;x<ox+w;x+=8){if(!includeLegacy&&C.legacy(x/T,y/T))continue;
      const tx=(x+4)/T,weights=C.biomeWeights(tx,ty),road=trailVisualRoad(tx,ty),bank=Math.min(Math.abs(tx-a),Math.abs(tx-b)),water=tx>=a&&tx<b,r=C.hash(x,y,27),bridge=C.landscapeBridge(tx,ty);
      if(bridge){bridgePixel(ctx,x,y,bridge,r);continue;}
      let col=wildColor(x+4,y+4,weights);
      const high=C.elevationSample(tx,ty);if(high?.id&&!water){col=high.east?districtReliefColor(col,x+4,y+4,high):hillColor(col,x+4,y+4,high);}else if(high&&high.edge>0&&!water){const blendZ=smooth((high.elevation-80)/520);col=blend(col,[190,203,197],blendZ*.66*high.edge);}
      let unpaved=col;
      if(water){const shallow=smooth(1-bank/.78),flow=wildNoise(x,y,28,81);col=blend([33+flow*8,75+flow*15,84+flow*12],[102,146,125],shallow);}
      else {if(bank<.54)col=blend(col,[141,124,83],smooth(1-bank/.54)*.75);unpaved=col;col=roadTint(col,road,x+4,y+4,weights);}
      if(water||!roadFineEdge(ctx,x,y,unpaved,road,weights))box(ctx,rgb(col.map(v=>v+(r-.5)*5)),x,y,8,8);detailGround(ctx,x,y,col,r,weights,road,water,bank);terrainFineDetail(ctx,x,y,col,r,weights,road,water);v91TerrainDetail(ctx,x,y,col,r,weights,road,water,bank);if(!water&&high)horizonRelief(ctx,x,y,col,high);
      if(water){if(bank<.25)box(ctx,'#a4bca1',x+2,y+1,4,1);else if(r>.91)box(ctx,'#64999b',x,y+3,5,1);
        for(const deck of C.landscapeBridges){const below=(y+4)-deck.to*T;if(below<0||below>16||tx<deck.left+.4||tx>deck.right-.4)continue;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.38*(1-below/18);box(ctx,'#243d3d',x,y,8,8);ctx.restore();if(Math.floor(x/8)%5===1&&below<12){box(ctx,'#413d32',x+1,y,5,8);box(ctx,'#827152',x+1,y,2,8);box(ctx,'#a1b1a1',x+5,y+6,3,1);}}}

      else if(road&&road.distance<road.width+.22){roadDetails(ctx,x,y,road,weights,r);}
      else if(r>.72){const shade=mixField(weights,k=>wildMaterials[k].shade),sun=mixField(weights,k=>wildMaterials[k].sun);box(ctx,rgb(blend(col,shade,.36)),x+1,y+4,2+r*3,2);if(r>.86)box(ctx,rgb(blend(col,sun,.48)),x+3,y+2,3,1);}
    }}
    heritageForecourt(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);skywayGroundFinish(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);
    const startX=Math.floor((ox-36)/24)*24,startY=Math.floor((oy-36)/24)*24;
    for(let gy=startY;gy<oy+h+36;gy+=24)for(let gx=startX;gx<ox+w+36;gx+=24){const x=gx+Math.floor(C.hash(gx,gy,127)*19),y=gy+Math.floor(C.hash(gx,gy,128)*19),tx=x/T,ty=y/T;
      if(x<0||y<0||x>=W*T||y>=H*T||(C.legacy(tx,ty)&&Math.min(C.WorldConfig.baseWidth-tx,C.WorldConfig.baseHeight-ty)>6))continue;const weights=C.biomeWeights(tx,ty),region=ecoChoice(weights,C.hash(gx,gy,151)),m=wildMaterials[region],road=trailVisualRoad(tx,ty),[a,b]=C.shore(ty),bank=Math.min(Math.abs(tx-a),Math.abs(tx-b));if(road&&road.distance<road.width+.8||tx>a-.45&&tx<b+.45||C.landscapeBridge(tx,ty))continue;
      const court=nearCourt(x,y);if(court<.94||C.eastCourt(tx,ty)||C.eastPool(tx,ty))continue;const habitat=wildNoise(x,y,118,91)*.73+wildNoise(x,y,48,92)*.27,r=C.hash(gx,gy,129),density=Object.entries(weights).reduce((s,[k,v])=>s+v*wildMaterials[k].density,0);if(r>density*(.12+1.12*smooth((habitat-.22)/.63))*smooth((court-.90)/.38))continue;
      const rng=random(Math.floor(C.hash(gx,gy,130)*4294967295)),p=m.leaves;
      const grove=wildNoise(x,y,145,312),understory=wildNoise(x,y,60,313);let kind=region==='frost'?(grove<.40?'stone':'snow'):region==='ridge'?(grove<.42?'stone':'grass'):region==='marsh'?(bank<1.4?'reed':grove<.5?'fern':'grass'):region==='basin'?(grove>.60&&understory>.43?'flowers':grove<.30?'fern':'grass'):(grove<.25?'shrub':grove<.48?'fern':grove<.57&&understory>.57?'mushroom':grove>.76?'flowers':'grass');
      if(region==='heath')kind=grove>.4?'flowers':'grass';else if(region==='coppervale')kind=grove<.64?'stone':'grass';else if(region==='glasswater')kind=grove>.45?'reed':'fern';else if(region==='goldgrass')kind=grove>.65?'flowers':'grass';else if(region==='moonbloom')kind=grove>.54?'mushroom':'fern';
      if(!eastPlant(ctx,x,y,region,grove,rng))wildPatch(ctx,x,y,kind,p,rng);
    }
    forestGroundFinish(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);
    for(const r of root.LQValleyDevelopment.reserves)if(r.x*T>ox-320&&r.x*T<ox+w+320&&r.y*T>oy-320&&r.y*T<oy+h+320)reserveApproach(ctx,r);
    for(const r of root.LQValleyDevelopment.tunnelPortals)if(r.x*T>ox-700&&r.x*T<ox+w+700&&r.y*T>oy-700&&r.y*T<oy+h+700)reserveApproach(ctx,r);
    hillTerrain(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);districtReliefTerrain(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);
    hillEscarpments(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);
    summitTerrain(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);
    riverFinish(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);
    waterwayTerrain(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);
    landmarkGround(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);eastGroundDetail(ctx,ox,oy,w,h);ctx.restore();if(!includeLegacy&&ox<C.WorldConfig.baseWidth*T&&oy<C.WorldConfig.baseHeight*T)ctx.clearRect(0,0,Math.min(w,C.WorldConfig.baseWidth*T-ox),Math.min(h,C.WorldConfig.baseHeight*T-oy));
  }

  // Composite the identical world-coordinate painter into the old hamlet's outer belt.
  // Preserve original trails, portal and archaeological footprints; density now fades on both sides.
  function legacyTransition(ctx){
    const size=C.WorldConfig.chunkSize*T,w=C.WorldConfig.baseWidth*T,h=C.WorldConfig.baseHeight*T;
    const oldSegments=TRAILS.flatMap(trail=>trail.slice(1).map((b,i)=>{const a=trail[i],dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1];return {x:a[0]+.5,y:a[1]+.5,dx,dy,inv:1/(dx*dx+dy*dy||1)};}));
    function originalDistance(x,y){let d=Infinity;for(const s of oldSegments){const px=x-s.x,py=y-s.y,t=clamp01((px*s.dx+py*s.dy)*s.inv);d=Math.min(d,(px-s.dx*t)**2+(py-s.dy*t)**2);}return Math.sqrt(d);}
    for(let cy=0;cy<Math.ceil(h/size);cy++)for(let cx=0;cx<Math.ceil(w/size);cx++){
      const ox=cx*size,oy=cy*size,cw=Math.min(size,w-ox),ch=Math.min(size,h-oy);
      if(ox+cw<=w-7*T&&oy+ch<=h-7*T)continue;
      const d=ctx.__lqDetailScale||1,layer=document.createElement('canvas');layer.width=cw*d;layer.height=ch*d;const lc=layer.getContext('2d');detailPaint(lc,d,c=>paintWild(c,cx,cy,cw,ch,true));
      // Use an actual alpha mask, not repeated translucent source-over fills.
      const mask=document.createElement('canvas');mask.width=cw*d;mask.height=ch*d;const mc=mask.getContext('2d');mc.scale(d,d);
      for(let y=0;y<ch;y+=8)for(let x=0;x<cw;x+=8){const tx=(ox+x+4)/T,ty=(oy+y+4)/T,d=Math.min(C.WorldConfig.baseWidth-tx,C.WorldConfig.baseHeight-ty),jitter=(wildNoise(ox+x,oy+y,95,447)-.5)*1.4;
        let a=smooth((6.5-d+jitter*smooth(d/2))/6.0);
        if(a<=0)continue;
        const road=trailVisualRoad(tx,ty),dist=originalDistance(tx,ty);
        if(root.LQValleyDevelopment.trailBuilt(tx,ty)&&dist<1.2&&(!road||road.distance>road.width+.25))a*=smooth((dist-.47)/.77);
        if(ty>=42&&tx>=24&&tx<=27)a*=.15; // existing English Town gate, not a new road destination
        if(ROCKS.some(rock=>rock.apron&&Math.abs(tx-rock.apron[1])<rock.apron[3]+.6&&Math.abs(ty-rock.apron[2])<rock.apron[4]+.6))a*=.12;
        mc.fillStyle=`rgba(0,0,0,${a})`;mc.fillRect(x,y,8,8);
      }
      lc.globalCompositeOperation='destination-in';lc.drawImage(mask,0,0);lc.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';ctx.drawImage(layer,ox,oy,cw,ch);
    }
  }

  const liveStreams=new WeakMap();
  const STREAM_SPARE_LIMIT=3,STREAM_POOL_LIMIT=80;
  function streamState(view){return view.streaming??={pending:[],signature:'',cache:new Map(),pool:[],serial:0,loads:0,releases:0,reused:0,textureHits:0,buildMs:0,peakMs:0};}
  function streamSprite(scene,view,x,y,key){
    const q=streamState(view),sprite=q.pool.pop();
    if(sprite){q.reused++;sprite.setTexture(key).setPosition(x,y).setOrigin(.5).setDepth(0).setAlpha(1).setScale(1).setFlip(false,false).setVisible(true).setActive(true);delete sprite.valleyTree;delete sprite.valleyOccludes;delete sprite.tunnelOccluder;return sprite;}
    return scene.add.image(x,y,key);
  }
  function releaseChunk(view,key,keep=true){
    const chunk=view.stream.get(key);if(!chunk)return;const scene=view.scene,q=streamState(view);
    for(const item of chunk.items){const i=scene.areaObjs.indexOf(item);if(i>=0)scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(!item.scene)continue;
      if(keep&&q.pool.length<STREAM_POOL_LIMIT){item.setVisible(false).setActive(false);item.setTexture('__MISSING');q.pool.push(item);}else item.destroy();}
    for(const id of chunk.ids){view.trees.delete(id);view.resources.delete(id);}
    view.stream.delete(key);q.releases++;
    if(keep)q.cache.set(key,{texture:chunk.texture,used:++q.serial});else if(scene.textures.exists(chunk.texture))scene.textures.remove(chunk.texture);
    while(q.cache.size>STREAM_SPARE_LIMIT){const [k,v]=q.cache.entries().next().value;q.cache.delete(k);if(scene.textures.exists(v.texture))scene.textures.remove(v.texture);}
  }
  function stream(scene,view,world,sim){
    if(!view?.stream||!scene.player)return;
    const q=streamState(view);liveStreams.set(scene,view);
    if(scene.inside){summitRelease(view);releaseDevelopment(view);if(view.stream.size||q.cache.size||q.pool.length)clearLandscape(view);return;}
    developmentObjects(view,world);
    const tiles=C.WorldConfig.chunkSize,size=tiles*T,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView;
    const minX=Math.max(0,Math.floor(rect.x/size)),minY=Math.max(0,Math.floor(rect.y/size)),maxX=Math.min(Math.ceil(W/tiles)-1,Math.floor(rect.right/size)),maxY=Math.min(Math.ceil(H/tiles)-1,Math.floor(rect.bottom/size));
    const px=Math.floor(scene.player.x/size),py=Math.floor(scene.player.y/size),signature=[minX,minY,maxX,maxY,px,py].join(',');
    if(signature!==q.signature){
      const dx=q.px===undefined?0:Math.sign(px-q.px),dy=q.py===undefined?0:Math.sign(py-q.py);q.px=px;q.py=py;q.signature=signature;
      const wanted=[];
      for(let cy=Math.max(0,minY-1);cy<=Math.min(Math.ceil(H/tiles)-1,maxY+1);cy++)for(let cx=Math.max(0,minX-1);cx<=Math.min(Math.ceil(W/tiles)-1,maxX+1);cx++){
        if((cx+1)*tiles<=C.WorldConfig.baseWidth&&(cy+1)*tiles<=C.WorldConfig.baseHeight)continue;
        const key=cx+','+cy;if(view.stream.has(key))continue;
        const visible=cx>=minX&&cx<=maxX&&cy>=minY&&cy<=maxY;
        wanted.push({cx,cy,key,priority:(visible?0:100)+Math.hypot(cx-px,cy-py)-.35*((cx-px)*dx+(cy-py)*dy)});
      }
      q.pending=wanted.sort((a,b)=>a.priority-b.priority);
      // Retain one extra ring to prevent rapid load/unload at a chunk boundary.
      for(const key of [...view.stream.keys()]){const [cx,cy]=key.split(',').map(Number);if(cx<minX-2||cx>maxX+2||cy<minY-2||cy>maxY+2)releaseChunk(view,key);}
    }
    if(!q.pending.length)return;
    const {cx,cy,key}=q.pending.shift();if(view.stream.has(key))return;
    const start=performance.now(),w=Math.min(size,W*T-cx*size),h=Math.min(size,H*T-cy*size),name='v91-desire-paths-material-'+key;
    if(q.cache.has(key)){q.textureHits++;q.cache.delete(key);}
    const tex=detailTexture(scene,name,w,h,ctx=>paintWild(ctx,cx,cy,w,h));
    const chunk={texture:tex,items:[],ids:[]};const add=(sprite)=>{scene.areaObjs.push(sprite);chunk.items.push(sprite);return sprite;};
    add(streamSprite(scene,view,cx*size,cy*size,tex).setOrigin(0).setDepth(-.1).setScale(1/detailFactor(tex)));
    for(const rock of C.outcropsByChunk.get(key)||[]){
      const b=C.bounds(rock.solid),pad=34,head=Math.ceil(rock.rise*T*1.22)+14;
      const left=Math.floor(b.x*T)-pad,top=Math.floor(b.y*T)-head;
      const w=Math.ceil(b.w*T)+pad*2,h=Math.ceil(b.h*T)+head+pad;
      const rockKey=texture(scene,'ecology-'+rock.id,w,h,ctx=>{
        ctx.translate(-left,-top);talus(ctx,rock);stratum(ctx,rock);
        if(rock.snow){const rng=random(geologySeed(rock.id)+337),crest=rock.solid.map(([x,y])=>[x*T,y*T-rock.rise*T*.93]);ctx.save();shape(ctx,'#c1d2cc',crest);ctx.clip();const r=C.bounds(crest);for(let i=0;i<r.w*r.h/110;i++){const x=r.x+rng()*r.w,y=r.y+rng()*r.h;box(ctx,pick(['#dbe2d3','#edf0dd','#a7bebd','#99b5b2'],rng),x,y,4+rng()*19,2+rng()*6);}ctx.restore();}
      });
      const sprite=add(streamSprite(scene,view,left,top,rockKey).setOrigin(0).setDepth(view.depthAt((b.y+b.h)*T)));if(rock.id==='outcrop2-4')sprite.tunnelOccluder=true;
    }
    for(const entry of C.chunks.get(key)||[]){const {x,y}=center(entry);chunk.ids.push(entry.id);
      if(entry.kind==='tree'){
        const snow=C.hash(entry.x,entry.y,528)<C.biomeWeights(entry.x,entry.y).frost;
        const mature=texture(scene,(snow?'snow-tree-':'tree-')+entry.species,130,126,ctx=>{
          tree(ctx,entry.species);
          if(snow){const img=ctx.getImageData(0,0,130,126),a=img.data;for(let yy=0;yy<92;yy++)for(let xx=0;xx<130;xx++){const j=(yy*130+xx)*4,r=a[j],g=a[j+1],b=a[j+2];if(a[j+3]>220&&g>r+6&&g>b-6){const patch=C.hash(Math.floor(xx/5),Math.floor(yy/3),entry.species+52),edge=yy<4||a[j-130*4*3+3]<80;if(edge||patch>.64){a[j]=Math.min(235,r*.45+127);a[j+1]=Math.min(240,g*.40+143);a[j+2]=Math.min(237,b*.45+150);}}}ctx.putImageData(img,0,0);}
        });
        const sprite=add(streamSprite(scene,view,x,y+4,mature).setOrigin(.5,112/126).setDepth(view.depthAt(y+4)));
        sprite.setFlipX(entry.visualSeed%5===0);sprite.valleyTree=entry.id;view.trees.set(entry.id,{sprite,mature,entry,stage:'mature'});
      }else{
        const k='node-'+(C.NODE_MATERIAL[entry.id]||entry.kind)+(entry.x>=168&&['rock','iron','stone'].includes(entry.kind)?'-'+C.eastBiome(entry.x,entry.y)+'-'+(Math.floor(entry.x+entry.y)%3):''),width=entry.kind==='berries'?56:66,height=44;
        const normal=texture(scene,k,width,height,ctx=>resource(ctx,entry,'full')),resting=texture(scene,k+'-spent',width,height,ctx=>resource(ctx,entry,'spent')),worked=entry.stock>1?texture(scene,k+'-worked',width,height,ctx=>resource(ctx,entry,'worked')):null;
        const sprite=add(streamSprite(scene,view,x,y,normal).setOrigin(.5,30/height).setDepth(view.depthAt(y)));view.resources.set(entry.id,{sprite,normal,resting,worked});
      }
    }
    view.stream.set(key,chunk);q.loads++;if(world)refresh(view,world,sim);
    const elapsed=performance.now()-start;q.buildMs+=elapsed;q.peakMs=Math.max(q.peakMs,elapsed);
  }
  function clearLandscape(view){const q=streamState(view);for(const key of [...view.stream.keys()])releaseChunk(view,key,false);for(const v of q.cache.values())if(view.scene.textures.exists(v.texture))view.scene.textures.remove(v.texture);q.cache.clear();for(const s of q.pool)if(s.scene)s.destroy();q.pool.length=0;q.pending.length=0;q.signature='';}
  function releaseStream(view){if(view){eastRelease(view);frontierRelease(view);clearTrailLayer(view);releaseTunnel(view);releaseReserves(view);removeMill(view);clearEchoSprites(view);summitRelease(view);natureClear(view,true);}releaseDevelopment(view);if(!view?.stream)return;clearLandscape(view);liveStreams.delete(view.scene);}
  function streamStats(scene){const view=liveStreams.get(scene);if(!view)return null;const q=streamState(view);return {active:view.stream.size,queued:q.pending.length,cached:q.cache.size,pool:q.pool.length,loads:q.loads,releases:q.releases,reused:q.reused,textureHits:q.textureHits,avgLoadMs:q.loads?q.buildMs/q.loads:0,peakLoadMs:q.peakMs,activeKeys:[...view.stream.keys()]};}

  // Axonometric survey (not a second live game camera). It projects the existing world data,
  // including actual resource states, roads, terrain heights and restored buildings.
  function renderValleyVista(world){const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment,S=root.LQValleySimulation,cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=768;cv.height=440;const ctx=cv.getContext('2d');ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;const dark=world.clock.minuteOfDay<375||world.clock.minuteOfDay>=1170,wet=world.weather.kind==='rain',mist=world.weather.kind==='mist';const bg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,440);bg.addColorStop(0,dark?'#142e41':wet?'#667f85':'#728f92');bg.addColorStop(1,dark?'#243d43':wet?'#aec1b7':'#c2cbb1');ctx.fillStyle=bg;ctx.fillRect(0,0,768,440);
    const stride=2,sx=2.23,sy=1.01,originX=335,originY=45,project=(x,y,z)=>[originX+(x-y)*sx,originY+(x+y)*sy-z*.116];const nx=Math.ceil(C.W/stride)+1,ny=Math.ceil(C.H/stride)+1,grid=Array.from({length:ny},(_,j)=>Array.from({length:nx},(_,i)=>{const x=Math.min(C.W,i*stride),y=Math.min(C.H,j*stride),v=C.groundSample(Math.min(x,C.W-.01),Math.min(y,C.H-.01)),z=Math.max(0,v.elevation||0);return {x,y,z,p:project(x,y,z)};}));
    const shade=(col,a,z=0)=>{const haze=.07+(1-z/660)*.035+(mist?.11:wet?.05:0);let result=col.map((v,i)=>v*a*(dark?[.53,.65,.80][i]:1));return rgb(blend(result,dark?[48,71,87]:[159,184,177],Math.max(0,haze)));};
    const zAt=(x,y)=>Math.max(0,C.groundSample(Math.min(C.W-.01,Math.max(0,x)),Math.min(C.H-.01,Math.max(0,y))).elevation||0);
    // Tile faces are sorted by the view-depth axis, not by load order or random seed.
    for(let d=0;d<nx+ny-2;d++)for(let j=Math.max(0,d-(nx-2));j<=Math.min(ny-2,d);j++){const i=d-j,a=grid[j][i],b=grid[j][i+1],c=grid[j+1][i+1],e=grid[j+1][i],x=(a.x+c.x)/2,y=(a.y+c.y)/2,z=(a.z+b.z+c.z+e.z)/4,[left,right]=C.shore(y);const water=x>=left&&x<=right||!!C.cascadeSample(x,y),weights=C.biomeWeights(x,y),col=water?[56,110,127]:wildColor(x*T,y*T,weights),slope=(a.z+e.z-b.z-c.z)*.009,light=Math.max(.60,Math.min(1.22,1+slope)),r=C.hash(i,j,917);shape(ctx,shade(col,light*(.96+r*.1),z),[a.p,b.p,c.p,e.p]);if(!water&&Math.max(a.z,b.z,c.z,e.z)-Math.min(a.z,b.z,c.z,e.z)>50){line(ctx,shade([159,159,140],.8,z),a.p[0],a.p[1],b.p[0],b.p[1],1);}else if(water&&r>.70)line(ctx,shade([136,188,186],1,z),a.p[0]+1,a.p[1]+1,b.p[0]-1,b.p[1]+1,1);}
    // River profile and road polylines reference the same functions as gameplay.
    for(const r of C.landscapeRoads){if(r.points.length<2)continue;ctx.strokeStyle=dark?'#a59a82':'#c9b28b';ctx.lineWidth=r.kind==='main'?2.6:1.45;ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();let first=true;for(let i=1;i<r.points.length;i++){const a=r.points[i-1],b=r.points[i],n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1])/1.1));for(let k=0;k<=n;k++){const f=k/n,x=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*f,y=a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*f,p=project(x,y,zAt(x,y));if(!D.trailBuilt(x,y)){first=true;continue;}first?ctx.moveTo(...p):ctx.lineTo(...p);first=false;}}ctx.stroke();}
    for(const [id,n]of Object.entries(D.trailWear(world))){const e=D.trailDecode(id);if(!e||n<4)continue;const x=e.x/T,y=e.y/T,bx=e.bx/T,by=e.by/T,a=project(x,y,zAt(x,y)),b=project(bx,by,zAt(bx,by));line(ctx,dark?"#a99a80":"#c6aa7a",...a,...b,n>=12?2:1);}
    for(const r of D.reserves){const [x,y]=project(r.x,r.y,zAt(r.x,r.y)),stage=D.reserveState(world,r.id).stage;shape(ctx,stage===3?'#9ebf82':stage>=1?'#a69b6f':'#857d64',[[x-5,y],[x,y-3],[x+6,y],[x+1,y+3]]);for(let i=0;i<3&&stage===3;i++)box(ctx,'#e6d49b',x-3+i*3,y-1+(i%2),1,1); }
    const objects=Object.values(C.TREES_BY_ID).map(t=>({x:t.x+.5,y:t.y+.5,tree:t}));for(const h of HOUSES)objects.push({x:h.x+h.w/2,y:h.y+h.h/2,house:h});for(const l of D.landmarks)objects.push({x:l.x+1.6,y:l.y+.2,site:l});objects.push({x:C.SUMMIT.x+.5,y:C.SUMMIT.y+.5,summit:true});objects.push({x:C.MILL.x,y:C.MILL.y,mill:true});objects.push({x:32.5,y:29.5,workshop:true},{x:34.5,y:35.5,annex:true});objects.sort((a,b)=>a.x+a.y-b.x-b.y);
    const labels=[];function miniBuilding(x,y,stage,pal,kind){const wide=kind==='house'?12:kind==='river'?11:8,h=stage>=8?(kind==='frost'?13:kind==='ridge'?15:10):Math.min(9,3+stage);const X=Math.round(x),Y=Math.round(y);shape(ctx,'#2f493d66',[[X-wide,Y],[X+3,Y-4],[X+wide+7,Y+1],[X+4,Y+5]]);if(stage<4){shape(ctx,'#776e58',[[X-wide/2,Y],[X,Y-3],[X+wide/2,Y],[X+4,Y+3]]);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)box(ctx,'#aaa78d',X-4+i*3,Y-2+i%2,2,2);return;}
      shape(ctx,pal[1],[[X-wide/2,Y-h],[X+1,Y-h+3],[X+1,Y+3],[X-wide/2,Y]]);shape(ctx,pal[0],[[X+1,Y-h+3],[X+wide/2+2,Y-h],[X+wide/2+2,Y],[X+1,Y+3]]);if(stage>=6){shape(ctx,pal[2],[[X-wide/2-2,Y-h],[X-2,Y-h-6],[X+wide/2+3,Y-h],[X+1,Y-h+3]]);line(ctx,pal[3],X-wide/2-2,Y-h,X-2,Y-h-6,1);}else{line(ctx,'#bdad86',X-4,Y-h,X+4,Y-2,1);line(ctx,'#ddc28e',X+3,Y-h,X-4,Y-2,1);}if(stage>=8){box(ctx,dark?'#f5ce76':'#35463f',X-2,Y-h+5,2,3);box(ctx,'#493b32',X+3,Y-4,2,4);}}
    for(const o of objects){const z=zAt(o.x,o.y),[x,y]=project(o.x,o.y,z);if(o.tree){const s=S.treeStage(world,o.tree);if(s==='stump'){box(ctx,'#77614d',x-1,y-1,2,2);continue;}const small=s==='sapling'||s==='young',n=small?3:5+Number(o.tree.protected)*2,conifer=[1,6,8].includes(o.tree.species)||C.biomeWeights(o.x,o.y).frost>.4;box(ctx,'#283e334d',x-2,y,7,2);box(ctx,dark?'#4d4c3e':'#6b6146',x,y-3,1,4);const p=conifer?['#254c41','#416a59','#8da99a']:['#355b41','#567b4f','#9aa974'];const cols=p.map((v,i)=>dark?['#233c3d','#345657','#668079'][i]:v);if(conifer){for(let k=0;k<2;k++)shape(ctx,cols[k],[[x,y-n-5+k*3],[x+n-k,y-2+k],[x-n+k,y-2+k]]);}else{shape(ctx,cols[0],[[x-n,y-4],[x-n+1,y-n-3],[x-1,y-n-5],[x+n-1,y-n-2],[x+n,y-4],[x+1,y]]);shape(ctx,cols[1],[[x-n+1,y-n-3],[x-1,y-n-5],[x+n-2,y-n-2],[x+1,y-3],[x-n+1,y-4]]);}box(ctx,cols[2],x-2,y-n-3,3,1);continue;}
      let stage=9,kind='house',pal=['#6d6254','#b4a080','#7d624d','#e2c18a'],label='';if(o.site){stage=D.heritageState(world,o.site.id).stage;kind=o.site.style;pal={forest:['#405a48','#849779','#627f52','#c0caa1'],river:['#735636','#b49a6b','#44716c','#d4bc86'],ridge:['#556369','#a0ada6','#79806f','#d2cbac'],marsh:['#65664a','#a6a17a','#87754a','#c9c19a'],frost:['#5e747c','#a9bdbe','#7e999d','#e1e2cd'],glade:['#516e60','#9eae91','#8d876f','#decf9e']}[kind];label=({forest:'Rootbound',river:'Silverwater',ridge:'Blackstone',marsh:'Reed Archive',frost:'Observatory',glade:'Last Glade'})[kind];}else if(o.mill){stage=D.millState(world).stage;kind='river';label='Watermill';pal=['#665746','#ac997d','#83664d','#d9b986'];}else if(o.summit){stage=D.summitState(world).stage;kind='frost';label='Cloudrest';}else if(o.house?.id==='ruined-warehouse'){stage=D.canUse(world)?8:1;label='Forgotten Hamlet';}else if(o.annex)stage=world.development.build.stage===7?8:Math.min(7,world.development.build.stage);else if(o.workshop)stage=D.canUse(world)?8:0;miniBuilding(x,y,stage,pal,kind);if(label)labels.push({x,y,label,stage});}
    const j=D.courierState(world).job;if(j){const p=j.path[j.cursor],x=p[0]/2,y=p[1]/2,[a,b]=project(x,y,zAt(x,y));box(ctx,'#493e2b',a-2,b-2,4,4);box(ctx,'#ffdb7d',a-1,b-2,3,3);}
    // Tiny cartographic labels are a separate annotation layer, not gameplay buildings.
    ctx.font='11px Georgia';ctx.textAlign='center';for(const p of labels){const yy=Math.round(p.y-22),xx=Math.round(p.x),width=ctx.measureText(p.label).width+10;box(ctx,'#203631dc',xx-width/2,yy-9,width,15);ctx.fillStyle=p.stage>=8?'#f4dab1':'#c6c6af';ctx.fillText(p.label,xx,yy+2);box(ctx,p.stage>=8?'#b8d78a':p.stage?'#d4a766':'#8e9384',xx-width/2+2,yy-7,2,10);}
    // Framing and engraved compass; no external backdrop.
    ctx.strokeStyle='#d7d3af';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeRect(9,9,750,422);ctx.strokeStyle='#435a50';ctx.strokeRect(12,12,744,416);ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.fillStyle=dark?'#e6d5ab':'#253f38';ctx.font='17px Georgia';ctx.fillText('CLOUDREST  /  THE LIVING VALLEY',26,33);ctx.font='11px Georgia';ctx.fillText('CURRENT WORLD SURVEY  ·  DAY '+world.clock.day+'  ·  '+world.weather.kind.toUpperCase(),27,49);
    return cv.toDataURL('image/png');}

  root.LQValleyArt={detailScale,detailPaint,detailTexture,detailFactor,detailGround,paintWild,ground,detailFloor,cutawayPanelOpacity,labWingArt,libraryAnnexArt,monumentPlans,monumentRender,cutawayArt,cutawayOpacity,specialFacade,windowPhase,roomWindowScene,constructionArt,blueprintArt,paintTrailChunk,archiveFloorArt,archiveFurnitureArt,archiveHatchArt,galleryFloorArt,galleryExhibitArt,monumentStairArt,horizonSkyMode,summitSky,horizonWaterAt,horizonWaterStrip,horizonWaterEffects,horizonAir,horizonCourt,horizonRelief,gardenSpecies,gardenPlant,gardenPick,paintBuildingPreview:eastPaint,tunnelEnter,tunnelFrame,renderValleyVista,heritageRoomEnter,heritageRoomNear,heritageRoomFrame,landscapePixel: (x,y)=>wildColor(x,y),natureGuide,observeNature,naturePositions,natureStats:scene=>natureViews.get(scene)?.metrics??null,stream,streamStats,releaseStream,map,refresh,effects,roomWindow,inventoryIcon,toolIcon,toolPose,fell,QUALITY};
})(globalThis);

</script>
<script id="lq841-valley-costumes">

(function (root, create) {
  'use strict';
  const costumes = create();
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = costumes;
  else root.LQValleyCostumes = costumes;
})(globalThis, function () {
  'use strict';
  const colors = {
    arven: { cloth: 0x676277, edge: 0x92899a, trim: 0xab9678, leather: 0x624a3e },
    lyra: { cloth: 0x75866b, edge: 0x9aa583, trim: 0xb4a071, leather: 0x526251 },
    traveler: { cloth: 0x786047, edge: 0x9e8261, trim: 0xa9a083, leather: 0x4d4137 },
    resident: { cloth: 0x557775, edge: 0x86a193, trim: 0xc2a57d, leather: 0x574436 },
  };

  function appearance(id, world) {
    const outfit = id === 'mara' ? (world?.residents.mara ? 'resident' : 'traveler') : id;
    const wet = world?.weather.kind === 'rain';
    const suffix = id === 'mara' ? '-' + outfit : '';
    return { outfit, wet, key: id + suffix + (wet ? '-wet' : '') };
  }

  function paint(g, direction, frame, state) {
    const side = direction === 'side';
    const diagonal = direction === 'dside' || direction === 'uside';
    const back = direction === 'up' || direction === 'uside';
    const left = side ? 7 : diagonal ? 6 : 5;
    const right = side ? 17 : diagonal ? 18 : 19;
    const { outfit, wet } = state;
    const { cloth, edge, trim, leather } = colors[outfit];
    const tip = (color,x,y,w,h,alpha=1) => g.fillStyle(color,alpha).fillRect(x,y,w,h);
    const swing = frame===1 ? 1 : frame===2 ? -1 : 0;
    const belt = side ? 8 : left+2;
    const beltWidth = right-belt-1;

    tip(cloth,left+2,20,right-left-4,6,.52);
    tip(leather,belt,24,beltWidth,2);
    tip(edge,belt,23,beltWidth,1,.64);
    if (outfit === 'arven') {
      tip(trim,side?10:9,18,side?5:7,2);
      tip(0x786850,side?9:11,20,2,4);
      tip(0xa49763,side?12:right-5,21,1,2);
      tip(leather,side?6:left+1,23,4,5);
      tip(0xd6b483,side?7:left+2,23,2,2);
      tip(0x827086,right-5,25,3,2);
      tip(edge,right-5,25,1,2);
    } else if (outfit === 'lyra') {
      if (!back) {
        tip(0xbaa57d,belt+1,22,Math.max(3,beltWidth-2),6);
        tip(0x806b4b,belt+2,25,4,2);
        tip(0x60764d,belt+3,24,2,2);
      }
      tip(leather,right-5,24,4,4);
      tip(0xa0af74,right-4,23,2,2);
      if (!side) {
        tip(0x967956,left-2,25+swing,2,2);
        tip(0x90754e,right,25-swing,2,2);
      }
    } else if (outfit === 'traveler') {
      const bagX=side ? 5 : back ? 8 : right-6;
      const bagY=20+(back?0:1);
      const bagW=back&&!side?8:5;
      tip(leather,bagX,bagY,bagW,7);
      tip(0x997d5b,bagX+1,bagY,bagW-2,4);
      tip(0xb39a76,bagX,bagY-1,bagW,2);
      tip(trim,back?11:side?10:left+3,18,2,6);
      tip(0xb29980,left+2,26,3,2);
      tip(0x7b6c5b,left+2,27,3,1);
      const boot=side?9:(frame===2?13:6);
      tip(0x92785b,boot,31,4,1);
      tip(0xa08b6d,boot+1,32,2,1);
    } else {
      if (!back) {
        tip(0xa28668,belt,21,beltWidth,7);
        tip(0x805e44,belt+1,24,Math.max(3,beltWidth-2),4);
        tip(trim,belt+2,24,2,2);
      }
      tip(leather,right-5,25,4,3);
      tip(0xbcb5a5,right-4,23,2,3);
      tip(0x7f9690,left+2,19,4,1);
    }
    if(wet) {
      tip(0x253b42,left+1,21, right-left-2,4,.33);
      tip(0xa6bbc0,left+2,19,3,1,.62);
      tip(0x95afb3,right-4,25,2,1,.55);
    }
  }

  return { appearance, paint };
});

</script>
<script id="lq841-valley-play">

(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const C = root.LQValleyContent;
  const S = root.LQValleySimulation;
  const Art = root.LQValleyArt;
  const Costumes = root.LQValleyCostumes;
  const Kit = root.LQValleyEquipment;
  const Manager = root.LQForgottenValley;
  const { ID, TILE: T, POINTS, NODES, REGION, HOUSES, NPCS, QUARRY, center } = C;
  const debugTerrain = new URLSearchParams(root.location.search).get('valley-debug')==='1';
  const runs = new WeakMap();
  const linked = new WeakSet();
  const motion = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
  const storyDay = 'a-light-in-the-dark';
  const esc = value => String(value ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, char => (
    { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[char]
  ));
  const distance = (a, b) => Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y);
  const isValley = scene => scene?.area?.id === ID;
  const current = () => runs.get(root.__lifequestGetScene?.());
  const button = (label, action, disabled = false) =>
    `<button type="button" data-valley-action="${esc(action)}"${disabled ? ' disabled' : ''}>${esc(label)}</button>`;
  const title = (eyebrow, name, text = '') => (
    `<p class="fv-kicker">${esc(eyebrow)}</p><h2 id="fv-panel-title">${esc(name)}</h2>${text ? '<p class="fv-copy">' + esc(text) + '</p>' : ''}`
  );
  const qualityKey = 'lifequest-valley-graphics';
  function initialQuality() {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem(qualityKey);
      if (stored !== null && /^[0-3]$/.test(stored)) return Number(stored);
    } catch {}
    return motion.matches ? 0 : 2;
  }
  let ui;
  let audio;

  function mount() {
    if (ui) return ui;
    const shell = document.createElement('section');
    shell.id = 'fv-shell';
    shell.hidden = true;
    shell.innerHTML = `
      <header class="fv-hud">
        <div class="fv-title"><span class="fv-emblem" aria-hidden="true"></span><div><b>Forgotten Valley</b><small id="fv-region">Forgotten Hamlet</small></div></div>
        <div id="fv-time"></div>
        <div id="fv-resources" aria-label="Resources"></div>
        <button type="button" id="fv-menu" data-valley-action="menu" aria-label="Valley menu">Menu <span>≡</span></button>
      </header>
      <aside id="fv-objective" aria-label="Current objective"><b></b><span></span></aside>
      <button type="button" id="fv-act" data-valley-action="interact" hidden></button>
      <div id="fv-toast" role="status" hidden></div>
      <div id="fv-progress" hidden role="progressbar" aria-label="Gathering resource" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100"><span></span></div>
      <div id="fv-hotbar" role="toolbar" aria-label="Tool hotbar" hidden></div>
      <div id="fv-scrim" hidden>
        <section id="fv-panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="fv-panel-title" tabindex="-1">
          <button id="fv-close" type="button" data-valley-action="close" aria-label="Close">×</button>
          <div id="fv-body"></div>
        </section>
      </div>
      <section id="fv-cinematic" aria-label="Opening cinematic" hidden>
        <div class="fv-letterbox top"></div><div class="fv-letterbox bottom"></div>
        <div class="fv-subtitles"><p></p><strong></strong></div>
        <button type="button" id="fv-skip" data-valley-action="skip">Skip <kbd>Esc</kbd></button>
      </section>`;
    document.body.append(shell);
    const get = id => shell.querySelector('#fv-' + id);
    ui = Object.fromEntries(['time', 'region', 'resources', 'objective', 'act', 'toast', 'progress','hotbar',
      'scrim', 'panel', 'body', 'cinematic', 'menu', 'skip'].map(id => [id, get(id)]));
    ui.shell = shell;
    shell.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const action = event.target.closest('[data-valley-action]')?.dataset.valleyAction;
      const run = current();
      if (action && run) safeAction(run, action);
      else if (run && event.target === ui.scrim) close(run);
    });
    shell.addEventListener('submit', event => {
      const run = current();
      if(event.target.id==='fv-split'){
        event.preventDefault();
        if(run?.panel==='split-stack'){
          const count=Number(new FormData(event.target).get('quantity'));
          safeAction(run,'split-now:'+run.splitting+':'+count);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (event.target.id !== 'fv-english') return;
      event.preventDefault();
      const answer = new FormData(event.target).get('reply');
      if (run?.speaker) respond(run, run.speaker, intent(run.speaker, String(answer ?? '')));
    });
    shell.addEventListener('input',event=>{
      if(event.target.id!=='fv-pack-search')return;
      const run=current();
      if(run?.panel!=='inventory')return;
      run.search=event.target.value;
      const caret=event.target.selectionStart;
      openInventory(run,false);
      const node=ui.panel.querySelector('#fv-pack-search');
      node.focus({preventScroll:true});
      node.setSelectionRange(caret,caret);
    });
    shell.addEventListener('change',event=>{
      if(event.target.id!=='fv-pack-sort')return;
      const run=current();
      if(run?.panel==='inventory'){
        run.sort=event.target.value;
        openInventory(run,false);
        ui.panel.querySelector('#fv-pack-sort').focus({preventScroll:true});
      }
    });
    shell.addEventListener('dragstart',event=>{
      const origin=event.target.closest('[data-pack-drag]');
      if(!origin)return;
      event.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',origin.dataset.packDrag);
      event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';
    });
    shell.addEventListener('dragover',event=>{
      if(event.target.closest('[data-pack-drop]'))event.preventDefault();
    });
    shell.addEventListener('drop',event=>{
      const zone=event.target.closest('[data-pack-drop]')?.dataset.packDrop;
      if(!zone)return;
      event.preventDefault();
      const run=current();
      if(run)safeAction(run,'drag-'+zone+':'+event.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'));
    });
    root.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
      const run = current();
      if (!run || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey || event.altKey) return;
      const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
      if (run.film) {
        if (key === 'tab') return;
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        if (key === 'escape' || key === 'enter' || key === ' ') finishFilm(run);
        return;
      }
      if (run.panel) {
        if (key === 'escape') {
          event.preventDefault();
          close(run);
        } else if (key === 'tab') {
          const list = [...ui.panel.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled])')];
          const first = list[0], last = list.at(-1);
          if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || document.activeElement === ui.panel)) {
            event.preventDefault();
            last?.focus();
          } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
            event.preventDefault();
            first?.focus();
          }
        }
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        return;
      }
      if (event.isComposing || legacyPanel(run.scene) || run.scene.switching ||
          event.target?.isContentEditable || ['INPUT','TEXTAREA','SELECT'].includes(event.target?.tagName)) return;
      if (key==='escape' && run.pending) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        cancelGather(run,true);
      } else if (/^[1-5]$/.test(key) && run.world) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        if (!event.repeat) safeAction(run,'hotbar:'+(Number(key)-1));
      } else if (key === 'i' && !event.repeat) {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();
        openInventory(run, false);
      }
    }, true);
    const release=()=>{
      const run=current();
      if(!run)return;
      run.scene.input?.keyboard?.resetKeys();
      run.scene.pMoving=false;
      const controls=root.__townControls;
      if(controls)Object.assign(controls,{up:false,down:false,left:false,right:false,actionRequested:false});
      cancelGather(run);
    };
    root.addEventListener('blur',release);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)release();});
    return ui;
  }

  function legacyPanel(scene) {
    return scene.chat?.isOpen || scene.journal?.isOpen || scene.settings?.isOpen ||
      scene.quests?.isOpen || scene.helper?.isOpen || scene.briefing?.isOpen ||
      root.LQV8?.isPanelOpen?.() ||
      Boolean(document.querySelector('#l11-shell:not([hidden]),#lq81-modal:not([hidden]),#rpg-panel:not([hidden]),#v10-panel:not([hidden]),#lq-cc:not([hidden])'));
  }

  function keyboard(scene, active) {
    const input = scene.input?.keyboard;
    if (input) {
      input.resetKeys?.();
      input.enabled = active;
    }
    scene.pMoving = false;
    const controls = root.__townControls;
    if (controls) controls.actionRequested = false;
  }

  function panel(run, name, html) {
    flushTrail(run.scene);
    cancelGather(run);
    if (!run.panel) {
      run.refocus = document.activeElement;
      run.wasEnabled = run.scene.input.keyboard.enabled;
    }
    run.panel = name;
    ui.body.innerHTML = html;
    ui.scrim.hidden = false;
    ui.panel.classList.toggle('fv-dialogue', name === 'arven' || name === 'lyra');
    ui.act.hidden = true;
    ui.hotbar.hidden = true;
    keyboard(run.scene, false);
    ui.panel.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }

  function close(run) {
    if (!run.panel) return;
    ui.scrim.hidden = true;
    ui.body.replaceChildren();
    run.panel = null;
    run.speaker = null;
    keyboard(run.scene, run.wasEnabled !== false);
    if (run.refocus?.isConnected) run.refocus.focus?.({ preventScroll: true });
  }

  function toast(run, message) {
    ui.toast.textContent = message;
    ui.toast.hidden = false;
    run.toastUntil = run.time + 3200;
  }

  function refreshWorld(run) {
    Art.refresh(run.view,run.world,S);
    const {area}=run.scene;
    if(area.valleyPhysicsRef!==area.__lqPrecise){area.valleyResourceFeatures=new Set(Object.keys(C.FEATURES));area.valleyPhysicsRef=area.__lqPrecise;}
    const active=area.valleyResourceFeatures??=new Set(Object.keys(C.FEATURES));
    const changes=[];
    for(const [id,polygon] of Object.entries(C.FEATURES)){
      const wants=S.blocksResource(run.world,id);
      if(wants===active.has(id))continue;
      changes.push([id,wants?polygon:null]);
      if(wants)active.add(id);
      else active.delete(id);
    }
    const open=S.openPit(S.quarryStage(run.world));
    if(open!==active.has(QUARRY.id)) {
      changes.push([QUARRY.id,open?C.quarryPit():null]);
      if(open)active.add(QUARRY.id);
      else active.delete(QUARRY.id);
    }
    for(const [id,polygon] of D.features(run.world)){
      if(!!polygon===active.has(id))continue;
      changes.push([id,polygon]);if(polygon)active.add(id);else active.delete(id);
    }
    if(changes.length) {
      root.LQPrecisePhysics.setTerrainFeatures(area,changes);
      for(const actor of run.actors)actor.phase=null;
      if(changes.some(([,shape])=>shape)&&run.scene.player?.active&&!run.scene.switching&&!run.scene._lqSurface)run.scene.unstick();
    }
  }

  function sync(run) {
    const stored = run.scene.store.game.forgottenValley;
    if (run.ref === stored && run.world) return;
    run.world = Manager.getState();
    run.ref = stored;
    if (run.world) refreshWorld(run);
  }

  function edit(run, update) {
    Manager.update(update);
    sync(run);
    updateHud(run);
  }
  function react(run, action) {
    edit(run, world => S.react(world, action, node=>growthClear(run,node)));
  }

  function growthClear(run,node) {
    const {x,y,w,h}=C.bounds(C.FEATURES[node.id]);
    const near=actor=>actor.x>=x*T-8&&actor.x<=(x+w)*T+8&&actor.y>=y*T-8&&actor.y<=(y+h)*T+8;
    return (run.scene.inside||!near(run.scene.player))&&!run.actors.some(actor=>near(actor.sprite));
  }

  function makeActor(scene, engine, id, world, view) {
    const def = NPCS[id];
    const dest = id === 'mara' && !world.residents.mara ? def.waiting :
      def.sites[world ? S.phase(world.clock) : 'morning'];
    const task=id==='mara'&&world?root.LQValleyDevelopment.workerTask(world):null;
    const pos = center(task?task.path[task.cursor]:dest);
    const costume = Costumes.appearance(id,world);
    paintCostume(scene,engine,id,costume);
    const sprite = scene.add.image(pos.x, pos.y, 'ch_' + costume.key + '_down_0')
      .setOrigin(.5, engine.origin).setDepth(view.depthAt(pos.y));
    const label = scene.add.text(pos.x, pos.y - 36, def.name, {
      fontFamily: 'Georgia', fontSize: '13px', color: '#f8e5bd', backgroundColor: '#21312bc9',
      padding: { x: 5, y: 3 },
    }).setOrigin(.5, 1).setDepth(23);
    scene.areaObjs.push(sprite, label);
    return { id, sprite, label, face: engine.face(), path: [], costume: costume.key, phase: null };
  }

  function paintCostume(scene,engine,id,costume) {
    if(scene.textures.exists('ch_'+costume.key+'_down_0')) return;
    const look = NPCS[id].look;
    const palette = costume.outfit === 'resident' ? {...look,outfit:0x557775,pants:0x3e5450} : look;
    engine.sprite(scene,costume.key,palette,(gfx,dir,frame)=>Costumes.paint(gfx,dir,frame,costume));
  }

  function attach(scene, engine) {
    mount();
    Manager.enter(scene);
    const view = Art.map(scene);
    let world = null, error;
    try { world = Manager.readScene(scene); }
    catch (failure) { error = failure; }
    const run = {
      scene, engine, world, view, error, ref: scene.store.game.forgottenValley,
      panel: null, pending: null, film: null, actionId: 0, hotbarSignature: '',
      time: 0, clock: 0, redraw: 0, pose: 0, region: 'hamlet', regionAt: 0, toastUntil: 0,
      first: !world?.opening?.seen, reduced: motion.matches, quality: initialQuality(), lastHud: '',
    };
    run.actors = ['arven','lyra'].map(id => makeActor(scene,engine,id,world,view));
    if(debugTerrain) {
      run.debug=scene.add.graphics().setName('valley-debug-geometry').setDepth(53);
      scene.areaObjs.push(run.debug);
    }
    runs.set(scene, run);
    if (!linked.has(scene)) {
      linked.add(scene);
      scene.events.once('shutdown', () => { detach(scene); linked.delete(scene); });
    }
    ui.shell.hidden = false;
    ui.toast.hidden = true;
    ui.progress.hidden = true;
    ui.hotbar.hidden = !world;
    document.body.classList.add('fv-active');
    const gate = scene.add.text(25.5*T, 42.2*T, '↓  South road to English Town', {
      fontFamily: 'Georgia', fontSize: '14px', color: '#ead3a2', backgroundColor: '#28382bd9',
      padding: { x: 7, y: 4 },
    }).setOrigin(.5).setDepth(23);
    scene.areaObjs.push(gate);
    if (world) {
      refreshWorld(run);
      updateHud(run);
    } else {
      ui.time.textContent = '';
      ui.resources.textContent = '';
      ui.objective.querySelector('b').textContent = 'Valley save paused';
      ui.objective.querySelector('span').textContent = 'Use Menu → Progress backup to export or import progress.';
      ui.act.hidden = true;
      if (error || root.__lqSaveRecoveryError) toast(run,error?.message || root.__lqSaveRecoveryError);
    }
    return run;
  }

  function detach(scene) {
    const run = runs.get(scene);
    if (!run) return;
    if (run.film) finishFilm(run, false);
    close(run);
    ambientStop(run);
    clearCourierVisual(run);
    crossReset(scene);skywayReset(scene);clearSurveyor(run);clearFrontierActors(run);clearEastActors(run);apprenticeClear(run);clearDistrictRunner(run);
    for(const v of run.commonViews?.values()||[])if(v.scene)v.destroy();run.cityWorkFx?.destroy();run.pocketGround?.destroy();run.utilityGround?.destroy();run.infraGround?.destroy();for(const g of run.infraCars?.values()||[])g.destroy();for(const g of run.homeViews?.values()||[])for(const a of [g.sprite,g.light])a.destroy();
    Art.releaseStream(run.view);
    runs.delete(scene);
    ui.shell.hidden = true;
    document.body.classList.remove('fv-active');
    restoreSummitCamera(run,scene.cameras.main);
    scene.cameras.main.setFollowOffset(0,0);
    scene.player?.setDepth(10);
  }

  const toolCondition=info=>info.tool.durability===0?'broken':Kit.worn(info)?'worn':'ready';
  const itemName=info=>info?.kind==='tool'?info.definition.name:info?.entry?.[0] || 'Empty';
  function itemIcon(info) {
    return info.kind==='tool'?Art.toolIcon(info.definition.icon,toolCondition(info)):Art.inventoryIcon(info.id);
  }
  function renderHotbar(run) {
    const world=run.world;
    const {hotbar,selected}=world.equipment;
    const rows=hotbar.map(ref=>Kit.itemInfo(world,ref));
    const signature=selected+';'+rows.map(info=>info?[info.ref,info.amount,info.tool?.durability,info.tool?.maxDurability].join(':'):'').join('|');
    if(signature===run.hotbarSignature)return;
    run.hotbarSignature=signature;
    const focused=ui.hotbar.contains(document.activeElement)?document.activeElement.dataset.valleyAction:null;
    ui.hotbar.innerHTML=rows.map((info,index)=>{
      const tool=info?.tool;
      const amount=tool?tool.durability+'/'+tool.maxDurability:info?.amount;
      const condition=tool?toolCondition(info):'';
      const name=itemName(info);
      const label='Slot '+(index+1)+' · '+name+(info?' · '+amount:'')+
        (condition==='broken'?' · Broken':condition==='worn'?' · Low durability':'');
      return '<button type="button" class="fv-slot '+condition+'" data-valley-action="hotbar:'+index+
        '" aria-pressed="'+(index===selected)+'" aria-label="'+esc(label)+'" title="'+esc(label)+'">'+
        '<span class="fv-slot-key">'+(index+1)+'</span>'+
        (info?'<img src="'+itemIcon(info)+'" alt=""><small>'+esc(amount)+'</small>':'<span class="fv-slot-empty">·</span>')+
        (tool?'<span class="fv-slot-wear" style="--wear:'+Math.floor(tool.durability/tool.maxDurability*100)+'%"></span>':'')+
        '</button>';
    }).join('');
    if(focused)ui.hotbar.querySelector('[data-valley-action="'+focused+'"]')?.focus({preventScroll:true});
  }

  function selectSlot(run, slot) {
    edit(run,world=>Kit.select(world,world.equipment.selected===slot?null:slot));
    if(run.pending)cancelGather(run,true);
  }

  function updateHud(run) {
    const world = run.world;
    if (!world) return;
    renderHotbar(run);
    const { day, minuteOfDay } = world.clock;
    const objective = S.objective(world);
    const r = world.resources;
    const marker=world.development.marker&&D.byId[world.development.marker];
    let region = run.scene.inside?.b.name || REGION[run.region].name;
    if(!run.scene.inside&&!C.legacy(run.scene.player.x/T,run.scene.player.y/T)){
      const labels={...Object.fromEntries(C.EAST_REGIONS.map(r=>[r.id,r.name])),heartwood:'Heartwood',deepwoods:'Southern Glade',basin:'Silverwater',marsh:'Silent Marsh',ridge:'Blackstone',frost:'Frostbound'};
      const blend=Object.entries(C.biomeWeights(run.scene.player.x/T,run.scene.player.y/T)).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
