      region=blend[1][1]>.22?labels[blend[0][0]]+' / '+labels[blend[1][0]]+' · Borderlands':REGION[blend[0][0]].name;
    }
    const height=!run.scene.inside&&C.elevationSample(run.scene.player.x/T,run.scene.player.y/T);if(height&&height.edge>.01){if(height.id)region=height.name||C.HIGHLANDS.find(h=>h.id===height.id)?.name||region;region+=' · '+Math.round(height.elevation)+' m';}
    if(activeCrossing(run.scene)){const b=activeCrossing(run.scene);region=b.name+' · upper deck · +'+Math.round(C.crossingZ(b,run.scene.player.x/T))+' units';}
    if(run.scene._lqSurface==='skyway')region='Windward Skyway · upper deck · +'+Math.round(C.skywayHeight(run.scene.player.x/T))+' units';
    const direction=marker?['N','NE','E','SE','S','SW','W','NW'][(Math.round(Math.atan2(marker.x+.5-run.scene.player.x/T,-(marker.y+.5-run.scene.player.y/T))*4/Math.PI)+8)%8]:'';
    const markText=marker&&!run.scene.inside?' · '+marker.name+' '+direction+' '+Math.round(Math.hypot(run.scene.player.x/T-marker.x-.5,run.scene.player.y/T-marker.y-.5))+' tiles':'';
    if(height&&height.edge>.01)region+=" · "+C.ALTITUDE_BANDS[C.altitudeBand(height.elevation)][0];
    const trailText=trailHud(run);
    const signature = [day, minuteOfDay, world.weather.kind, objective.detail, region, markText,trailText, r.wood, r.stone, r.food].join('|');
    if (signature === run.lastHud) return;
    run.lastHud = signature;
    const time = String(Math.floor(minuteOfDay / 60)).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(minuteOfDay % 60).padStart(2, '0');
    ui.time.innerHTML = '<b>Day ' + day + '</b> <span>' + time + ' · ' + esc(S.seasonAt(world.clock)) + ' · ' + esc(world.weather.kind) + '</span>';
    ui.region.textContent = region+markText+trailText;
    ui.resources.innerHTML = ['wood', 'stone', 'food'].map(key =>
      '<span><i style="color:' + C.INVENTORY[key][6] + '" aria-hidden="true">' + C.INVENTORY[key][5] +
      '</i> ' + (r[key] || 0) + ' <em>' + key + '</em></span>').join('');
    ui.objective.querySelector('b').textContent = objective.title;
    ui.objective.querySelector('span').textContent = objective.detail;
  }

  function laborRoute(run){
    const actor=run.actors.find(a=>a.id==='mara'),area=run.scene.area;
    if(!actor||!run.world.residents.mara)throw new Error('Mara has not joined the village.');
    const start={x:Math.floor(actor.sprite.x/T),y:Math.floor(actor.sprite.y/T)};
    function leg(a,b){const key=p=>p.y*area.w+p.x,prev=new Map([[key(a),null]]),q=[a];
      for(let i=0;i<q.length&&i<5000;i++){const at=q[i];if(at.x===b.x&&at.y===b.y){const out=[];let k=key(at);while(k!==null){out.push({x:k%area.w,y:Math.floor(k/area.w)});k=prev.get(k);}return out.reverse();}
        for(const [dx,dy] of [[1,0],[0,1],[-1,0],[0,-1]]){const v={x:at.x+dx,y:at.y+dy};if(v.x<0||v.y<0||v.x>=area.w||v.y>=area.h||area.blocked[v.y][v.x]||prev.has(key(v)))continue;prev.set(key(v),key(at));q.push(v);}}
      throw new Error('No safe route to the supply point or workshop.');}
    const path=leg(start,D.PICK),pickup=path.length-1;path.push(...leg(D.PICK,D.WORK).slice(1));const work=path.length-1;path.push(...leg(D.WORK,D.SHELF).slice(1));return {path,pickup,work};
  }
  function workshopPose(run,actor,costume,frame){
    const scene=run.scene,base='ch_'+costume.key+'_up_0',key=base+'-work-'+frame;
    if(!scene.textures.exists(key)){
      const src=scene.textures.get(base).getSourceImage(),tex=scene.textures.createCanvas(key,24,38),ctx=tex.getContext();
      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;ctx.drawImage(src,0,0,24,38);
      // Preserve the original hair, head, torso and planted legs; redraw only sleeve/hand strips.
      ctx.clearRect(1,20,4,8);ctx.clearRect(19,20,5,8);
      const b=(c,x,y,w,h)=>{ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);};
      const reach=[0,2,4][frame];
      b('#2f3028',1,20,4,5);b('#557775',2,20,3,4);b('#b78260',2,24,3,2);
      b('#2f3028',18,19,5,5);b('#557775',19,20,3,3);b('#2f3028',19,23-reach,4,5);b('#6e8b81',20,23-reach,2,3);b('#b78260',20,25-reach,3,2);
      // A small in-game craft prop is held by the moving hand. No walk-frame limb motion.
      b('#352d25',21,20-reach,2,6);b('#ba9160',21,20-reach,1,5);b('#89958e',18,19-reach,6,2);b('#c7c6ac',18,19-reach,5,1);
      tex.refresh();tex.setFilter(1);
    }
    if(actor.sprite.texture.key!==key)actor.sprite.setTexture(key);actor.sprite.setFlipX(false);
  }
  const sameLaborTile=(sprite,pt)=>Math.floor(sprite.x/T)===pt.x&&Math.floor(sprite.y/T)===pt.y;
  function laborVisual(run,actor,t,dt,paused){
    const {scene,engine}=run;
    if(actor.phase!=='labor'){actor.laborCursor=0;if(sameLaborTile(actor.sprite,t.path[t.cursor]))actor.laborCursor=t.cursor;}
    actor.path=[];actor.phase='labor';
    if(scene.inside){const p=center(t.path[t.cursor]);actor.sprite.setPosition(p.x,p.y);actor.laborCursor=t.cursor;}
    let moving=false,budget=paused?0:Math.min(dt,80)*.16;
    while(budget>0&&actor.laborCursor<=t.cursor){const target=center(t.path[actor.laborCursor]);const dx=target.x-actor.sprite.x,dy=target.y-actor.sprite.y,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
      if(dist>.01){const step=Math.min(budget,dist);engine.turn(actor.face,dx,dy);actor.face.phase+=step;actor.sprite.setPosition(actor.sprite.x+dx/dist*step,actor.sprite.y+dy/dist*step);budget-=step;moving=true;if(step<dist)break;}
      if(actor.laborCursor>=t.cursor)break;actor.laborCursor++;
    }
    const costume=Costumes.appearance(actor.id,run.world);if(costume.key!==actor.costume){paintCostume(scene,engine,actor.id,costume);actor.costume=costume.key;}
    if(t.stage==='working'&&!moving){if(!paused)actor.workPoseFrame=run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/180)%3;workshopPose(run,actor,costume,actor.workPoseFrame||0);}else engine.animate(actor.sprite,actor.costume,actor.face,moving);
    // Original sprite stays intact; a separate pixel prop shows cargo/work. Not a replacement walking animation.
    if(!actor.laborProp){actor.laborProp=scene.add.graphics();scene.areaObjs.push(actor.laborProp);}
    const g=actor.laborProp;g.clear();g.setVisible(!scene.inside);g.setDepth(run.view.depthAt(actor.sprite.y)+.01);
    const x=Math.round(actor.sprite.x),y=Math.round(actor.sprite.y-13);
    if(t.cargo&&t.stage!=='working'){g.fillStyle(0x34291f).fillRect(x-7,y-5,14,10);g.fillStyle(0x9c7548).fillRect(x-6,y-4,12,8);g.fillStyle(0xdbb47b).fillRect(x-5,y-4,10,2);g.fillStyle(0x61452e).fillRect(x-1,y-2,2,7);}
    actor.sprite.setDepth(run.view.depthAt(actor.sprite.y));actor.label.setPosition(actor.sprite.x,actor.sprite.y-36);actor.label.setText('Mara · '+({assigned:'Preparing',traveling:t.cargo?'To workshop':'To supplies',collecting:'Collecting',working:'Crafting',delivering:'Delivering',blocked:'Shelf full'}[t.stage]||t.stage));
  }
  function updateActors(run, dt, paused) {
    const { scene, actors, engine, world } = run;
    const hour = S.phase(world.clock);
    const rain = world.weather.kind === 'rain';
    const warehouse = world.buildings['ruined-warehouse'];
    if(world.visitor && !actors.some(actor=>actor.id==='mara')) {
      actors.push(makeActor(scene,engine,'mara',world,run.view));
    }
    for (const actor of actors) {
      const busy = actor.id === 'arven' && warehouse.status === 'repairing';
      const waiting = actor.id==='mara' && !world.residents.mara;
      const task=actor.id==='mara'&&!waiting?D.workerTask(world):null;
      if(task){actor.sprite.setVisible(!scene.inside);actor.label.setVisible(!scene.inside);laborVisual(run,actor,task,dt,paused);continue;}
      if(actor.laborProp){actor.laborProp.clear();actor.laborProp.setVisible(false);actor.label.setText(NPCS[actor.id].name);}
      const workshopJob=false;
      const phase = waiting ? 'waiting' : busy ? 'repair' : workshopJob ? 'workshop' : world.campfire.lit && S.isDark(world.clock) ? 'dusk' :
        rain ? 'shelter' : hour;
      const def=NPCS[actor.id];
      const site = waiting ? def.waiting : phase==='workshop'?{x:32,y:30}:def.sites[phase];
      actor.sprite.setVisible(!scene.inside);
      actor.label.setVisible(!scene.inside);
      if (!paused && phase !== actor.phase) {
        actor.phase = phase;
        actor.path = scene.area.findPath(actor.sprite, site);
      }
      if (scene.inside) continue;
      const target = actor.path[0];
      let moving = false;
      if (!paused && target) {
        const range = distance(target, actor.sprite);
        const step = dt * .093;
        if (range <= step) {
          actor.sprite.setPosition(target.x, target.y);
          actor.path.shift();
        } else {
          const dx = target.x - actor.sprite.x, dy = target.y - actor.sprite.y;
          engine.turn(actor.face, dx, dy);
          actor.face.phase += step;
          actor.sprite.setPosition(actor.sprite.x + (dx / range) * step, actor.sprite.y + (dy / range) * step);
          moving = true;
        }
      } else if (busy && !paused && !run.reduced) {
        engine.turn(actor.face, -1, -1);
        actor.face.phase += dt * .045;
        moving = true;
      }
      actor.sprite.setDepth(run.view.depthAt(actor.sprite.y));
      actor.label.setPosition(actor.sprite.x, actor.sprite.y - 36);
      const costume=Costumes.appearance(actor.id,world);
      if(costume.key!==actor.costume) {
        paintCostume(scene,engine,actor.id,costume);
        actor.costume=costume.key;
      }
      engine.animate(actor.sprite,actor.costume,actor.face,moving);
    }
  }

  function regionOf(player) {
    const x = player.x / T, y = player.y / T;
    if(!C.legacy(x,y))return C.wildernessRegion(x,y);
    if (x >= 46 && y < 12) return 'tower';
    if (x >= 44 && y < 23) return 'highlands';
    if (x >= 37) return 'river';
    if (x < 21 && y < 11) return 'ruins';
    if (x < 17) return 'forest';
    return 'hamlet';
  }

  function resourceApproach(node) {
    const point=center(node);
    if(node.kind==='fish')point.x=C.shore(point.y/T)[0]*T-7;
    return point;
  }

  const quarryApproach=()=>({x:center(QUARRY).x,y:center(QUARRY).y+20});
  function reachable(scene,point,range,exempt) {
    return !scene.inside&&!scene._lqSurface&&!scene.blocked(scene.player.x,scene.player.y)&&
      distance(scene.player,point)<=range&&root.LQPrecisePhysics.segmentClear(scene.area,scene.player,point,exempt);
  }

  const resourceRange=node=>node.kind==='water'?33:node.kind==='tree'?56:44;
  const resourceName=node=>node.kind==='tree'?'Cut '+node.name:node.name;

  function canReachQuarry(scene) {
    return scene.player.y>=center(QUARRY).y+17&&reachable(scene,quarryApproach(),34);
  }

  const summitPoint=()=>({x:C.SUMMIT.ax*T,y:C.SUMMIT.ay*T});
  function summitNear(run){const p=run.scene.player,h=C.elevationSample(p.x/T,p.y/T);return !!h&&h.elevation>570&&reachable(run.scene,summitPoint(),56);}
  function summitPanel(run){if(!summitNear(run))throw new Error('Follow the stone switchbacks to Cloudrest.');if(!run.world.development.summit)edit(run,D.visitSummit);const s=D.summitState(run.world),stage=D.summitStages[s.stage],next=D.summitStages[s.stage+1],e=C.elevationSample(run.scene.player.x/T,run.scene.player.y/T);
    const has=next&&Object.entries(next.cost).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n);
    const reading=s.observations.at(-1);
    // Live schematic of the valley from its actual state; explicitly not a static vista image.
    let survey='';if(s.stage>=8){const pts=[{x:28,y:15,n:'Warehouse',ok:D.canUse(run.world)},{x:34,y:35,n:'Storehouse',ok:run.world.development.build.stage===7},{x:C.SUMMIT.x,y:C.SUMMIT.y,n:'Cloudrest',ok:true},...D.landmarks.map(l=>({x:l.x,y:l.y,n:l.name,ok:D.heritageState(run.world,l.id).stage>=8}))];
      survey='<svg viewBox="0 0 '+C.W+' '+C.H+'" class="v852-atlas" aria-label="Live schematic of restoration"><rect width="'+C.W+'" height="'+C.H+'" fill="#344b4b"/>';for(const r of C.landscapeRoads){if(r.points.length)survey+='<polyline points="'+r.points.map(p=>p.join(',')).join(' ')+'" stroke="#d3c39f" stroke-width=".7" fill="none"/>';}for(let y=0;y<C.H;y++){const[a,b]=C.shore(y+.5);survey+='<rect x="'+a+'" y="'+y+'" width="'+(b-a)+'" height="1" fill="#78a7a6"/>';}for(const p of pts)survey+='<rect x="'+(p.x-2)+'" y="'+(p.y-2)+'" width="4" height="4" fill="'+(p.ok?'#e7c27e':'#7f7871')+'" stroke="#253b3e" stroke-width=".6"/>';survey+='</svg><p class="fv-copy">Live survey · Gold: operational. Grey: not operational. '+pts.filter(p=>p.ok).length+'/'+pts.length+' surveyed buildings operational.</p>'+button('Record a valley observation','summit-observe',reading?.at===D.now(run.world))+button('Look down over the living valley','summit-vista');
      if(s.stage===9){const day=run.world.clock.day,m=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay;const prediction=S.weatherAt?S.weatherAt({day:day+(m>=1380?1:0),minuteOfDay:(m+60)%1440}):null;if(prediction)survey+='<p class="fv-copy">Next hour forecast: '+esc(prediction)+'.</p>';}}
    panel(run,'summit',title('CLOUDREST · '+Math.round(e.elevation)+' m · TERRACE '+e.tier,stage.name,stage.desc)+'<div class="lq-labor-track">'+D.summitStages.map((v,i)=>'<span class="'+(i<=s.stage?'on':'')+'"><i>'+(i+1)+'</i>'+esc(v.name)+'</span>').join('')+'</div>'+(next?'<article class="v852-row"><b>'+esc(next.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(next.desc)+'</small><p>'+esc(costs(next.cost))+'</p>'+(!D.canUse(run.world)?'<p>Restore the hamlet warehouse first.</p>':'')+button('Carry out restoration','summit-work',!has||!D.canUse(run.world))+'</article>':'')+ '<p class="fv-copy">Field English: '+esc(s.stage<3?'The old stones need to be surveyed.':s.stage<8?'We should repair the shelter before winter.':'The village lies below the plateau. Describe what has changed.')+'</p>'+survey+(reading?'<p class="fv-copy">Last observation: '+esc(reading.weather)+' · '+reading.restored+' operational sites.</p>':'')+button('Close','close'));
  }
  function summitAction(run,action){if(run.panel!=='summit'||!summitNear(run))throw new Error('Return to Cloudrest first.');if(action==='summit-observe'){edit(run,D.observeSummit);sound('bell');return summitPanel(run);}if(action!=='summit-work')throw new Error('Unknown lookout action.');const stage=D.summitState(run.world).stage,next=D.summitStages[stage+1];if(!next||!D.canUse(run.world)||!Object.entries(next.cost).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n))throw new Error('Bring all required materials.');close(run);const node={id:C.SUMMIT.id,kind:'stone',name:next.name,x:C.SUMMIT.ax-.5,y:C.SUMMIT.ay-.5,seconds:2.8+stage*.13};beginWork(run,node,null,summitPoint(),false,56);run.pending.summit=stage;}
  function summitCamera(run,camera,bias,dt){const p=run.scene.player,h=C.elevationSample(p.x/T,p.y/T);const z=h?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(h.elevation-55)/610)):0;
    if(z>.005&&run.summitCameraZoom===undefined){run.summitCameraZoom=camera.zoom;const b=camera.getBounds?.();run.summitOldBounds=b?{x:b.x,y:b.y,width:b.width,height:b.height}:{x:0,y:0,width:C.W*T,height:C.H*T};run.summitSkyMargin=0;}
    if(run.summitCameraZoom===undefined){if(camera.followOffset.y!==bias)camera.setFollowOffset(0,bias);return;}
    const goal=run.summitCameraZoom*(1-(h?.id?Math.min(.15,h.elevation/2100):z*.12)),alpha=1-Math.exp(-Math.min(80,dt)/420),next=camera.zoom+(goal-camera.zoom)*alpha;
    const targetMargin=(h?.id?0:z*365),margin=run.summitSkyMargin+(targetMargin-run.summitSkyMargin)*alpha;if(Math.abs(margin-run.summitSkyMargin)>.25){run.summitSkyMargin=margin;const b=run.summitOldBounds;camera.setBounds(b.x,b.y-Math.round(margin),b.width,b.height+Math.round(margin));}
    if(Math.abs(next-camera.zoom)>.0002)camera.setZoom(next);const off=bias-z*42;camera.setFollowOffset(0,camera.followOffset.y+(off-camera.followOffset.y)*alpha);
    if(z===0&&Math.abs(camera.zoom-run.summitCameraZoom)<.001&&run.summitSkyMargin<2){restoreSummitCamera(run,camera);}
  }

  function restoreSummitCamera(run,camera){if(run.summitCameraZoom!==undefined){camera.setZoom(run.summitCameraZoom);const b=run.summitOldBounds;if(b)camera.setBounds(b.x,b.y,b.width,b.height);delete run.summitCameraZoom;delete run.summitOldBounds;delete run.summitSkyMargin;}}

  function hillNear(run,h){const p=run.scene.player,q=C.highlandSample(p.x/T,p.y/T);return !run.scene.inside&&q?.id===h.id&&q.elevation>h.max*.91&&reachable(run.scene,{x:h.x*T,y:h.y*T},55);}
  function hillPanel(run,id){const h=C.HIGHLANDS.find(h=>h.id===id);if(!h||!hillNear(run,h))throw new Error('Follow the trail to the viewpoint.');const first=!D.surveyState(run.world).visited[id];if(first){edit(run,w=>D.discoverHill(w,id));sound('bell');}
    const z=C.highlandSample(run.scene.player.x/T,run.scene.player.y/T).elevation;panel(run,'hill-view',title('HIGHLAND EXPLORATION · '+Math.round(z)+' m',h.name,h.notes)+'<p class="fv-copy">Field English: The trail leads up to the '+(h.theme==='green'?'hill':'plateau')+'. The valley lies below us.</p><p class="fv-copy">'+(first?'New viewpoint recorded in your permanent field journal.':'Viewpoint already recorded.')+'</p>'+button('Highland journal','highland-guide')+button('Return to trail','close'));}
  function echoNear(run,e){const p=run.scene.player;if(run.scene.inside)return false;const q=C.echoPoint(e,run.time/1000);const a=C.elevationSample(p.x/T,p.y/T),b=C.elevationSample(q.x/T,q.y/T);return Math.abs((a?.elevation||0)-(b?.elevation||0))<45&&reachable(run.scene,q,70);}
  function echoPanel(run,id,sequence=[]){const e=C.ECHOES.find(e=>e.id===id);if(!e||!echoNear(run,e))throw new Error('The light spirit has drifted away. Follow its glow.');run.echoPuzzle={id,sequence};const seen=!!D.surveyState(run.world).echoes[id];panel(run,'echo',title('LANTERN HUNT · GENTLE CAPTURE',e.name,e.note)+'<p class="fv-copy">A fictional light spirit. Tune your lantern to copy its magical imprint; the creature stays free. Remember its three signs.</p><div class="lq-echo-runes">'+[0,1,2].map(i=>'<span class="'+(sequence[i]?'lit':'')+'">'+(sequence[i]?esc(sequence[i]):'·')+'</span>').join('')+'</div>'+(seen?'<p class="fv-copy">Imprint already in your journal. No duplicate reward.</p>':'<div class="fv-grid">'+['leaf','sun','moon'].map(k=>button(k,'echo-rune:'+k)).join('')+'</div>')+button('Release / return','close'));}
  function echoRune(run,k){const p=run.echoPuzzle;if(run.panel!=='echo'||!p)throw new Error('No lantern puzzle active.');const e=C.ECHOES.find(e=>e.id===p.id);if(!echoNear(run,e)){close(run);throw new Error('The light spirit moved outside the lantern range.');}if(!['leaf','sun','moon'].includes(k))throw new Error('Unknown rune.');const seq=p.sequence.concat(k);if(k!==e.pattern[seq.length-1]){sound('gather');echoPanel(run,e.id,[]);toast(run,'The lantern faded. Try the signs again.');return;}if(seq.length<3){sound('gather');return echoPanel(run,e.id,seq);}edit(run,w=>D.collectEcho(w,e.id,seq));run.echoPuzzle=null;close(run);run.scene._lqEchoBurst={...C.echoPoint(e,run.time/1000),at:run.time/1000};sound('bell');toast(run,'Light imprint recorded: '+e.name);}
  function highlandGuide(run){const s=D.surveyState(run.world);panel(run,'highland-guide',title('HIGHLAND EXPEDITION','Peaks & light spirits','Explore the connected hill trails, reach the viewpoint, then find the glowing spirits and copy their signs. All discoveries are saved.')+'<div class="nature-intro"><strong>'+Object.keys(s.visited).length+'/2 peaks · '+Object.keys(s.echoes).length+'/3 imprints</strong></div><h3 class="nature-section">Viewpoints</h3>'+C.HIGHLANDS.map(h=>'<article class="nature-card"><b>'+esc(h.name)+'</b><small>'+h.max+' m · '+(s.visited[h.id]?'RECORDED':'UNEXPLORED')+'</small><p>'+esc(h.notes)+'</p></article>').join('')+'<h3 class="nature-section">Light spirits</h3>'+C.ECHOES.map(e=>'<article class="nature-card"><b>'+esc(e.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(e.vi)+' · '+(s.echoes[e.id]?'RECORDED':'FIND IN THE WILD')+'</small><p>'+esc(C.HIGHLANDS.find(h=>h.id===e.site)?.name||'Cloudrest')+'</p></article>').join('')+'<p class="fv-copy">Expedition reward: '+(s.reward?'Research scroll claimed.':'A research scroll for the Cloudrest upgrade. Complete both viewpoints and all three imprints.')+'</p>'+button('Claim research scroll','highland-reward',s.reward||Object.keys(s.visited).length!==C.HIGHLANDS.length||Object.keys(s.echoes).length!==C.ECHOES.length)+button('Back','menu'));}

  const millPoint=()=>({x:C.MILL.ax*T,y:C.MILL.ay*T});
  function millNear(run){return reachable(run.scene,millPoint(),58);}
  function millPanel(run){if(!millNear(run))throw new Error('Follow the riverside trail to the watermill.');if(!run.world.development.mill)edit(run,D.visitMill);const w=run.world,s=D.millState(w),next=D.millStages[s.stage+1],costs=b=>Object.entries(b).map(([k,n])=>n+' '+C.INVENTORY[k][0]).join(' · ')||'No materials needed',has=b=>Object.entries(b).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n);let h=title('SILVERWATER · WATERWAY','The old watermill','A wheel once turned the grain of the entire valley. Restore its masonry and bring the river back to work.');h+=`<div class="mill-status"><small>RESTORATION</small><b>${esc(D.millStages[s.stage].name)}</b><div class="mill-segments">${D.millStages.map((v,i)=>`<i class="${i<=s.stage?'done':''}" title="${esc(v.name)}"></i>`).join('')}</div><span>${s.stage+1} / 10 state milestones</span></div>`;if(next)h+=`<div class="mill-card"><b>${esc(next.name)}</b><p>${esc(costs(next.cost))}</p>${button('Restore next stage','mill-build',!D.canUse(w)||!has(next.cost))}</div>`;
    if(s.stage>=8){h+=`<div class="mill-card"><b>Millrace gate · ${s.gate?'Open':'Closed'}</b><p>${s.gate?'The river charges the mill slowly as game time advances.':'The wheel is at rest; the power reserve is retained.'}</p><div class="mill-power"><i style="width:${s.power/24*100}%"></i></div><small>${s.power} / 24 water-power units · ${s.runs} operations completed</small>${button(s.gate?'Close sluice gate':'Open sluice gate','mill-gate')}</div>`;for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(D.millRecipes)){h+=`<div class="mill-card"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b><p>${esc(costs(r.input))} → ${esc(costs(r.output))} · ${r.power} power</p>${s.stage<r.tier?'<small>Unlock the bakery upgrade first.</small>':button('Work at the mill','mill-work:'+id,!D.millReady(w,id))}</div>`;}}
    if(s.stage>=8)h+=button('Enter the working mill','mill-enter');h+='<blockquote>“The wheel turns whenever the gate is open. We need wheat to make flour.”</blockquote>'+button('Leave','close');panel(run,'mill',h);}
  function millAction(run,action){if(run.panel!=='mill'||!millNear(run))throw new Error('Return to the watermill.');if(action==='mill-enter')return enterHeritageRoom(run,C.MILL.id);const w=run.world,s=D.millState(w),r=action.startsWith('mill-work:')?action.split(':')[1]:null;if(action==='mill-gate'){edit(run,v=>D.millGate(v,s.gate));sound('gather');return millPanel(run);}const next=D.millStages[s.stage+1];if(r){if(!D.millReady(w,r))throw new Error('The mill is not ready.');}else if(action!=='mill-build'||!next||!D.canUse(w)||!Object.entries(next.cost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n))throw new Error('Bring all required materials.');const name=r?D.millRecipes[r].name:next.name,p=millPoint();close(run);beginWork(run,{id:C.MILL.id,kind:'stone',name,x:C.MILL.ax-.5,y:C.MILL.ay-.5,seconds:r?3.0:2.2+s.stage*.12},null,p,false,58);run.pending.mill={stage:s.stage,runs:s.runs,recipe:r};}

  const activeCrossing=scene=>C.crossingOf(scene._lqSurface);
  function crossNear(run){const scene=run.scene,p=scene.player;if(scene.inside)return null;const active=activeCrossing(scene);if(active){const side=C.crossingEnd(active,p.x/T,p.y/T);return {b:active,side,upper:true};}if(scene._lqSurface)return null;for(const b of C.CROSSINGS){const side=C.crossingEnd(b,p.x/T,p.y/T);if(side&&reachable(scene,C.crossingAnchor(b,side),30))return {b,side,upper:false};}return null;}
  function crossTarget(run){if(run.scene.time.now<(run.scene._lqCrossDoorUntil||0))return {kind:'crossing',label:'Continue exploring'};const n=crossNear(run);if(!n)return null;return {kind:'crossing',label:n.upper?(n.side?'Descend · '+n.side:'Follow the upper deck'):(D.crossingState(run.world,n.b.id).stage===3?'Ascend '+n.b.name:'Inspect '+n.b.name),data:n};}
  function crossPanel(run,b,side){const n=crossNear(run);if(!n||n.b!==b||n.side!==side||n.upper)throw new Error('Stand at the approach.');if(!D.crossingState(run.world,b.id).seen)edit(run,w=>D.crossingVisit(w,b.id));run.crossSide=side;run.crossId=b.id;const s=D.crossingState(run.world,b.id),next=D.crossingCosts[s.stage+1];panel(run,'crossing',title('EASTERN CROSSINGS · '+b.style.toUpperCase(),b.name,b.description)+'<div class="lq-labor-track">'+D.crossingStages.map((v,i)=>'<span class="'+(i<=s.stage?'on':'')+'"><i>'+(i+1)+'</i>'+esc(v)+'</span>').join('')+'</div><p class="fv-copy">Upper surface: '+b.rise+' units above the ground at the center. The approaches stay at ground level. '+(b.style==='water'?'The lake remains impassable beneath the deck.':'The lower passage remains independent.')+'</p>'+(next?'<article class="v852-row"><b>'+esc(D.crossingStages[s.stage+1])+'</b><p>'+esc(costs(next))+'</p>'+button('Restore crossing','cross-build',!D.canUse(run.world)||!Object.entries(next).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n))+'</article>':button('Ascend ramp','cross-ascend')+'<p class="fv-copy">Completed crossings: '+s.trips+' · Walk along the deck and press E at the other end.</p>')+button('Close','close'));}
  function crossReset(scene){if(!activeCrossing(scene))return;delete scene._lqSurface;delete scene._lqCrossEntry;if(scene._lqOrigin!==undefined){scene.player?.setOrigin(.5,scene._lqOrigin);delete scene._lqOrigin;}}
  function crossSave(scene){const r=scene.store.game.rpg;if(!r)return;const p=scene.player,b=activeCrossing(scene);r.lastPosition={area:ID,x:Math.round(p.x),y:Math.round(p.y),...(b?{surface:C.crossingSurface(b),entry:scene._lqCrossEntry}:{})};scene.store.save();}
  function crossFrame(run){const scene=run.scene,b=activeCrossing(scene);if(!b)return;const p=scene.player;if(scene.inside){crossReset(scene);return;}if(!C.crossingPass(b,p.x/T,p.y/T)||D.crossingState(run.world,b.id).stage!==3){const a=C.crossingAnchor(b,p.x/T<(b.left+b.right)/2?'west':'east');crossReset(scene);p.setPosition(a.x,a.y);scene.unstick();crossSave(scene);return;}if(scene._lqOrigin===undefined)scene._lqOrigin=p.originY;p.setOrigin(.5,scene._lqOrigin+C.crossingZ(b,p.x/T)/Math.max(1,p.displayHeight));p.setDepth(18);}
  function crossRestorePosition(scene,point){const b=C.crossingOf(point?.surface),run=runs.get(scene);if(!b||!run?.world||scene.inside||D.crossingState(run.world,b.id).stage!==3||!['west','east'].includes(point.entry)||!C.crossingPass(b,Number(point.x)/T,Number(point.y)/T))return false;scene._lqSurface=C.crossingSurface(b);scene._lqCrossEntry=point.entry;scene.player.setPosition(Number(point.x),Number(point.y));crossFrame(run);return true;}
  function crossInteract(run,target){if(run.scene.time.now<(run.scene._lqCrossDoorUntil||0))return;const n=crossNear(run);if(!n||!n.side)return;const scene=run.scene;if(!n.upper)return crossPanel(run,n.b,n.side);const entry=scene._lqCrossEntry;if(entry!==n.side)edit(run,w=>D.crossingTrip(w,n.b.id,entry,n.side));const a=C.crossingAnchor(n.b,n.side);crossReset(scene);scene.player.setPosition(a.x,a.y);scene._lqCrossDoorUntil=scene.time.now+650;crossSave(scene);sound('bell');toast(run,entry!==n.side?'Crossing recorded · Ground level':'Returned to the approach');}
  function crossAction(run,action){if(action==='cross-guide')return crossGuide(run);if(action.startsWith('cross-route:')){const b=C.crossingById[action.split(':')[1]];if(!b||run.panel!=='cross-guide'||!D.crossingState(run.world,b.id).seen)throw new Error('Discover this crossing first.');return crossGuide(run,b);}
    const n=crossNear(run);if(run.panel!=='crossing'||!n||n.upper||n.b.id!==run.crossId||n.side!==run.crossSide)throw new Error('Return to the crossing approach.');const scene=run.scene,s=D.crossingState(run.world,n.b.id);if(action==='cross-ascend'){if(s.stage!==3)throw new Error('Restore the crossing first.');flushTrail(scene);close(run);const a=C.crossingAnchor(n.b,n.side);scene._lqSurface=C.crossingSurface(n.b);scene._lqCrossEntry=n.side;scene.player.setPosition(a.x,a.y);scene._lqCrossDoorUntil=scene.time.now+400;crossFrame(run);crossSave(scene);sound('gather');return;}const next=D.crossingCosts[s.stage+1];if(action!=='cross-build'||!next||!D.canUse(run.world)||!Object.entries(next).every(([k,v])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=v))throw new Error('Bring the listed supplies.');const a=C.crossingAnchor(n.b,n.side);close(run);beginWork(run,{id:n.b.id,kind:'stone',name:D.crossingStages[s.stage+1],x:a.x/T-.5,y:a.y/T-.5,seconds:2.3+s.stage*.35},null,a,false,35);run.pending.crossing={id:n.b.id,stage:s.stage,side:n.side};}
  function crossGuide(run,goal){const world=run.world,scene=run.scene,known=C.CROSSINGS.filter(b=>D.crossingState(world,b.id).seen);let result='';if(goal){const network=D.crossingNetwork(world,scene.area),target=C.crossingAnchor(goal,'west'),p=scene.player;let starts=[...network.nodes.values()].filter(n=>n.surface===(scene._lqSurface||'ground')&&Math.hypot(n.x-p.x,n.y-p.y)<48).sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-p.x,a.y-p.y)-Math.hypot(b.x-p.x,b.y-p.y));if(!scene._lqSurface)starts=starts.filter(n=>root.LQPrecisePhysics.segmentClear(scene.area,p,{x:n.x,y:n.y}));const end={surface:'ground',...target},route=starts.slice(0,6).map(a=>network.route(a,end)).filter(Boolean).sort((a,b)=>a.distance-b.distance)[0];if(route){const steps=route.path.filter((n,i)=>i===0||n.surface!==route.path[i-1].surface||i===route.path.length-1);result='<article class="v852-row"><b>'+esc(goal.name)+' · '+Math.round(route.distance/T)+' tiles</b><p>'+steps.map(n=>esc(n.surface+' ('+Math.round(n.x/T*2)/2+', '+Math.round(n.y/T*2)/2+')')).join(' → ')+'</p><p>Route is guidance only. Reach each portal manually.</p></article>';}else result='<p class="fv-copy">No continuous learned route to this approach. Explore and connect your trails or restore the crossing.</p>';}
    return panel(run,'cross-guide',title('TRANSPORT ATLAS','Eastern crossings','Six restored crossings use independent upper surfaces. The route planner connects only explored trails and restored connections, including Windward Skyway and Blackstone Passage. It does not move you automatically.')+result+known.map(b=>{const s=D.crossingState(world,b.id);return '<article class="v852-row"><b>'+esc(b.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(D.crossingStages[s.stage])+' · '+s.trips+' crossings · ('+b.left+', '+b.y+')</small>'+button('Check route to western approach','cross-route:'+b.id,s.stage!==3||!!scene.inside)+'</article>';}).join('')+(known.length?'':'<p class="fv-copy">No eastern crossings discovered yet.</p>')+button('Back','menu'));}

  function skywayNear(run){const p=run.scene.player;if(run.scene.inside)return null;const side=C.skywayEndpoint(p.x/T,p.y/T);if(!side)return null;return run.scene._lqSurface==='skyway'?side:reachable(run.scene,C.skywayAnchor(side),30)?side:null;}
  function skywayTarget(run){const scene=run.scene,p=scene.player,side=skywayNear(run);if(scene._lqSurface==='skyway'){if(side)return {kind:'skyway',label:'Descend · '+side,data:side};if(Math.abs(p.x/T-C.SKYWAY.surveyX)<.6)return {kind:'skyway-survey',label:'Survey the road below'};if(surveyorNear(run))return {kind:'skyway-scout',label:'Speak to Neris'};return {kind:'skyway-route',label:'Follow the deck to an exit'};}return side?{kind:'skyway',label:D.skywayState(run.world).stage>=8?'Ascend Windward Skyway':'Inspect Windward Skyway',data:side}:surveyorNear(run)?{kind:'skyway-scout',label:'Speak to Neris'}:null;}
  function skywayPanel(run,side){if(skywayNear(run)!==side||run.scene._lqSurface==='skyway')throw new Error('Stand at a Skyway entrance.');const s=D.skywayState(run.world),next=D.skywayStages[s.stage+1];run.skywaySide=side;panel(run,'skyway',title('WINDWARD SKYWAY · '+(s.stage>=8?'OPEN':'RESTORATION'),D.skywayStages[s.stage].name,'The old elevated crossing passes above the Blackstone road. The lower trail remains open. Rebuild the piers and deck to walk high above the valley route.')+'<div class="lq-labor-track">'+D.skywayStages.map((v,i)=>'<span class="'+(i<=s.stage?'on':'')+'"><i>'+(i+1)+'</i>'+esc(v.name)+'</span>').join('')+'</div>'+(next?'<article class="v852-row"><b>'+esc(next.name)+'</b><p>'+esc(costs(next.cost))+'</p>'+button('Work on crossing','skyway-build',!D.canUse(run.world)||!Object.entries(next.cost).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n))+'</article>':'')+(s.stage>=8?button('Ascend ramp','skyway-ascend')+'<p class="fv-copy">Crossings completed: '+s.crossings+' · '+(s.surveyed?'Road survey recorded':'Survey the road from the middle of the deck.')+'</p>':'')+'<p class="fv-copy">Field English: The bridge crosses over the road. People can walk underneath it. The western ramp leads up to the deck.</p>'+button('Close','close'));}
  function skywaySavePosition(scene){const r=scene.store.game.rpg;if(!r)return;const p=scene.player,upper=scene._lqSurface==='skyway';r.lastPosition={area:ID,x:Math.round(p.x),y:Math.round(p.y),...(upper?{surface:'skyway',entry:scene._lqSkywayEntry}: {})};scene.store.save();}
  function skywayAction(run,action){const scene=run.scene;if(action.startsWith('skyway-answer:')){if(run.panel!=='skyway-survey'||scene._lqSurface!=='skyway'||Math.abs(scene.player.x/T-C.SKYWAY.surveyX)>=.6)throw new Error('Make the observation from the upper deck.');if(action!=='skyway-answer:under'){toast(run,'Look again: the old road is below your feet.');return;}if(!D.skywayState(run.world).surveyed)edit(run,D.surveySkyway);sound('bell');return skywaySurveyPanel(run);}const side=run.skywaySide,s=D.skywayState(run.world);if(run.panel!=='skyway'||skywayNear(run)!==side||scene._lqSurface==='skyway')throw new Error('Return to the entrance.');if(action==='skyway-ascend'){if(s.stage<8)throw new Error('Complete the bridge first.');close(run);const p=C.skywayAnchor(side);scene._lqSurface='skyway';scene._lqSkywayEntry=side;scene.player.setPosition(p.x,p.y);skywaySavePosition(scene);sound('gather');toast(run,'Upper deck · Walk along the ramp · E at either end to descend.');return;}
    if(action!=='skyway-build')throw new Error('Unknown Skyway action.');const next=D.skywayStages[s.stage+1];if(!next||!D.canUse(run.world)||!Object.entries(next.cost).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n))throw new Error('Bring the required materials.');const p=C.skywayAnchor(side);close(run);beginWork(run,{id:C.SKYWAY.id,kind:'stone',name:next.name,x:p.x/T-.5,y:p.y/T-.5,seconds:2.3+s.stage*.13},null,p,false,35);run.pending.skyway={stage:s.stage,side};}
  function skywaySurveyPanel(run){const s=D.skywayState(run.world);panel(run,'skyway-survey',title('UPPER DECK · FIELD OBSERVATION','The road below','You are standing on a separate walkable surface, '+Math.round(C.skywayHeight(run.scene.player.x/T))+' world units above the ground foot-plane.')+(s.surveyed?'<p class="fv-copy">Survey recorded: The lower road passes underneath the bridge. Both levels remain open.</p>':'<p class="fv-copy">Which sentence accurately describes the scene?</p>'+button('The old road passes underneath the bridge.','skyway-answer:under')+button('The old road passes above the bridge.','skyway-answer:above')+button('The old road ends at the bridge.','skyway-answer:end'))+button('Continue exploring','close'));}
  function skywayInteract(run,target){const scene=run.scene;if(target.kind==='skyway-scout')return surveyorTalk(run);if(target.kind==='skyway-survey'){if(scene._lqSurface!=='skyway'||Math.abs(scene.player.x/T-C.SKYWAY.surveyX)>=.6)throw new Error('Walk to the center of the upper deck.');return skywaySurveyPanel(run);}
    if(target.kind==='skyway-route')return;
    const side=target.data;if(scene._lqSurface!=='skyway')return skywayPanel(run,side);
    if(skywayNear(run)!==side)throw new Error('Walk to the end of the ramp.');const entry=scene._lqSkywayEntry;if(entry!==side)edit(run,w=>D.recordSkywayCrossing(w,entry,side));skywayReset(scene);const p=C.skywayAnchor(side);scene.player.setPosition(p.x,p.y);skywaySavePosition(scene);sound('gather');toast(run,'Ground level · '+(entry!==side?'Crossing recorded':'Returned to the approach'));}
  function skywayReset(scene){delete scene._lqSurface;delete scene._lqSkywayEntry;if(scene._lqOrigin!==undefined){scene.player?.setOrigin(.5,scene._lqOrigin);delete scene._lqOrigin;}}
  function skywayFrame(run){const scene=run.scene,p=scene.player;if(scene._lqSurface!=='skyway')return;if(scene.inside||!C.skywayPassable(p.x/T,p.y/T)){skywayReset(scene);return;}if(D.skywayState(run.world).stage<8){const exit=C.skywayAnchor(p.x/T<C.SKYWAY.surveyX?'west':'east');skywayReset(scene);p.setPosition(exit.x,exit.y);skywaySavePosition(scene);return;}if(scene._lqOrigin===undefined)scene._lqOrigin=p.originY;const z=C.skywayHeight(p.x/T);p.setOrigin(.5,scene._lqOrigin+z/Math.max(1,p.displayHeight));p.setDepth(18);}
  function playerSurfaceBlocked(scene,x,y){const b=activeCrossing(scene);if(b)return !C.crossingPass(b,x/T,y/T);if(scene._lqSurface!=='skyway')return scene.blocked(x,y);return !C.skywayPassable(x/T,y/T);}
  function restoreSkywayPosition(scene,point){if(C.crossingOf(point?.surface))return crossRestorePosition(scene,point);if(point?.surface!=='skyway'||!['east','west'].includes(point.entry)||scene.inside||!C.skywayPassable(Number(point.x)/T,Number(point.y)/T))return false;const run=runs.get(scene);if(!run?.world||D.skywayState(run.world).stage<8)return false;scene._lqSurface='skyway';scene._lqSkywayEntry=point.entry;scene.player.setPosition(Number(point.x),Number(point.y));skywayFrame(run);return true;}

  function surveyorPose(run){if(run.scene.inside||D.skywayState(run.world).stage<8)return null;return C.skywaySurveyor(run.world.clock.minuteOfDay+run.clock/500);}
  function surveyorNear(run,p=surveyorPose(run)){const scene=run.scene;if(!p||scene.inside||(scene._lqSurface||'ground')!==p.surface)return false;return Math.hypot(scene.player.x-p.x*T,scene.player.y-p.y*T)<42&&(p.surface==='skyway'||reachable(scene,{x:p.x*T,y:p.y*T},42));}
  function surveyorTalk(run){const p=surveyorPose(run);if(!surveyorNear(run,p))throw new Error('Meet Neris on the same level of the path.');panel(run,'skyway-scout',title('WINDWARD SURVEY OFFICE','Neris · trail surveyor',p.label+'. I document the restored crossing and the old road beneath it.')+'<blockquote>“Could you describe the difference between the bridge and the road below?”</blockquote><p class="fv-copy">Vocabulary: ramp – a sloping path; underneath – below something; deck – the walking surface of a bridge.</p>'+button('Return to the trail','close'));}
  function clearSurveyor(run){const v=run.surveyorVisual;if(!v)return;for(const o of [v.sprite,v.label,v.notes]){const i=run.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(o);if(i>=0)run.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(o.scene)o.destroy();}delete run.surveyorVisual;}
  function surveyorFrame(run,dt){const p=surveyorPose(run),scene=run.scene,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView;if(!p||run.film||p.x*T<rect.x-100||p.x*T>rect.right+100||p.y*T<rect.y-100||p.y*T>rect.bottom+100){clearSurveyor(run);return;}
    const eng=run.engine,key='neris-surveyor';if(!scene.textures.exists('ch_'+key+'_down_0'))eng.sprite(scene,key,{...NPCS.lyra.look,outfit:0x637f72,pants:0x485357,hair:0x574b37},(g,dir,f)=>Costumes.paint(g,dir,f,{outfit:'traveler',wet:false}));let v=run.surveyorVisual;if(!v){const sprite=scene.add.image(p.x*T,p.y*T,'ch_'+key+'_down_0').setOrigin(.5,eng.origin),label=scene.add.text(0,0,'Neris',{fontFamily:'Georgia',fontSize:'12px',color:'#f3dfb3',backgroundColor:'#293b33d7',padding:{x:5,y:3}}).setOrigin(.5,1).setDepth(23),notes=scene.add.graphics();scene.areaObjs.push(sprite,label,notes);v=run.surveyorVisual={sprite,label,notes,face:eng.face(),previous:null};}
    const x=p.x*T,y=p.y*T,depth=p.surface==='skyway'?18.025:run.view.depthAt(y);if(p.moving){eng.turn(v.face,p.dx,p.dy);if(v.previous)v.face.phase+=Math.min(30,Math.hypot(x-v.previous.x,y-v.previous.y));}else if(p.writing)eng.turn(v.face,0,1);v.previous={x,y};eng.animate(v.sprite,key,v.face,p.moving);v.sprite.setPosition(x,y).setOrigin(.5,eng.origin+p.z/Math.max(1,v.sprite.displayHeight)).setDepth(depth);v.label.setPosition(x,y-p.z-40).setText('Neris · '+p.label);v.label.setVisible(Math.hypot(scene.player.x-x,scene.player.y-y)<115);v.notes.clear().setDepth(depth+.01);if(p.writing){const g=v.notes,sx=Math.round(x+3),sy=Math.round(y-p.z-17),f=run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/180)%4;g.fillStyle(0x4e4131).fillRect(sx-7,sy-2,15,10);g.fillStyle(0xe1d1a4).fillRect(sx-6,sy-1,6,8).fillRect(sx+1,sy-1,6,8);g.fillStyle(0x9e8c65).fillRect(sx,sy,1,7);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)g.fillStyle(0x726953,.7).fillRect(sx-5,sy+1+i*2,4,1);g.fillStyle(0x8a6a43).fillRect(sx+2+f,sy+1,1,6);}}

  function reserveNear(run,id){const r=D.reserveById[id];return !!r&&!run.scene.inside&&run.scene._lqSurface!=='skyway'&&reachable(run.scene,{x:r.x*T,y:r.y*T},64);}
  function reservePanel(run,id){if(!reserveNear(run,id))throw new Error('Return to the habitat survey marker.');const r=D.reserveById[id],s=D.reserveState(run.world,id),st=['Disturbed soil','Soil prepared','Establishing','Living habitat'][s.stage],cost=bag=>Object.entries(bag).map(([k,n])=>n+' '+C.INVENTORY[k][0]).join(' · '),ready=D.heritageState(run.world,id).stage>=8;run.reserveId=id;let body='<div class="rewild-card"><small>'+esc(r.habitat)+' / '+esc(st)+'</small><p>'+esc(r.note)+'</p>';
    if(s.stage===0)body+='<p>Stabilize the plot using '+esc(cost(r.prepare))+'.</p>'+(!ready?'<p>Restore the nearby hall to stage 8 first.</p>':'')+button('Prepare soil','reserve-prepare',!ready);
    if(s.stage===1)body+='<p>Plant local cuttings using '+esc(cost(r.seed))+'.</p>'+button('Plant habitat','reserve-sow');
    if(s.stage===2){const rem=Math.max(0,s.readyAt-D.now(run.world)),fraction=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,100*(1-rem/r.minutes)));body+='<div class="rewild-progress"><i style="width:'+fraction+'%"></i></div><p>'+rem+' game minutes until established. Explore while it grows.</p>'+button('Water once · −60 min (1 water)','reserve-water',s.watered||rem<=1);}
    if(s.stage===3){body+='<p>Habitat established · local wildlife can return.</p>';if(!s.surveyed)body+='<h3>'+esc(r.question)+'</h3>'+r.answers.map((a,i)=>button(a,'reserve-answer:'+i)).join('');else body+='<p>✓ Survey recorded in the valley archive.</p>';}
    return panel(run,'reserve',title('VALLEY STEWARDSHIP',r.name)+body+'</div>'+button('Habitat atlas','reserve-atlas')+button('Back','close'));}
  function reserveAtlas(run){const n=D.reserves.filter(r=>D.reserveState(run.world,r.id).surveyed).length;const states=['Disturbed','Prepared','Growing','Established'];const rows=D.reserves.map(r=>{const s=D.reserveState(run.world,r.id);return '<article class="rewild-card"><small>'+esc(r.habitat)+' · '+r.x+' / '+r.y+'</small><h3>'+esc(r.name)+'</h3><p>'+states[s.stage]+(s.surveyed?' · Surveyed':'')+'</p>'+button('Inspect nearby habitat','reserve-open:'+r.id,!reserveNear(run,r.id))+'</article>';}).join('');return panel(run,'reserve-atlas',title('VALLEY STEWARDSHIP','Living habitats',n+' / 6 surveys complete. Restore halls, plant native cover and observe wildlife.')+rows+button('Claim research scroll','reserve-reward',n!==6||run.world.development.reserveReward)+button('Back','menu'));}
  function reserveAction(run,action){if(action==='reserve-atlas')return reserveAtlas(run);if(action.startsWith('reserve-open:'))return reservePanel(run,action.split(':')[1]);if(action==='reserve-reward'&&run.panel==='reserve-atlas'){edit(run,D.reserveReward);sound('bell');return reserveAtlas(run);}const id=run.reserveId;if(run.panel!=='reserve'||!reserveNear(run,id))throw new Error('Stand near the habitat marker.');edit(run,w=>{if(action==='reserve-prepare')D.reservePrepare(w,id);else if(action==='reserve-sow')D.reserveSow(w,id);else if(action==='reserve-water')D.reserveWater(w,id);else if(action.startsWith('reserve-answer:'))D.reserveSurvey(w,id,Number(action.split(':')[1]));else throw new Error('Unknown stewardship action.');});sound('gather');return reservePanel(run,id);}

  function tunnelNear(run){const p=run.scene.player;if(run.scene.inside||run.scene._lqSurface)return null;return D.tunnelPortals.find(a=>reachable(run.scene,{x:a.x*T,y:a.y*T},44));}
  function tunnelStation(run){const scene=run.scene;if(!scene.inside?.tunnel)return null;const x=(scene.player.x-20000)/T,y=scene.player.y/T;return D.tunnelLayout.stations.map(s=>({s,d:Math.hypot(x-s.x,y-s.y)})).filter(v=>v.d<.92&&D.tunnelWalkable(v.s.x,v.s.y)).sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d)[0]?.s;}
  function tunnelPanel(run,side){const p=tunnelNear(run);if(!p||p.id!==side)throw new Error('Stand at the stair on the surface.');const s=D.tunnelState(run.world),n=D.tunnelStages[s.stage+1];run.tunnelSide=side;let h=title('BLACKSTONE · OLD WATERWAY','The Lantern Passage', 'The ancient footway joins the northern and southern faces of Blackstone. Restore the arches and lights to reopen the route.');h+=`<div class="underpass-card"><small>${esc(D.tunnelStages[s.stage].name)} · ${s.stage}/3</small><div class="underpass-progress"><i style="width:${s.stage/3*100}%"></i></div><p>Surface stairs: North / South · Underground inscriptions: ${Object.keys(s.inscriptions).length}/3 · Completed crossings: ${s.crossings}</p></div>`;if(n){h+='<p class="fv-copy">Restore Blackstone Stoneworks first. '+esc(n.name+' · '+costs(n.cost))+'</p>'+button(n.name,'tunnel-build',D.heritageState(run.world,'ridge-marker').stage<8||!Object.entries(n.cost).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n));}else{h+=button('Descend into the passage','tunnel-enter');if(!s.surveyed&&s.crossings&&Object.keys(s.inscriptions).length===3)h+='<p class="fv-copy">Where does the water gather?</p>'+button('Above the roof','tunnel-quiz:0')+button('In the lower basin','tunnel-quiz:1')+button('In the northern lantern','tunnel-quiz:2');if(s.surveyed)h+='<p class="fv-copy">Survey complete · Research Scroll recorded.</p>';}panel(run,'tunnel',h+button('Close','close'));}
  function tunnelInteract(run,target){if(target.kind==='tunnel')return tunnelPanel(run,target.data);const st=tunnelStation(run);if(!st||st.id!==target.data)throw new Error('Approach the marked station.');if(st.text){if(!D.tunnelState(run.world).inscriptions[st.id])edit(run,w=>D.tunnelRead(w,st.id));sound('gather');panel(run,'tunnel-inscription',title('INSCRIPTION · FIELD ENGLISH',st.name)+`<blockquote>${esc(st.text)}</blockquote><p class="fv-copy">Copied in the expedition journal.</p>`+button('Continue exploring','close'));return;}const scene=run.scene,from=scene.inside.tunnel.side,to=st.id,portal=D.tunnelPortals.find(p=>p.id===to);if(from!==to)edit(run,w=>D.tunnelCross(w,from,to));scene.inside.out={x:portal.x*T,y:portal.y*T};scene.leaveBuilding();sound('gather');toast(run,'Returned to the '+to+' stair.');}
  function tunnelAction(run,action){const p=tunnelNear(run);if(run.panel!=='tunnel'||!p||p.id!==run.tunnelSide)throw new Error('Return to the passage entrance.');if(action==='tunnel-build'){edit(run,w=>D.tunnelRestore(w,D.tunnelState(w).stage));sound('gather');return tunnelPanel(run,p.id);}if(action.startsWith('tunnel-quiz:')){edit(run,w=>D.tunnelSurvey(w,Number(action.split(':')[1])));sound('bell');return tunnelPanel(run,p.id);}if(action==='tunnel-enter'){if(!D.tunnelOpen(run.world))throw new Error('Restore all three stages.');const scene=run.scene;close(run);restoreSummitCamera(run,scene.cameras.main);if(!Art.tunnelEnter(scene,run.world,p.id))throw new Error('The passage is unavailable.');const rpg=scene.store.game.rpg;if(rpg){delete rpg.lastInterior;rpg.lastPosition={area:ID,x:p.x*T,y:p.y*T};scene.store.save();}sound('gather');toast(run,'Explore the cavern · E at inscriptions or stairs.');return;}}

  const frontierPoint=p=>({x:p.ax*T,y:p.ay*T});
  function frontierNear(run,id){const p=D.frontierById[id];return p&&reachable(run.scene,frontierPoint(p),48)&&Math.abs(C.frontierAltitude(run.scene.player.x/T,run.scene.player.y/T)-C.frontierAltitude(p.ax,p.ay))<45;}
  function frontierPanel(run,id){const p=D.frontierById[id];if(!p||!frontierNear(run,id))throw new Error('Walk to the station first.');if(!run.world.development.frontier?.sites[id])edit(run,w=>D.frontierVisit(w,id));run.frontierSite=id;const w=run.world,s=D.frontierState(w,id),z=Math.round(C.frontierAltitude(p.ax,p.ay)),stage=s.stage,recipe=D.frontierRecipe(w,id);const states=D.frontierNames.map((n,i)=>`<span style="display:inline-block;padding:4px 7px;margin:2px;border-radius:4px;background:${i===stage?'#88784d':i<stage?'#365d4b':'#263e38'};color:${i===stage?'#fff4d7':'#c5d4c3'}">${i+1} · ${esc(n)}</span>`).join('');const task=!s.solved?`<p class="fv-copy">${esc(p.note)}</p><h3>${esc(p.question)}</h3>${p.answers.map((v,i)=>button(v,'frontier-solve:'+i)).join('')}`:stage<8?`<h3>Next work: ${esc(D.frontierNames[stage+1])}</h3><p class="fv-copy">${esc(costs(D.frontierCost(id,stage+1)))}</p>${button('Begin restoration','frontier-restore',!D.canUse(w)||!Object.entries(D.frontierCost(id,stage+1)).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n))}`:'';
    let service='';if(stage>=8){const message=p.kind==='spring'?'Refills once per game day.':p.kind==='rest'?'Advances simulation by '+(stage===9?60:30)+' game minutes, including production and weather.':p.kind==='wind'?'Records the simulation forecast '+(stage===9?180:90)+' minutes ahead.':p.kind==='granary'?(3-s.runs)+' supply contracts left.':p.kind==='beacon'?'Complete all ten landmarks and eight altitude bands, including Cloudrest.':'Bring materials directly from the same valley backpack.';service=`<h3>${esc(p.role)}</h3><p class="fv-copy">${esc(message)}</p>${Object.keys(recipe.input).length?`<p>${esc(costs(recipe.input))} → ${esc(costs(recipe.output))}</p>`:Object.keys(recipe.output).length?`<p>${esc(costs(recipe.output))}</p>`:''}${s.reading?`<p class="fv-copy">Recorded forecast: ${esc(s.reading.weather)} · Day ${Math.floor(s.reading.nextAt/1440)+1}, ${String(Math.floor(s.reading.nextAt%1440/60)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(s.reading.nextAt%60).padStart(2,'0')}</p>`:''}${button(p.role,'frontier-service',(p.kind==='wind'||p.kind==='spring')&&s.lastDay===w.clock.day||p.kind==='granary'&&s.runs>=3||p.kind==='beacon'&&w.development.frontier.reward||!Object.entries(recipe.input).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n))}${stage<9?`<h3>Specialize</h3><p>${esc(costs(D.frontierCost(id,9)))}</p>${button('Upgrade station','frontier-restore',!Object.entries(D.frontierCost(id,9)).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n))}`:''}`;}
    panel(run,'frontier-site',title('MOUNTAIN EXPEDITION · '+z+' m',p.name,p.note)+`<div class="nature-intro"><strong>${stage+1}/10</strong><span>${esc(p.role)} · ${esc(p.keeper)} ${stage>=8?'(day shift)':'(station closed)'}</span></div><div>${states}</div>${task}${service}<p class="fv-copy">Work is local, materials are charged only when the work finishes. Walking away cancels the operation.</p>`+button('Expedition register','frontier-guide')+button('Close','close'));}
  function frontierGuide(run){const w=run.world,f=w.development.frontier||{sites:{},bands:{},reward:false};const three=[...C.HIGHLANDS.map(h=>({name:h.name,z:h.max,active:!!D.surveyState(w).visited[h.id]})),{name:C.SUMMIT.name,z:656,active:D.summitState(w).stage>=8}];const done=three.filter(p=>p.active).length+C.FRONTIER.filter(p=>D.frontierState(w,p.id).stage>=8).length;const rows=C.ALTITUDE_BANDS.map((b,i)=>{const o=f.bands[i];return `<article class="v852-row"><b>${i} · ${esc(b[0])}</b><small>${b[1]}${Number.isFinite(b[2])?'–'+b[2]:'+'} m · ${o?'Recorded at '+o.z+' m':'Explore on foot'}</small><div style="height:5px;background:${o?'#91b98a':'#40534a'};border-radius:4px"></div></article>`;}).join('');const sites=three.map(p=>`<article class="v852-row"><b>${esc(p.name)}</b><small>${p.z} m · ${p.active?'Complete':'Explore'}</small></article>`).join('')+C.FRONTIER.map(p=>{const s=D.frontierState(w,p.id),z=Math.round(C.frontierAltitude(p.ax,p.ay));return `<article class="v852-row"><b>${esc(p.name)}</b><small>${z} m · ${s.stage+1}/10 · ${esc(p.role)}</small><p>${f.sites[p.id]?`Recorded location (${p.ax}, ${p.ay})`:'Explore the '+(p.x<80?p.y<40?'Whispering Hill':'Sunstone Plateau':'Cloudrest')+' area.'}</p>${frontierNear(run,p.id)?button('Inspect','frontier-open:'+p.id):''}</article>`;}).join('');return panel(run,'frontier-guide',title('THE LIVING FRONTIER','Expedition register','Ten existing and new landmarks, eight measured altitude bands, seven restoration stations. No long road appears before you walk it.')+`<div class="nature-intro"><strong>${done}/10 landmarks · ${Object.keys(f.bands).length}/8 bands</strong><span>${f.reward?'Beacon reward collected':'Explore and restore to unlock the final beacon'}</span></div>`+button('Open expedition atlas','dev-atlas')+`<h3>Actual altitude readings</h3>${rows}<h3>Landmarks and stations</h3>${sites}`+button('Back','menu'));}
  function frontierAction(run,action){if(action==='frontier-guide')return frontierGuide(run);if(action.startsWith('frontier-open:'))return frontierPanel(run,action.slice(14));const id=run.frontierSite,p=D.frontierById[id];if(run.panel!=='frontier-site'||!frontierNear(run,id))throw new Error('Return to this station.');const s=D.frontierState(run.world,id);if(action.startsWith('frontier-solve:')){edit(run,w=>D.frontierSolve(w,id,Number(action.slice(15))));sound('bell');return frontierPanel(run,id);}if(action==='frontier-restore'){if(!s.solved||s.stage>=9||!D.canUse(run.world))throw new Error('Survey and restore the warehouse first.');const expected=s.stage;close(run);beginWork(run,{id:'frontier-'+id,kind:'stone',name:D.frontierNames[expected+1],x:p.ax-.5,y:p.ay-.5,seconds:2.2+expected*.10},null,frontierPoint(p),false,48);run.pending.frontier={id,stage:expected};return;}if(action==='frontier-service'){if(p.kind==='rest'){edit(run,w=>D.frontierService(w,id,s.runs,n=>S.step(w,n,node=>growthClear(run,node))));sound('bell');return frontierPanel(run,id);}close(run);beginWork(run,{id:'frontier-'+id,kind:'stone',name:p.role,x:p.ax-.5,y:p.ay-.5,seconds:2.2},null,frontierPoint(p),false,48);run.pending.frontier={id,runs:s.runs};return;}throw new Error('Unknown expedition action.');}
  function clearFrontierActors(run,id){for(const [key,v] of [...(run.frontierActors||new Map())]){if(id&&key!==id)continue;for(const o of [v.sprite,v.label,v.work]){const i=run.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(o);if(i>=0)run.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(o.scene)o.destroy();}run.frontierActors.delete(key);}}
  function frontierActorFrame(run,dt){const scene=run.scene;if(scene.inside||run.film){clearFrontierActors(run);return;}const rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,eng=run.engine,map=run.frontierActors??=new Map();for(const p of C.FRONTIER){const s=D.frontierDuty(run.world,p.id,run.world.clock.minuteOfDay+run.clock/500);if(!s||s.x*T<rect.x-90||s.x*T>rect.right+90||s.y*T<rect.y-90||s.y*T>rect.bottom+90){clearFrontierActors(run,p.id);continue;}const x=s.x*T,y=s.y*T,key='frontier-keeper-'+p.id;if(!scene.textures.exists('ch_'+key+'_down_0'))eng.sprite(scene,key,{...NPCS.lyra.look,outfit:p.outfit,pants:0x48544d,hair:0x5d473c},(g,dir,f)=>Costumes.paint(g,dir,f,{outfit:'traveler',wet:false}));let v=map.get(p.id);if(!v){const sprite=scene.add.image(x,y,'ch_'+key+'_down_0').setOrigin(.5,eng.origin),label=scene.add.text(0,0,p.keeper,{fontFamily:'Georgia',fontSize:'12px',color:'#f3dfb3',backgroundColor:'#293b33d7',padding:{x:5,y:3}}).setOrigin(.5,1).setDepth(23),work=scene.add.graphics();scene.areaObjs.push(sprite,label,work);v={sprite,label,work,face:eng.face(),previous:null};map.set(p.id,v);}if(s.moving){eng.turn(v.face,s.dx,s.dy);if(v.previous)v.face.phase+=Math.min(24,Math.hypot(x-v.previous.x,y-v.previous.y));}else eng.turn(v.face,0,-1);v.previous={x,y};eng.animate(v.sprite,key,v.face,s.moving);const depth=run.view.depthAt(y);v.sprite.setPosition(x,y).setDepth(depth);v.label.setPosition(x,y-40).setVisible(Math.hypot(scene.player.x-x,scene.player.y-y)<110);v.work.clear().setDepth(depth+.001);if(s.working){const f=run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/210)%4,g=v.work;g.fillStyle(0x443e35).fillRect(x-7,y-20,14,8);g.fillStyle(0xd7ca98).fillRect(x-6,y-19,12,6);g.fillStyle(0x71624a).fillRect(x+f-3,y-18,1,4);}}}

  const eastPoint=p=>({x:p.ax*T,y:p.ay*T});
  function eastNear(run,id){const p=D.eastById[id];return !!p&&reachable(run.scene,eastPoint(p),55);}
  function districtInside(run){const h=run.scene.inside?.heritage;return h&&!h.upper&&D.eastById[h.id]?{h,p:D.eastById[h.id],o:Art.heritageRoomNear(run.scene)}:null;}
  function civicAuxPanel(run,p,s){const f=D.civicFacility(run.world,p.id);let out='';if(p.id==='fen-aqueduct')out=`<h3>Water feed</h3><p class="fv-copy">${D.civicPumpOpen(run.world,p.id)?'Open — water can flow.':'Closed — no processing until reopened.'}</p>`+button(D.civicPumpOpen(run.world,p.id)?'Close feed valve':'Open feed valve','district-civic:gate');
    else if(['heath-weaver','prairie-clothier'].includes(p.id))out=`<h3>Workshop banner</h3><p class="fv-copy">Choose a new fabric colour. Each alteration consumes one woven cloth and changes the outside flag.</p>`+['Sage','Heather','Gold'].map((n,i)=>button(n,'district-civic:banner:'+i,f.banner===i||!(run.world.resources['woven-cloth']>0))).join('');
    else if(p.id==='prairie-kitchen')out=`<h3>Community table</h3><p class="fv-copy">Use three prepared portions to provide a shared meal for the other restored Prairie workshops. Each receives +2 trust; one meal per day. Meals served: ${f.meals||0}.</p>`+button('Share three portions','district-civic:meal',!(s.stock.food>=3)||f.mealDay===Math.floor(S.total(run.world)/1440))+(f.guests?`<p>Last recipients: ${esc(f.guests.map(id=>D.eastById[id].keeper).join(', '))}</p>`:'');
    else if(p.id==='heath-maproom'){const n=D.civicTrailSurvey(run.world);out=`<h3>Explored trail survey</h3><p>${n.total} eastern trail segments · ${n.mature} traversable by the courier</p>`+Object.entries(n.regions).map(([k,v])=>`<p class="fv-copy">${esc(C.EAST_BY_REGION[k].name)} · ${v}</p>`).join('')+button('Record current survey','district-civic:survey',!n.total)+ (f.survey?`<p class="fv-copy">Saved survey: ${f.survey.total} segments, day ${f.survey.day}.</p>`:'');}
    else if(p.id==='moon-bindery')out=`<h3>Permanent accession</h3><p class="fv-copy">Archive a completed field book from the local stock. One accession per day. Archived volumes: ${f.archived||0}.</p>`+button('Accession one field book','district-civic:archive',!(s.stock['field-book']>0)||f.archiveDay===Math.floor(S.total(run.world)/1440));
    else if(p.id==='moon-academy'){const n=f.lessons||0,a=D.civicLessons[n];out=`<h3>Valley English course · ${n}/${D.civicLessons.length}</h3>`+(a?`<p>${esc(a[0])}</p>${a[1].map((x,i)=>button(x,'district-civic:lesson:'+i)).join('')}<p class="fv-copy">Final lesson consumes one bound field book and awards one research scroll.</p>`:'<p>Course completed. Your achievement is recorded in the academy archive.</p>');}
    return out;
  }

    function masteryPanelContent(run,p){if(D.branchJob(run.world,p.id))return '<p>Collect the specialist commission before beginning or continuing a supervised batch.</p>';const w=run.world,s=D.eastState(w,p.id),m=D.masteryState(w,p.id),prof=D.masteryBySite[p.id];if(!prof)return '';const rank=D.masteryRank(m.completed),names=['Novice','Apprentice','Journeyman','Master'],missing=D.masteryMissing(w,p.id),need=Object.values(missing).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),used=Object.values(s.stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);let html=`<section style="margin:12px 0;padding:12px;border:1px solid #577967;border-radius:10px;background:linear-gradient(145deg,#233a33,#182825)"><small style="color:#c9b580;letter-spacing:.12em">GUILD PRACTICUM · ${esc(prof.role.toUpperCase())}</small><h3 style="margin:5px 0">${esc(names[rank])} · ${esc(p.keeper)}</h3><meter min="0" max="6" value="${m.completed}" style="width:100%"></meter><p class="fv-copy">${m.completed}/6 verified batches · Future orders ${100-D.masteryFactor(w,p.id)}% shorter. No extra products are granted.</p><p>Recipe: ${esc(costs(p.input))} → ${esc(costs(p.output))}</p>`;
    if(m.completed===6)html+='<p>Master certificate earned. Your mentor remembers your verified work.</p>';
    else if(m.phase===0){const q=D.masteryQuestion(w,p.id);html+='<p>1 · Discuss the production technique with your mentor.</p><h3>'+esc(q.prompt)+'</h3>'+q.answers.map((a,i)=>button(a,'mastery-begin:'+i,!!s.job||D.civicPolicy(w,p.id)>0)).join('');if(s.job||D.civicPolicy(w,p.id))html+='<p class="fv-copy">Finish current orders and stop standing production to enrol in a new supervised batch.</p>';}
    else if(m.phase===1){html+='<p>2 · Prepare the actual ingredients at this station.</p><p class="fv-copy">'+(need?'Missing: '+esc(costs(missing)):'All required ingredients are present.')+'</p>'+button('Stage missing ingredients from backpack','mastery-stage',!need||used+need>48||!!s.job||s.runs!==m.baseline||D.civicPolicy(w,p.id)>0||!Object.entries(missing).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n))+button('Commission supervised batch','mastery-commission',!!need||!!s.job||s.runs!==m.baseline||D.civicPolicy(w,p.id)>0);if(s.runs!==m.baseline||s.job)html+='<p class="fv-copy">Another order has intervened. Close this plan and enrol again.</p>';}
    else{html+='<p>3 · Return and check the completed order with your mentor.</p><p class="fv-copy">'+(m.phase===3?'Batch ready for inspection.':s.job?'Production: '+s.job.progress+'/'+D.eastDuration(p,s)+' minutes.':'Order was cancelled; reset the plan to restart.')+'</p>'+button('Review verified batch','mastery-review',m.phase!==3);}
    if(m.phase)html+=button('Close training plan (no stock refund)','mastery-reset');return html+'</section>';}
  function masteryStationAction(run,action){const d=districtInside(run);if(run.panel!=='district-room'||!d||d.o?.action!=='work')throw new Error('Stand at the actual workshop station.');const [cmd,arg]=action.slice(8).split(':');edit(run,w=>D.masteryAction(w,d.p.id,cmd,Number(arg)));sound('gather');return districtRoomPanel(run);}
  function masteryApprenticeNote(run,a){const s=D.masteryState(run.world,a.site),rank=D.masteryRank(s.completed);return `<p class="fv-copy">Mentor’s workshop practicum: ${s.completed}/6 verified batches · ${['Novice','Apprentice','Journeyman','Master'][rank]}. Visit the actual work station to practise ${esc(D.masteryBySite[a.site]?.role||a.role)}.</p>`;}
  // Shared original NPC sprite skeleton. Tools and materials have occupation-specific movement.
  function masteryTool(g,tool,x,y,f,skin=0xd6a880){const t=f/12,Y=y-8,ink=0x473a30,wood=0xb58e5b,hi=0xf0d49d,metal=0xbac9cb,green=0x86ac72,blue=0x6da6bc,red=0xc17a55,k=Math.floor(Math.sin(t*Math.PI*2)*4),lift=Math.floor(Math.abs(Math.sin(t*Math.PI))*6);const r=(c,a,b,w,h)=>g.fillStyle(c).fillRect(Math.round(x+a),Math.round(Y+b),w,h);const line=(c,a,b,d,e)=>g.lineStyle(2,c,1).lineBetween(x+a,Y+b,x+d,Y+e);const bench=()=>{r(ink,-10,3,20,5);r(wood,-9,2,18,3);r(ink,-8,7,2,3);r(ink,6,7,2,3);};const sheet=()=>{r(ink,-9,-1,18,7);r(hi,-8,0,16,5);};const bowl=()=>{r(ink,-8,0,16,7);r(red,-7,1,14,5);r(hi,-5,0,10,2);};switch(tool){
  case 'saw':bench();r(wood,-9,-1,17,3);r(metal,-9+k,-5,12,3);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)r(hi,-8+k+i*3,-2,1,1);break;
  case 'join':bench();r(wood,-7,-3,14,5);line(metal,-5,-5,5,-5);r(hi,k,-7,2,4);break;
  case 'press':bench();r(red,-6,-5,12,8);r(ink,-9,-8,18,2);r(metal,-1,-8+lift,3,7-lift%4);r(hi,-2,0,4,2);break;
  case 'dye':bowl();r(0x8571b1,-6,1,12,3);r(hi,-4+k,-5,8,6);r(0xb19adc,-3+k,-2,6,3);break;
  case 'mill':r(ink,-7,-3,14,10);r(wood,-6,-2,12,8);line(metal,-5,k,5,-k);r(hi,-6+f%12,7,2,1);break;
  case 'route':sheet();line(0x588b78,-6,3,0,1);line(0x588b78,0,1,6,4);r(red,-6+f%12,1,2,2);break;
  case 'brick':bench();for(let i=0;i<3;i++)r(red,-8+i*6,0,5,3);r(hi,-3,-8+lift,6,2);r(ink,-1,-10+lift,2,3);break;
  case 'metal':bench();r(metal,-6,-1,12,3);r(ink,-2,-11+lift,2,8);r(metal,-5,-12+lift,8,3);if(f>7)r(hi,4,-3,1,1);break;
  case 'gem':bench();r(0x9bcac8,-4,-4,8,7);r(hi,-2,-4,3,2);r(metal,k,-7,2,8);break;
  case 'water':r(ink,-9,-1,18,8);r(blue,-8,0,16,6);line(0xaad7df,-6,k%3,6,k%3);r(metal,0,-8,4,8);r(blue,4,-5+lift,2,3);break;
  case 'plant':bowl();r(green,-1,-9,2,10);r(green,-5,-7,5,3);r(0xa7c67a,1,-5,5,3);r(blue,7,-9+lift,1,2);break;
  case 'pot':r(ink,-7,4,14,2);r(red,-5,-3,10,7);r(hi,-4,-3,8,1);r(wood,-7+k,-7,2,8);break;
  case 'bake':bench();r(hi,-8,-2,16,5);r(wood,k-4,-5,8,3);r(ink,k-6,-4,2,1);r(ink,k+4,-4,2,1);break;
  case 'seed':sheet();for(let i=0;i<5;i++)r(wood,-6+i*3,3,1,1);r(hi,k,-6+lift,2,2);break;
  case 'market':r(ink,-8,0,16,7);r(wood,-7,1,14,5);r(hi,-5,2,3,3);r(red,1,2,4,3);r(hi,k,-5+lift,3,2);break;
  case 'archive':sheet();for(let i=0;i<3;i++)r(wood,-5,1+i,10,1);line(metal,k,-7,k+2,0);r(red,6,0,2,5);break;
  case 'tea':bowl();r(hi,-4,-2,8,3);r(hi,5,0,3,4);r(0xd2e0d4,-2,-3-lift,1,3);break;
  case 'paper':bench();r(0xa9c1b3,-8,-2,16,5);r(hi,-7,-1,14,3);line(wood,-7,k,7,k);break;
  case 'loom':bench();r(0x786997,-8,-5,16,7);for(let i=0;i<5;i++)r(hi,-7+i*3,-4,1,5);r(metal,-8+f%12,-1,5,2);break;
  case 'map':sheet();line(0x6586a0,-6,4,5,0);line(0x6586a0,-5,0,6,4);r(red,k,-1,2,2);break;
  case 'repair':bench();r(metal,-4,-2,8,5);r(ink,-2,0,4,2);line(metal,-5,-7+lift,3,0);break;
  case 'herb':sheet();r(green,-6,0,4,4);r(green,1,0,4,4);r(wood,-1,-7+lift,2,3);break;
  case 'cook':bowl();line(wood,k,-7,k+3,2);if(f%3===0)r(hi,-2,-5-lift,1,2);break;
  case 'stitch':sheet();r(0x8577a2,-7,0,14,5);line(hi,-6,2,k,3);r(metal,k,-5+lift,1,5);break;
  case 'teach':sheet();r(wood,-1,0,1,5);r(ink,-6,1,4,1);r(ink,2,1,4,1);line(metal,k,-6,k,1);break;
  }
  // Hands follow work height and alternate with the tool. Maintain original body silhouette.
  const handX=['saw','join','bake','paper','loom'].includes(tool)?k:Math.floor(k/2);
  const handY=['metal','brick','repair','cook','seed'].includes(tool)?-5+Math.floor(lift/2):-2;
  r(ink,-7,-5,3,4);r(skin,-6,-4,2,3);r(ink,3+handX,handY,3,3);r(skin,4+handX,handY,2,2);
  }

  function livingPanel(run,p,o){const w=run.world,open=D.livingOpen(w,p.id,o.id);let h='';if(['stock','aux','ledger'].includes(o.id))h+=`<section style="border:1px solid #8a7852;background:#25332d;padding:10px;border-radius:8px;margin:10px 0"><small style="color:#e0c78a;letter-spacing:.1em">PHYSICAL INTERACTION</small><p>${esc(o.name)} · ${open?'Open':'Closed'}</p>${button(open?'Close object':'Open object','living-toggle')}</section>`;
    if(o.id==='rest'){const d=D.livingShift(w,p.id,w.clock.minuteOfDay);h+=`<p>Resident: ${esc(p.keeper)} · ${esc(d.activity)} · ${D.charterState(w)?.houses[p.id]?'Furnished lodging':'Staff rest corner'}</p>${button('Sit on visitor bench · 10 minutes','living-sit')}`;}
    if(o.id==='ledger'&&open){const a=D.livingContacts(w,p.id);h+='<h3>Recent neighbour transactions</h3>'+ (a.length?a.map(e=>`<p class="fv-copy">${esc(D.eastById[e.other]?.keeper||e.other)} · ${e.bought?'Bought':'Sold'} ${esc(C.INVENTORY[e.key][0])} · ${e.n} copper · Day ${Math.floor(e.at/1440)+1}</p>`).join(''):'<p class="fv-copy">No recorded retail transactions yet.</p>')+button('Read journal · 10 minutes','living-read');}
    if(o.id==='aux'&&open&&p.id==='mon-terminal')h+='<h3>Freight handcart</h3><p class="fv-copy">'+(D.livingCart(w)?'Commissioned. The eastern courier uses it to accelerate eligible ground deliveries.':'Commission a handcart using '+esc(costs(D.livingCartCost))+'. Requires an idle courier.')+'</p>'+button('Build freight handcart','living-cart',D.livingCart(w)||!!D.districtCargo(w).job||!Object.entries(D.livingCartCost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n));
    if(o.id==='stock'&&open&&p.id==='mon-aqueduct'){h+='<h3>Water distribution</h3><p class="fv-copy">Send actual stored water along surveyed routes to operating facilities. For dedicated pumped pipelines, see the infrastructure section below.</p>';if(!D.districtCargo(w).job)for(const dest of C.EAST_SITES.filter(q=>q.id!==p.id&&q.input.water&&D.eastState(w,q.id).stage>=8)){const ds=D.eastState(w,dest.id),n=Math.min(5,D.eastState(w,p.id).stock.water||0,48-Object.values(ds.stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0));if(n>0)h+=button('Deliver '+n+' water → '+dest.name,'district-send:'+dest.id+':water:'+n);}}
    return h;}
  function livingStationAction(run,action){const d=districtInside(run);if(run.panel!=='district-room'||!d?.o)throw Error('Stand beside the actual room object.');const {p,o}=d;if(action==='living-toggle')edit(run,w=>D.livingToggle(w,p.id,o.id));else if(action==='living-cart'&&o.id==='aux'&&D.livingOpen(run.world,p.id,o.id))edit(run,w=>D.livingBuildCart(w,p.id));else if(action==='living-sit'&&o.id==='rest'||action==='living-read'&&o.id==='ledger'&&D.livingOpen(run.world,p.id,o.id)){edit(run,w=>S.step(w,10));run.livingPlayerPose={kind:action==='living-sit'?'sit':'read',x:run.scene.player.x,y:run.scene.player.y,until:run.time+3000,room:p.id};close(run);return;}else throw Error('Use the correct station and open the object first.');sound('gather');return districtRoomPanel(run);}
  // Reuse the existing pixel sprite's palette, outline and head. A seated frame bends the legs; work/read frames shift torso and arms.
  function livingPoseTexture(scene,sprite,kind,frame){const base=sprite.texture.key.split('-v93-')[0],id=base+'-v93-'+kind+'-'+frame;if(scene.textures.exists(id))return id;const src=scene.textures.get(base).getSourceImage(),W=src.width,H=src.height,t=scene.textures.createCanvas(id,W,H),ctx=t.getContext();ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;const leg=Math.max(1,Math.round(H*.24)),top=H-leg;
    const pad=Math.max(0,H-38),rect=(c,x,y,w,h)=>{ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.fillRect(x,y+pad,w,h);};
    if(kind==='sleep'){ctx.save();ctx.translate(W/2,H-10);ctx.rotate(-Math.PI/2);ctx.scale(.62,.70+(frame===2?.025:0));ctx.drawImage(src,-W/2,-H/2);ctx.restore();}
    else if(kind==='sit'){const n=Math.min(3+(frame===2?1:0),leg-1);ctx.drawImage(src,0,0,W,top,0,n,W,top);ctx.drawImage(src,0,top,W,leg,0,top+n,W,leg-n);}
    else if(kind==='work'||kind==='read'||kind==='carry'){const n=[0,1,2,1][frame%4],head=Math.floor(H*.55);ctx.drawImage(src,0,0);if(kind==='work'&&W===24&&H>=38){const ax=base.includes('_left_')?3:17,ay=pad+21;ctx.clearRect(ax,ay,4,7);ctx.drawImage(src,ax,ay,4,7,ax,ay-n,4,7);}if(kind==='read'){rect('#43362c',6,23,12,6);rect('#e8d6a9',7,23,4,4);rect('#e8d6a9',13,23,4,4);rect('#947a51',11,24,1,4);if(frame===2)rect('#f4e4c3',12,22,4,2);rect('#9c8560',8,25,2,1);rect('#9c8560',14,25,2,1);}else if(kind==='carry'){rect('#4e3828',5,23,14,8);rect('#a57b4f',6,24,12,6);rect('#d9b97f',6,24,12,1);rect('#785733',11,25,2,5);if(frame===2){rect('#e5c690',7,23,10,1);rect('#d0a570',11,25,2,3);}}else if(n){const x=base.includes('_left_')?4:17;rect('#bfc8c1',x,23-n,2,4);rect('#7e6446',x,26-n,2,2);}}
    else ctx.drawImage(src,0,0);t.refresh();return id;}

  function livingPose(run,sprite,kind,frame){if(!['sit','sleep','work','read','carry'].includes(kind))return;frame=run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/190)%4;const tex=livingPoseTexture(run.scene,sprite,kind,frame);if(sprite.texture.key!==tex)sprite.setTexture(tex);}
  function livingPlayerFrame(run){const a=run.livingPlayerPose,s=run.scene,p=s.player;if(!a)return;if(s.inside?.heritage?.id!==a.room||Math.hypot(p.x-a.x,p.y-a.y)>2||run.time>a.until){if(p.texture.key.includes('-v93-'))p.setTexture(p.texture.key.split('-v93-')[0]);delete run.livingPlayerPose;return;}livingPose(run,p,a.kind,0);}
  function livingCabinetFrame(run){const r=run.scene.inside,h=r?.heritage;if(!h||!D.eastById[h.id])return;const scene=run.scene,k=r.k;if(!h.livingFx){h.livingFx=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(10.25);r.objs.push(h.livingFx);}const g=h.livingFx;g.clear();for(const o of h.layout.objects){if(!['stock','aux','ledger'].includes(o.id))continue;const x=20000+o.x*T*k,y=o.y*T*k,W=o.w*T*k,H=o.h*T*k,open=D.livingOpen(run.world,h.id,o.id),a=h.livingMotion??={};a[o.id]=(a[o.id]??0)+((open?1:0)-(a[o.id]??0))*(run.reduced?1:.18);const t=a[o.id];if(t<.03)continue;const R=(col,xx,yy,ww,hh)=>g.fillStyle(col).fillRect(Math.round(xx),Math.round(yy),Math.max(1,Math.round(ww)),Math.max(1,Math.round(hh)));if(o.id==='ledger'){const bx=x+W*.5,by=y+H*.55;R(0x46362a,bx-12*k,by-7*k,24*k,13*k);R(0xe5d3a2,bx-11*k,by-6*k,10*k*t,11*k);R(0xf4e3b9,bx+1*k,by-6*k,10*k*t,11*k);for(let i=0;i<3;i++){R(0x9b805c,bx-9*k,by-3*k+i*3*k,7*k*t,k);R(0x9b805c,bx+2*k,by-3*k+i*3*k,7*k*t,k);}}else{const bx=x+W*.18,by=y+H*.36,bw=W*.64,bh=H*.4;R(0x302a23,bx,by,bw,bh);R(0x846746,bx,by+bh-3*k,bw,3*k);const stock=D.eastState(run.world,h.id).stock,goods=Object.keys(stock).filter(key=>stock[key]>0);for(let i=0;i<Math.min(6,goods.length);i++){const xx=bx+4*k+i*(bw-8*k)/6;R(0x59432f,xx,by+4*k,7*k,bh-7*k);R([0xb8a273,0x8da29b,0xc68f64][i%3],xx+k,by+5*k,5*k,bh-9*k);}const wing=bw*.25*t;R(0x694c36,bx-wing,by,wing,bh);R(0xb18c59,bx+bw,by,wing,bh);R(0xd6b97c,bx-wing+2*k,by+2*k,k,bh-4*k);}}}
  function livingCartArt(g,x,y,dx,dy,time,move,n){const k=move?Math.floor(time/95)%4:0,sign=dx<0?-1:1,xx=x-sign*13,yy=y+5;const R=(c,a,b,w,h)=>g.fillStyle(c).fillRect(Math.round(xx+a),Math.round(yy+b),w,h);R(0x302c29,-13,-1,26,4);R(0x47392d,-10,-17,20,17);R(0x986e43,-9,-16,18,12);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)R(0xc09b65,-8,-15+i*4,16,1);R(0x624830,-10,-5,20,4);for(const a of [-10,7]){R(0x292a29,a,-4,5,7);R(0x7e7461,a+1,-3,3,5);R(0xd9c39b,a+1+(k%2),-3+Math.floor(k/2)*2,1,2);}for(let i=0;i<Math.min(3,Math.ceil(n/2));i++){R(0x53432e,-8+i*6,-24,6,9);R(0xbfa06c,-7+i*6,-23,4,7);R(0xe3ce9c,-7+i*6,-23,4,1);}g.lineStyle(2,0x6b5239,1).lineBetween(xx+sign*10,yy-8,x, y-12);}
  // A real sale can generate a visible evening errand on an already learned ground route.
  // This is a visual follow-up to the charter's existing market settlement, not a second transaction.
  function livingErrand(run,id,m){if(m<1110||m>=1230)return null;const p=D.eastById[id],day=Math.floor(D.now(run.world)/1440),e=D.livingContacts(run.world,id).find(q=>q.bought&&q.other!==id&&Math.floor(q.at/1440)===day);if(!e)return null;const dest=D.eastById[e.other];if(!dest||dest.region!==p.region)return null;const cache=run.livingErrands??=new Map(),key=id+'|'+day+'|'+e.other;if(cache.size>100)cache.clear();if(!cache.has(key)){const path=D.districtRoute(run.scene.area,id,e.other,run.world);cache.set(key,path&&path.length>1&&path.length<=200?path:null);}const path=cache.get(key);if(!path)return null;const t=(m-1110)/120,u=t<.42?t/.42:t<.58?1:1-(t-.58)/.42,q=D.livingPoint(path.map(a=>({x:a[0]/2,y:a[1]/2})),u);return {...q,moving:t<.42||t>=.58,working:false,dx:t<.42?1:-1,errand:true,activity:t<.42?'Visiting market':t<.58?'Collecting purchase':'Returning home'};}

  function infraPanel(run,p,o){const mode=p.id==='mon-terminal'&&o.id==='aux'?'rail':p.id==='mon-aqueduct'&&o.id==='stock'?'water':null,w=run.world;if(!mode||!D.livingOpen(w,p.id,o.id))return '';const all=D.infraLines(w),lines=Object.entries(all).filter(([,l])=>l.mode===mode),canPay=c=>Object.entries(c).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n);let h=`<section style="margin:16px 0;padding:12px;border:1px solid #a48a53;border-radius:10px;background:#24352e"><small style="letter-spacing:.14em;color:#edd392">V94 • PUBLIC INFRASTRUCTURE</small><h3>${mode==='rail'?'Narrow-gauge freight railway':'Silverwater pumped network'}</h3><p class="fv-copy">Survey a learned ground route, fund construction, then dispatch real stock. ${mode==='rail'?'Freight shuttles run 06:00–21:00; storms and snow halt travel.':'The valve controls flow; pumps stop in snow. Water is delivered one unit at a time.'} Construction and shipments advance in game time.</p>`;
    for(const [id,l] of lines){const dest=D.eastById[l.to],job=l.job,source=D.eastById[l.position?l.to:p.id],target=D.eastById[l.position?p.id:l.to];h+=`<article class="v852-row"><b>${esc(dest.name)}</b><small>${l.path.length-1} half-tile segments · ${esc(l.phase)} · ${l.delivered} units delivered · ${l.journeys} completed runs</small>`;if(l.phase==='surveyed'){const c=D.infraCost(mode,l.path.length);h+=`<p>${esc(costs(c))} · ${D.infraDuration(l.path.length)} minutes</p>`+button('Fund construction','infra-build:'+id,!canPay(c));}else if(l.phase==='building')h+=`<p>Construction: ${Math.max(0,l.readyAt-D.now(w))} minutes remaining.</p>`;else {if(mode==='water')h+=button(l.gate?'Close valve':'Open valve','infra-gate:'+id)+'<p>'+esc(D.utilityWaterPlan(w,id).label)+'</p>'+button(D.utilityAuto(w)[id]?'Disable automatic supply':'Maintain two recipes of water','infra-auto:'+id);if(job){h+=`<p>${esc(job.key||'Empty return')} × ${job.n} · ${Math.round(job.progress/(l.path.length-1)*100)}% along route${mode==='water'?' · '+job.remaining+' units remaining':''}</p><meter min="0" max="${l.path.length-1}" value="${job.progress}"></meter>`+button('Cancel before departure','infra-cancel:'+id,job.progress!==0);}else{h+=`<p>${esc(source.name)} → ${esc(target.name)}</p>`;const stock=D.eastState(w,source.id).stock,space=48-Object.values(D.eastState(w,target.id).stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);for(const k of D.eastKeys(target.id).filter(k=>mode==='rail'||k==='water')){const n=Math.min(stock[k]||0,space,mode==='rail'?12:10);if(n>0)h+=button('Send '+n+' '+C.INVENTORY[k][0],'infra-send:'+id+':'+k+':'+n,mode==='water'&&!l.gate);}if(mode==='rail')h+=button('Dispatch empty shuttle','infra-send:'+id+'::0');}}h+='</article>';}
    if(lines.length<4){h+='<h4>Survey a new corridor · 1 survey chart</h4>';const list=D.infraCandidates(w,mode).filter(d=>!all[D.infraId(mode,d.id)]);h+=list.length?list.map(d=>button('Survey → '+d.name,'infra-survey:'+mode+':'+d.id,!canPay({'survey-chart':1}))).join(''):'<p class="fv-copy">Restore another compatible nearby facility.</p>';}return h+button('Refresh network','district-refresh')+'</section>';}
  function infraAction(run,action){const d=districtInside(run);if(run.panel!=='district-room'||!d?.o||!D.livingOpen(run.world,d.p.id,d.o.id))throw Error('Open the actual infrastructure station.');const mode=d.p.id==='mon-terminal'&&d.o.id==='aux'?'rail':d.p.id==='mon-aqueduct'&&d.o.id==='stock'?'water':null;if(!mode)throw Error('Visit the railway terminal or aqueduct.');const [cmd,id,k,n]=action.split(':');if(cmd==='infra-survey'){if(id!==mode)throw Error('Wrong utility.');edit(run,w=>D.infraSurvey(w,run.scene.area,mode,k));}else{if(D.infraLines(run.world)[id]?.mode!==mode)throw Error('Wrong utility link.');edit(run,w=>{if(cmd==='infra-build')D.infraBuild(w,id);else if(cmd==='infra-gate')D.infraGate(w,id);else if(cmd==='infra-auto'&&mode==='water')D.utilityAutoToggle(w,id);else if(cmd==='infra-send')D.infraDispatch(w,id,k,Number(n));else if(cmd==='infra-cancel')D.infraCancel(w,id);else throw Error('Unknown infrastructure action.');});}sound('gather');return districtRoomPanel(run);}
  function infraVehicle(g,x,y,dx,dy,cargo,time,moving){const sign=dx<0?-1:1,vertical=Math.abs(dy)>Math.abs(dx);const R=(col,a,b,w,h)=>g.fillStyle(col).fillRect(Math.round(x+a),Math.round(y+b),w,h);g.fillStyle(0x172c27,.3).fillEllipse(x,y+3,36,12);if(vertical){R(0x302f2a,-9,-26,18,30);R(0x36534c,-7,-25,14,22);R(0x738f78,-7,-24,14,2);R(0x252d2e,-6,-21,12,9);R(0x9bc0bb,-4,-20,8,5);R(0xc69b54,-7,-10,14,3);R(0x684c36,-8,-6,16,11);R(0xb58c5b,-6,-5,12,7);if(cargo)R(0xdfbd7c,-5,-5,10,5);R(0x252c29,-11,-20,3,8);R(0x252c29,8,-20,3,8);}else{R(0x292f2c,-19,-4,38,6);R(0x2e4942,-18,-22,15,18);R(0x73947d,-18,-23,15,3);R(0x20302f,-16,-18,10,7);R(0x92b6b3,-15,-17,8,4);R(0x547861,-3,-16,17,12);R(0x9eaf88,-2,-16,14,2);R(0x4c3d31,14,-12,7,9);R(0xd4ad64,14,-11,5,2);if(cargo){R(0x664735,0,-23,12,9);R(0xc8a46c,1,-22,10,7);R(0xe4ca91,1,-22,10,1);}for(const p of [-13,8]){R(0x202927,p,-3,7,7);R(0x98937b,p+1,-2,5,5);R(0x4d6159,p+2+Math.floor(time/100)%2,-1,2,3);}R(0x343b32,-2,-22,5,6);R(0x9da18b,-3,-24,7,2);R(0xf1ce83,sign>0?18:-20,-8,2,3);if(moving){const f=Math.floor(time/150)%4;g.fillStyle(0xd4d6bd,.22).fillCircle(x-1-f*2,y-26-f*3,2+f);}}}
  function pocketPoint(r){return {x:r.x,y:r.y};}
  function pocketNear(run,id){const r=D.civicPockets(run.world)[id];return !!r&&reachable(run.scene,pocketPoint(r),42);}
