  function pocketPanel(run,id){if(!pocketNear(run,id))throw Error('Visit the roadside plaza.');run.pocketId=id;const w=run.world,r=D.civicPockets(w)[id],p=D.cityProjects(w)[id];let b=title('CIVIC WALKWAY','Roadside mosaic court',D.eastById[p.from].name+' ↔ '+D.eastById[p.to].name);if(r.status!=='open')b+='<p>Construction · '+Math.max(0,r.ends-D.now(w))+' game minutes remaining.</p>';else b+='<p>Public water reserve: '+r.water+'/8 · Recorded visits: '+r.visits+'</p><p>Follow the stone path to either hall. The reserve holds only water donated by travellers.</p>'+button('Donate 1 water','pocket-donate',r.water>=8||!(w.resources.water>0))+button('Take 1 water','pocket-take',!r.water)+button('Rest 20 game minutes','pocket-rest');return panel(run,'pocket',b+button('Close','close'));}
  function pocketAction(run,action){if(run.panel!=='pocket'||!pocketNear(run,run.pocketId))throw Error('Visit the roadside plaza.');const id=run.pocketId;edit(run,w=>{if(action==='pocket-donate')D.pocketWater(w,id,true);else if(action==='pocket-take')D.pocketWater(w,id,false);else if(action==='pocket-rest'){D.pocketVisit(w,id);S.step(w,20,node=>growthClear(run,node));}else throw Error('Unknown plaza action.');});return pocketPanel(run,id);}
  function pocketPaint(g,r,theme,night){const {x,y,ax,ay}=r,pal=[0xb7a77c,0xb4a3bd,0xbb8c63,0x9ab7a9,0xc8b47b,0x9faac0],c=pal[theme%6];g.lineStyle(12,0x534e3d,1).lineBetween(ax,ay,x,y).lineStyle(8,c,1).lineBetween(ax,ay,x,y);g.fillStyle(0x454d3c).fillRect(x-14,y-14,28,28);g.fillStyle(r.status==='open'?c:0x897757).fillRect(x-12,y-12,24,24);for(let dy=-10;dy<12;dy+=4)for(let dx=-10;dx<12;dx+=4){if((dx+dy)%8===0)g.fillStyle(0xe5d7b4,.45).fillRect(x+dx,y+dy,3,3);}if(r.status==='building'){g.fillStyle(0xc1a675).fillRect(x-7,y-2,14,3).fillRect(x-6,y+3,12,3);return;}g.fillStyle(0x566e65).fillRect(x-5,y-5,10,10);g.fillStyle(0xdad6b2).fillRect(x-3,y-3,6,6);g.fillStyle(r.water?0x568b9c:0xa69877).fillRect(x-2,y-2,4,4);for(const dx of [-10,8])for(const dy of [-10,8])g.fillStyle(night?0xb5a57a:0xf3dba7).fillRect(x+dx,y+dy,2,2);}
  function pocketFrame(run){const sc=run.scene,entries=Object.entries(D.civicPockets(run.world));if(sc.inside||run.film||!entries.length){run.pocketGround?.setVisible(false);return;}if(!run.pocketGround){run.pocketGround=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(.93);sc.areaObjs.push(run.pocketGround);}const g=run.pocketGround,r=sc.cameras.main.worldView,night=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay>=1140||run.world.clock.minuteOfDay<360;const sig=entries.map(([id,a])=>id+a.status+a.water).join('|')+'@'+Math.floor(r.x/128)+','+Math.floor(r.y/128)+','+Math.ceil(r.width/128)+','+Math.ceil(r.height/128)+night;g.setVisible(true);if(sig===run.pocketSig)return;run.pocketSig=sig;g.clear();for(const [id,a]of entries){if(a.x<r.x-160||a.x>r.right+160||a.y<r.y-160||a.y>r.bottom+160)continue;const p=D.cityProjects(run.world)[id];pocketPaint(g,a,C.EAST_REGIONS.findIndex(v=>v.id===D.eastById[p.from].region),night);}}
  function pocketLedger(run,entries){const w=run.world;return '<h3>Roadside civic courts</h3><p>Build a walkable mosaic lay-by on a completed stone corridor. Donate water, rest, or read directions. No new obstacle is placed in the travel lane.</p>'+entries.map(([id,p])=>{const r=D.civicPockets(w)[id],g=D.cityProgress(w,id),near=!run.scene.inside&&eastNear(run,p.from);return '<article class="charter-row"><b>'+esc(D.eastById[p.from].name+' → '+D.eastById[p.to].name)+'</b><small>'+esc(r?r.status+' · ('+(r.x/T).toFixed(2)+', '+(r.y/T).toFixed(2)+') · water '+r.water+'/8':g.paved===g.total?'Eligible for roadside survey':'Finish the stone road first')+'</small>'+(!r?button('Survey & build · '+costs(D.pocketCost),'charter-city-pocket:'+id,!near||g.paved!==g.total||!g.total||!Object.entries(D.pocketCost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n)):'')+'</article>';}).join('');}
  function branchPanel(run,p){const w=run.world,d=D.branchDef(w,p.id),j=D.branchJob(w,p.id),s=D.eastState(w,p.id);if(!d)return '';let b='<h3>Specialist commission · '+esc(d.name)+'</h3><p>'+esc(costs(d.cost)+' → '+d.n+' '+C.INVENTORY[d.out][0]+' · '+d.minutes+' game minutes')+'</p>';if(j)return b+'<p>'+esc(D.now(w)>=j.ends?'Ready for collection':(j.ends-D.now(w))+' minutes remaining')+'</p>'+button('Collect specialist result','district-branch-collect',D.now(w)<j.ends)+button('Refresh','district-refresh');const answers=d.answers.map((a,i)=>({a,i,rank:C.hash(i,s.runs+Math.floor(D.now(w)/1440),1025)})).sort((a,b)=>a.rank-b.rank);return b+'<p>'+esc(d.question)+'</p>'+answers.map(v=>button(v.a,'district-branch-start:'+v.i,!!s.job||!Object.entries(d.cost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n))).join('');}

  function utilitySitePanel(run,id){const w=run.world,r=D.utilityLinks(w)[id],afford=c=>Object.entries(c).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n);let b='';if(id===D.utilityHub&&D.eastState(w,id).stage>=8){b='<h3>Mechanical drive · Wind Loft</h3><p>Optional low-capacity transmission. Up to two simultaneous export slots in wind; one in mild weather; none in storms or snow. The mill retains its own separate drive.</p>'+button(w.development.utilities1024?.enabled===false?'Start export drive':'Stop export drive','east-power-switch');for(const [to,l]of Object.entries(D.utilityLinks(w)))b+='<article class="v852-row"><b>'+esc(D.eastById[to].name)+'</b><small>'+esc(l.phase)+' · '+l.path.length+' survey points</small></article>';if((D.charterState(w)?.era??0)>=2)b+=D.utilityCandidates(w).map(p=>button('Survey drive to '+p.name,'east-power-survey:'+p.id,Object.keys(D.utilityLinks(w)).length>=4||!afford({'survey-chart':1}))).join('');}
    if(r){b+='<h3>Mechanical transmission</h3><p>'+esc(r.phase==='open'?D.utilityStatus(w,id).label:r.phase==='building'?Math.max(0,r.readyAt-D.now(w))+' game minutes remaining':'Survey complete · '+costs(D.utilityCost(r.path.length)))+'</p>';if(r.phase==='surveyed')b+=button('Install mechanical drive','east-power-build',!!D.eastState(w,id).job||!afford(D.utilityCost(r.path.length)));if(r.phase==='open')b+=button(r.enabled?'Disengage drive (pauses work)':'Engage drive','east-power-toggle');}return b;}
  function utilityBody(run){const w=run.world,kind=S.weatherAt(w.clock),capacity=D.utilityCapacity(w,kind),a=Object.entries(D.utilityLinks(w)),water=Object.entries(D.infraLines(w)).filter(([,l])=>l.mode==='water');return '<div class="charter-hero"><div class="charter-emblem">✺</div><div><small>V102.4 · RESOURCE UTILITIES</small><h3>Water & mechanical drive</h3><p>Actual restored sources, funded links and current production constraints. Visit source and receiver entrances to survey and commission mechanical connections.</p></div></div><h3>Wind Loft · '+capacity+' export slots</h3>'+a.map(([id,r])=>'<article class="charter-row"><b>'+esc(D.eastById[id].name)+'</b><small>'+esc(r.phase+' · '+D.utilityStatus(w,id).label)+'</small></article>').join('')+'<h3>Silverwater · '+(D.eastState(w,'mon-aqueduct').stock.water||0)+' water in reserve</h3>'+water.map(([id,l])=>'<article class="charter-row"><b>'+esc(D.eastById[l.to].name)+'</b><small>'+esc(D.utilityWaterPlan(w,id).label)+' · '+(D.utilityAuto(w)[id]?'Automatic':'Manual')+'</small></article>').join('')+'<p>Automatic water agreements maintain up to two recipes of water in the destination store, using actual source stock. Configure at the aqueduct stock station.</p>';}
  function utilityFrame(run){const scene=run.scene,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,links=Object.values(D.utilityLinks(run.world));if(scene.inside||run.film||!links.length){run.utilityGround?.setVisible(false);return;}if(!run.utilityGround){run.utilityGround=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(.91);scene.areaObjs.push(run.utilityGround);}const g=run.utilityGround;g.setVisible(true);const sig=links.map(l=>l.phase+l.enabled+l.path.length).join('|')+'@'+Math.floor(rect.x/128)+','+Math.floor(rect.y/128)+','+Math.ceil(rect.width/128)+','+Math.ceil(rect.height/128);if(sig===run.utilitySig)return;run.utilitySig=sig;g.clear();for(const l of links)for(let i=1;i<l.path.length;i++){const a=l.path[i-1],b=l.path[i],x=a[0]*16,y=a[1]*16,u=b[0]*16,v=b[1]*16;if(Math.max(x,u)<rect.x-180||Math.min(x,u)>rect.right+180||Math.max(y,v)<rect.y-180||Math.min(y,v)>rect.bottom+180)continue;if(l.phase!=='open'){if(i%4===0)g.fillStyle(0xd9bb7c).fillRect(x+9,y-6,3,6);continue;}g.lineStyle(5,0x453c32,1).lineBetween(x+9,y-7,u+9,v-7);g.lineStyle(2,0xbda779,1).lineBetween(x+9,y-8,u+9,v-8);if(i%4===0)g.fillStyle(0x5b4733).fillRect(x+6,y-9,7,9).fillStyle(l.enabled?0x9abf77:0xb67d66).fillRect(x+8,y-9,3,3);}}

  function infraFrame(run){const scene=run.scene,lines=D.infraLines(run.world),rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView;if(scene.inside||run.film){run.infraGround?.setVisible(false);for(const g of run.infraCars?.values()||[])g.setVisible(false);return;}const list=Object.entries(lines);if(!list.length)return;const cars=run.infraCars??=new Map();if(!run.infraGround){run.infraGround=scene.add.graphics().setDepth(.9);scene.areaObjs.push(run.infraGround);}const g=run.infraGround;g.setVisible(true);const sig=list.map(([id,l])=>id+l.phase+l.gate).join('|')+'@'+Math.floor(rect.x/128)+','+Math.floor(rect.y/128)+','+Math.ceil(rect.width/128)+','+Math.ceil(rect.height/128);if(sig!==run.infraSig){run.infraSig=sig;g.clear();for(const [,l] of list){for(let i=1;i<l.path.length;i++){const a=l.path[i-1],b=l.path[i],x=a[0]*16,y=a[1]*16,u=b[0]*16,v=b[1]*16;if(Math.max(x,u)<rect.x-180||Math.min(x,u)>rect.right+180||Math.max(y,v)<rect.y-180||Math.min(y,v)>rect.bottom+180)continue;const len=Math.hypot(u-x,v-y),nx=-(v-y)/len,ny=(u-x)/len;if(l.phase!=='open'){if(i%3===0)g.fillStyle(l.phase==='surveyed'?0xe5c87e:0xa77d4c).fillRect(x-2,y-4,4,8);continue;}if(l.mode==='rail'){g.lineStyle(14,0x66604d,1).lineBetween(x,y,u,v);g.lineStyle(2,0x44392e,1).lineBetween(x+nx*6,y+ny*6,x-nx*6,y-ny*6);for(const off of [-4,4]){g.lineStyle(3,0x333d3a,1).lineBetween(x+nx*off,y+ny*off,u+nx*off,v+ny*off);g.lineStyle(1,0xd0c6a1,1).lineBetween(x+nx*off,y+ny*off-1,u+nx*off,v+ny*off-1);}}else{g.lineStyle(7,0x283b36,1).lineBetween(x,y+9,u,v+9);g.lineStyle(4,0x6a9b96,1).lineBetween(x,y+8,u,v+8);g.lineStyle(1,0xafc7b1,1).lineBetween(x,y+7,u,v+7);if(i%4===0)g.fillStyle(0x465c55).fillRect(x-3,y+5,6,7);}}if(l.mode==='water'&&l.phase==='open'){const a=l.path[0];g.fillStyle(0x263e39).fillRect(a[0]*16-5,a[1]*16+4,10,11).fillStyle(l.gate?0x98bd7b:0xb97760).fillRect(a[0]*16-3,a[1]*16+5,6,4);}}}
    for(const [id,l] of list){let v=cars.get(id);if(l.phase!=='open'||l.mode!=='rail'){v?.setVisible(false);continue;}const path=l.path,j=l.job,dist=path.length-1,k=j?Math.min(dist,j.progress):0,target=l.position?dist-k:k,tracks=run.infraMotion??=new Map();let m=tracks.get(id);if(!m){m={from:target,to:target,at:run.time};tracks.set(id,m);}const sample=()=>m.from+(m.to-m.from)*Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(run.time-m.at)/750));if(m.to!==target){m.from=sample();m.to=target;m.at=run.time;}const pos=sample(),idx=Math.max(0,Math.min(dist,Math.floor(pos))),a=path[idx],b=path[Math.min(dist,idx+1)],q=pos-idx,x=(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*q)*16,y=(a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*q)*16;if(x<rect.x-80||x>rect.right+80||y<rect.y-80||y>rect.bottom+80){v?.setVisible(false);continue;}if(!v){v=scene.add.graphics();cars.set(id,v);scene.areaObjs.push(v);}v.setVisible(true).clear().setDepth(run.view.depthAt(y)+.003);infraVehicle(v,x,y,b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1],j?.n||0,run.time,Math.abs(pos-target)>.01||!!j&&j.progress<dist);}}

  // V95: residences use their own footprint, real interiors and the original NPC sprite renderer.
  function homeAddress(run,p){const h=D.homeBySite[p.id];if(!h)return '';const r=D.homeState(run.world,h.id);return `<h3>Independent residence</h3><p>${esc(h.name)} · (${h.ax.toFixed(1)}, ${h.ay.toFixed(1)}) · ${esc(D.homeStages[r.stage])}</p><p class="fv-copy">Visit the physical plot to build. Existing staff accommodation is retained as a work break area.</p>`;}
  function homeNear(run,h){return !run.scene.inside&&reachable(run.scene,{x:h.ax*T,y:h.ay*T},53);}
  function homePanel(run,id){const h=D.homeById[id];if(!h||!homeNear(run,h))throw Error('Stand at the residential plot.');const w=run.world,r=D.homeState(w,id),p=D.eastById[h.site],staff=D.charterState(w)?.houses[h.site];run.homeSite=id;let b=`<p>${esc(p.keeper)} · ${esc(p.name)} · ${esc(D.homeStages[r.stage])}</p><p class="fv-copy">This independent house uses the existing resident's wage and market account. It does not create a second resident or income stream.</p>`;if(!staff)b+='<p>First restore the resident’s workshop and furnish staff accommodation through Civilization Charter.</p>';else if(r.readyAt)b+=`<p>Construction underway · ${Math.max(0,r.readyAt-D.now(w))} game minutes left.</p>`;else if(r.stage<3){b+=`<h3>${esc(D.homeStages[r.stage+1])}</h3><p>${esc(costs(D.homeCosts[r.stage]))} · ${D.homeMinutes[r.stage]} minutes</p>`+button('Fund next phase','home-build',!Object.entries(D.homeCosts[r.stage]).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n));}else b+=`<p>${D.homeAt(w,id,w.clock.minuteOfDay)?'Resident at home':'Resident away at work or commuting'} · ${r.visits} successful visits</p>`+button('Enter cottage','home-enter');return panel(run,'home-plot',title('THE ART OF A LIVING WORLD',h.name,'A residence with its own address, garden, rooms and daily routine.')+b+button('Close','close'));}
  function homeRoomPanel(run){const id=run.scene.inside?.heritage?.id,h=D.homeById[id],o=Art.heritageRoomNear(run.scene);if(!h||!o)return;const w=run.world,p=D.eastById[h.site],r=D.homeState(w,id),s=D.roomState(w,id),at=D.homeAt(w,id,w.clock.minuteOfDay);let b=`<p>${esc(p.keeper)} · ${at?'At home':'Away'} · Visits ${r.visits}</p>`;if(o.id==='lamp'){edit(run,w=>D.roomAction(w,id,'lamp'));toast(run,D.roomState(run.world,id).lamp?'Lantern lit':'Lantern extinguished');return;}if(o.id==='pantry'){const stock=s.stock||{},used=Object.values(stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);b+=button(D.domesticState(w,id).pantry?'Close cupboard':'Open cupboard','home-toggle:pantry');b+=`<h3>Household provisions ${used}/36</h3>`;for(const k of ['food','water'])b+=`<p>${esc(C.INVENTORY[k][0])}: ${stock[k]||0}</p>`+button('Store one','home-store:'+k,used>=36||!(w.resources[k]>0))+button('Take one','home-take:'+k,!(stock[k]>0));}else if(o.id==='bed'){b+='<p>Personal bed and quilt. The resident rests here after the evening shift.</p>';}else if(o.id==='hearth'){b+='<p>Share supplies with the resident; the charter records the daily need.</p>'+button('Offer 1 food','home-feed:food',!at||!(w.resources.food>0))+button('Offer 1 water','home-feed:water',!at||!(w.resources.water>0))+button('Serve food from cupboard','home-serve:food',!at||!(s.stock?.food>0))+button('Serve water from cupboard','home-serve:water',!at||!(s.stock?.water>0));}else if(o.id==='table'){b+='<h3>Evening conversation</h3><p>How would you greet a neighbour at home?</p>';const correct=C.HOMES.indexOf(h)%3,answers=['Give me your belongings.','I do not need to be polite.'];answers.splice(correct,0,'Good evening! May I sit with you?');b+=answers.map((a,i)=>button(a,'home-greet:'+i,!D.homeSocial(w,id,w.clock.minuteOfDay)||r.lastVisit===Math.floor(D.now(w)/1440))).join('');}else b+=`<p>Here ${esc(p.keeper)} keeps personal journals and mementos from ${esc(p.name)}.</p><p>Neighbour meetings: ${D.neighbourRecord(w,id).meetings}</p>`+button(D.domesticState(w,id).books?'Close journal':'Open journal','home-toggle:books')+button(D.domesticState(w,id).curtains?'Draw curtains open':'Close curtains','home-toggle:curtains');return panel(run,'home-room',title(h.name.toUpperCase(),o.name,'Domestic life · permanent household')+b+button('Close','close'));}
  function homeAction(run,action){const [cmd,arg]=action.split(':');if(run.panel==='home-plot'){const h=D.homeById[run.homeSite];if(!h||!homeNear(run,h))throw Error('Return to the plot.');if(cmd==='home-build'){edit(run,w=>D.homeStart(w,h.id));return homePanel(run,h.id);}if(cmd==='home-enter'){if(!D.roomOpen(run.world,h.id))throw Error('Finish construction first.');const s=run.scene,p=s.player,out={x:p.x,y:p.y};edit(run,w=>D.visitRoom(w,h.id));close(run);restoreSummitCamera(run,s.cameras.main);if(!Art.heritageRoomEnter(s,run.world,h.id,out))throw Error('House unavailable.');toast(run,'Home · E at an object · Exit at the threshold.');return;}}const id=run.scene.inside?.heritage?.id,h=D.homeById[id],o=Art.heritageRoomNear(run.scene);if(run.panel!=='home-room'||!h||!o)throw Error('Stand beside the actual household object.');if((cmd==='home-store'||cmd==='home-take')&&o.id==='pantry')edit(run,w=>D.roomTransfer(w,id,cmd==='home-store'?'in':'out',arg,1));else if(cmd==='home-feed'&&o.id==='hearth'&&D.homeAt(run.world,id,run.world.clock.minuteOfDay))edit(run,w=>D.charterProvision(w,h.site,arg));else if(cmd==='home-greet'&&o.id==='table')edit(run,w=>D.homeGreet(w,id,Number(arg)));else if(cmd==='home-serve'&&o.id==='hearth')edit(run,w=>D.domesticServe(w,id,arg));else if(cmd==='home-toggle'&&(o.id===arg||o.id==='books'&&arg==='curtains'))edit(run,w=>D.domesticToggle(w,id,arg));else throw Error('That action is not available here.');sound('gather');return homeRoomPanel(run);}
  function homePaint(ctx,style,stage,building,night,variant=0){const R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>{ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h));},P=(c,pts)=>{ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.beginPath();pts.forEach(([x,y],i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y));ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();};const colors=[['#4c5141','#9b6b46','#bd9562','#e6ce99','#884e37','#be7750'],['#4f4751','#746b88','#b1a0a9','#eadbc7','#645472','#a1819c'],['#574537','#a16d48','#cc9864','#e2c190','#8e573a','#c28453'],['#384d48','#65887b','#a7b7a0','#d9dbc0','#435f58','#7c9a83'],['#54523a','#9f9256','#ccb974','#f0db9f','#847044','#b49c5c'],['#3d4d50','#778e91','#a6b5b2','#e0dfcf','#475e70','#8199a5']][style];const [shadow,wallDark,wall,trim,roofDark,roof]=colors;
    // Low surrounding garden and individually clustered stepping stones.
    P('#26392c66',[[14,106],[110,101],[151,122],[132,138],[20,137],[4,123]]);for(let i=0;i<36;i++){let x=7+(i*37+style*13)%144,y=111+(i*11)%22;R(i%3===0?'#849464':'#536d4d',x,y,3+i%3,2);if(i%5===0){R('#4b6845',x+1,y-3,2,5);R(['#d6b378','#d4a9af','#d7d8aa'][i%3],x,y-4,3,2);}}for(let i=0;i<4;i++){R('#534e43',66+i%2*4,120+i*4,27-i%2*3,5);R('#aaa28a',67+i%2*4,119+i*4,25-i%2*3,3);}if(stage>0||building){P('#393c34',[[28,83],[128,83],[139,113],[27,117],[16,106]]);P('#a59b83',[[29,86],[127,86],[135,111],[27,114],[20,106]]);for(let j=0;j<3;j++)for(let i=0;i<8;i++)R(['#7d796b','#b8aa8d','#8d8575'][(i+j)%3],26+i*13+j%2*3,92+j*7,10,4);}
    if(stage===0){for(const x of [28,124]){R('#6d5237',x,82,3,30);R('#ddbc71',x,83,3,4);}R('#9f8355',29,91,94,1);R('#9f8355',29,104,94,1);}if(building&&stage===1){for(const x of [29,55,101,126]){R(shadow,x,42,5,65);R(trim,x+1,43,1,61);}R(wallDark,29,48,103,5);P(wallDark,[[29,45],[79,17],[132,45],[128,49],[79,23],[33,49]]);if(building)for(const x of [37,110])R('#9a7549',x,62,4,50);}
    if(stage>=2||building&&stage>=2){R(shadow,24,48,112,61);R(wallDark,27,49,106,56);R(wall,29,51,98,49);P(wallDark,[[127,51],[136,45],[136,103],[127,109]]);if(style===2||style===5){for(let j=0;j<7;j++)for(let i=0;i<8;i++)R(j%2?'#9d886c':wallDark,30+i*12+j%2*4,54+j*6,9,1);}else{for(let j=0;j<7;j++)R(wallDark,30,54+j*7,95,1);for(const x of [31,58,98,124]){R(wallDark,x,51,4,48);R(trim,x+1,52,1,48);}}R(shadow,63,72,28,35);R(wallDark,66,74,22,30);for(let y=77;y<103;y+=5)R(trim,68,y,1,3);R('#d5b47b',83,89,3,3);R('#5c5b4e',60,107,34,4);R('#c2b396',59,105,36,3);for(const x of [39,107]){R(shadow,x-2,62,19,20);R(trim,x,63,15,17);R(night&&stage===3?'#edb960':'#536f73',x+2,65,11,13);R(night&&stage===3?'#ffe0a0':'#a5c5bc',x+3,66,3,5);R(wallDark,x+7,64,2,15);R(wallDark,x+1,72,13,2);R(trim,x-2,80,19,3);}const roofY=style===1||style===5?13:24;P(shadow,[[16,54],[76,roofY-4],[145,51],[140,59],[21,60]]);P(roofDark,[[19,51],[76,roofY],[139,49],[137,55],[22,56]]);P(roof,[[23,48],[76,roofY+2],[131,47],[131,51],[23,51]]);for(let x=a+3+(y%2)*4;x<b-3;x+=9)R(roofDark,x,y-3,1,3);}R(trim,18,54,120,2);R(shadow,18,56,120,3);if(style===0||style===2||style===4){R(shadow,105,17,13,27);R(wallDark,108,18,8,24);R(trim,104,15,15,4);for(let y=22;y<40;y+=5)R(shadow,108,y,8,1);}if(style===1||style===5){P(shadow,[[66,35],[78,25],[90,35],[90,48],[66,48]]);R(trim,70,35,16,12);R(night?'#e4b873':'#698d94',72,37,12,8);R(wallDark,77,35,2,11);}if(stage===3){for(const x of [35,112]){R(wallDark,x,89,15,7);R(trim,x,88,15,2);for(let j=0;j<4;j++){R('#597647',x+j*4,84-j%2*3,3,5);R(j%2?'#e8c891':'#ac7e89',x+j*4,83-j%2*3,3,2);}}if(style===0){for(const x of     if(building){for(const x of [22,136]){R('#654a33',x,62,3,54);R('#bc965f',x,62,1,54);}for(let y=76;y<111;y+=13)R('#b28c58',21,y,118,3);for(let i=0;i<5;i++)R(wallDark,8+i*5,126-i%2*2,12,3);}}
  // V96: twelve authored silhouettes. The ground footprint and doorway remain V95-compatible.
  function homePaint96(ctx,style,stage,building,night,variant=0){
    if(stage<2){homePaint(ctx,style,stage,building,night,variant);return;}
    const palettes=[['#34463b','#6a4834','#a87c51','#dab985','#583d36','#97624c','#c88c63'],['#3d404c','#635665','#a393a4','#e6d7ca','#4e465e','#806d8b','#b39ab4'],['#4c4039','#84644e','#bca188','#e8d6ab','#684837','#a46b49','#db9960'],['#29463f','#4d7066','#92b5a1','#d1dbc0','#36554d','#638579','#a2b79a'],['#4d4833','#88724a','#d0b878','#f0dca2','#695333','#a58a4e','#d7bc77'],['#30444b','#5a727b','#a2b7ba','#e1e4d7','#42586d','#708da3','#a5bacc']];
    const [ink,shade,wall,trim,rd,roof,hil]=palettes[style];
    const R=(c,x,y,w,h)=>{ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.round(w),Math.round(h));};
    const P=(c,pts)=>{ctx.fillStyle=c;ctx.beginPath();pts.forEach(([x,y],i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y));ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();};
    const line=(c,a,b)=>{let [x,y]=a.map(Math.round),[X,Y]=b.map(Math.round),dx=Math.abs(X-x),dy=-Math.abs(Y-y),sx=x<X?1:-1,sy=y<Y?1:-1,e=dx+dy;for(;;){R(c,x,y,1,1);if(x===X&&y===Y)break;let z=2*e;if(z>=dy){e+=dy;x+=sx}if(z<=dx){e+=dx;y+=sy}}};
    const masonry=(x,y,w,h,kind)=>{R(ink,x-2,y-2,w+4,h+3);R(shade,x,y,w,h);R(wall,x+2,y+1,w-5,h-3);P(shade,[[x+w-6,y],[x+w,y-4],[x+w,y+h],[x+w-6,y+h]]);if(kind==='stone'){for(let j=0;j<h-4;j+=6){for(let i=0;i<w-8;i+=12){let xx=x+3+i+(j%12?3:0);if(xx+6<x+w-4){R(shade,xx,y+j+5,Math.min(9,x+w-4-xx),1);R(trim,xx,y+j+1,Math.min(5,x+w-4-xx),1);R(shade,xx,y+j+1,1,4);}}}}else if(kind==='wood'){for(let j=6;j<h-2;j+=6){R(shade,x+2,y+j,w-8,1);R(trim,x+4,y+j+1,w-13,1);}for(let i=8;i<w-6;i+=22){R(shade,x+i,y,3,h);R(trim,x+i,y+2,1,h-4);}}else{for(let j=5;j<h-2;j+=8)for(let i=4;i<w-9;i+=13)if((i+j+style)%3===0){R(trim,x+i,y+j,3,1);R(shade,x+i+2,y+j+1,2,1);}}R(shade,x,y+h-5,w,5);R(trim,x+1,y+h-6,w-5,1);};
    const pitched=(x,y,w,rise,mode='gable')=>{const left=x-5,right=x+w+5,mid=x+w*.48,top=y-rise;let poly=mode==='hip'?[[left,y],[x+13,top],[x+w-17,top],[right,y]]:mode==='mansard'?[[left,y],[x+7,top+9],[x+19,top],[x+w-16,top],[x+w-5,top+9],[right,y]]:mode==='lean'?[[left,y-5],[left,top],[right,y-4],[right,y]]:[[left,y],[mid,top],[right,y]];P(ink,poly.map(([a,b])=>[a,b+2]));P(rd,poly);ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();poly.forEach(([a,b],i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(a,b):ctx.moveTo(a,b));ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();R(roof,left,top,right-left,y-top-2);for(let j=top+2;j<y;j+=5){R(rd,left,j,right-left,1);for(let i=left+((j%10)?3:0);i<right;i+=9){R(rd,i,j+1,1,3);R(hil,i+2,j+1,4,1);}}if(mode==='gable'||mode==='lean')P(rd,[[mid,top],[right,y],[mid+11,y],[mid+1,top+4]]);ctx.restore();for(let i=0;i<poly.length-1;i++)line(hil,poly[i],poly[i+1]);R(ink,left,y,right-left,4);R(trim,left+1,y,right-left-2,1);};
    const window=(x,y,w=14,h=17,arched=false)=>{R(ink,x-2,y-2,w+4,h+4);R(trim,x,y,w,h);R(night&&stage===3?'#eaba68':'#456874',x+2,y+2,w-4,h-4);R(night&&stage===3?'#ffe7a5':'#9fc1ba',x+3,y+3,Math.max(2,w/3),Math.max(3,h/3));R(shade,x+Math.floor(w/2),y+1,2,h-2);R(shade,x+1,y+Math.floor(h/2),w-2,2);if(arched){R(ink,x-2,y-2,4,4);R(ink,x+w-2,y-2,4,4);}R(trim,x-3,y+h,w+6,2);R(shade,x-3,y+h+2,w+6,1);};
    const door=(x=69,y=76,w=22,h=30)=>{R(ink,x-3,y-3,w+6,h+4);R(trim,x-1,y-1,w+2,h);R(shade,x+2,y+2,w-4,h-2);for(let i=4;i<w-2;i+=5)R(wall,x+i,y+3,1,h-5);R(trim,x+w-6,y+Math.round(h*.55),2,2);R(ink,x-5,y+h,w+10,4);R(trim,x-6,y+h-1,w+12,2);};
    const chimney=(x,y,h=25)=>{R(ink,x-1,y-1,13,h+2);R(shade,x,y,11,h);R(wall,x+1,y+1,6,h-2);for(let i=4;i<h;i+=5)R(ink,x+1,y+i,10,1);R(trim,x-2,y-2,15,3);};
    const porch=(x,y,w)=>{R(ink,x,y+2,w,5);R(shade,x,y+6,w,4);for(let i=x+3;i<x+w;i+=w-8){R(ink,i,y+5,4,24);R(trim,i+1,y+6,1,22);}P(rd,[[x-3,y+2],[x+4,y-9],[x+w-3,y-9],[x+w+4,y+2]]);R(hil,x+3,y-8,w-6,1);R(trim,x-3,y+2,w+7,1);};
    // Garden silhouette: varied beds, flagstone approach, low hedges outside doors.
    P('#23362e99',[[15,101],[127,100],[151,116],[139,134],[23,137],[5,123]]);
    for(let y=112;y<137;y+=4)for(let x=9;x<150;x+=5){const z=(x*71+y*31+style*19+variant*17)%17;if(z<5)R(z<2?'#819563':'#506d4b',x,y,2+z%3,1);}
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){R('#5b5e4a',66+i%2*2,110+i*5,29-i%2*2,5);R('#b2a88a',67+i%2*2,110+i*5,26-i%2*2,3);}
    const plant=(x,y,i)=>{R('#39543e',x,y-5,5,8);R('#71935a',x-2,y-3,5,3);R('#9ca96b',x+3,y-5,3,3);R(['#d8aa75','#c294a9','#e1d4a0'][i%3],x+1,y-7,3,3);};
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){plant(19+i*5,116+(i%3)*4,i);plant(106+i*4,115+(i%3)*4,i+style);}
    const kind=style===2||style===5?'stone':style===1||style===4?'plaster':'wood';
    const id=style*2+variant;
    // The blocks are placed within the same reserved lot. Front threshold stays x=80.
    if(id===0){chimney(103,16,30);masonry(29,54,100,52,kind);pitched(29,54,100,34);masonry(23,75,30,32,kind);pitched(23,75,30,15,'lean');window(43,65);window(104,65);door();porch(62,77,36);}
    if(id===1){masonry(36,38,84,68,kind);pitched(36,38,84,25);masonry(24,70,42,36,kind);pitched(24,70,42,17);window(52,46);window(88,46);window(36,80,13,15);window(102,69);door();R(trim,44,67,69,2);}
    if(id===2){masonry(29,65,102,41,kind);pitched(29,65,102,21,'hip');masonry(54,39,52,34,kind);pitched(54,39,52,18,'mansard');window(70,46,20,17,true);window(40,76,13,17);window(108,76,13,17);door();}
    if(id===3){masonry(30,59,100,47,kind);pitched(30,59,100,25,'mansard');masonry(91,30,37,76,'stone');pitched(91,30,37,18);window(102,40,14,17,true);window(43,69);window(104,68);door();R(hil,95,15,29,2);R(ink,108,7,2,10);R(trim,100,7,17,2);}
    if(id===4){masonry(25,62,108,44,kind);pitched(25,62,108,20,'hip');chimney(31,22,45);window(44,72,17,20,true);window(103,72,17,20,true);door();porch(58,76,45);}
    if(id===5){masonry(33,40,94,66,kind);pitched(33,40,94,21,'mansard');window(44,48,15,17,true);window(72,48,15,17,true);window(100,48,15,17,true);R(shade,31,71,99,4);R(trim,31,70,99,2);for(let i=35;i<126;i+=10)R(trim,i,67,2,7);window(44,79,13,18);window(104,79,13,18);door();}
    if(id===6){masonry(27,58,110,40,kind);pitched(27,58,110,21,'hip');R(ink,25,97,113,7);R(wall,27,97,109,2);for(let x=34;x<133;x+=22){R(ink,x,101,5,10);R(trim,x+1,101,1,9);}window(44,67);window(108,67);door(69,71,22,28);R(shade,64,102,32,4);R(trim,67,106,26,2);}
    if(id===7){masonry(40,48,85,58,kind);pitched(40,48,85,31);masonry(23,76,38,29,kind);pitched(23,76,38,15,'lean');window(48,58,15,19);window(102,60,15,19);window(30,83,16,13);door();porch(63,77,34);}
    if(id===8){masonry(23,67,114,39,kind);pitched(23,67,114,26,'hip');chimney(113,25,27);window(38,77,20,16);window(104,77,20,16);door();porch(53,78,55);}
    if(id===9){masonry(29,47,102,59,kind);pitched(29,47,102,24);masonry(23,81,38,24,'wood');pitched(23,81,38,14);window(44,57);window(101,57);window(32,88,16,12);window(107,82,13,16);door();R(trim,34,74,91,2);}
    if(id===10){masonry(31,42,98,64,kind);pitched(31,42,98,27,'mansard');window(43,49,21,22,true);window(95,49,21,22,true);R(shade,31,76,98,4);window(42,82,17,16);window(104,82,17,16);door();}
    if(id===11){masonry(30,60,100,46,kind);pitched(30,60,100,26);masonry(92,74,43,32,'wood');pitched(92,74,43,17,'lean');window(39,71,18,19);window(103,82,23,15);door();porch(30,88,34);}
    if(stage===3){for(const [x,y] of [[37,102],[110,102]]){R(ink,x-1,y,18,6);R(shade,x,y,16,5);R(trim,x,y,16,1);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)plant(x+i*4,y-1,i);}R(trim,95,80,4,7);R(night?'#ffe1a3':'#8c794e',96,81,2,4);}
    if(building){for(const x of [22,136]){R('#60492e',x,62,3,54);R('#b59664',x+1,62,1,53);}for(let y=78;y<112;y+=14)R('#9e8055',23,y,114,3);for(let i=0;i<4;i++)R(shade,12+i*5,126-i%2*2,12,3);}
  }

  function homeExteriorFrame(run){const s=run.scene,rect=s.cameras.main.worldView,map=run.homeViews??=new Map(),night=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay>=1140||run.world.clock.minuteOfDay<375;for(const h of C.HOMES){let v=map.get(h.id),x=h.x*T,y=(h.y+1.05)*T;if(s.inside||run.film||x<rect.x-150||x>rect.right+150||y<rect.y-150||y>rect.bottom+150){if(v){v.sprite.setVisible(false);v.light.setVisible(false);}continue;}const r=D.homeState(run.world,h.id),m=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay,lit=night&&D.homeAt(run.world,h.id,m)&&D.roomState(run.world,h.id).lamp&&!D.domesticState(run.world,h.id).curtains&&((m>=1140&&m<1260)||(m>=330&&m<360)),key='v96-home-'+h.style+'-'+h.variant+'-'+r.stage+'-'+!!r.readyAt+'-'+lit;if(!s.textures.exists(key)){const t=s.textures.createCanvas(key,160,140);homePaint96(t.getContext(),h.style,r.stage,!!r.readyAt,lit,h.variant);t.refresh();}if(!v){const sprite=s.add.image(x,y+20,key).setOrigin(.5,1),light=s.add.graphics();s.areaObjs.push(sprite,light);v={sprite,light};map.set(h.id,v);}v.sprite.setTexture(key).setVisible(true).setDepth(run.view.depthAt(y));const g=v.light;g.setVisible(true).clear().setDepth(run.view.depthAt(y)+.002);if(lit&&r.stage===3){for(const off of [-34,34])for(let i=3;i>=0;i--)g.fillStyle(0xffc878,.025).fillEllipse(x+off,y-39,34+i*10,22+i*8);}}}
  function domesticFrame(run){const s=run.scene,r=s.inside,h=r?.heritage;if(!h||!D.homeById[h.id])return;const k=r.k,d=D.domesticState(run.world,h.id);if(!h.domesticFx){h.domesticFx=s.add.graphics().setDepth(10.24);r.objs.push(h.domesticFx);}const g=h.domesticFx;g.clear();const R=(col,x,y,w,z)=>g.fillStyle(col).fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(1,Math.round(w)),Math.max(1,Math.round(z)));const ease=(o,flag)=>{const a=h.domesticMotion??={};return a[o]=(a[o]??0)+((flag?1:0)-(a[o]??0))*(run.reduced?1:.20);};for(const o of h.layout.objects){if(!['pantry','books','hearth'].includes(o.id))continue;const x=20000+o.x*T*k,y=o.y*T*k,W=o.w*T*k,H=o.h*T*k;if(o.id==='pantry'){const t=ease('pantry',d.pantry);if(t<.025)continue;const bx=x+W*.18,by=y+H*.18,bw=W*.64,bh=H*.66;R(0x392f27,bx,by,bw,bh);for(let i=1;i<=2;i++)R(0x9b7950,bx,by+bh*i/3,bw,2*k);const stock=D.roomState(run.world,h.id).stock||{};for(let i=0;i<Math.min(5,stock.food||0);i++){R(0x997443,bx+4*k+i*7*k,by+4*k,5*k,8*k);R(0xe5bb70,bx+5*k+i*7*k,by+4*k,3*k,3*k);}for(let i=0;i<Math.min(4,stock.water||0);i++){R(0x74a4b4,bx+4*k+i*8*k,by+bh/3+4*k,6*k,9*k);R(0xa6c9c9,bx+5*k+i*8*k,by+bh/3+5*k,2*k,5*k);}let wing=bw*.36*t;R(0x715339,bx-wing,by,wing,bh);R(0xa78760,bx+bw,by,wing,bh);}else if(o.id==='books'){const t=ease('books',d.books);if(t<.025)continue;R(0x4b382a,x+W*.25,y+H*.30,W*.5,H*.46);R(0xe5d2a9,x+W*.27,y+H*.32,W*.21*t,H*.38);R(0xf3e3bf,x+W*.52,y+H*.32,W*.21*t,H*.38);for(let i=0;i<3;i++)R(0x958064,x+W*.30,y+H*.4+i*3*k,W*.13*t,k);}else if(D.homeAt(run.world,h.id,run.world.clock.minuteOfDay)&&D.roomState(run.world,h.id).lamp){const n=run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.clock/70)%3;R(0x533625,x+W*.34,y+H*.40,W*.32,H*.28);R(0xcf7540,x+W*.42,y+H*.49-n*k,8*k,8*k+n*k);R(0xf7cf7c,x+W*.46,y+H*.53,4*k,5*k);}}
    const t=ease('curtains',d.curtains);if(t>.02){const W=h.layout.w*T*k;for(const xx of [20000+22*k,20000+W-135*k]){const width=51*k*t;for(const x of [xx+4*k,xx+110*k-width]){R(0x514759,x,14*k,width,36*k);for(let j=0;j<width;j+=6*k)R(0x88728a,x+j,14*k,2*k,35*k);R(0xa18d9d,x,14*k,width,2*k);}}}
  }

  function homeResidentFrame(run){const s=run.scene,r=s.inside,h=r?.heritage,d=D.homeById[h?.id];if(!d)return;const m=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay+run.clock/500,at=D.homeAt(run.world,d.id,m),v=h.homeResident;if(!at){v?.sprite.setVisible(false);v?.label.setVisible(false);return;}const p=D.eastById[d.site],k=r.k,eng=run.engine,key='east-keeper-'+p.id,q=D.homeSchedule(run.world,d.id,m),station=q.to,paths=h.homePaths??=new Map(),route=q.from+'>'+q.to;if(!paths.has(route))paths.set(route,D.livingRoomPath(d.id,q.from,q.to));const pos=D.livingPoint(paths.get(route),q.t);if(!pos)return;const x=20000+pos.x*T*k,y=pos.y*T*k;if(!s.textures.exists('ch_'+key+'_down_0'))eng.sprite(s,key,{...NPCS.lyra.look,outfit:p.outfit,pants:0x48544d,hair:0x5d473c},(g,dir,f)=>Costumes.paint(g,dir,f,{outfit:'traveler',wet:false}));let a=v;if(!a){const sprite=s.add.image(x,y,'ch_'+key+'_down_0').setOrigin(.5,eng.origin).setScale(k),label=s.add.text(x,y-38*k,p.keeper,{fontSize:'11px',color:'#f6deb4',backgroundColor:'#293b33d7',padding:{x:4,y:2}}).setOrigin(.5,1).setDepth(31);r.objs.push(sprite,label);a=h.homeResident={sprite,label,face:eng.face()};}const dx=x-a.sprite.x,dy=y-a.sprite.y;if(q.pose==='walk'){eng.turn(a.face,dx,dy);a.face.phase+=Math.min(16,Math.hypot(dx,dy)/k);}else eng.turn(a.face,0,-1);a.sprite.setPosition(x,y).setVisible(true).setDepth(s.player.y>y?9.86:10.12);eng.animate(a.sprite,key,a.face,q.pose==='walk');if(q.pose!=='walk')livingPose(run,a.sprite,q.pose,0);a.label.setPosition(x,y-38*k).setVisible(Math.hypot(s.player.x-x,s.player.y-y)<135*k).setText(p.keeper+' · '+(station==='bed'?'Resting':station==='books'?'Reading':'At home'));}

  function districtRoomPanel(run){const d=districtInside(run);if(!d?.o)return;const {h,p,o}=d,s=D.eastState(run.world,p.id),m=D.districtMemory(run.world,p.id),pr=D.districtProfile(p.id);if(o.action==='lamp'){edit(run,w=>D.roomAction(w,p.id,'lamp'));sound('gather');return toast(run,D.roomState(run.world,p.id).lamp?'Lantern lit':'Lantern extinguished');}if(o.action==='inspect')edit(run,w=>D.roomAction(w,p.id,o.id));if(['stock','aux','ledger'].includes(o.id)&&!D.livingOpen(run.world,p.id,o.id))return panel(run,'district-room',title(p.name.toUpperCase(),o.name,p.note)+livingPanel(run,p,o)+button('Close','close'));let body='';if(o.id==='stock'){const used=Object.values(s.stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);body=`<p class="fv-copy">${used}/48 · Same stock as outdoor workshop</p>`+D.eastKeys(p.id).map(k=>`<article class="v852-row"><b>${esc(C.INVENTORY[k][0])}</b><small>Here ${s.stock[k]||0} · Backpack ${run.world.resources[k]||0}</small>${[1,5].map(n=>button('Deposit '+n,'district-stock:in:'+k+':'+n,(run.world.resources[k]||0)<n||used+n>48)+button('Withdraw '+n,'district-stock:out:'+k+':'+n,(s.stock[k]||0)<n)).join('')}</article>`).join('');const c=D.districtCargo(run.world);body+='<h3>Eastern trail deliveries</h3>';if(c.job){const j=c.job;body+=`<p>${esc(D.eastById[j.from].name)} → ${esc(D.eastById[j.to].name)} · ${j.cursor+1}/${j.path.length} steps · ${esc(C.INVENTORY[j.key][0])} × ${j.n}</p>`+button('Cancel before departure','district-cancel:'+j.id,j.cursor!==0)+button('Refresh','district-refresh');}else{body+='<p class="fv-copy">Walk a continuous level-8 trail between workshops. The runner follows the explored route, rests after dark, and waits if storage is full.</p>';for(const dest of C.EAST_SITES.filter(q=>q.id!==p.id&&D.eastState(run.world,q.id).stage>=8)){for(const key of D.eastKeys(dest.id)){if(!(s.stock[key]>0))continue;const n=Math.min(5,s.stock[key],48-Object.values(D.eastState(run.world,dest.id).stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0));if(n>0)body+=button('Send '+n+' '+C.INVENTORY[key][0]+' → '+dest.name,'district-send:'+dest.id+':'+key+':'+n);}}}}
    if(o.id==='stock'){const cs=D.civicContractState(run.world);body+='<h3>Standing supply agreements</h3><p class="fv-copy">Each agreement authorises four deliveries (eight after logistics research) when the destination needs inputs and a learned trail exists.</p>';for(const [id,c] of Object.entries(cs.contracts)){if(c.from===p.id)body+=`<article class="v852-row"><b>${esc(C.INVENTORY[c.key][0])} → ${esc(D.eastById[c.to].name)}</b><small>${c.left} deliveries remaining</small>${button('End agreement','district-contract-remove:'+id)}</article>`;}for(const dest of C.EAST_SITES.filter(q=>q.id!==p.id&&D.eastState(run.world,q.id).stage>=8)){for(const key of Object.keys(p.output)){if(!dest.input[key]||Object.values(cs.contracts).some(c=>c.from===p.id&&c.to===dest.id&&c.key===key))continue;body+=button('Supply '+C.INVENTORY[key][0]+' → '+dest.name,'district-contract:'+dest.id+':'+key);}}}
    else if(o.action==='work'){body=`<p>${esc(costs(p.input)+' → '+costs(p.output))}</p><p class="fv-copy">${D.eastDuration(p,s)} work minutes · ${s.runs} completed orders</p>`+(s.job?`<meter min="0" max="${D.eastDuration(p,s)}" value="${s.job.progress}"></meter><p>${s.job.progress}/${D.eastDuration(p,s)} · ${esc(s.job.phase)}</p>`+button('Cancel unstarted order','district-order-cancel',s.job.progress!==0):button('Commission one order','district-order',!!D.branchJob(run.world,p.id)||!Object.entries(p.input).every(([k,n])=>(s.stock[k]||0)>=n)))+button('Refresh','district-refresh')+`<h3>Standing production</h3><p class="fv-copy">${D.civicPolicy(run.world,p.id)} additional orders authorised. Work pauses when materials or storage are unavailable.</p>`+[0,2,4,8].map(n=>button(n?'Authorise '+n+' additional orders':'Stop automatic orders','district-policy:'+n)).join('');}
    else if(o.id==='rest'){body=`<p>${esc(pr.name)}’s rest corner · ${esc(pr.hobby)}</p><p class="fv-copy">Leave provisions for the keeper or use the visitor bench to advance the in-game clock.</p>`+button('Leave 1 food','district-provision',!(run.world.resources.food>0)||m.mealDay===Math.floor(S.total(run.world)/1440))+button('Rest 30 game minutes','district-rest');}
    else if(o.id==='ledger'){body=`<h3>${esc(pr.name)} · ${esc(pr.trait)}</h3><p>${esc(pr.line)}</p><p class="fv-copy">${esc(pr.hobby)} · Neighbours: ${esc(pr.neighbors.join(', '))}</p><p>Trust ${m.trust}/100 · Conversations ${m.talks} · Orders acknowledged ${m.help}/${s.runs}</p><h3>${esc(p.question)}</h3>`+p.answers.map((a,i)=>button(a,'district-talk:'+i,m.lastDay===Math.floor(S.total(run.world)/1440))).join('')+button('Discuss completed work','district-help',m.help>=s.runs);}
    else {body=`<p>${esc(p.note)}</p><p class="fv-copy">${esc(o.name)} · recorded in your field journal.</p>`;if(o.id==='aux'){body+=civicAuxPanel(run,p,s)+`<h3>Preventive maintenance</h3><p class="fv-copy">A repair kit reduces the next order's working time by 15%. ${s.maintenance?'Machine prepared.':''}</p>`+button('Install one repair kit','district-maintain',!!s.job||!!s.maintenance||!(run.world.resources['repair-kit']>0));}}return panel(run,'district-room',title(p.name.toUpperCase(),o.name,p.note)+body+(o.action==='work'?'<p>Drive: '+esc(D.utilityStatus(run.world,p.id).label)+'</p>'+masteryPanelContent(run,p)+branchPanel(run,p):'')+charterStation(run,p,o)+(o.id==='rest'?homeAddress(run,p):'')+livingPanel(run,p,o)+infraPanel(run,p,o)+button('Close','close'));}
  function districtAction(run,action){const d=districtInside(run);if(run.panel!=='district-room'||!d?.o)throw new Error('Stand at the workshop station.');const {p,o}=d,s=D.eastState(run.world,p.id);if(action==='district-refresh')return districtRoomPanel(run);if(o.action==='work'&&action.startsWith('district-branch-start:')){edit(run,w=>D.branchStart(w,p.id,Number(action.split(':')[1])));return districtRoomPanel(run);}if(o.action==='work'&&action==='district-branch-collect'){edit(run,w=>D.branchCollect(w,p.id));return districtRoomPanel(run);}if(['stock','aux','ledger'].includes(o.id)&&!D.livingOpen(run.world,p.id,o.id))throw Error('Open this object first.');if(action.startsWith('district-stock:')&&o.id==='stock'){const [,dir,k,n]=action.split(':');edit(run,w=>D.eastTransfer(w,p.id,k,Number(n),dir==='in'));}else if(action.startsWith('district-send:')&&o.id==='stock'){const [,dest,k,n]=action.split(':');edit(run,w=>D.districtSend(w,run.scene.area,p.id,dest,k,Number(n)));}else if(action.startsWith('district-cancel:')&&o.id==='stock')edit(run,w=>D.districtCancel(w,Number(action.split(':')[1])));else if(action==='district-maintain'&&o.id==='aux')edit(run,w=>D.civicMaintenance(w,p.id));else if(action.startsWith('district-civic:')&&o.id==='aux'){const [,a,n]=action.split(':');edit(run,w=>D.civicService(w,p.id,a,n===undefined?undefined:Number(n)));}else if(action.startsWith('district-policy:')&&o.action==='work')edit(run,w=>D.civicSetPolicy(w,p.id,Number(action.split(':')[1])));else if(action.startsWith('district-contract:')&&o.id==='stock'){const [,dest,key]=action.split(':');edit(run,w=>D.civicSetContract(w,p.id,dest,key,Math.min(5,D.eastById[dest].input[key])));}else if(action.startsWith('district-contract-remove:')&&o.id==='stock')edit(run,w=>D.civicRemoveContract(w,Number(action.split(':')[1]),p.id));else if(action==='district-order'&&o.action==='work')edit(run,w=>D.eastOrder(w,p.id,s.runs));else if(action==='district-order-cancel'&&o.action==='work')edit(run,w=>D.eastCancel(w,p.id,s.runs));else if(action.startsWith('district-talk:')&&o.id==='ledger')edit(run,w=>D.districtTalk(w,p.id,Number(action.split(':')[1])));else if(action==='district-help'&&o.id==='ledger')edit(run,w=>D.districtHelp(w,p.id));else if(action==='district-provision'&&o.id==='rest')edit(run,w=>D.districtProvision(w,p.id));else if(action==='district-rest'&&o.id==='rest')edit(run,w=>S.step(w,30));else throw new Error('This action belongs to a different station.');sound('gather');return districtRoomPanel(run);}
  function districtKeeperFrame(run){const scene=run.scene,r=scene.inside,h=r?.heritage,p=D.eastById[h?.id];if(!p)return;const minute=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay+run.clock/500,shift=D.livingShift(run.world,p.id,minute);if(!shift)return;if(D.homeBySite[p.id]&&D.homeState(run.world,D.homeBySite[p.id].id).stage===3&&(minute<420||minute>=1080)){const v=h.districtKeeper;if(v){v.sprite.setVisible(false);v.label.setVisible(false);v.props.clear();}return;}const job=D.eastState(run.world,p.id).job,k=r.k,eng=run.engine,key='east-keeper-'+p.id;let v=h.districtKeeper;const paths=h.livingPaths??=new Map(),route=shift.from+'>'+shift.station;if(!paths.has(route))paths.set(route,D.livingRoomPath(p.id,shift.from,shift.station));const pos=D.livingPoint(paths.get(route),shift.t);if(!pos)return;const x=20000+pos.x*T*k,y=pos.y*T*k;
    if(!scene.textures.exists('ch_'+key+'_down_0'))eng.sprite(scene,key,{...NPCS.lyra.look,outfit:p.outfit,pants:0x48544d,hair:0x5d473c},(g,dir,f)=>Costumes.paint(g,dir,f,{outfit:'traveler',wet:false}));if(!v){const sprite=scene.add.image(x,y,'ch_'+key+'_down_0').setOrigin(.5,eng.origin).setScale(k),label=scene.add.text(x,y-38*k,p.keeper,{fontSize:'11px',color:'#f6deb4',backgroundColor:'#293b33d7',padding:{x:4,y:2}}).setOrigin(.5,1).setDepth(31),props=scene.add.graphics();r.objs.push(sprite,label,props);v=h.districtKeeper={sprite,label,props,face:eng.face()};}
    const dx=x-v.sprite.x,dy=y-v.sprite.y;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)>.01){eng.turn(v.face,dx,dy);v.face.phase+=Math.min(16,Math.hypot(dx,dy)/k);}else eng.turn(v.face,0,shift.station==='work'?-1:1);v.sprite.setVisible(true).setPosition(x,y).setDepth(scene.player.y>y?9.86:10.12);eng.animate(v.sprite,key,v.face,shift.move);const drive=D.utilityStatus(run.world,p.id),paused=shift.pose==='work'&&!drive.ok;if(!shift.move)livingPose(run,v.sprite,paused?'idle':shift.pose,run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/220)%2);v.label.setVisible(Math.hypot(scene.player.x-x,scene.player.y-y)<135*k).setPosition(x,y-38*k).setText(p.keeper+' · '+(paused?drive.label:shift.activity));v.props.clear().setDepth(v.sprite.depth+.01);if(shift.pose==='work'&&job&&drive.ok&&k===1)masteryTool(v.props,D.masteryBySite[p.id]?.tool||'archive',x,y,run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/110)%12);}

  function clearDistrictRunner(run){const v=run.districtRunner;if(!v)return;for(const o of [v.sprite,v.label,v.props]){const i=run.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(o);if(i>=0)run.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(o.scene)o.destroy();}delete run.districtRunner;}
  function districtRunnerFrame(run){const c=D.districtCargo(run.world),j=c.job,scene=run.scene;if(scene.inside||!j||run.film)return clearDistrictRunner(run);const a=j.path[j.cursor],b=j.path[Math.min(j.cursor+1,j.path.length-1)],m=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay,move=m>=360&&m<1200&&j.cursor<j.path.length-1,f=move?Math.min(1,Math.max(0,run.clock/500)):0,x=(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*f)*16,y=(a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*f)*16,rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView;if(x<rect.x-80||x>rect.right+80||y<rect.y-80||y>rect.bottom+80)return clearDistrictRunner(run);const eng=run.engine,key='district-lark';if(!scene.textures.exists('ch_'+key+'_down_0'))eng.sprite(scene,key,{...NPCS.lyra.look,outfit:0x537c84,pants:0x514c43,hair:0x94684a},(g,dir,f)=>Costumes.paint(g,dir,f,{outfit:'traveler',wet:false}));let v=run.districtRunner;if(!v){const sprite=scene.add.image(x,y,'ch_'+key+'_down_0').setOrigin(.5,eng.origin),label=scene.add.text(x,y-38,'Lark · Eastern courier',{fontSize:'11px',color:'#f7dfb3',backgroundColor:'#283b36',padding:{x:4,y:2}}).setOrigin(.5,1).setDepth(23),props=scene.add.graphics();scene.areaObjs.push(sprite,label,props);v=run.districtRunner={sprite,label,props,face:eng.face()};}eng.turn(v.face,b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);v.face.phase+=Math.min(16,Math.hypot(x-v.sprite.x,y-v.sprite.y));eng.animate(v.sprite,key,v.face,move);const layer=j.levels?.[j.cursor]||'ground',span=C.crossingById[layer],z=span?C.crossingZ(span,x/T):0,py=y-z;v.sprite.setPosition(x,y).setOrigin(.5,eng.origin+z/Math.max(1,v.sprite.displayHeight)).setDepth(span?18.025:run.view.depthAt(y));v.label.setPosition(x,py-38).setText(span?'Lark · upper deck':'Lark · Eastern courier');v.props.clear().setDepth(v.sprite.depth+.001);if(j.cursor>=j.pickup&&D.livingCart(run.world)&&!span){livingCartArt(v.props,x,py,b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1],run.time,move,j.n);}else if(j.cursor>=j.pickup){v.props.fillStyle(0x493727).fillRect(x-7,py-21,14,10).fillStyle(0xad8659).fillRect(x-6,py-20,12,8).fillStyle(0xe5c794).fillRect(x-6,py-20,12,2).fillStyle(0x72543b).fillRect(x-2,py-20,2,8);}}

  function eastPanel(run,id,record=false){const p=D.eastById[id];if(!p||!eastNear(run,id))throw new Error('Walk to the eastern workshop.');if(record||!run.world.development.east?.[id])edit(run,w=>D.eastVisit(w,id));run.eastSite=id;const s=D.eastState(run.world,id),states=D.eastStages.map((n,i)=>`<span style="display:inline-block;padding:4px 6px;margin:2px;background:${i===s.stage?'#88784d':i<s.stage?'#365d4b':'#263e38'};border-radius:4px">${i+1} · ${esc(n)}</span>`).join('');const construction=D.constructionState(run.world,id);let task='';if(!s.solved)task=`<p class="fv-copy">${esc(p.note)}</p><h3>${esc(p.question)}</h3>${p.answers.map((a,i)=>({a,i,rank:C.hash(i,C.EAST_SITES.indexOf(p),1021)})).sort((a,b)=>a.rank-b.rank).map(q=>button(q.a,'east-solve:'+q.i)).join('')}`;else if(construction){const left=construction.ends-D.now(run.world);task=`<h3>Construction in progress: ${esc(D.eastStages[construction.target])}</h3><meter min="0" max="${construction.ends-construction.started}" value="${D.now(run.world)-construction.started}"></meter><p>${left} game minutes remaining · reserved ${esc(costs(construction.cost))}</p>`+button('Refresh construction','east-refresh')+button('Cancel before work starts','east-construction-cancel',D.now(run.world)!==construction.started||!!construction.crew||!!construction.team);if(construction.team){const team=D.teamAt(run.world,id);task+='<h3>Two-person construction crew</h3>'+team.members.map(m=>'<p>'+esc(D.apprenticesById[m.id].name)+' · '+esc(m.role)+' · '+esc(costs(m.cargo))+' · '+(D.now(run.world)>=m.arrival?'Delivered':'In transit')+'</p>').join('')+'<p>On-site material reserve: '+esc(costs(D.teamStash(run.world,id)))+'</p>';}else if(construction.crew)task+='<p>Assigned: '+esc(D.apprenticesById[construction.crew].name)+' · assisted phase</p>';else if(construction.target>=3&&construction.target<=7&&D.now(run.world)===construction.started)task+='<h3>Construction workforce</h3><p>Explore a route from a local operating workshop. Reserve one apprentice and 1 food, or a two-person artisan/logistics team and 2 food, before time advances.</p>'+button('Assign artisan and logistics team','east-team',!(run.world.resources.food>=2)||D.crewCandidates(run.world,id).length<2||!!D.teamAt(run.world,id))+D.crewCandidates(run.world,id).map(a=>button('Assign '+a.name+' · '+a.role,'east-crew:'+a.id,!(run.world.resources.food>0))).join('');}else if(s.stage<9)task=`<h3>${esc(D.eastStages[s.stage+1])}</h3><p>${esc(costs(D.eastCost(id,s.stage+1)))}</p>${p.monument&&s.stage>=2&&!D.monumentReady(run.world,id)?'<p class="fv-copy">Complete the corresponding three partner works phases in the charter before laying the foundation.</p>':''}${s.stage<8?'<p>'+D.constructionMinutes(id,s.stage+1)+' game minutes · materials reserved immediately</p>':''}${button('Begin restoration','east-restore',!D.canUse(run.world)||!!s.job||(s.stage>=2&&!D.monumentReady(run.world,id))||!Object.entries(D.eastCost(id,s.stage+1)).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n))}`;
    if(s.stage===9){task+='<h3>Specialization · '+esc(D.specializationDefs[s.specialization]?.name||'Unassigned')+'</h3>'+Object.entries(D.specializationDefs).map(([k,b])=>'<p>'+esc(b.name)+' · '+esc(b.detail)+' · '+esc(costs(b.cost))+'</p>'+button('Choose '+b.name,'east-special:'+k,!!s.job||s.specialization===k||k==='conservation'&&!D.charterState(run.world)?.houses[id]||!Object.entries(b.cost).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n))).join('');}if(s.stage>=8){const keys=D.eastKeys(id);task+=`<h3>Local workshop · ${esc(p.keeper)}</h3><p>${esc(costs(p.input))} → ${esc(costs(p.output))} · ${D.eastDuration(p,s)} work minutes</p><p class="fv-copy">Stock ${Object.values(s.stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)}/48 · Shift 07:00–18:00 · ${s.runs} orders completed</p>`+keys.map(k=>`<article class="v852-row"><b>${esc(C.INVENTORY[k][0])}</b><small>In workshop: ${s.stock[k]||0} · Backpack: ${run.world.resources[k]||0}</small>${button('Deposit 1','east-deposit:'+k,!(run.world.resources[k]>0))}${button('Withdraw 1','east-withdraw:'+k,!(s.stock[k]>0))}</article>`).join('')+(s.job?`<h3>${esc(s.job.phase==='waiting'?'Awaiting storage space':'Order in progress')}</h3><meter min="0" max="${D.eastDuration(p,s)}" value="${s.job.progress}"></meter><p>${s.job.progress}/${D.eastDuration(p,s)} minutes · reserved input</p>${button('Cancel unstarted order','east-cancel',!!s.job.progress)}`:button('Commission one order','east-order',!Object.entries(p.input).every(([k,n])=>(s.stock[k]||0)>=n)))+button('Refresh status','east-refresh')+button('Enter workshop hall','east-enter')+requestPanelContent(run,p);}
    if(id==='mon-library'&&s.stage>=8){const a=D.annexState(run.world);task+='<h3>Upper reading wing</h3><p>'+(!a?'Extend the upper hall with four walkable rows and two English study desks. '+esc(costs(D.annexCost))+' · '+D.annexMinutes+' game minutes':a.status==='building'?Math.max(0,a.ends-D.now(run.world))+' game minutes remaining':'Reading wing open · '+a.read.length+'/2 readings completed')+'</p>'+(!a?button('Build reading wing','east-annex',!!s.job||!Object.entries(D.annexCost).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n)):button('Refresh wing status','east-refresh'));}
    task+=utilitySitePanel(run,id);if(D.wingDefs[id]&&s.stage>=8){const d=D.wingDefs[id],r=D.wingState(run.world,id);task+='<h3>'+esc(d.name)+'</h3><p>'+esc(!r?costs(d.cost)+' · '+d.minutes+' game minutes':r.status==='building'?Math.max(0,r.ends-D.now(run.world))+' minutes remaining':'Open · '+r.read.length+'/2 investigations')+'</p>'+(!r?button('Build '+d.name,'east-wing',!!s.job||!Object.entries(d.cost).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n)):button('Refresh wing','east-refresh'));}
    panel(run,'east-site',title(C.REGION[p.region].name.toUpperCase(),p.name,p.note)+`<div class="nature-intro"><strong>${s.stage+1}/10</strong><span>${esc(p.keeper)}${p.role?' · '+esc(p.role):''} · ${s.visits} visits</span></div><div>${states}</div>${task}`+button('View building blueprint','east-blueprint')+button('Eastern atlas','east-guide')+button('Close','close'));}
  function eastGuide(run){const rows=C.EAST_REGIONS.map(r=>`<h3>${esc(r.name)}</h3><p class="fv-copy">${esc(r.desc)} · explore near (${r.x}, ${r.y})</p>`+C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>p.region===r.id).map(p=>{const s=D.eastState(run.world,p.id),known=!!run.world.development.east?.[p.id];return `<article class="v852-row"><b>${known?esc(p.name):'Undiscovered workshop'}</b><small>${known?`${s.stage+1}/10 · ${esc(p.keeper)} · ${s.runs} orders`:'Explore on foot'}</small>${eastNear(run,p.id)?button('Inspect','east-open:'+p.id):''}</article>`;}).join('')).join('');panel(run,'east-guide',title('THE EASTERN LANDS','Eastern district register','Six districts, fifty workshops and twelve independent monumental sites. Survey them, restore their halls and establish supply routes.')+button('Open expedition atlas','dev-atlas')+rows+button('Back','menu'));}
  function eastAction(run,action){if(action==='east-guide')return eastGuide(run);if(action.startsWith('east-open:'))return eastPanel(run,action.slice(10),true);const id=run.eastSite,p=D.eastById[id];if(run.panel!=='east-site'||!eastNear(run,id))throw new Error('Return to the workshop.');const s=D.eastState(run.world,id);if(action==='east-team'){edit(run,w=>D.teamAssign(w,run.scene.area,id));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action.startsWith('east-crew:')){edit(run,w=>D.crewAssign(w,run.scene.area,id,action.slice(10)));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action.startsWith('east-special:')){edit(run,w=>D.specializationSet(w,id,action.slice(13)));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action==='east-power-switch'&&id===D.utilityHub){edit(run,w=>D.utilitySwitch(w));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action.startsWith('east-power-survey:')&&id===D.utilityHub){edit(run,w=>D.utilitySurvey(w,run.scene.area,action.slice('east-power-survey:'.length)));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action==='east-power-build'){edit(run,w=>D.utilityBuild(w,id));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action==='east-power-toggle'){edit(run,w=>D.utilityToggle(w,id));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action==='east-wing'){edit(run,w=>D.wingStart(w,id));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action==='east-annex'){if(id!=='mon-library')throw Error('Return to the library.');edit(run,w=>D.annexStart(w));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action==='east-enter')return enterHeritageRoom(run,id);if(action==='east-blueprint'||action.startsWith('east-blueprint-floor:')){const floors=D.blueprintFloors(run.world,id),idx=action==='east-blueprint'?0:Number(action.split(':')[1]),f=floors[idx];if(!f)throw Error('Unknown floor.');const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=400;c.height=224;const target=Math.min(9,s.stage+1);Art.blueprintArt(c.getContext('2d'),p,f.layout,target,f.name);return panel(run,'east-site',title('BLUEPRINT STUDIO',p.name,f.name)+'<p>'+esc(f.status)+' · '+f.layout.w+' × '+f.layout.h+' tiles · '+f.layout.objects.length+' planned objects</p>'+floors.map((v,i)=>button(v.name+' · '+v.status,'east-blueprint-floor:'+i,i===idx)).join('')+'<img alt="Elevation and selected floor collision plan" style="width:100%;image-rendering:pixelated" src="'+c.toDataURL()+'"><p>Fixed site: '+p.x+', '+p.y+' · '+esc(costs(D.eastCost(id,target)))+'</p><p>'+(s.stage<8?D.constructionMinutes(id,target)+' game minutes':'Operational or specialization')+'</p>'+button('Back to site','east-refresh'));}if(action==='east-construction-cancel'){edit(run,w=>D.constructionCancel(w,id));return eastPanel(run,id);}if(action.startsWith('east-solve:'))edit(run,w=>D.eastSolve(w,id,Number(action.slice(11))));else if(action==='east-restore'){if(!s.solved||s.stage>=9||s.job)throw new Error('Check restoration conditions.');const stage=s.stage;if(stage<8){edit(run,w=>D.constructionStart(w,id,stage));return eastPanel(run,id);}close(run);beginWork(run,{id:'east-'+id,kind:'stone',name:D.eastStages[stage+1],x:p.ax-.5,y:p.ay-.5,seconds:2.2+stage*.12},null,eastPoint(p),false,55);run.pending.east={id,stage};return;}else if(action.startsWith('east-deposit:'))edit(run,w=>D.eastTransfer(w,id,action.slice(13),1,true));else if(action.startsWith('east-withdraw:'))edit(run,w=>D.eastTransfer(w,id,action.slice(14),1,false));else if(action==='east-order')edit(run,w=>D.eastOrder(w,id,s.runs));else if(action==='east-cancel')edit(run,w=>D.eastCancel(w,id,s.runs));else if(action!=='east-refresh')throw new Error('Unknown eastern action.');return eastPanel(run,id);}
  function clearEastActors(run,id){for(const[key,s]of [...(run.eastActors||new Map())]){if(id&&id!==key)continue;for(const o of [s.sprite,s.label,s.work]){const i=run.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(o);if(i>=0)run.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(o.scene)o.destroy();}run.eastActors.delete(key);}}
  function eastActorFrame(run){const scene=run.scene;if(scene.inside||run.film){clearEastActors(run);return;}const rect=scene.cameras.main.worldView,eng=run.engine,map=run.eastActors??=new Map();for(const p of C.EAST_SITES){const m=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay+run.clock/500,nearSite=p.ax*T>rect.x-320&&p.ax*T<rect.right+320&&p.ay*T>rect.y-320&&p.ay*T<rect.bottom+320,errand=nearSite?livingErrand(run,p.id,m):null,home=D.homeDuty(run.world,p.id,m),s=home!==undefined?home:errand||D.eastDuty(run.world,p.id,m);if(!s||s.x*T<rect.x-90||s.x*T>rect.right+90||s.y*T<rect.y-90||s.y*T>rect.bottom+90){clearEastActors(run,p.id);continue;}const x=s.x*T,y=s.y*T,key='east-keeper-'+p.id;if(!scene.textures.exists('ch_'+key+'_down_0'))eng.sprite(scene,key,{...NPCS.lyra.look,outfit:p.outfit,pants:0x48544d,hair:0x5d473c},(g,dir,f)=>Costumes.paint(g,dir,f,{outfit:'traveler',wet:false}));let v=map.get(p.id);if(!v){const sprite=scene.add.image(x,y,'ch_'+key+'_down_0').setOrigin(.5,eng.origin),label=scene.add.text(0,0,p.keeper,{fontFamily:'Georgia',fontSize:'12px',color:'#f3dfb3',backgroundColor:'#293b33d7',padding:{x:5,y:3}}).setOrigin(.5,1).setDepth(23),work=scene.add.graphics();scene.areaObjs.push(sprite,label,work);v={sprite,label,work,face:eng.face(),prev:null};map.set(p.id,v);}if(s.moving){eng.turn(v.face,s.dx,s.dy||0);if(v.prev)v.face.phase+=Math.min(24,Math.hypot(x-v.prev.x,y-v.prev.y));}else eng.turn(v.face,s.dx||0,s.dy||-1);v.prev={x,y};eng.animate(v.sprite,key,v.face,s.moving);const depth=run.view.depthAt(y);v.sprite.setPosition(x,y).setDepth(depth);v.label.setPosition(x,y-40).setText(p.keeper+(s.errand?' · '+s.activity:'')).setVisible(Math.hypot(scene.player.x-x,scene.player.y-y)<110);v.work.clear().setDepth(depth+.001);if(s.working){const f=run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/110)%12;masteryTool(v.work,D.masteryBySite[p.id]?.tool||'archive',x,y,f);}}}


  // V90 apprentices reuse the original pixel character engine and are culled with the camera.
  const peopleColours=[0x9b684a,0x637e69,0x70618d,0xb2985d,0x567887,0x8b5362,0x697357,0x9a825d];
  const peopleHair=[0x46382c,0x755137,0x302e35,0xb2915b,0x8e5537,0x59514a,0x3e322a];
  function apprenticeLook(a){const base={...NPCS.lyra.look,outfit:peopleColours[a.look%8],pants:peopleColours[(a.look+4)%8],hair:peopleHair[a.look%7],skin:[0xf1c5a2,0xd6a880,0xb87f5c,0x9d6c4d,0xe4b28e,0xc58d6b][a.look%6],style:['short','long','bun','short','long'][a.look%5],eye:[0x342d29,0x55443a,0x334f4c][a.look%3]};if(['saw','pot','gear','stone'].includes(a.prop))base.apron=0x655347;else if(a.prop==='basket'||a.prop==='cup')base.apron=0xd1bc92;if(a.prop==='leaf'&&a.look%2===0)base.hat={kind:'cap',color:0x887546};if(a.prop==='book'&&a.look%3===0)base.glasses=true;return base;}
  function apprenticeClear(run,id){for(const[key,v]of [...(run.apprenticeActors||new Map())]){if(id&&id!==key)continue;for(const o of [v.sprite,v.label,v.prop]){const i=run.scene.areaObjs.indexOf(o);if(i>=0)run.scene.areaObjs.splice(i,1);if(o.scene)o.destroy();}run.apprenticeActors.delete(key);}}
  function apprenticeRoute(run,a){const scene=run.scene,p=D.eastById[a.site],map=run.apprenticeRoutes??=new Map();if(map.has(a.id))return map.get(a.id);const P=root.LQPrecisePhysics,test=q=>!scene.blocked(q.x,q.y),x=p.ax*T,y=p.ay*T;let result=null;for(const dy of [26,42,58,74]){for(const sign of [a.look%2?1:-1,a.look%2?-1:1]){const A={x:x+sign*36,y:y+dy},B={x:x+sign*82,y:y+dy+12};if(test(A)&&test(B)&&P.segmentClear(scene.area,A,B)&&Array.from({length:13},(_,i)=>test({x:A.x+(B.x-A.x)*i/12,y:A.y+(B.y-A.y)*i/12})).every(Boolean)){result=[A,B];break;}}if(result)break;}map.set(a.id,result);return result;}
  function apprenticeProp(g,a,x,y,f){const Y=y-17,B=0x493c32,L=0xc6ae78;g.fillStyle(B);switch(a.prop){case 'saw':g.fillRect(x-9,Y+2,18,5);g.fillStyle(L).fillRect(x-8,Y+3,16,2);g.fillStyle(0xd9dfda).fillRect(x-7+f,Y-2,9,3);break;case 'leaf':g.fillRect(x-5,Y+2,11,7);g.fillStyle(0x6d9b59).fillRect(x-3,Y-5,3,8).fillRect(x+1,Y-7+f%3,4,9);break;case 'basket':g.fillRect(x-7,Y,14,8);g.fillStyle(0xc39d5d).fillRect(x-6,Y+1,12,6);g.fillStyle(0xe4c17e).fillRect(x-4+f%3,Y+1,3,5);break;case 'gear':g.fillRect(x-7,Y,14,7);g.fillStyle(0xbcc8c7).fillRect(x-6+f,Y-4,4,10).fillRect(x-8+f,Y-3,8,3);break;case 'water':g.fillRect(x-6,Y,12,8);g.fillStyle(0x81bfca).fillRect(x-5,Y+1,10,4);g.fillStyle(0xc6e8e5).fillRect(x-5+f,Y+1,3,1);break;case 'book':g.fillRect(x-8,Y,16,8);g.fillStyle(0xecd8a4).fillRect(x-7,Y+1,6,6).fillRect(x+1,Y+1,6,6);g.fillStyle(0x9a8052).fillRect(x-6,Y+2+f%3,4,1).fillRect(x+2,Y+4,4,1);break;case 'cloth':g.fillRect(x-7,Y,14,8);g.fillStyle(0xb88072).fillRect(x-6,Y+1,12,5);g.fillStyle(0xf1d3b3).fillRect(x-5+f,Y+2,1,5);break;case 'stone':g.fillRect(x-7,Y+2,14,6);g.fillStyle(0x9e9e93).fillRect(x-5,Y,10,6);g.fillStyle(0xcfcec2).fillRect(x-4+f%5,Y+1,3,1);break;default:g.fillRect(x-5,Y,10,8);g.fillStyle(0xd4ad78).fillRect(x-4,Y+1,8,6);g.fillStyle(0xf1e3bb).fillRect(x-2,Y-3+f%3,1,2);}}
  function apprenticeFrame(run){const scene=run.scene;if(scene.inside||run.film){apprenticeClear(run);return;}const r=scene.cameras.main.worldView,eng=run.engine,map=run.apprenticeActors??=new Map(),minute=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay+run.clock/500;for(const a of D.apprentices){const p=D.eastById[a.site],trip=D.crewDuty(run.world,a.id,minute)||D.gatheringDuty(run.world,a.id,minute),d=trip||D.apprenticeDuty(run.world,a.id,minute);if(!d||(!trip&&(p.ax*T<r.x-170||p.ax*T>r.right+170||p.ay*T<r.y-170||p.ay*T>r.bottom+170))){apprenticeClear(run,a.id);continue;}const path=trip?null:apprenticeRoute(run,a);if(!trip&&!path){apprenticeClear(run,a.id);continue;}const u=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,d.t)),x=trip?trip.x:path[0].x+(path[1].x-path[0].x)*u,y=trip?trip.y:path[0].y+(path[1].y-path[0].y)*u,key='v90-'+a.id;if(x<r.x-90||x>r.right+90||y<r.y-90||y>r.bottom+90){apprenticeClear(run,a.id);continue;}let v=map.get(a.id);if(!scene.textures.exists('ch_'+key+'_down_0'))eng.sprite(scene,key,apprenticeLook(a),(g,dir,f)=>Costumes.paint(g,dir,f,{outfit:'traveler',wet:false}));if(!v){const sprite=scene.add.image(x,y,'ch_'+key+'_down_0').setOrigin(.5,eng.origin),label=scene.add.text(x,y-40,a.name,{fontFamily:'Georgia',fontSize:'11px',color:'#f8e5bd',backgroundColor:'#21312bd9',padding:{x:4,y:2}}).setOrigin(.5,1).setDepth(23),prop=scene.add.graphics();scene.areaObjs.push(sprite,label,prop);v={sprite,label,prop,face:eng.face()};map.set(a.id,v);}const dx=x-v.sprite.x,dy=y-v.sprite.y;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)>.01){eng.turn(v.face,dx,dy);v.face.phase+=Math.min(16,Math.hypot(dx,dy));}else eng.turn(v.face,0,1);eng.animate(v.sprite,key,v.face,d.moving);const depth=run.view.depthAt(y);v.sprite.setPosition(x,y).setDepth(depth);v.label.setPosition(x,y-40).setText(a.name+' · '+d.phase).setVisible(Math.hypot(scene.player.x-x,scene.player.y-y)<105);v.prop.clear().setDepth(depth+.002);if(trip?.loaded){v.prop.fillStyle(0x513e30,1).fillRect(x-8,y-22,16,11).fillStyle(0xb28b58,1).fillRect(x-7,y-21,14,8).fillStyle(0xe2c48d,1).fillRect(x-6,y-21,12,2).fillStyle(0x745437,1).fillRect(x-2,y-21,2,8);}else if(trip?.teamRole==='logistics'&&!d.moving)masteryTool(v.prop,'archive',x,y,run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/125)%12,apprenticeLook(a).skin);else if(trip&&!d.moving&&trip.crew&&trip.phase!=='Waiting for team')masteryTool(v.prop,({3:'brick',4:'join',5:'brick',6:'saw',7:'repair'}[trip.target]||'repair'),x,y,run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/125)%12,apprenticeLook(a).skin);else if(trip&&!d.moving)apprenticeProp(v.prop,{...a,prop:['book','basket','cup'][a.look%3]},x,y,run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/260)%4);else if(!d.moving&&d.phase!=='Resting')masteryTool(v.prop,D.masteryBySite[a.site]?.tool||'archive',x,y,run.reduced?0:Math.floor(run.time/125)%12,apprenticeLook(a).skin);}}
  function apprenticePanel(run,id){const a=D.apprenticesById[id];if(!a||!D.apprenticeActive(run.world,id))throw Error('Restore the workshop first.');const v=run.apprenticeActors?.get(id);if(!v||!reachable(run.scene,v.sprite,53))throw Error('Walk over to the apprentice during their shift.');const p=D.eastById[a.site],s=D.eastState(run.world,a.site),l=D.apprenticeship(run.world,id),key=Object.keys(p.input)[0];run.apprenticeId=id;const crew=D.crewDuty(run.world,id);if(crew)return panel(run,'apprentice',title('CONSTRUCTION CREW',a.name,D.eastById[crew.site].name)+'<p>'+esc(crew.phase)+' · Reserved construction materials. I will return to my workshop after this phase.</p>'+button('Close','close'));const trip=D.gatheringDuty(run.world,id);if(trip?.phase==='At the gathering'){const q=D.gatheringQuestion(id),record=D.gatheringRecord(run.world,id),done=record.lastDay===Math.floor(D.now(run.world)/1440);return panel(run,'apprentice',title('COMMUNITY CIRCLE',a.name,D.commonById[trip.common].event)+'<p>'+esc(a.role)+' · '+record.count+' remembered conversations</p>'+(done?'<p>Thanks for your thoughtful contribution today. See you at the next gathering!</p>':'<p>'+esc(q.prompt)+'</p>'+q.answers.map((ans,i)=>button(ans,'apprentice-gather:'+i)).join(''))+button('Close','close'));}let b=`<div class="charter-hero"><div class="charter-emblem">${l.stage===3?'III':'I–III'}</div><div><small>${esc(a.role.toUpperCase())}</small><h3>${esc(a.name)} · ${esc(a.trait)}</h3><p>Mentor: ${esc(p.keeper)} · ${esc(p.name)}</p><p>${esc(a.goal)}</p></div></div><p>${esc(a.task)}</p><p class="fv-copy">Guild training ${l.stage}/3 · Production orders: ${s.runs}. Your previous contribution is remembered.</p>`;if(!l.stage)b+=`<p>${esc(p.note)}</p><h3>${esc(p.question)}</h3>`+p.answers.map((ans,i)=>button(ans,'apprentice-learn:'+i)).join('');else if(l.stage===1)b+=`<p>“Could you bring one ${esc(C.INVENTORY[key][0])} for the next order?”</p>`+button('Contribute 1 '+C.INVENTORY[key][0],'apprentice-supply',!(run.world.resources[key]>0)||Object.values(s.stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)>=48);else if(l.stage===2)b+='<p>“Let us finish a real order, then review what we learned.”</p>'+button('Review completed order','apprentice-review',s.runs<=l.baseline);else b+='<p>“Thank you for helping me learn my trade. I remember the material and the order we completed together.”</p>';return panel(run,'apprentice',title('GUILD APPRENTICESHIP',a.name,a.role)+b+masteryApprenticeNote(run,a)+button('Close','close'));}
  function apprenticeAction(run,action){const id=run.apprenticeId,v=run.apprenticeActors?.get(id);if(run.panel!=='apprentice'||!v||!reachable(run.scene,v.sprite,53))throw Error('Talk to the apprentice in person.');if(D.crewDuty(run.world,id))throw Error('The apprentice is assigned to construction.');const [cmd,arg]=action.split(':');const op=cmd.slice('apprentice-'.length);edit(run,w=>op==='gather'?D.gatheringTalk(w,id,Number(arg)):D.apprenticeLesson(w,id,op,Number(arg)));sound('gather');return apprenticePanel(run,id);}
  function requestPanelContent(run,p){const w=run.world,active=D.regionalCargo(w),qs=D.regionalRequests.filter(q=>q.from===p.id||q.to===p.id);if(!qs.length)return '';return '<h3>Inter-district commissions</h3><p class="fv-copy">A sealed consignment uses actual workshop stock. Carry it to the destination on foot. Only one may be carried at a time.</p>'+qs.map(q=>{const l=D.regionalState(w,q.id),dest=D.eastById[q.to],src=D.eastById[q.from],here=q.from===p.id;let b=`<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(q.name)}</b><small>${esc(src.name)} → ${esc(dest.name)} · (${dest.ax.toFixed(1)}, ${dest.ay.toFixed(1)}) · ${q.n} × ${esc(C.INVENTORY[q.key][0])} · ${['Available','In transit','Delivered'][l.stage]}</small>`;if(l.stage===0&&here)b+=button('Collect sealed shipment','regional-accept:'+q.id,!!active||D.eastState(w,q.to).stage<8||(D.eastState(w,q.from).stock[q.key]||0)<q.n);if(l.stage===1&&here)b+=button('Return shipment','regional-return:'+q.id);if(l.stage===1&&!here){const good=`I have brought ${q.n} ${C.INVENTORY[q.key][0]} from ${src.name}.`,bad=`I have brought ${q.n+1} ${C.INVENTORY[q.key][0]} from ${src.name}.`;b+='<p>Confirm the delivery quantity in English:</p>'+[0,1].map(i=>button(i===D.regionalAnswer(q)?good:bad,'regional-deliver:'+q.id+':'+i)).join('');}return b+'</article>';}).join('');}
  function regionalAction(run,action){const [cmd,id,arg]=action.split(':'),q=D.regionalById[id],p=run.eastSite;if(!q||run.panel!=='east-site'||!eastNear(run,p))throw Error('Visit the workshop in person.');if(cmd==='regional-accept'&&p===q.from)edit(run,w=>D.regionalAccept(w,id));else if(cmd==='regional-return'&&p===q.from)edit(run,w=>D.regionalReturn(w,id));else if(cmd==='regional-deliver'&&p===q.to)edit(run,w=>D.regionalDeliver(w,id,Number(arg)));else throw Error('This is not the consignor or recipient.');sound('gather');return eastPanel(run,p);}

  function chooseTarget(run) {
    const { scene, world } = run;
    const player = scene.player;
    if(scene.inside?.tunnel){const st=tunnelStation(run);return st?{kind:"tunnel-station",label:st.name,data:st.id}:null;}
    if(scene.inside?.heritage){const o=Art.heritageRoomNear(scene);return o?{kind:'room',label:o.action==='lamp'?'Toggle lantern':o.name}:null;}
    if (scene.inside) return { kind: 'room', label: scene.inside.b.id === 'ruined-warehouse' ? 'Inspect storage' : 'Look around' };
    const cross=crossTarget(run);if(cross)return cross;
    const sky=skywayTarget(run);if(sky)return sky;
    const tp=tunnelNear(run);if(tp)return {kind:"tunnel",label:"Blackstone · "+tp.id+" stair",data:tp.id};
    const choices = [];
    const add = (kind, point, label, data, range = 48) => {
      const gap = distance(player, point);
      if (gap <= range) choices.push({ kind, label, data, gap });
    };
    for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(D.civicPockets(world)))if(pocketNear(run,id))add("pocket",pocketPoint(r),"Roadside mosaic court",id,42);
    for(const p of D.commons){if(commonNear(run,p))add("commons",commonPoint(p),p.name,p.id,42);}
    for(const h of C.HOMES){const pt={x:h.ax*T,y:h.ay*T};if(reachable(scene,pt,53))add("homeland",pt,h.name,h.id,53);}
    let hand=null;
    for(const [id,drop] of Object.entries(world.ground.drops)){
      const gap=distance(player,drop);
      if(gap<=40&&(!hand||gap<hand.gap)&&reachable(scene,drop,40)) {
        hand={kind:'pickup',label:'Pick up '+C.INVENTORY[drop.item][0]+' ×'+drop.quantity,data:id,gap};
      }
    }
    if(hand&&hand.gap<=21)return hand;
    for(const [id,v] of run.apprenticeActors||[])if(reachable(scene,v.sprite,53))add("apprentice",v.sprite,"Talk to "+D.apprenticesById[id].name,id,53);
    if(scene.player.x>174*T)for(const p of C.EAST_SITES)if(eastNear(run,p.id))add("east",eastPoint(p),p.name,p.id,55);
    for(const p of C.FRONTIER)if(frontierNear(run,p.id))add("frontier",frontierPoint(p),p.name,p.id,48);
    for(const h of C.HIGHLANDS)if(hillNear(run,h))add('hill',{x:h.x*T,y:h.y*T},h.name,h.id,55);
    for(const e of C.ECHOES)if(!D.surveyState(world).echoes[e.id]&&echoNear(run,e))add('echo',C.echoPoint(e,run.time/1000),'Light spirit · '+e.name,e.id,70);
    if(millNear(run))add("mill",millPoint(),C.MILL.name,null,58);
    if(summitNear(run))add('summit',summitPoint(),'Cloudrest Lookout',null,56);
    for(const site of D.targets){const pt=center({x:site.ax,y:site.ay});if(reachable(scene,pt,56))add('development',pt,site.name,site.id,56);}
    for(const r of D.reserves)if(reserveNear(run,r.id))add("reserve",{x:r.x*T,y:r.y*T},r.name,r.id,64);
    if(hand)choices.push(hand);
    add('well', center(POINTS.well), 'Inspect the old well', null, 52);
    if (!world.quests['where-am-i'].letter) add('letter', center(POINTS.letter), 'Pick up the letter');
    add('fire', center(POINTS.fire), 'Campfire', null, 49);
    for (const actor of run.actors) {
      if(reachable(scene,actor.sprite,53))add('npc',actor.sprite,'Talk to '+NPCS[actor.id].name,actor.id,53);
    }
    for (const node of C.nodesNear(player.x,player.y)) {
      if (!S.nodeStock(world, node)) continue;
      if (node.kind === 'water' && (!world.quests['where-am-i'].well || !world.quests['the-herbalist'].lyra)) continue;
      const point=resourceApproach(node);
      const range=resourceRange(node);
      if(reachable(scene,point,range,node.id))add('resource',point,resourceName(node),node,range);
    }
    if(S.quarryStage(world)!=='hidden'&&canReachQuarry(scene))add('quarry',quarryApproach(),QUARRY.name,null,34);
    const door = scene.doorCooldown <= 0 ? scene.doorAhead() : null;
    if (door) {
      add('door', { x: door.door*T + 16, y: (door.y + door.h)*T + 25 },
        door.id === 'ruined-warehouse' ? 'Inspect the warehouse' : 'Enter ' + door.name, door, 55);
    }
    const protectedTree=Object.values(C.TREES_BY_ID).find(tree=>tree.protected);
    const oak=center(protectedTree);
    if(reachable(scene,oak,58,protectedTree.id))add('landmark',oak,'The Whispering Tree','oak',58);
    for (const [key, label] of [['wayfarer','Inspect the Wayfarers’ Arch'],
        ['arch','The Blackstone Arch'],['ruins','The Nameless Square']]) {
      add('landmark', center(POINTS[key]), label, key, key === 'oak' ? 58 : 52);
    }
    return choices.sort((a, b) => a.gap - b.gap)[0] || null;
  }

  function interact(scene) {
    const run = runs.get(scene);
    if (!run) return false;
    if (!run.world || run.panel || run.film || run.pending || scene.switching || legacyPanel(scene)) return true;
    const target = chooseTarget(run);
    if (!target) return true;
    try {
      switch (target.kind) {
        case 'crossing':crossInteract(run,target);break;
        case 'skyway': case 'skyway-survey': case 'skyway-route': case 'skyway-scout': skywayInteract(run,target);break;
        case 'npc':
          if(target.data==='mara')visitorPanel(run);
          else dialog(run,target.data);
          break;
        case 'tunnel': case 'tunnel-station': tunnelInteract(run,target);break;
        case 'pocket':pocketPanel(run,target.data);break;case 'commons':commonPanel(run,target.data);break;
        case 'homeland':homePanel(run,target.data);break;
        case 'east': eastPanel(run,target.data,true);break;
        case 'apprentice':apprenticePanel(run,target.data);break;
        case 'frontier': frontierPanel(run,target.data);break;
        case 'reserve': reservePanel(run,target.data); break;
        case 'resource': startGather(run, target.data); break;
        case 'hill': hillPanel(run,target.data); break;
        case 'echo': echoPanel(run,target.data); break;
        case 'summit': summitPanel(run); break;
        case 'mill': millPanel(run); break;
        case 'development': settlementPanel(run,target.data); break;
        case 'pickup': {
          let caught;
          edit(run,world=>{caught=S.pickup(world,target.data);});
          const text='+'+caught.amount+' '+C.INVENTORY[caught.key][0];
          toast(run,text+(caught.left?' · '+caught.left+' left on the ground':''));
          sound('gather');
          break;
        }
        case 'quarry': quarryPanel(run); break;
        case 'door':
          if (target.data.id === 'ruined-warehouse') warehousePanel(run);
          else scene.enterBuilding(target.data);
          break;
        case 'room': roomPanel(run); break;
        case 'well':
          react(run, 'inspect-well');
          panel(run,'well', title('Forgotten Hamlet','The Well of Memory',
            'The stones are warm. At the bottom, water moves against a ring of seven cuts.') +
            (run.world.quests['the-herbalist'].lyra ? button('Draw clean water', 'water') : '') + button('Back', 'close'));
          break;
        case 'letter':
          react(run, 'take-letter');
          panel(run,'letter', title('A faded letter','To the one who wakes') +
            '<blockquote>' + esc(C.LETTER) + '</blockquote>' + button('Fold the letter', 'close'));
          break;
        case 'fire': campPanel(run); break;
        case 'landmark': inspectLandmark(run, target.data); break;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      toast(run, error.message);
    }
    return true;
  }

  function startGather(run, node) {
    if (run.pending) return;
    const range=node.kind==='water'&&run.world.quests['the-herbalist'].lyra?52:resourceRange(node);
    if (!reachable(run.scene,resourceApproach(node),range,node.id)) throw new Error('Find a clear approach to the resource.');
    const {tool} = S.checkGather(run.world,node.id);
    beginWork(run,node,tool,resourceApproach(node),false,range);
  }

  function beginWork(run,node,tool,target,quarry,range) {
    const {scene}=run, {x,y}=scene.player;
    run.pending={id:++run.actionId,node,quarry,target,range,elapsed:0,
      duration:node.seconds*1000/(tool?.efficiency||1),
      toolId:tool?.id??null,toolName:tool?.definition.name,phase:'prepare',start:{x,y}};
    // Aim at the face of the work while keeping the feet at the safe approach.
    if(quarry)target=center(QUARRY);
    if(distance({x,y},target)>6)run.engine.turn(scene.pFace,target.x-x,target.y-y);
    ui.toast.hidden=true;
    run.toastUntil=0;
    ui.act.hidden = true;
    ui.progress.hidden = false;
    ui.progress.setAttribute('aria-label', quarry?node.label:node.name);
    paintProgress(run.pending);
  }

  const actionStage=time => time<.24?'prepare':time<.62?'perform':time<.81?'impact':'recover';
  const stageLabel={ prepare:'Preparing', perform:'Working', impact:'Gathering', recover:'Finishing' };
  function paintProgress(task) {
    const progress=Math.min(100,Math.round(task.elapsed/task.duration*100));
    const phase=actionStage(task.elapsed/task.duration);
    ui.progress.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',progress);
    ui.progress.style.setProperty('--fv-fill',progress+'%');
    if(ui.progress.dataset.phase!==phase||task.elapsed===0) {
      ui.progress.dataset.phase=phase;
      ui.progress.firstElementChild.textContent=stageLabel[phase]+(task.toolName?' · '+task.toolName:'');
    }
    task.phase=phase;
  }

  function cancelGather(run, announce=false) {
    if(!run.pending)return;
    run.pending=null;
    ui.progress.hidden=true;
    if(announce)toast(run,'Gathering cancelled.');
  }

  function gatherFrame(run,dt) {
    const pending = run.pending;
    if (!pending) return;
    if (distance(run.scene.player,pending.start)>15 ||
        !(pending.roomWork?run.scene.inside?.heritage?.id===pending.roomWork.id&&Art.heritageRoomNear(run.scene)?.action==='work':pending.quarry?canReachQuarry(run.scene):reachable(run.scene,pending.target,pending.range,pending.node.id))) {
      cancelGather(run,true);
      return;
    }
    pending.elapsed+=Math.min(80,dt);
    if(pending.elapsed<pending.duration) {
      paintProgress(pending);
      return;
    }
    completeGather(run,pending);
  }

  function dropPoint(scene,aim=scene.player) {
    const {x,y}=scene.player;
    const range=Math.max(1,distance(aim,{x,y}));
    const candidates=[{x:x+(aim.x-x)*10/range,y:y+(aim.y-y)*10/range},{x,y}];
    for(const entry of candidates) {
      const point={x:Math.round(entry.x),y:Math.round(entry.y)};
      if(!scene.blocked(point.x,point.y)&&reachable(scene,point,16)&&
          !scene.area.warpAt(Math.floor(point.x/T),Math.floor(point.y/T)))return point;
    }
    throw new Error('Move to clear ground before setting the materials down.');
  }

  function completeGather(run,pending) {
    if(run.pending?.id!==pending.id)return;
    cancelGather(run);
