    if(pending.roomWork){const p=pending.roomWork;try{if(run.scene.inside?.heritage?.id!==p.id||Art.heritageRoomNear(run.scene)?.action!=='work')throw new Error('Return to the work station.');if(p.millRecipe&&Art.heritageRoomNear(run.scene)?.id!==p.station)throw new Error('Return to this machine.');edit(run,w=>p.millRecipe?(p.local?D.millLocalWork:D.millWork)(w,p.millRecipe,p.runs):(p.local?D.workHeritageLocal:D.workHeritage)(w,p.id,p.runs));sound('gather');toast(run,'Operation complete · supplies updated');heritageRoomPanel(run);}catch(e){toast(run,e.message);}return;}
    if(pending.east){const p=pending.east;try{if(!eastNear(run,p.id))throw new Error("Return to the site.");edit(run,w=>D.eastRestore(w,p.id,p.stage));sound("gather");return eastPanel(run,p.id);}catch(e){toast(run,e.message);}return;}
    if(pending.frontier){const p=pending.frontier;try{if(!frontierNear(run,p.id))throw new Error("Return to the station.");edit(run,w=>p.stage!==undefined?D.frontierRestore(w,p.id,p.stage):D.frontierService(w,p.id,p.runs));sound("gather");toast(run,"Station work complete");return frontierPanel(run,p.id);}catch(e){toast(run,e.message);}return;}
    if(pending.crossing){const p=pending.crossing;try{const n=crossNear(run);if(!n||n.upper||n.b.id!==p.id||n.side!==p.side)throw new Error("Return to the approach.");edit(run,w=>D.crossingBuild(w,p.id,p.stage));sound("gather");crossPanel(run,n.b,n.side);}catch(e){toast(run,e.message);}return;}
    if(pending.skyway){const p=pending.skyway;try{if(skywayNear(run)!==p.side)throw new Error('Return to the ramp.');edit(run,w=>D.restoreSkyway(w,p.stage));sound('gather');skywayPanel(run,p.side);}catch(e){toast(run,e.message);}return;}
    if(pending.mill){const p=pending.mill;try{if(!millNear(run))throw new Error("Return to the mill.");edit(run,w=>p.recipe?D.millWork(w,p.recipe,p.runs):D.restoreMill(w,p.stage));sound("gather");toast(run,"Watermill · work completed");millPanel(run);}catch(e){toast(run,e.message);}return;}
    if(pending.heritage){const p=pending.heritage;try{if(!devNear(run,p.id))throw new Error('Return to the site.');edit(run,w=>p.service?D.workHeritage(w,p.id,p.runs):D.restoreHeritage(w,p.id,p.stage,p.branch));sound('gather');toast(run,p.service?'Operation completed · supplies updated':'Restoration completed');settlementPanel(run,p.id);}catch(e){toast(run,e.message);}return;}
    if(pending.summit!==undefined){try{if(!summitNear(run))throw new Error('Return to the lookout.');let stage;edit(run,w=>{stage=D.restoreSummit(w,pending.summit);});toast(run,'Cloudrest · '+D.summitStages[stage].name);sound(stage===8?'bell':'gather');}catch(e){toast(run,e.message);}return;}
    try {
      let amount,stage;
      const point=dropPoint(run.scene,pending.target);
      edit(run, world => {
        if(pending.quarry)({amount,stage}=S.excavate(world,pending.node.id,pending.toolId,point));
        else amount=S.gather(world,pending.node.id,pending.toolId,point);
      });
      if(pending.node.kind==='tree'&&!run.reduced)Art.fell(run.view,pending.node.id,run.scene.player);
      const materials=amount?amount+' '+C.INVENTORY[pending.node.resource][0]+' on the ground.':'Loose gravel cleared.';
      const result=stage==='exhausted'?materials+' Pick up the quarry tablet.':materials;
      const tool=Kit.selectedTool(run.world);
      const notice=pending.toolId&&Kit.worn(tool) ?
        (tool.tool.durability===0?' · Tool broken':' · Tool wearing out') : '';
      toast(run,result+notice);
      sound('gather');
    } catch (error) {
      toast(run, error.message);
    }
  }

  function quarryPanel(run) {
    if(!canReachQuarry(run.scene))throw new Error('Approach the Silverwater Cut from the south.');
    const stage=S.quarryStage(run.world);
    const copy={
      unmarked:'A pale buckle in the shale catches your eye. Survey it from the grassy lip.',
      marked:'Two stakes mark the edge. A mining hammer can clear the gravel.',
      cleared:'The gravel is gone. Follow the fracture in the buried shelf.',
      revealed:'Rust-colored ore crosses the open cut. A worked edge is visible underneath.',
      exhausted:'The remaining stone is barren. Pick up the quarry tablet to record its tally in your journal.',
      preserved:'A rope and stakes mark the preserved cut.',
      restored:'The pit is filled and the ground can be crossed again.',
    };
    const step=QUARRY.stages.find(entry=>entry.id===stage);
    const choices=step?button(step.label,'quarry-work'):
      stage==='unmarked'?button('Survey and mark the cut','quarry-survey'):
      (stage==='exhausted'?button('Preserve the excavation','quarry-preserved'):'')+
      (stage==='exhausted'||stage==='preserved'?button('Backfill the cut','quarry-restored'):'');
    panel(run,'quarry',title(REGION[QUARRY.region].name,QUARRY.name,copy[stage])+choices+button('Back','close'));
  }

  // Settlement actions use the existing modal router and the same transaction manager.
  const D=root.LQValleyDevelopment;
  const costs=bag=>Object.entries(bag||{}).map(([id,n])=>`${n} ${C.INVENTORY[id][0]}`).join(' · ')||'No materials';
  const devBag=(bag,scope)=>Object.entries(bag).filter(([,n])=>n>0).map(([id,n])=>`<div class="v852-supply"><img src="${Art.inventoryIcon(id)}" alt=""><span>${esc(C.INVENTORY[id][0])} <b>×${n}</b></span>${button('Take 1',`dev-take:${scope}:${id}:1`)}${n>1?button('All',`dev-take:${scope}:${id}:${n}`):''}</div>`).join('')||'<p class="fv-copy">No items waiting.</p>';
  const devNear=(run,id)=>{const t=D.byId[id];return t&&!run.scene.inside&&reachable(run.scene,center({x:t.ax,y:t.ay}),64);};
  function heritageSection(run,id){const def=D.heritageDefs[id],s=D.heritageState(run.world,id),ready=D.canUse(run.world),e=run.world.development.exploration[id];if(!def)return '';const available=bag=>Object.entries(bag).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n);
    const steps=['Ruined',...def.stages];const task=s.stage<8?{name:steps[s.stage+1],cost:D.heritageCost(id,s.stage+1,null)}:null;
    const service=s.stage>=8?D.heritageService(run.world,id):null;
    return `<section class="lq-reborn"><div class="lq-reborn-eyebrow">HERITAGE RESTORATION · ${s.stage+1}/10</div><h3>${esc(def.role)}</h3><p>${esc(def.history)}</p><div class="lq-reborn-meter" aria-label="Restoration progress"><i style="width:${s.stage/9*100}%"></i></div><div class="lq-reborn-stages">${steps.map((a,i)=>`<span class="${i<=s.stage?'done':''}">${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')} · ${esc(a)}</span>`).join('')}</div><p class="lq-reborn-english">FIELD ENGLISH · ${esc(def.english)}</p>${!e?.solved?'<p>Solve the inscription to unlock restoration.</p>':!ready?'<p>Restore the hamlet warehouse to begin.</p>':task?`<article class="lq-reborn-task"><b>${esc(task.name)}</b><small>${esc(costs(task.cost))}</small>${button('Perform restoration','heritage-build',!available(task.cost))}</article>`:s.stage===8?`<h4>Choose a permanent specialization</h4>${def.branches.map((b,i)=>`<article class="lq-reborn-task"><b>${esc(b.name)}</b><small>${esc(costs(b.cost))} · ${esc(b.label)}</small>${button('Specialize',`heritage-branch:${i}`,!available(b.cost))}</article>`).join('')}`:`<p>Specialization: <b>${esc(def.branches[s.branch].name)}</b></p>`}${service?`<article class="lq-reborn-task"><b>${esc(service.label)}</b><small>${service.forecast?'Records current and predicted weather':esc(costs(service.input)+' → '+costs(service.output))}</small><small>${def.limited?'Contracts '+s.runs+'/3':'Completed operations: '+s.runs}</small>${button('Carry out work','heritage-service',!ready||!!(def.limited&&s.runs>=3)||!!(service.input&&!available(service.input)))}</article>`:''}${s.lastReading?`<p>Reading: ${esc(s.lastReading.weather)} · Forecast: ${esc(s.lastReading.next)}</p>`:''}</section>`;
  }
  function heritageAction(run,action){const id=run.devSite,def=D.heritageDefs[id],s=D.heritageState(run.world,id);if(run.panel!=='development'||!def||!devNear(run,id)||!D.canUse(run.world)||!run.world.development.exploration[id]?.solved)throw new Error('Return to the surveyed heritage site.');const [verb,i]=action.split(':');let branch=null,service=false,bag,name;
    if(verb==='heritage-build'&&s.stage<8){bag=D.heritageCost(id,s.stage+1,null);name=def.stages[s.stage];}
    else if(verb==='heritage-branch'&&s.stage===8&&['0','1'].includes(i)){branch=Number(i);bag=D.heritageCost(id,9,branch);name=def.branches[branch].name;}
    else if(verb==='heritage-service'&&s.stage>=8){service=true;const r=D.heritageService(run.world,id);bag=r.input||{};name=r.label;if(def.limited&&s.runs>=3)throw new Error('Contracts completed.');}else throw new Error('Work order not available.');
    if(!Object.entries(bag).every(([k,n])=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>=n))throw new Error('Bring the required materials.');
    const t=D.byId[id],target=center({x:t.ax,y:t.ay});close(run);beginWork(run,{id,kind:'stone',name,x:t.ax,y:t.ay,seconds:2.1+(service?1:s.stage*.15)},null,target,false,64);run.pending.heritage={id,stage:s.stage,runs:s.runs,branch,service};
  }

  function settlementPanel(run,id){
    const t=D.byId[id];if(!devNear(run,id))throw new Error('Walk to the marked location first.');
    if(t.kind==='landmark'&&!run.world.development.exploration[id])edit(run,w=>D.visit(w,id));
    const d=run.world.development;
    const back=button('Close','close');
    let html='';
    if(t.kind==='workshop'){
      const a=d.workshop,can=D.canUse(run.world);
      const active=a.queue[0], labor=active&&D.task(run.world,active.id), stock=Object.values(a.output).reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0);
      const labels={reserved:'Awaiting worker',assigned:'Preparing to travel',traveling:'On the road',collecting:'Collecting supplies',working:'Crafting',delivering:'Delivering to shelf',blocked:'Output shelf full'};
      const worker=labor?.worker==='mara'?'Mara · Joiner':labor?.worker==='player'?'You · Apprentice':'Unassigned';
      const pct=labor?Math.floor(labor.progress/D.recipes[active.recipe].minutes*100):0;
      const stageIndex=labor?['reserved','assigned','traveling','collecting','working','delivering','blocked'].indexOf(labor.stage):0;
      const phases=['Reserve','Assign','Travel','Collect','Craft','Deliver'];
      const material=(bag)=>Object.entries(bag).map(([id,n])=>`<span class="lq-labor-mat"><img src="${Art.inventoryIcon(id)}" alt="">${n} ${esc(C.INVENTORY[id][0])}</span>`).join('');
      const queue=a.queue.map((j,i)=>{const r=D.recipes[j.recipe],t=D.task(run.world,j.id);return `<article class="v852-row lq-labor-order"><b>#${j.id} · ${esc(r.name)}</b><small>${esc(labels[t.stage])} · ${t.legacy?'Recovered order · ':''}${esc(costs(j.escrow))} reserved</small>${!['delivering','blocked'].includes(t.stage)?button('Cancel & return supplies',`dev-cancel:${j.id}`):''}</article>`;}).join('');
      const available=r=>Object.entries(r.input).every(([id,n])=>(run.world.resources[id]||0)>=n);
      html=title(`WORKSHOP · LEVEL ${a.level}/5`,D.levels[a.level-1],can?'A living workshop: reserve materials, choose a worker, follow the delivery.':'Restore the original warehouse first.')+
      `<section class="lq-labor-hero"><div class="lq-labor-kicker">PRODUCTION CONTROL <span>${active?'JOB #'+active.id:'READY'}</span></div><h3>${active?esc(D.recipes[active.recipe].name):'The workshop is quiet'}</h3><p>${active?esc(labels[labor.stage])+' · '+esc(worker):'Add a recipe below to reserve materials.'}</p><div class="lq-labor-track">${phases.map((s,i)=>`<span class="${active&&labor.worker==='player'&&(i===2||i===3)?'skip':active&&i<=Math.min(5,stageIndex)?'on':''}"><i>${i+1}</i>${s}</span>`).join('')}</div>${active?`<div class="lq-labor-progress"><strong>${pct}%</strong><div class="lq-labor-meter" role="progressbar" aria-label="Work completed" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="100" aria-valuenow="${pct}"><i style="width:${pct}%"></i></div><small>${labor.progress}/${D.recipes[active.recipe].minutes} game min of actual work</small></div><div class="lq-labor-materials">${material(active.escrow)}</div>${labor.worker==='mara'?`<p class="lq-labor-route">Route ${labor.cursor+1}/${labor.path.length} tiles · ${labor.cargo?'Cargo collected':'Supplies reserved'}</p>`:''}`:''}</section>`+
      (active&&labor.stage==='reserved'?`<div class="lq-labor-actions">${button('Assign Mara',`dev-assign-mara:${active.id}`,!can||!run.world.residents.mara)}${button('Work myself',`dev-assign-player:${active.id}`,!can)}</div><p class="fv-copy">${run.world.residents.mara?'Mara collects from the supply point, works and delivers while you explore.':'Mara must join the settlement first. You can craft the order yourself.'}</p>`:active&&labor.worker==='player'&&['working','delivering'].includes(labor.stage)?button(labor.stage==='delivering'?'Deliver finished goods':'Craft for 6 game min',`dev-manual:${active.id}`):active?`<p class="fv-copy">${labor.stage==='blocked'?'Clear space on the output shelf to receive the finished order.':'Close this panel to let the village clock and worker advance.'}</p>`:'')+
      `<div class="v852-kpis"><span>${a.queue.length}/${D.QUEUE}<small>Orders</small></span><span>${stock}/${D.LIMIT}<small>Shelf units</small></span><span>${a.completed}<small>Completed</small></span></div><h3>Output shelf</h3>${devBag(a.output,'workshop')}<h3>Reserved orders</h3>${queue||'<p class="fv-copy">No pending orders.</p>'}<h3>Recipes</h3><div class="v852-recipes lq-labor-recipes">${Object.entries(D.recipes).map(([key,r])=>`<article class="v852-row"><div class="lq-labor-recipe-title"><img src="${Art.inventoryIcon(Object.keys(r.output)[0])}" alt=""><b>${esc(r.name)}</b></div><small>Tier ${r.level} · ${r.minutes} work min</small><div class="lq-labor-materials">${material(r.input)}</div><small>Produces · ${esc(costs(r.output))}</small><em>${esc(r.english)}</em>${button(r.level>a.level?`Level ${r.level} required`:!available(r)?'Missing materials':'Reserve order',`dev-enqueue:${key}`,!can||r.level>a.level||a.queue.length>=D.QUEUE||!available(r))}</article>`).join('')}</div>`+
      (a.level<5?`<h3>Next workshop tier</h3><p class="fv-copy">${esc(D.levels[a.level])} · ${esc(costs(D.upgrades[a.level]))}</p>${button('Upgrade workshop','dev-upgrade',!can||!!a.queue.length)}`:'')+
      `<h3>Recent deliveries</h3>${run.world.labor.history.slice(-5).reverse().map(e=>`<div class="v852-row"><b>${esc(D.recipes[e.recipe].name)}</b><small>${e.worker==='mara'?'Mara':'You'} · Day ${Math.floor(e.at/1440)+1} · Order #${e.id}</small></div>`).join('')||'<p class="fv-copy">No completed deliveries recorded in this version.</p>'}`+back;

    }else if(t.kind==='farm'){
      const f=d.farm[id],stage=D.cropStage(f),can=D.canUse(run.world),c=f.crop&&D.crops[f.crop];
      html=title('HAMLET · COMMUNITY GARDEN',t.name,can?'Grow crops with game time and changing weather.':'Restore the original warehouse to unlock gardening.')+
      `<div class="v852-kpis"><span>${['Empty','Seed','Sprout','Young','Ripening','Mature'][stage]}<small>Crop stage</small></span><span>${f.moisture}%<small>Soil moisture</small></span><span>${f.harvests}<small>Harvests</small></span></div>`+
      (c?`<h3>${esc(c.name)}</h3><meter min="0" max="10000" value="${f.growth}"></meter><p class="fv-copy">${Math.floor(f.growth/100)}% grown · Water and rain help the crop grow.</p>${button('Water · 1 water','dev-water',f.moisture>=90||!can)}${button('Harvest crop','dev-harvest',stage!==5||!can)}`:
      `<h3>Choose a crop</h3><div class="v852-recipes">${Object.entries(D.crops).map(([key,v])=>`<article class="v852-row"><b>${esc(v.name)}</b><small>${v.hours} ideal game hours · ${v.yield} produce + 1 seed</small>${button('Plant · '+C.INVENTORY[v.seed][0],`dev-plant:${key}`,!can)}</article>`).join('')}</div>`)+
      `<h3>Harvest basket</h3>${devBag(f.output,id)}<p class="fv-copy">English: The soil is dry. Could you bring some water?</p>`+back;
    }else if(t.kind==='build'){
      const b=d.build,p=b.pending,next=D.stages[b.stage+1];
      html=title('COMMUNITY PROJECT',t.name,'Restore this fixed annex beside the garden with crafted materials.')+
      `<ol class="v852-stages">${D.stages.map((s,i)=>`<li class="${i<=b.stage?'done':p?.stage===i?'working':''}"><b>${i+1}. ${esc(s.name)}</b><small>${i<=b.stage?'Complete':p?.stage===i?`Working · ${Math.max(0,p.ends-D.now(run.world))} min left`:esc(costs(s.cost))}</small></li>`).join('')}</ol>`+
      (next&&!p?button('Deliver materials & begin '+next.name,'dev-build',!D.canUse(run.world)):p?'<p class="fv-copy">Close the panel and allow game time to pass.</p>':'<p class="fv-copy">The annex is operational. Settlement prosperity has increased.</p>')+
      (b.stage===7?`<h3>Annex storage · ${Object.values(b.storage).reduce((a,n)=>a+n,0)}/120</h3>${devBag(b.storage,id)}<h3>Deposit from backpack</h3>${Object.keys(Kit.limits).filter(k=>(run.world.resources[k]||0)>0).map(k=>`<div class="v852-supply"><span>${esc(C.INVENTORY[k][0])} ×${run.world.resources[k]}</span>${button('Store 1',`dev-deposit:${k}:1`)}${button('All',`dev-deposit:${k}:${run.world.resources[k]}`)}</div>`).join('')||'<p class="fv-copy">No materials to store.</p>'}`:'')+
      `<p class="fv-copy">Delivered: ${esc(costs(b.delivered))}</p>`+back;
    }else{
      const e=d.exploration[id],locked=t.prereq&&!d.exploration[t.prereq]?.solved;
      html=title('FIELD EXPEDITION · '+C.REGION[t.region].name,t.name,locked?'The inscription is incomplete. Find the previous record to interpret its symbols.':t.clue)+
      (e.solved?'<p class="fv-copy">Inscription recorded in your journal.</p>':locked?`<p class="fv-copy">Read ${esc(D.byId[t.prereq].name)} first.</p>`:
      `<h3>${esc(t.question)}</h3><div class="v852-recipes">${t.answers.map((a,i)=>button(a,`dev-solve:${i}`)).join('')}</div>`)+
      (e.solved?`<h3>Cache contents</h3>${devBag(e.loot,id)}`:'')+heritageSection(run,id)+(D.heritageState(run.world,id).stage>=8?button('Enter restored hall','room-enter:'+id):'')+button(d.marker===id?'Clear expedition marker':'Mark this landmark',d.marker===id?'dev-mark:clear':`dev-mark:${id}`)+back;
    }
    panel(run,'development',html);run.devSite=id;
  }
  function developmentAtlas(run){
    const d=run.world.development,player=run.scene.inside?.out||run.scene.player;
    let svg='<svg class="v852-atlas" viewBox="0 0 '+C.W+' '+C.H+'" role="img" aria-label="Valley discovery map"><rect width="'+C.W+'" height="'+C.H+'" rx="2" fill="#182b29"/>';
    const colors={amberwood:[103,112,65],heath:[105,99,111],coppervale:[151,105,70],glasswater:[70,107,91],goldgrass:[138,139,72],moonbloom:[58,91,87],hamlet:'#536a4b',basin:[98,120,90],heartwood:[61,102,80],ridge:[113,118,108],frost:[154,172,169],deepwoods:[55,91,80],marsh:[82,102,93]};
    const regionAt=(x,y)=>C.legacy(x,y)?'hamlet':C.wildernessRegion(x,y);
    const known=(x,y)=>regionAt(x,y)==='hamlet'||!!run.world.regions[regionAt(x,y)];
    const mapColor=(x,y)=>{if(C.legacy(x,y))return colors.hamlet;const weights=C.biomeWeights(x,y),rgb=[0,0,0];for(const [key,v] of Object.entries(weights))for(let i=0;i<3;i++)rgb[i]+=colors[key][i]*v;return '#'+rgb.map(n=>Math.min(255,Math.round(n/4)*4).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');};
    const paths={};for(let y=0;y<C.H;y+=4)for(let x=0;x<C.W;x+=4){if(!known(x+1,y+1))continue;const color=mapColor(x+1,y+1);(paths[color]??=[]).push(`M${x} ${y}h${Math.min(4,C.W-x)}v${Math.min(4,C.H-y)}h-${Math.min(4,C.W-x)}z`);}
    for(const [color,p] of Object.entries(paths))svg+=`<path d="${p.join('')}" fill="${color}"/>`;
    for(let y=0;y<C.H;y+=1){const [a,b]=C.shore(y+.5);if(known((a+b)/2,y+.5))svg+=`<rect x="${a}" y="${y}" width="${b-a}" height="1" fill="#51818a"/>`;}
    // Every atlas stroke comes from the actual path geometry. Fog still follows the existing discovery state.
    for(const route of [...C.TRAILS.map(points=>({id:'legacy',kind:'trail',points:points.map(([x,y])=>[x+.5,y+.5])})),...C.landscapeRoads])for(let i=1;i<route.points.length;i++){
      const [a,b]=route.points[i-1],[c,d]=route.points[i],n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(c-a,d-b)/1.5));let visible='';
      for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const f=k/n,g=(k+1)/n,x=a+(c-a)*f,y=b+(d-b)*f,xx=a+(c-a)*g,yy=b+(d-b)*g;if(known((x+xx)/2,(y+yy)/2)&&D.trailBuilt((x+xx)/2,(y+yy)/2))visible+=`M${x.toFixed(2)} ${y.toFixed(2)}L${xx.toFixed(2)} ${yy.toFixed(2)}`;}
      if(visible)svg+=`<path data-road="${esc(route.id)}" d="${visible}" fill="none" stroke="#d6c29b" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity=".82" stroke-width="${route.kind==='main'?1.05:.75}"/>`;
    }
    const drawn={};for(const[id,n]of Object.entries(D.trailWear(run.world))){const p=D.trailDecode(id);if(!p||!known(p.x/T,p.y/T))continue;(drawn[D.pathCare(run.world).roads[id]?"road-"+D.pathCare(run.world).roads[id]:n]??=[]).push(`M${p.x/T} ${p.y/T}L${p.bx/T} ${p.by/T}`);}for(const[n,d]of Object.entries(drawn))svg+=`<path data-desire-level="${n}" d="${d.join('')}" fill="none" stroke="${n.startsWith('road-')?'#cbd0c3':'#e7c38c'}" stroke-width="${n.startsWith('road-')?1.05:.18+Number(n)*.035}" stroke-linecap="round" opacity="${n.startsWith('road-')?1:.3+Number(n)*.034}"/>`;
    for(const b of C.CROSSINGS)if(D.crossingState(run.world,b.id).seen||known((b.left+b.right)/2,b.y)){const q=D.crossingState(run.world,b.id);svg+=`<path data-crossing="${b.id}" d="M${b.left} ${b.y}H${b.right}" stroke="${q.stage===3?'#a6d9d0':'#b7a68a'}" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-dasharray="${q.stage===3?'0':'1 1'}"/>`;}
    if(known(C.SKYWAY.surveyX,C.SKYWAY.y)){const b=C.SKYWAY,q=D.skywayState(run.world);svg+=`<path data-surface="skyway" d="M${b.left} ${b.y}H${b.right}" stroke="${q.stage>=8?'#f0d58a':'#b3afa0'}" stroke-width="1.25" stroke-dasharray="${q.stage>=8?'0':'1.5 1.5'}"/><rect x="${b.surveyX-1}" y="${b.y-1}" width="2" height="2" fill="${q.stage>=8?'#d5f3a5':'#b4af9e'}" stroke="#263e35" stroke-width=".4"/>`; }
    if(known(158,8))svg+=`<path d="${C.CASCADE.map((p,i)=>(i?'L':'M')+p.x+' '+p.y).join(' ')}" stroke="#88bbcc" stroke-width="1.4" fill="none"/>`;
    if(d.mill?.seen)svg+=`<circle cx="${C.MILL.x}" cy="${C.MILL.y}" r="1.6" fill="${d.mill.stage>=8?'#d5f3a5':d.mill.stage?'#e1b775':'#b4af9e'}" stroke="#1a2926" stroke-width=".6"/>`;
    for(const b of C.landscapeBridges)if(known((b.left+b.right)/2,(b.from+b.to)/2))svg+=`<rect data-bridge="${b.from}" x="${b.left}" y="${b.from+.25}" width="${b.right-b.left}" height="${b.to-b.from-.5}" rx=".25" fill="#bfa47c" stroke="#66513a" stroke-width=".25"/>`;
    for(const l of D.landmarks)if(d.exploration[l.id])svg+=`<circle cx="${l.x+.5}" cy="${l.y+.5}" r="${d.marker===l.id?3:1.6}" fill="${D.heritageState(run.world,l.id).stage>=8?'#d5f3a5':D.heritageState(run.world,l.id).stage>0?'#e1b775':'#b4af9e'}" stroke="#1a2926" stroke-width=".6"/>`;
    for(const h of C.HIGHLANDS)if(known(h.x,h.y)){const on=D.surveyState(run.world).visited[h.id];svg+=`<path data-peak="${h.id}" d="M${h.x} ${h.y-2.5}l2.2 4h-4.4z" fill="${on?'#e3c07c':'#b2c5ae'}" stroke="#34483f" stroke-width=".5"/>`;}
    for(const r of D.reserves)if(known(r.x,r.y)||D.reserveState(run.world,r.id).stage){const stage=D.reserveState(run.world,r.id).stage;svg+=`<rect data-habitat="${r.id}" x="${r.x-1.4}" y="${r.y-1.4}" width="2.8" height="2.8" rx=".5" stroke="#203529" stroke-width=".5" fill="${stage===3?'#9dd184':stage?'#dbc381':'#b6aa88'}"/>`;}
    for(const p of D.tunnelPortals)if(known(p.x,p.y)||D.tunnelState(run.world).stage){svg+=`<path data-underpass="${p.id}" d="M${p.x-1},${p.y+1} L${p.x},${p.y-1.5} L${p.x+1},${p.y+1}Z" fill="${D.tunnelOpen(run.world)?'#f0d192':'#9b9b90'}" stroke="#253a42" stroke-width=".4"/>`;}
    for(const p of C.FRONTIER)if(d.frontier?.sites[p.id]){const st=D.frontierState(run.world,p.id).stage;svg+=`<rect data-frontier="${p.id}" x="${p.x-1}" y="${p.y-1}" width="2" height="2" rx=".3" fill="${st>=8?'#bde196':st?'#e3bd79':'#b9b3a2'}" stroke="#263c37" stroke-width=".45"/>`;}
    for(const pool of C.EAST_POOLS)if(known(pool[0][0],pool[0][1]))svg+=`<polygon points="${pool.map(a=>a.join(',')).join(' ')}" fill="#548783"/>`;
    for(const p of C.EAST_SITES)if(d.east?.[p.id]){const st=D.eastState(run.world,p.id).stage;svg+=`<rect data-east="${p.id}" x="${p.x-1}" y="${p.y-1}" width="2" height="2" rx=".3" fill="${st>=8?'#bde196':st?'#e3bd79':'#b9b3a2'}" stroke="#263c37" stroke-width=".45"/>`;}
    svg+=`<circle cx="${player.x/T}" cy="${player.y/T}" r="1.8" fill="#fff" stroke="#333" stroke-width=".7"/></svg>`;
    panel(run,'development-atlas',title('FIELD NOTEBOOK','Expedition atlas','White dot: current position. Pale lines: old road fragments. Amber lines: your trails (20 levels). Grey: ruins. Green: operational. Dark areas unexplored.')+svg+(known(C.SKYWAY.surveyX,C.SKYWAY.y)?'<article class="v852-row"><b>Windward Skyway</b><small>Two surfaces · '+(D.skywayState(run.world).stage>=8?'Upper deck open':'Upper deck closed')+' · '+D.skywayState(run.world).crossings+' crossings</small><p>Follow the Blackstone trail toward (109, 52). Access via either approach; the original trail remains below.</p></article>':'')+
      D.landmarks.filter(l=>d.exploration[l.id]).map(l=>`<article class="v852-row"><b>${esc(l.name)}</b><small>${esc(C.REGION[l.region].name)} · ${d.exploration[l.id].solved?'Inscription solved':'Observed'} · ${D.heritageState(run.world,l.id).stage+1}/10 · ${esc(D.heritageDefs[l.id].role)}</small>${button(d.marker===l.id?'Clear marker':'Set marker',d.marker===l.id?'dev-mark:clear':`dev-mark:${l.id}`)}</article>`).join('')+button('Eastern districts','east-guide')+button('Expedition register','frontier-guide')+button('Living habitats','reserve-atlas')+button('Back','menu'));
  }
  function developmentAction(run,action){
    if(action==='dev-trail-journal')return trailJournal(run);
    if(action==='path-regrowth'){if(run.panel!=='trail-journal')throw new Error('Open Trail Journal.');edit(run,w=>D.pathSetRegrow(w,!D.pathCare(w).regrow));return trailJournal(run);}
    if(action.startsWith('path-engineer:')){if(run.panel!=='trail-journal'||run.scene.inside||run.scene._lqSurface)throw new Error('Stand on the surface and open Trail Journal.');const [,id,stage]=action.split(':'),p=run.scene.player;edit(run,w=>D.pathEngineer(w,id,Number(stage),p.x,p.y,(x,y)=>!run.scene.blocked(x,y)));run.scene._lqPathBuildPulse={id,at:run.scene.time.now,stage:Number(stage)+1};sound('gather');toast(run,'Roadbed completed · '+D.pathLabels[Number(stage)+1]);return trailJournal(run);}
    if(action==='dev-atlas')return developmentAtlas(run);
    const [verb,key,item,n]=action.split(':');
    if(verb==='dev-mark'&&run.panel==='development-atlas'){edit(run,w=>D.mark(w,key==='clear'?null:key));return developmentAtlas(run);}
    if(run.panel!=='development'||!devNear(run,run.devSite))throw new Error('Return to the interaction point.');
    const id=run.devSite,t=D.byId[id];
    edit(run,w=>{
      const d=w.development;
      if(verb==='dev-mark'&&t.kind==='landmark')D.mark(w,key==='clear'?null:id);
      else if(verb==='dev-enqueue'&&t.kind==='workshop')D.enqueue(w,key);
      else if(verb==='dev-cancel'&&t.kind==='workshop')D.cancel(w,Number(key));
      else if(verb==='dev-assign-mara'&&t.kind==='workshop')D.assign(w,Number(key),'mara',laborRoute(run));
      else if(verb==='dev-assign-player'&&t.kind==='workshop')D.assign(w,Number(key),'player');
      else if(verb==='dev-manual'&&t.kind==='workshop')D.manual(w,Number(key),(minutes,id)=>S.step(w,minutes,node=>growthClear(run,node),id));
      else if(verb==='dev-upgrade'&&t.kind==='workshop')D.upgrade(w);
      else if(verb==='dev-plant'&&t.kind==='farm')D.plant(w,id,key);
      else if(verb==='dev-water'&&t.kind==='farm')D.water(w,id);
      else if(verb==='dev-harvest'&&t.kind==='farm')D.harvest(w,id);
      else if(verb==='dev-build'&&t.kind==='build')D.build(w);
      else if(verb==='dev-deposit'&&t.kind==='build')D.deposit(w,key,Number(item));
      else if(verb==='dev-solve'&&t.kind==='landmark')D.solve(w,id,Number(key));
      else if(verb==='dev-take'&&key===id){const bag=t.kind==='workshop'?d.workshop.output:t.kind==='farm'?d.farm[id].output:t.kind==='landmark'?d.exploration[id].loot:t.kind==='build'?d.build.storage:null;D.take(w,bag,item,Number(n));D.tick(w,D.now(w),w.weather.kind);}
      else throw new Error('Unknown settlement action.');
    });
    return settlementPanel(run,id);
  }

  const charterMetricNames={operating:'Operating eastern halls',homes:'Furnished staff lodgings',food:'Food in accessible inventories',water:'Water in accessible inventories',served:'Households provisioned since yesterday',regions:'Biomes with an operating hall',orders:'Completed eastern orders',trails:'Trail segments at level 8+',roads:'Engineered segments',bridges:'Restored eastern crossings',heritage:'Restored heritage halls',habitats:'Established reserves',lessons:'Academy lessons',research:'Research licences',sales:'Actual retail purchases',paid:'Paid work orders',affinity:'Mean keeper trust',treasury:'Treasury copper'};
  const charterMoney=n=>Number(n||0).toLocaleString('en-US');
  // V100: real district atlas, worksite commissioning and visible survey stakes.
  const cityTerrainCache=new Map();
  function cityTerrain(region,x0,y0,W,H){const key=[region,x0,y0,W,H].join(','),cached=cityTerrainCache.get(key);if(cached)return cached;const colors={water:'#486d78',forest:'#46624c',scrub:'#716a65',shale:'#86664f',scree:'#91918a',riverbank:'#58756b',meadow:'#7c8050'},groups=new Map();for(let y=y0;y<y0+H;y++)for(let x=x0;x<x0+W;x++){const t=C.groundSample(x+.5,y+.5),c=colors[t.surface]||'#657260',e=Math.min(4,Math.max(0,t.elevation||0)),id=c+'|'+e;groups.set(id,(groups.get(id)||'')+`M${x} ${y}h1v1h-1z`);}const out=[...groups].map(([k,d])=>{const [c,e]=k.split('|');return `<path fill="${c}" fill-opacity="${.63+Number(e)*.075}" d="${d}"/>`;}).join('');cityTerrainCache.set(key,out);return out;}
  function cityAtlas(w,region,origin=null){const p=C.EAST_SITES.filter(s=>s.region===region),h=C.HOMES.filter(s=>D.eastById[s.site]?.region===region),projects=Object.entries(D.cityProjects(w)).filter(([,q])=>D.eastById[q.from]?.region===region);const xs=p.map(s=>s.ax).concat(h.map(s=>s.ax)),ys=p.map(s=>s.ay).concat(h.map(s=>s.ay));const x0=Math.floor(Math.min(...xs)-5),x1=Math.ceil(Math.max(...xs)+5),y0=Math.floor(Math.min(...ys)-5),y1=Math.ceil(Math.max(...ys)+5),W=x1-x0,H=y1-y0;const R=C.EAST_REGIONS.find(r=>r.id===region),small=Math.max(W,H)/55,xy=(a,b)=>`${a},${b}`;let grid='';for(let x=Math.ceil(x0/5)*5;x<=x1;x+=5)grid+=`<path d="M${x} ${y0}V${y1}" stroke="#cbd3b0" stroke-opacity=".09" stroke-width=".16"/>`;for(let y=Math.ceil(y0/5)*5;y<=y1;y+=5)grid+=`<path d="M${x0} ${y}H${x1}" stroke="#cbd3b0" stroke-opacity=".09" stroke-width=".16"/>`;let routes='';const road=D.pathCare(w).roads;for(const [id,q] of projects){routes+=`<polyline points="${q.path.map(([x,y])=>xy(x/2,y/2)).join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="#9dafac" stroke-opacity=".65" stroke-width="${small*.6}" stroke-dasharray=".7 .6"/>`;for(const k of D.cityEdges(q.path)){const t=D.trailDecode(k),s=road[k]||0,busy=q.pending?.edges.includes(k);if(s||busy)routes+=`<path d="M${t.x/T} ${t.y/T}L${t.bx/T} ${t.by/T}" stroke="${busy?'#e4ab5a':s===2?'#f5ddab':'#b9ac87'}" stroke-width="${small*.85}" stroke-linecap="round"/>`;}}let nodes='';for(const [id,a]of Object.entries(D.civicPockets(w)))if(D.eastById[D.cityProjects(w)[id].from].region===region)nodes+=`<circle cx="${a.x/T}" cy="${a.y/T}" r="${small*.7}" fill="${a.status==='open'?'#f0d39b':'#a88f62'}" stroke="#3a4941" stroke-width=".2"><title>Roadside civic court</title></circle>`;for(const [i,s] of p.entries()){const st=D.eastState(w,s.id),color=st.stage>=8?'#93cbb0':st.stage>0?'#d8b369':'#8a9390';nodes+=`<g><title>${esc(s.name)} · ${esc(D.eastStages[st.stage])}</title>${s.id===origin?`<circle cx="${s.ax}" cy="${s.ay}" r="${small*2.2}" fill="none" stroke="#fff0bd" stroke-width=".3"/>`:''}<rect x="${s.ax-small}" y="${s.ay-small}" width="${small*2}" height="${small*2}" rx="${s.monument?0:small*.4}" fill="${color}" stroke="#1c2d2c" stroke-width=".2"/><text x="${s.ax}" y="${s.ay+.36*small}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="${small*1.18}" font-weight="700" fill="#192a28">${i+1}</text></g>`;}for(const s of h){const k=small*.9;nodes+=`<g><title>${esc(s.name)} · ${esc(D.homeStages[D.homeState(w,s.id).stage])}</title><path d="M${s.ax-k} ${s.ay}L${s.ax} ${s.ay-k}L${s.ax+k} ${s.ay}V${s.ay+k}H${s.ax-k}Z" fill="${D.homeState(w,s.id).stage===3?'#cfabdc':'#6e6579'}" stroke="#242e32" stroke-width=".2"/></g>`;}return `<div class="city-atlas"><svg role="img" aria-label="Schematic district plot map" viewBox="${x0} ${y0} ${W} ${H}"><rect x="${x0}" y="${y0}" width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="${R.palette[0]}"/>${cityTerrain(region,x0,y0,W,H)}${grid}${routes}${nodes}</svg><div class="city-key"><span>■ Operating</span><span>■ Restoring</span><span>■ Ruins</span><span>⌂ Residences</span><span>┄ Surveyed · amber: works</span></div><div class="city-index">${p.map((s,i)=>`<div><b>${i+1}</b> ${esc(s.name)}</div>`).join('')}</div><small>Schematic terrain sampled from the game · entrance coordinates and surveyed routes.</small></div>`;}
  function cityBody(run){const w=run.world,reg=C.EAST_REGIONS.find(r=>r.id===run.cityRegion)||C.EAST_REGIONS[0],r=reg.id,sites=C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>p.region===r),active=sites.filter(p=>D.eastState(w,p.id).stage>=8);if(!active.some(p=>p.id===run.cityOrigin))run.cityOrigin=active[0]?.id||null;const origin=run.cityOrigin,v=D.citySummary(w,r),near=id=>!run.scene.inside&&eastNear(run,id),afford=c=>Object.entries(c).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n);let b='<div class="charter-hero"><div class="charter-emblem">X</div><div><small>V100 · CIVIL ENGINEERING OFFICE</small><h3>District masterplan</h3><p>Survey a real walking corridor, lay gravel, then pave it. Completed surfaces enter the existing trail and freight routing system.</p></div></div><div class="city-regions">'+C.EAST_REGIONS.map(q=>button(q.name,'charter-city-region:'+q.id,q.id===r)).join('')+'</div><div class="charter-kpis">'+charterKpi('Operating halls',v.active+'/'+v.sites,'In this district')+charterKpi('Independent homes',v.housed+'/'+v.homes,'Completed residences')+charterKpi('Input shortages',v.shortages,'Idle halls missing ingredients')+charterKpi('Paved projects',v.complete+'/'+v.projects,v.pending+' under construction')+'</div>'+cityAtlas(w,r,origin);b+='<h3>Commission a corridor</h3><p class="fv-copy">Stand at an operating hall in the district. Walk the entire route to another operating hall until each segment is at trail level 8 or above; bring one survey chart. Ground routes only. Survey records are limited to 24.</p>';if(active.length){b+='<div class="city-origins">'+active.map(s=>button((s.id===origin?'Selected: ':'')+s.name,'charter-city-origin:'+s.id,s.id===origin)).join('')+'</div>';if(origin){const p=D.eastById[origin],candidates=D.cityCandidates(w,origin);b+=`<p class="fv-copy">${esc(p.name)} · entrance (${p.ax}, ${p.ay}) · ${near(origin)?'At survey office':'Visit the entrance to commission work'}</p>`;b+=candidates.length?candidates.map(s=>`<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(s.name)}</b><small>Entrance (${s.ax}, ${s.ay})</small>${button('Survey · 1 chart','charter-city-survey:'+origin+':'+s.id,!near(origin)||!afford({'survey-chart':1})||Object.keys(D.cityProjects(w)).length>=24)}</article>`).join(''):'<p class="fv-copy">Restore another nearby hall or discard an unused survey.</p>';}}else b+='<p class="fv-copy">Restore two halls to begin planning.</p>';b+='<h3>Construction ledger</h3>';const entries=Object.entries(D.cityProjects(w)).filter(([,q])=>D.eastById[q.from]?.region===r);if(!entries.length)b+='<p class="fv-copy">No surveyed construction corridors in this district.</p>';for(const [id,q] of entries){const s=D.cityProgress(w,id),n=D.cityNext(w,id),j=q.pending;b+=`<article class="charter-row city-project"><b>${esc(D.eastById[q.from].name)} → ${esc(D.eastById[q.to].name)}</b><small>${s.total} half-tile segments · ${q.batches} paid batches · ${s.learned}/${s.total} currently learned</small><label>Gravel foundation <b>${s.gravel}/${s.total}</b></label><meter min="0" max="${s.total}" value="${s.gravel}"></meter><label>Dressed stone <b>${s.paved}/${s.total}</b></label><meter min="0" max="${s.total}" value="${s.paved}"></meter>${j?`<p>Work in progress · ${Math.max(0,j.ends-D.now(w))} game minutes remaining. Close the charter to advance time.</p>`:n?`<p>Next: ${n.stage===1?'gravel bed':'dressed stone'} · ${n.edges.length} segments · ${n.minutes} minutes · ${esc(costs(n.cost))}${!n.ready?' · Prosperous Town required':''}</p>${button('Commission next batch','charter-city-build:'+id+':'+n.stage,!near(q.from)||!n.ready||!afford(n.cost))}`:'<p>Fully paved — available to the freight route solver.</p>'}${!j&&!D.civicPockets(w)[id]?button('Discard survey record (keep roads)','charter-city-discard:'+id):''}</article>`;}b+=pocketLedger(run,entries);b+='<h3>Walkable civic network</h3><p class="fv-copy">A connection is counted only when every surveyed segment has a built surface. A standalone hall is not yet part of this completed-road network.</p>'+active.map(p=>{const links=entries.filter(([id,q])=>{const z=D.cityProgress(w,id);return (q.from===p.id||q.to===p.id)&&z.total>0&&z.gravel===z.total;});return '<article class="charter-row"><b>'+esc(p.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(links.length?links.map(([,q])=>D.eastById[q.from===p.id?q.to:q.from].name).join(' · '):'No completed surveyed road link')+'</small></article>';}).join('');b+='<h3>District production watch</h3>'+active.map(p=>{const t=D.eastState(w,p.id),missing=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(p.input).filter(([k,n])=>(t.stock[k]||0)<n).map(([k,n])=>[k,n-(t.stock[k]||0)]));return `<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(p.name)}</b><small>${t.job?'Order in progress':Object.keys(missing).length?'Missing '+esc(costs(missing)):'All recipe inputs available'} · ${t.runs} completed orders</small></article>`;}).join('');return b;}
  function cityAction(run,action){if(run.panel!=='charter'||run.charterTab!=='city')throw Error('Open District atlas in the charter first.');const [, ,cmd,...args]=action.split(':')[0].split('-');const values=action.split(':').slice(1);if(cmd==='region'){if(!C.EAST_REGIONS.some(r=>r.id===values[0]))throw Error('Unknown district.');run.cityRegion=values[0];run.cityOrigin=null;}else if(cmd==='origin'){const p=D.eastById[values[0]];if(!p||p.region!==run.cityRegion&&p.region!==(run.cityRegion||C.EAST_REGIONS[0].id)||D.eastState(run.world,p.id).stage<8)throw Error('Choose an operating hall in this district.');run.cityOrigin=p.id;}else if(cmd==='survey'){const [a,b]=values;if(run.scene.inside||!eastNear(run,a))throw Error('Visit the originating hall.');edit(run,w=>D.citySurvey(w,run.scene.area,a,b));}else if(cmd==='build'){const [id,stage]=values,p=D.cityProjects(run.world)[id];if(!p||run.scene.inside||!eastNear(run,p.from))throw Error('Visit the originating hall.');edit(run,w=>D.cityBuild(w,run.scene.area,id,Number(stage)));}else if(cmd==='pocket'){const id=values[0],p=D.cityProjects(run.world)[id];if(!p||run.scene.inside||!eastNear(run,p.from))throw Error('Visit the originating hall.');edit(run,w=>D.pocketBuild(w,run.scene.area,id));}else if(cmd==='discard'){edit(run,w=>D.cityDiscard(w,values[0]));}else throw Error('Unknown planning action.');return charterPanel(run,'city');}
  function cityWorkFrame(run){const sc=run.scene;if(sc.inside||run.film){run.cityWorkFx?.setVisible(false);return;}const jobs=Object.values(D.cityProjects(run.world)).filter(p=>p.pending);if(!jobs.length){run.cityWorkFx?.setVisible(false);return;}const r=sc.cameras.main.worldView;if(!run.cityWorkFx){run.cityWorkFx=sc.add.graphics().setDepth(.92);sc.areaObjs.push(run.cityWorkFx);}const g=run.cityWorkFx;g.setVisible(true);const sig=jobs.map(p=>p.pending.edges.join(',')).join('|')+'@'+Math.floor(r.x/64)+','+Math.floor(r.y/64);if(sig===run.cityWorkSig)return;run.cityWorkSig=sig;g.clear();for(const p of jobs)for(const k of p.pending.edges){const t=D.trailDecode(k),x=(t.x+t.bx)/2,y=(t.y+t.by)/2;if(x<r.x-80||x>r.right+80||y<r.y-80||y>r.bottom+80)continue;g.fillStyle(0x2a251e,.25).fillEllipse(x,y+2,12,5);g.lineStyle(3,0xd6a15b,.8).lineBetween(t.x,t.y,t.bx,t.by);g.fillStyle(0x64482b,1).fillRect(x-1,y-9,2,12);g.fillStyle(0xeabf71,1).fillRect(x+1,y-9,5,4);g.fillStyle(0x9e7040,1).fillRect(x+2,y-6,3,1);}}

  // V101 public realm: native pixel textures, seasonal planting, local interactions.
  function commonPaint(c,style,stage,pending,tended,night,phase=0){
    const P={wood:['#a98b60','#705b42','#c8b382','#e3b967','#74935c'],mosaic:['#a89ab2','#625570','#dac6c7','#d88fb2','#7c799a'],stone:['#bd9579','#775d50','#e0bba1','#df9a69','#8e9161'],water:['#849e9a','#456d6a','#c5d2ae','#9bc8d2','#689b80'],market:['#c7ae7e','#82724c','#e9d4a5','#e6a450','#9b9f5b'],library:['#9b94ab','#58596f','#ccc9d4','#c6a8dc','#788a83']}[style];
    const [base,shade,light,accent,leaf]=P,R=(x,y,w,h,col)=>{c.fillStyle=col;c.fillRect(x,y,w,h);};c.clearRect(0,0,80,56);c.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    const rand=(x,y)=>((Math.imul(x+19,374761393)^Math.imul(y+23,668265263))>>>0)%97;
    if(!stage){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let x=i%2?74:3,y=i<2?4:49;R(x,y,2,6,'#4c3d2e');R(x-1,y,4,2,accent);}for(let x=8;x<74;x+=5){R(x,3,2,1,shade);R(x,53,2,1,shade);}if(pending)for(let x=12;x<68;x+=9)R(x,43,6,3,'#a38b65');return;}
    // Bevelled paving with clipped corners and handcrafted pixel clusters.
    for(let y=3;y<54;y++){let inset=y<9?9-y:y>47?y-47:0;R(3+inset,y+1,74-2*inset,1,'#303c2c');R(3+inset,y,74-2*inset,1,y<8?light:base);}
    for(let y=8;y<49;y+=6){for(let x=7+(y%12?0:4);x<73;x+=9){R(x,y,7,4,rand(x,y)>62?light:base);R(x,y+4,8,1,shade);R(x+7,y,1,4,shade);if(rand(x,y)>43)R(x+1,y+1,3,1,light);}}
    for(let x=8;x<72;x+=4){R(x,5,3,2,light);R(x,51,3,1,shade);}
    if(stage===1){if(pending){for(let x=10;x<70;x+=8){R(x,11,5,3,'#805d3d');R(x,10,5,1,'#d0aa76');}}return;}
    function bed(x,y,w){R(x,y,w,10,shade);R(x+1,y+1,w-2,7,'#514d36');R(x+1,y,w-2,1,light);for(let k=2;k<w-2;k+=4){let j=rand(x+k,y)%3;R(x+k,y+3+j,2,4,leaf);R(x+k-1,y+3+j,4,1,leaf);if(tended){R(x+k,y+1+j,2,2,j===1?'#f4e3b3':accent);R(x+k,y+1+j,1,1,'#fff0bb');}}R(x,y+9,w,1,'#3e4236');}
    bed(8,8,20);bed(52,8,20);bed(8,39,20);bed(52,39,20);
    // Distinct paving designs; centre stays a clear walking strip.
    if(style==='mosaic'){for(let y=21;y<36;y+=3)for(let x=30;x<50;x+=3)R(x,y,2,2,(x+y)%2?accent:shade);}
    if(style==='stone'){for(let x=30;x<50;x+=5){R(x,9,4,9,light);R(x+1,10,2,7,shade);}}
    if(style==='water'){R(28,20,24,16,shade);R(30,21,20,13,'#477d88');R(31,22,18,2,'#89b9bc');for(let j=0;j<3;j++)R(32+(phase+j*4)%9,25+j*3,7,1,'#b9d9ca');}
    if(style==='wood'){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){R(30+j*5,23,4,12,j%2?shade:light);R(31+j*5,24,1,8,accent);}}
    if(style==='market'){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){R(29+j*6,22,5,3,accent);R(29+j*6,32,5,3,shade);}}
    if(style==='library'){for(let j=0;j<4;j++){R(30+j*5,24,4,10,j%2?shade:light);R(31+j*5,25,2,8,accent);}}
    if(stage<3)return;
    function bench(x,y){R(x,y+5,15,3,'#34372d');R(x+1,y+3,2,5,shade);R(x+12,y+3,2,5,shade);R(x,y,15,4,'#644b35');R(x+1,y,13,1,'#d3ad72');R(x+1,y+2,13,1,'#ad8153');}
    bench(8,23);bench(57,23);
    function lamp(x,y){R(x,y+6,5,2,'#414033');R(x+2,y,1,8,'#4d4b38');R(x,y-2,5,4,'#443c2e');R(x+1,y-2,3,3,night?'#ffdf86':accent);R(x+1,y-3,3,1,shade);if(night){c.globalAlpha=.12;R(x-3,y-5,11,10,'#ffd580');c.globalAlpha=1;}}
    lamp(5,17);lamp(70,17);lamp(5,38);lamp(70,38);
    if(pending)R(38,48,4,4,accent);
  }
  // Event props are ground decals, leaving the existing walking strip unobstructed.
  function gatheringDecor(c,style){const R=(x,y,w,h,col)=>{c.fillStyle=col;c.fillRect(x,y,w,h);};const palettes={wood:['#d3a360','#73533b'],mosaic:['#d699bb','#66528b'],stone:['#d4a38a','#79594c'],water:['#9fd3c7','#497c79'],market:['#e0bd69','#82623d'],library:['#c1adce','#65577f']},[a,b]=palettes[style];
    // Folded pennants along the north side, stitched pixel edging.
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){let x=19+i*5,y=7+Math.floor(Math.sin(i*Math.PI/8)*3);R(x,y,5,1,'#534937');R(x+1,y+1,3,2,i%2?a:b);R(x+2,y+3,1,2,i%2?a:b);}
    // Distinct display mats are not solid furniture or collision obstacles.
    for(const x of [9,55]){R(x,31,16,6,b);R(x+1,32,14,4,a);for(let k=2;k<15;k+=4)R(x+k,32,1,4,b);}
    if(style==='wood'){for(let x=12;x<24;x+=4){R(x,32,3,3,'#806042');R(x,32,2,1,'#eed0a2');}}
    else if(style==='mosaic'){for(let x=12;x<24;x+=4){R(x,32,3,3,['#a65981','#7777a2','#e8c59c'][x%3]);}}
    else if(style==='stone'){for(let x=12;x<24;x+=4){R(x,32,3,3,'#a9a39a');R(x,32,2,1,'#e1d3b7');}}
    else if(style==='water'){for(let x=12;x<24;x+=4){R(x,32,3,3,'#456e80');R(x+1,32,1,2,'#b3d9da');}}
    else if(style==='market'){for(let x=12;x<24;x+=4){R(x,32,3,3,'#936b3d');R(x,32,2,1,'#f5ce82');}}
    else {for(let x=12;x<24;x+=4){R(x,32,3,3,'#6c4e75');R(x+1,32,1,3,'#dfd3b6');}}
  }

  const commonPoint=p=>({x:p.x*T,y:p.y*T});
  function commonNear(run,p){return !!p&&reachable(run.scene,commonPoint(p),42);}
  function commonPanel(run,id){const p=D.commonById[id];if(!commonNear(run,p))throw Error('Visit the commons in person.');run.commonId=id;const w=run.world,r=D.commonState(w,id),ready=D.commonReady(w,id),active=D.commonEventOpen(w,id),day=Math.floor(D.now(w)/1440),afford=cost=>Object.entries(cost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n);let body=title('THE AGE OF WONDERS',p.name,'A public space shared by the district. Every improvement uses real materials.');body+=`<p>Phase ${r.stage}/3 · ${esc(['Reserved ground','Paved court','Planted garden','Open commons'][r.stage])} · ${r.events} gatherings completed.</p>`;if(!ready)body+='<p>Restore '+esc(D.eastById[p.site].name)+' and found the charter first.</p>';else if(r.pending)body+='<p>Construction in progress · '+(r.pending.ends-D.now(w))+' game minutes remaining.</p>';else if(r.stage<3){const c=D.commonCosts[r.stage];body+='<p>'+esc(costs(c))+' · '+D.commonMinutes[r.stage]+' minutes · Era '+(r.stage+1)+'</p>'+button('Commission phase','commons-build',!afford(c)||D.charterState(w).era<r.stage);}if(r.stage>=2)body+=button(r.tended===day?'Garden tended today':'Water the garden · 1 water','commons-tend',r.tended===day||!afford({water:1}));if(r.stage===3){const attendees=D.gatheringRoutes.filter(a=>a.common===id&&D.gatheringDuty(w,a.id)?.phase==='At the gathering');body+='<p class="fv-copy">'+(attendees.length?'Present: '+attendees.map(a=>esc(D.apprenticesById[a.id].name)).join(', '):'No apprentices at the court yet. Restored workshops send their apprentices during the first 55 minutes.')+'</p>';body+=`<h3>${esc(p.event)}</h3><p>Every seventh day, cycle day ${p.weekday+1}, ${Math.floor(p.hour/60)}:00–${Math.floor((p.hour+120)/60)}:00. Requires a tended garden and suitable weather. Contribution: 1 food, 1 water.</p>`;if(active)body+='<blockquote>'+esc(p.question)+'</blockquote>'+p.answers.map((a,i)=>button(a,'commons-attend:'+i,!afford({food:1,water:1}))).join('');else body+='<p>'+ (r.lastEvent===day?'Gathering completed today.':'The next gathering is not open yet.')+'</p>';}return panel(run,'commons',body+button('Back','close'));}
  function commonAction(run,action){if(run.panel!=='commons'||!commonNear(run,D.commonById[run.commonId]))throw Error('Stand at the public court.');const id=run.commonId;edit(run,w=>{if(action==='commons-build')D.commonBuild(w,id);else if(action==='commons-tend')D.commonTend(w,id);else if(action.startsWith('commons-attend:'))D.commonAttend(w,id,Number(action.split(':')[1]));else throw Error('Unknown public activity.');});return commonPanel(run,id);}
  function commonsBody(run){const w=run.world;return '<h3>Six district commons</h3><p class="fv-copy">Visit each plotted site to build, tend and attend its weekly English gathering. No remote construction.</p>'+D.commons.map(p=>{const r=D.commonState(w,p.id);return `<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(p.name)}</b><small>${esc(D.eastById[p.site].name)} · (${p.x}, ${p.y.toFixed(2)}) · Phase ${r.stage}/3 · ${r.events} gatherings</small><p>${esc(p.event)} · ${D.commonEventOpen(w,p.id)?'Open now':r.pending?'Under construction':'Scheduled'}</p></article>`;}).join('');}
  function commonsFrame(run){const sc=run.scene,rect=sc.cameras.main.worldView,map=run.commonViews??=new Map(),m=run.world.clock.minuteOfDay,day=Math.floor(D.now(run.world)/1440),night=m>=1140||m<375;for(const p of D.commons){let v=map.get(p.id);const x=p.x*T,y=p.y*T,visible=!sc.inside&&!run.film&&x>=rect.x-80&&x<=rect.right+80&&y>=rect.y-70&&y<=rect.bottom+70;if(!visible){v?.setVisible(false);continue;}const r=D.commonState(run.world,p.id),phase=p.style==='water'&&!run.reduced?Math.floor(run.time/270)%4:0,event=D.gatheringHours(run.world,p.id,m)?.phase==='At the gathering',key=['v102-common',p.style,r.stage,!!r.pending,r.tended===day,night,phase,event].join('-');if(!sc.textures.exists(key)){const t=sc.textures.createCanvas(key,80,56);commonPaint(t.getContext(),p.style,r.stage,!!r.pending,r.tended===day,night,phase);if(event)gatheringDecor(t.getContext(),p.style);t.refresh();}if(!v||!v.scene){v=sc.add.image(x,y,key).setOrigin(.5,.5).setDepth(.86);sc.areaObjs.push(v);map.set(p.id,v);}v.setTexture(key).setVisible(true);}}

  function charterNav(active){return '<nav class="charter-tabs">'+[['overview','Era & charter'],['people','Households'],['guilds','Guild apprentices'],['requests','Regional requests'],['market','Commerce'],['research','Research'],['chains','Supply network'],['calendar','Festivals'],['works','Public works'],['city','District atlas'],['utilities','Utilities'],['commons','Public gardens'],['legacy','Chronicle']].map(([k,n])=>button(n,'charter-tab:'+k,k===active)).join('')+'</nav>';}
  const charterKpi=(n,v,detail)=>`<div class="charter-kpi"><small>${esc(n)}</small><strong>${esc(String(v))}</strong><span>${esc(detail)}</span></div>`;
  function charterPriceChart(c){const data=c.ledger.filter(e=>e.kind==='sale');if(!data.length)return '<p class="fv-copy">No recorded sales yet. Prices appear here after completed transactions.</p>';return [...new Set(data.map(e=>e.key))].map(key=>{const q=data.filter(e=>e.key===key).slice(-8),max=Math.max(...q.map(e=>e.n));return `<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(C.INVENTORY[key][0])} · copper per unit</b><div class="charter-bars">${q.map(e=>`<div title="Day ${Math.floor(e.at/1440)+1} · ${esc(D.eastById[e.id].name)}"><strong>${e.n}</strong><i style="height:${Math.round(e.n/max*54)}px"></i><small>D${Math.floor(e.at/1440)+1}</small></div>`).join('')}</div><small>Last ${q.length} actual sales · local shop prices may differ.</small></article>`;}).join('');}
  function charterPanel(run,tab='overview'){
    const w=run.world,c=D.charterState(w),m=D.charterMetrics(w);run.charterTab=tab;
    if(!c)return panel(run,'charter',title('THE FOUNDING CHARTER','A promise to the valley','A new, optional settlement ledger. Furnish staff lodgings, fund real wages and build a community whose progress depends on actual work.')+'<div class="charter-hero"><div class="charter-emblem">VII</div><div><small>A VALLEY WORTH KEEPING</small><h3>Every home begins with a promise.</h3><p>Restore the old warehouse. Sign the charter with 1 paper sheet and 2 copper. The two coins enter the community treasury.</p></div></div>'+button('Sign the charter','charter-found',!D.canUse(w)||!(w.resources['paper-sheet']>=1&&w.resources.copper>=2))+button('Back','menu'));
    const head=title('THE LIVING CIVILIZATION','Valley charter · '+D.charterEras[c.era],'An audited record of workshops, residents, supplies and decisions. Not a single XP score.')+charterNav(tab);let body='';
    if(tab==='overview'){const req=D.charterRequirements(w);body=`<div class="charter-hero"><div class="charter-emblem">${['I','II','III','IV','V','VI','VII'][c.era]}</div><div><small>RATIFIED CIVILIZATION ERA</small><h3>${esc(D.charterEras[c.era])}</h3><p>Era titles remain in your chronicle; shortages are shown separately. The final era is a local milestone, not global completion.</p></div></div><div class="charter-era">${D.charterEras.map((n,i)=>`<span class="${i===c.era?'now':i<c.era?'done':''}" title="${esc(n)}">${['I','II','III','IV','V','VI','VII'][i]}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="charter-kpis">${charterKpi('Operating halls',m.operating+'/'+C.EAST_SITES.length,'Actual stage ≥ 8')}${charterKpi('Staff lodging',m.homes,'Furnished alcoves')}${charterKpi('Treasury',charterMoney(c.treasury),'Copper in reserve')}${charterKpi('Trade purchases',m.sales,'Completed retail sales')}</div>`;
      body+='<h3>'+ (c.era<6?'Conditions for '+esc(D.charterEras[c.era+1]):'Golden Age · current conditions')+'</h3><div class="charter-conditions">'+req.map(r=>`<div><span>${esc(charterMetricNames[r.key])}<b>${charterMoney(r.value)} / ${charterMoney(r.target)}</b></span><meter min="0" max="${r.target}" value="${Math.min(r.target,r.value)}" class="${r.ok?'met':''}"></meter></div>`).join('')+'</div>'+button('Ratify next era','charter-promote',c.era===6||!req.every(r=>r.ok));
      body+='<h3>Community economy</h3><p class="fv-copy">Contribute copper from your own backpack. Each completed order by a housed keeper pays 2 copper when the treasury can afford it; otherwise the order is recorded as unpaid. No coins are minted by the charter.</p>'+[5,10,20].map(n=>button('Contribute '+n+' copper','charter-fund:'+n,(w.resources.copper||0)<n)).join('')+'<div class="charter-kpis">'+charterKpi('Contributed',charterMoney(c.deposits),'From player inventory')+charterKpi('Wages transferred',charterMoney(c.wages),'Cumulative')+charterKpi('Food',m.food,'Pantry, backpack & halls')+charterKpi('Water',m.water,'Pantry, backpack & halls')+'</div>';
      body+='<h3>Current bottlenecks</h3>'+C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>D.eastState(w,p.id).stage>=8).map(p=>{const s=D.eastState(w,p.id),missing=Object.entries(p.input).filter(([k,n])=>(s.stock[k]||0)<n),msg=s.job?(s.job.phase==='waiting'?'Output waiting for space':!D.civicPumpOpen(w,p.id)?'Water valve closed':!D.utilityStatus(w,p.id).ok?D.utilityStatus(w,p.id).label:'Order running'):missing.length?'Missing '+costs(Object.fromEntries(missing)):'Inputs ready';return `<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(p.name)}</b><small>${esc(msg)} · ${s.runs} completed · stock ${Object.values(s.stock).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)}/48</small></article>`;}).join('');
    }else if(tab==='utilities'){body=utilityBody(run);
    }else if(tab==='commons'){body=commonsBody(run);
    }else if(tab==='city'){body=cityBody(run);
    }else if(tab==='people'){body='<p class="fv-copy">Furnish the existing worker alcove at each restored hall, using real supplies. This is assigned staff accommodation, not a detached house. Visit the rest corner to provide meals and water.</p>'+C.EAST_REGIONS.map(r=>'<h3>'+esc(r.name)+'</h3>'+C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>p.region===r.id&&D.eastState(w,p.id).stage>=8).map(p=>{const h=c.houses[p.id],pr=D.districtProfile(p.id),s=D.eastState(w,p.id);return `<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(p.keeper)} <span>${esc(pr.trait)}</span></b><small>${esc(p.name)} · ${h?'Furnished lodging':'No charter lodging'} · ${s.runs} orders</small>${h?`<div class="charter-person"><span>Wallet ${h.wallet} copper</span><span>Paid ${h.paidRuns} · Unpaid ${h.unpaidRuns}</span><span>Meal day ${h.lastMeal<0?'—':h.lastMeal+1} · Water day ${h.lastWater<0?'—':h.lastWater+1}</span><span>Wear ${h.wear}%</span></div>`:''}</article>`;}).join('')).join('');
    }else if(tab==='guilds'){body='<h3>Mentors & apprentices</h3><p class="fv-copy">62 keeper positions and 38 apprenticeship positions unlock when their workshop operates. Meet apprentices outside during their shift, contribute a material and complete a new order.</p>'+C.EAST_REGIONS.map(r=>'<h3>'+esc(r.name)+'</h3>'+D.apprentices.filter(a=>D.eastById[a.site].region===r.id).map(a=>{const l=D.apprenticeship(w,a.id),p=D.eastById[a.site];return `<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(a.name)} · ${esc(a.role)}</b><small>${esc(p.name)} · Mentor ${esc(p.keeper)} · ${D.apprenticeActive(w,a.id)?'Training '+l.stage+'/3':'Restore workshop'}</small></article>`;}).join('')).join('');}else if(tab==='requests'){const active=D.regionalCargo(w);body='<h3>Regional commissions</h3><p class="fv-copy">Collect consignments from the source workshop panel; deliver at the recipient. '+(active?'Carrying: '+esc(active.name):'No sealed shipment currently carried.')+'</p>'+D.regionalRequests.map(q=>`<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(q.name)}</b><small>${esc(D.eastById[q.from].name)} → ${esc(D.eastById[q.to].name)} · ${q.n} × ${esc(C.INVENTORY[q.key][0])} · ${['Available','In transit','Delivered'][D.regionalState(w,q.id).stage]}</small></article>`).join('');}else if(tab==='market'){const day=Math.floor(D.now(w)/1440);body='<div class="charter-note">Each evening at 18:30 residents place local orders according to their budget and unmet needs. Goods leave the shop’s actual stock; payment moves from the buyer’s purse to the till. This is a ledger-based local market, not physical shopper pathfinding.</div>'+C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>D.eastState(w,p.id).stage>=8&&Object.keys(p.output).some(k=>D.charterCanSell(w,p.id,k))).map(p=>{const s=c.shops[p.id],goods=Object.keys(p.output).filter(k=>D.charterCanSell(w,p.id,k));return `<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(p.name)}</b><small>${s?.till||0} copper in till · ${s?.sales||0} purchases</small>${goods.map(k=>`<div class="charter-offer"><span>${esc(C.INVENTORY[k][0])}<small>${D.eastState(w,p.id).stock[k]||0} stock · ${D.charterDemand(w,k,p.region,day)} remaining buyers</small></span><strong>${D.charterQuote(w,p.id,k)} c</strong><em>${s?.keys[k]?'Open':'Closed'}</em></div>`).join('')}</article>`;}).join('')+'<h3>Recorded sale prices</h3>'+charterPriceChart(c)+'<h3>Recent transactions</h3>'+c.ledger.slice().reverse().slice(0,25).map(e=>`<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(e.kind==='sale'?C.INVENTORY[e.key][0]+' sale':e.kind)}</b><small>Day ${Math.floor(e.at/1440)+1} · ${esc(e.id?D.eastById[e.id].name:'Community treasury')}${e.buyer?' → '+esc(D.eastById[e.buyer].keeper):''} · ${e.n} ${e.kind==='maintenance'?'kit':'copper'}</small></article>`).join('');
    }else if(tab==='research'){body='<div class="charter-hero"><div class="charter-emblem">✧</div><div><small>KNOWLEDGE NETWORK</small><h3>Ten disciplines · three stages</h3><p>Foundation licences retain their original effects. Each advanced stage improves future orders at its research partner by a further 8 percentage points; ongoing orders keep their rate.</p></div></div>'+Object.entries(D.studyBranches).map(([key,t])=>{const level=D.studyLevel(w,key);return `<article class="charter-row v87-study"><b>${esc(t.name)}</b><small>${esc(D.eastById[t.site].name)} · ${level}/3</small><p>${esc(D.charterTech[key].detail)}</p><div class="v87-nodes">${t.levels.map((q,i)=>`<div class="${i<level?'complete':i===level?'next':''}"><strong>${i+1} · ${esc(q.label)}</strong><small>${i===0?'Foundation effect':i*8+'% shorter future orders at partner'} · ${esc(D.charterEras[q.era])}</small><small>${esc(costs(q.cost))}</small>${i===level?button('Research',i===0?'charter-research:'+key:'charter-study:'+key+':'+level,c.era<q.era||D.eastState(w,t.site).stage<8||!Object.entries(q.cost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n)):''}</div>`).join('')}</div></article>`;}).join('')+'<h3>Maintenance reserve</h3><p>'+c.kits+'/30 repair kits</p>'+button('Deposit repair kit','charter-kit',!(w.resources['repair-kit']>0)||c.kits>=30);
    }else if(tab==='calendar'){body='<div class="charter-hero"><div class="charter-emblem">✿</div><div><small>YEAR '+(D.festivalYear(w)+1)+' / DAY '+D.festivalDay(w)+'</small><h3>The valley calendar</h3><p>Two days for each event. Visit its restored venue, answer an English question and contribute real supplies. Community morale rises once per year per celebration.</p></div></div>'+D.festivals.map(f=>{const live=D.festivalActive(w,f),done=D.festivalDone(w,f.id),near=!run.scene.inside&&eastNear(run,f.site),venue=D.eastState(w,f.site).stage>=8;return `<article class="charter-row"><small>${live?'HAPPENING NOW':'DAYS '+f.day+'–'+(f.day+1)} · ${esc(D.eastById[f.site].name)}</small><b>${esc(f.name)}</b><p>${esc(f.activity)} · ${esc(costs(f.cost))}</p><small>Community morale +${f.morale} · ${done?'Participated this year':!venue?'Restore venue':near?'At venue':'Visit the venue entrance'}</small>${live&&near&&venue&&!done?'<p>'+esc(f.question)+'</p>'+f.answers.map((a,i)=>button(a,'charter-festival:'+f.id+':'+i,!Object.entries(f.cost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n))).join(''):''}</article>`;}).join('');
    }else if(tab==='chains'){body='<div class="charter-hero"><div class="charter-emblem">⇄</div><div><small>SUPPLY NETWORK</small><h3>Follow the material</h3><p>Dispatch actual stock to a consuming workshop using the existing physical freight runner. Once the goods arrive, start its next production order. Routes respect terrain and restored crossings.</p></div></div>'+D.chainRoutes.map(r=>{const v=D.chainStatus(w,r);return `<article class="charter-row"><b>${esc(r.name)}</b><div class="v87-route"><span>${esc(D.eastById[r.from].name)}<small>${v.stock} in stock</small></span><strong>→ ${esc(C.INVENTORY[r.key][0])} →</strong><span>${esc(D.eastById[r.to].name)}<small>${v.need} per order · ${v.space} storage free</small></span></div><small>${v.delivered} delivered in recent freight history</small>${button('Dispatch '+v.quantity+' units','charter-chain:'+r.id,!v.ready)}</article>`;}).join('');
    }else if(tab==='works'){const complete=D.publicWorks.filter(p=>D.publicState(w,p.id).stage===3).length;body='<div class="charter-kpis">'+charterKpi('Partner works',complete+'/10','Completed improvements')+charterKpi('Under construction',D.publicWorks.filter(p=>D.publicState(w,p.id).pending).length,'World-clock projects')+'</div><p class="fv-copy">These are upgrades to existing partner halls, not ten new standalone mega-buildings. Finish three paid phases to reduce the partner hall’s future order duration by 10%. Leave the panel to let time advance. Existing orders retain their original duration.</p>'+D.publicWorks.map(p=>{const v=D.publicState(w,p.id),ready=D.publicReady(w,p),stage=['Survey & materials','Structural repairs','Equipment fitting','Operational'][v.stage],cost=p.cost[v.stage],wait=v.pending?Math.max(0,v.pending.ends-D.now(w)):0;return `<article class="charter-row public-row"><small>${esc(D.charterEras[p.era])} · ${esc(D.charterTech[p.tech].name)}</small><b>${esc(p.name)}</b><p>${esc(p.detail)}</p><small>Partners: ${esc(D.eastById[p.site].name)} + ${esc(D.eastById[p.partner].name)}</small><meter min="0" max="3" value="${v.stage}"></meter><small>${v.stage}/3 · ${esc(stage)}${v.pending?' · '+wait+' game min remaining':''}</small>${v.stage===3?'<p>Operational · 10% shorter future production orders.</p>':v.pending?'<p>Materials committed. Work in progress.</p>':'<p>Next phase: '+esc(costs(cost))+' · '+p.minutes[v.stage]+' game min</p>'+button('Commission phase','charter-works:'+p.id+':'+v.stage,!ready||!Object.entries(cost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n))}${!ready?'<small>Locked: restore both halls, research and ratify the required era.</small>':''}</article>`;}).join('');
    }else{body='<h3>Chronicle of decisions</h3>' +c.legacy.slice().reverse().map(e=>`<article class="charter-row"><small>DAY ${Math.floor(e.at/1440)+1} · ${esc(e.event.toUpperCase())}</small><b>${esc(e.detail)}</b></article>`).join('');}
    panel(run,'charter',head+body+(tab==='works'?'<h3>Independent public sites · 12</h3>'+C.EAST_SITES.filter(p=>p.monument).map(p=>{const q=D.eastState(w,p.id);return `<article class="charter-row"><small>${esc(p.project?'PUBLIC PROJECT':'SIGNATURE LANDMARK')} · ${esc(C.REGION[p.region].name)} · (${p.x}, ${p.y})</small><b>${esc(p.name)}</b><p>${esc(p.note)}</p><meter min="0" max="9" value="${q.stage}"></meter><small>${esc(D.eastStages[q.stage])} · ${D.monumentReady(w,p.id)?'Foundation authorised':'Partner works required'}</small>${button('Inspect at entrance','east-open:'+p.id,run.scene.inside||!eastNear(run,p.id))}</article>`;}).join(''):'')+button('Refresh ledger','charter-tab:'+tab)+button('Back','menu'));
  }
  function charterStation(run,p,o){const c=D.charterState(run.world);if(!c)return '';const w=run.world,h=c.houses[p.id];let b='';if(o.id==='rest'){b='<h3>Assigned staff accommodation</h3>'+(!h?'<p class="fv-copy">Furnish this alcove for '+esc(p.keeper)+' · '+esc(costs(D.charterHomeCost))+'</p>'+button('Furnish staff lodging','charter-house',!Object.entries(D.charterHomeCost).every(([k,n])=>(w.resources[k]||0)>=n)):`<p class="fv-copy">${esc(p.keeper)} · personal purse ${h.wallet} copper · paid orders ${h.paidRuns}</p>`+button('Provide 1 food','charter-provision:food',!(w.resources.food>0)||(h.lastMeal===Math.floor(D.now(w)/1440)||D.districtMemory(w,p.id).mealDay===Math.floor(D.now(w)/1440)))+button('Provide 1 water','charter-provision:water',!(w.resources.water>0)||h.lastWater===Math.floor(D.now(w)/1440)));}else if(o.id==='aux'&&h){b='<h3>Equipment condition</h3><p>Wear '+h.wear+'% · affects the duration of the next order, not work already in progress.</p><meter min="0" max="100" value="'+h.wear+'"></meter>'+button('Service worn equipment · 1 kit','charter-service',!h.wear||!!D.eastState(w,p.id).job||!(w.resources['repair-kit']>0));}else if(o.id==='stock'){const goods=Object.keys(p.output).filter(k=>D.charterCanSell(w,p.id,k)),s=c.shops[p.id];if(goods.length)b='<h3>Local retail</h3>'+goods.map(k=>'<p>'+esc(C.INVENTORY[k][0])+' · '+D.charterQuote(w,p.id,k)+' copper/unit</p>'+button(s?.keys[k]?'Suspend sales':'Offer to residents','charter-license:'+k)).join('')+button('Collect till (up to 10 copper)','charter-collect',!s?.till);b+=button('Refresh inventory','district-refresh');}return b;}
  function charterAction(run,action){if(action.startsWith('charter-city-'))return cityAction(run,action);if(action==='charter-open')return charterPanel(run);if(action.startsWith('charter-tab:')){if(run.panel!=='charter')throw new Error('Open the charter.');return charterPanel(run,action.slice(12));}if(run.panel==='district-room'){const d=districtInside(run);if(!d?.o)throw new Error('Stand by the correct station.');const {p,o}=d;if(action==='charter-house'&&o.id==='rest')edit(run,w=>D.charterHouse(w,p.id));else if(action.startsWith('charter-provision:')&&o.id==='rest')edit(run,w=>D.charterProvision(w,p.id,action.slice(18)));else if(action==='charter-service'&&o.id==='aux')edit(run,w=>D.charterService(w,p.id));else if(action.startsWith('charter-license:')&&o.id==='stock'){const key=action.slice(16);edit(run,w=>D.charterLicense(w,p.id,key,!D.charterState(w).shops[p.id]?.keys[key]));}else if(action==='charter-collect'&&o.id==='stock')edit(run,w=>D.charterCollect(w,p.id));else throw new Error('Visit the matching workshop station.');sound('gather');return districtRoomPanel(run);}if(run.panel!=='charter')throw new Error('Open the charter.');if(action==='charter-found')edit(run,w=>D.charterFound(w));else if(action.startsWith('charter-fund:'))edit(run,w=>D.charterFund(w,Number(action.slice(13))));else if(action==='charter-promote')edit(run,w=>D.charterPromote(w));else if(action.startsWith('charter-research:'))edit(run,w=>D.charterResearch(w,action.slice(17)));else if(action==='charter-kit')edit(run,w=>D.charterKit(w));else if(action.startsWith('charter-study:')){const [,id,level]=action.split(':');edit(run,w=>D.studyAdvance(w,id,Number(level)));}else if(action.startsWith('charter-festival:')){const [,id,answer]=action.split(':'),f=D.festivals.find(f=>f.id===id);if(!f||run.scene.inside||!eastNear(run,f.site))throw Error('Visit the festival venue first.');edit(run,w=>D.festivalJoin(w,id,Number(answer)));}else if(action.startsWith('charter-chain:')){edit(run,w=>D.chainSend(w,run.scene.area,Number(action.slice(14))));}else if(action.startsWith('charter-works:')){const [,id,stage]=action.split(':');edit(run,w=>D.publicStart(w,id,Number(stage)));}else throw new Error('Unknown charter action.');sound('gather');return charterPanel(run,run.charterTab||'overview');}

  function menu(run) {
    if(ambientEnabled())ambientStart(run);
    panel(run, 'menu', title('The Wild World','Valley menu') +
      '<div class="fv-grid">' + button('Backpack · I', 'inventory', !run.world) +
      button('Day journal', 'journal', !run.world) + button('Trail journal · 20 levels','dev-trail-journal',!run.world) + button('Replay opening', 'replay', !run.world || !!run.scene.inside) +
      button('Graphics · ' + Art.QUALITY[run.quality].name, 'effects') +
      button('Civilization charter · 7 eras','charter-open',!run.world)+button('Eastern crossings · 6 spans','cross-guide',!run.world)+button('Eastern districts · 6 biomes','east-guide',!run.world)+button('Mountain expedition · 10 landmarks','frontier-guide',!run.world)+button('Highland expedition','highland-guide', !run.world || !!run.scene.inside) + button('Wildlife field guide', 'nature-guide', !run.world || !!run.scene.inside) + button('World map', 'world-map') + button('Expedition atlas', 'dev-atlas', !run.world) + button('Valley workforce','guild-roster',!run.world)+button('Living habitats','reserve-atlas',!run.world)+button('Ambient audio · '+(ambientEnabled()?'On':'Off'),'ambient-toggle')+button('Courier & trade routes','caravan',!run.world) + button('Progress backup', 'backup') + button('Recovery snapshots', 'recovery') + '</div>' +
      '<p class="fv-copy fv-help">WASD or arrows to walk. E to interact or pick up a pile. 1–5 selects a tool. Walk down through a doorway to leave a room.</p>');
  }

  function natureGuidePanel(run){
    const data=Art.natureGuide(run.scene),names=Object.keys(run.world.development.fieldGuide||{});
    const near=data.nearby.map(a=>{const d=data.species[a.kind];return '<div class="nature-entry"><div><b>'+esc(d.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(d.vi)+' · '+a.distance+' tiles away</small></div>'+button('Observe','nature-observe:'+a.id)+'</div>';}).join('');
    const catalog=Object.entries(data.species).map(([key,d])=>'<article class="nature-card"><span class="nature-tag">'+(names.includes(key)?'OBSERVED':'FIELD NOTES')+'</span><b>'+esc(d.name)+'</b><small>'+esc(d.vi)+' · '+esc(d.habitat)+'</small><p>'+esc(d.note)+'</p></article>').join('');
    return panel(run,'nature-guide',title('VALLEY NATURALIST','Wildlife field guide','Observe nearby wildlife to practice English. Observations are saved in your expedition journal.')+'<div class="nature-intro"><strong>'+names.length+' / '+Object.keys(data.species).length+'</strong><span>Species recorded in your journal</span></div><h3 class="nature-section">Nearby wildlife</h3>'+(near||'<p class="fv-copy">No animals in observation range. Explore another habitat or time of day.</p>')+'<h3 class="nature-section">Species reference</h3><div class="nature-catalog">'+catalog+'</div>'+button('Back','menu'));
  }
  function journal(run) {
    const tasks = C.QUESTS.map(([id, name, detail]) =>
      '<li class="' + (run.world.quests[id].done ? 'fv-done' : '') + '"><b>' + esc(name) +
      '</b><span>' + esc(run.world.quests[id].done ? 'Complete' : detail) + '</span></li>').join('');
    const marks=[['wayfarer-stone','Wayfarers’ Arch'],['quarry-tally','Silverwater Cut'],...D.landmarks.map(l=>[l.id,l.name])]
      .filter(([id])=>run.world.clues[id])
      .map(([id,label])=>'<p class="fv-copy"><b>'+esc(label)+'</b><br>'+esc(run.world.clues[id].text)+'</p>').join('');
    panel(run,'journal',title('Forgotten Hamlet','Day journal') +
      '<ol class="fv-quests">' + tasks + '</ol>' +
      marks+button('Back', 'menu'));
  }

  function openInventory(run, atStorage) {
    if (!run.world) return;
    const world=run.world;
    const stock=world.storage;
    const amount=key=>Kit.amountOf(world,key);
    const normalize=text=>text.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replaceAll('đ','d');
    const query=normalize(run.search||'').trim();
    const keys=Object.keys(C.INVENTORY).filter(key=>atStorage?
      Object.hasOwn(Kit.limits,key)&&(amount(key)||stock[key]):
      amount(key)&&(!run.category||C.INVENTORY[key][2]===run.category)&&
      normalize(C.INVENTORY[key].slice(0,2).join(' ')).includes(query));
    keys.sort((a,b)=>run.sort==='amount'?amount(b)-amount(a)||a.localeCompare(b):
      C.INVENTORY[a][0].localeCompare(C.INVENTORY[b][0]));
    const item = key => {
      const [english, viet, category, info, rarity] = C.INVENTORY[key];
      const count=amount(key),protectedItem=Kit.protectedItem(key);
      const sizes=count&&!protectedItem?Kit.stacks(world,key):null;
      const stack=(n)=>'<span class="fv-stack">'+esc(n)+'</span>';
      const piles=sizes?'<div class="fv-stack-row" aria-label="Stacks">'+
        (sizes.whole?stack(sizes.whole>1?sizes.whole+' × '+Kit.limit(key):Kit.limit(key)):'')+
        (sizes.partial?stack(sizes.partial):'')+sizes.split.map(stack).join('')+'</div>':'';
      const sameStack=sizes?.whole?Kit.limit(key):sizes?.partial;
      const controls=atStorage?
        '<small>Stored '+(stock[key]||0)+'</small>'+
        button('Store 1','store:'+key,!count)+button('Take 1','take:'+key,!stock[key])+
        button('Store all','store-all:'+key,!count)+button('Take all','take-all:'+key,!stock[key]):
        (!protectedItem&&Object.hasOwn(Kit.limits,key)?
          (sameStack>1?button('Split stack','split:'+key,Kit.slotsUsed(world)>=Kit.capacity(world)):'')+
          (sizes?.split.length?button('Merge stacks','merge:'+key):'')+
          (!run.scene.inside?button('Put down…','put-down:'+key):''):'');
      return '<li'+(atStorage?' draggable="true" data-pack-drag="'+esc(key)+'"':'')+
        '><span class="fv-item-icon"><img alt="" src="' + Art.inventoryIcon(key) +
        '"></span><div><b>' + esc(run.viet ? english + ' · ' + viet : english) +
        '</b><small>' + esc(category + ' · ' + rarity+(protectedItem?' · Protected':'')) + '</small><p>' + esc(info) +
        '</p>'+piles+'</div><div class="fv-item-amount"><b>×'+count+'</b>'+controls+'</div></li>';
    };
    const categories = ['Tools',...new Set(Object.values(C.INVENTORY).map(value => value[2]))];
    const categoryButtons = atStorage ? '' : '<div class="fv-filters">' +
      button('All', 'category:') + categories.map(name => button(name, 'category:' + name)).join('') + '</div>';
    const total = Object.values(stock).reduce((sum, amount) => sum + amount, 0);
    const tools = atStorage || (run.category && run.category!=='Tools') ? '' : Object.entries(world.equipment.instances)
      .filter(([,tool])=>normalize(Kit.definitions[tool.definitionId].name+' '+Kit.definitions[tool.definitionId].vietnamese).includes(query))
      .sort(([,a],[,b])=>Kit.definitions[a.definitionId].name.localeCompare(Kit.definitions[b.definitionId].name))
      .map(([id])=>inventoryTool(run,id)).join('');
    const packed=Kit.slotsUsed(world),max=Kit.capacity(world);
    const meter='<p class="fv-pack-meter"><span>Pack '+world.backpack.tier+' · '+packed+'/'+max+' slots</span>'+
      '<meter min="0" max="'+max+'" value="'+Math.min(max,packed)+'" aria-label="Backpack capacity"></meter></p>';
    const slots=atStorage?'<div class="fv-pack-targets"><span data-pack-drop="store">To warehouse</span>'+
      '<span data-pack-drop="take">To backpack</span></div>':
      '<div class="fv-pack-slots" aria-label="Tool slots">'+world.equipment.hotbar.map((ref,index)=>
        '<span data-pack-drop="slot:'+index+'"><b>'+(index+1)+'</b> '+esc(itemName(Kit.itemInfo(world,ref)))+'</span>').join('')+'</div>';
    const search=atStorage?'':'<div class="fv-pack-find"><input type="search" id="fv-pack-search" maxlength="60" '+
      'aria-label="Search backpack" placeholder="Search English or Vietnamese" value="'+esc(run.search||'')+'">'+
      '<select id="fv-pack-sort" aria-label="Sort backpack"><option value="name">By name</option>'+
      '<option value="amount"'+(run.sort==='amount'?' selected':'')+'>By quantity</option></select></div>';
    const rows=tools+keys.map(item).join('');
    panel(run, atStorage ? 'storage' : 'inventory', title(atStorage ? 'The Storehouse' : 'Field kit',
      atStorage ? 'Warehouse inventory' : 'Backpack', atStorage ? total+' / 120 units stored' : '')+
      meter+slots+search+categoryButtons+(rows?'<ul class="fv-inventory">'+rows+'</ul>':'<p class="fv-copy">No matching items.</p>')+
      button(run.viet ? 'Hide Vietnamese' : 'Show Vietnamese', 'vietnamese') + ' ' + button('Back', atStorage ? 'warehouse' : 'menu'));
  }

  function quantityPanel(run,key,drop) {
    if(!Object.hasOwn(Kit.limits,key)||(drop&&run.scene.inside))return;
    const sizes=Kit.stacks(run.world,key);
    const stack=sizes.whole?Kit.limit(key):sizes.partial;
    const max=drop?run.world.resources[key]:stack-1;
    if(max<1)throw new Error('That stack is no longer available.');
    const name=C.INVENTORY[key][0];
    run.splitting=key;
    run.puttingDown=drop;
    panel(run,'split-stack',title('Backpack',(drop?'Put down ':'Split ')+name)+
      '<form id="fv-split" class="fv-pack-find"><label for="fv-quantity">Quantity</label>'+
      '<input id="fv-quantity" name="quantity" type="number" min="1" max="'+max+'" value="1" required>'+
      '<button type="submit">'+(drop?'Put down':'Split stack')+'</button></form>'+button('Back','inventory'));
  }

  function inventoryTool(run,id) {
    const info=Kit.itemInfo(run.world,Kit.toolRef(id));
    const {tool,definition}=info;
    const selected=Kit.selectedTool(run.world)?.id===id;
    const seat=run.world.equipment.hotbar.indexOf(info.ref)+1;
    const name=definition.name+(run.viet?' · '+definition.vietnamese:'');
    return '<li draggable="true" data-pack-drag="'+esc(id)+'"><span class="fv-item-icon"><img alt="" src="'+itemIcon(info)+'"></span><div><b>'+esc(name)+
      '</b><small>'+esc(definition.category+' · '+Kit.TIER[definition.tier].name)+(seat?' · Slot '+seat:'')+
      '</small><p>'+esc(definition.description)+'</p></div><div class="fv-item-amount"><b>'+
      tool.durability+'/'+tool.maxDurability+'</b>'+button(selected?'Equipped':'Equip','equip:'+id,selected)+
      button('Assign','assign:'+info.ref)+'</div></li>';
  }

  function assignPanel(run, ref) {
    const info=Kit.itemInfo(run.world,ref);
    if(info?.kind!=='tool')throw new Error('Only tools can go in the hotbar.');
    run.assigning=ref;
    const slots=run.world.equipment.hotbar.map((entry,index)=>
      button((index+1)+' · '+itemName(Kit.itemInfo(run.world,entry)),'place:'+index,entry===ref));
    panel(run, 'assign', title('Hotbar','Assign '+itemName(info),
      'If this item already has a slot, the two slots swap.')+
      '<div class="fv-grid">'+slots.join('')+'</div><p>'+button('Back','inventory')+'</p>');
  }

  function loanRack(run) {
    const rows=Kit.starter.map(type=>{
      const status=Kit.rackStatus(run.world,type);
      const name=Kit.definitions[type].name;
      const label=status==='borrow'?'Borrow '+name:status==='refit'?'Refit '+name:name+' is usable';
      return button(label,'rack:'+type,status==='usable');
    });
    panel(run,'loan-rack',title('Arven’s House','Loan rack',
      'Arven keeps salvaged parts here. A loan or refit is available when you have no usable tool of that kind.')+
      '<div class="fv-grid">'+rows.join('')+'</div><p>'+button('Back','close')+'</p>');
  }

  function warehousePanel(run) {
    const building = run.world.buildings['ruined-warehouse'];
    const ready = S.ready(run.world);
    const canRepair = run.world.quests['the-herbalist'].done;
    const caption = ready ? 'A sound roof and an oil lamp. The shelves are ready for supplies.' :
      building.status === 'repairing' ? 'Arven is working on the frame. The roof and storage will be ready soon.' :
      'The roof is split and the door has come loose. Repair requires 20 wood, 10 stone and 8 food for the work.';
    panel(run, 'warehouse', title('Forgotten Hamlet', 'The warehouse', caption) +
      '<div class="fv-grid">' +
      (ready ? button('Open storage', 'storage') : building.status === 'damaged' ?
        button('Confirm repair', 'repair', !canRepair) : '') +
      (!run.scene.inside ? button('Look inside', 'inside-warehouse') : '') + button('Back', 'close') + '</div>' +
